The browser detects speech bursts, the speech recognition on the Spark checks the phrase, nothing goes to the internet. The screen stays on; on a phone keep the app in the foreground.

**The "Me" window:** grouped like the Features page (conversation, my data, my day, mail and contacts, home, on the go and devices, access); on a computer it starts with an *Overview* (state per page, e.g. "2 calendars connected"), on a phone the state sits below each list entry. Notifications and the daily briefing have the page *Notifications*. The button with your name (or "Guest") at the top of the history and the settings icon below the face open the window. On the left: Conversation, logged in Memory, Log and Security, depending on features Documents, Calendar, E-mail, Smart home, Voice, Von selbst (speaking up by itself) and Raum-Modus (room mode); as a guest "Sign in". Conversation holds listening (stop on silence, live transcript, barge-in and its sensitivity), answer (voice, profiles only; speaking rate, answer length) and display. Guests always hear the default voice. Logged in, settings live in the profile on the Spark and apply on every device, including speakers with a device key; as a guest only in the browser. "Hey Spark" is set per device (it keeps microphone and screen awake). Defaults for guests and new profiles: Settings → Defaults. The speaking rate keeps the pitch and also works for other apps that send `speed` to the TTS API (vllm-omni backend).

**Own documents:** logged-in profiles upload own files under "Me" (PDF, Word, Excel, PowerPoint, OpenDocument, text, Markdown, HTML, CSV, e-mail as .eml; up to 20 MB each, at most 200 per profile). When asked, the assistant searches them and names document and page below the answer. Guests upload nothing, no profile sees another's documents. Can be turned off under Features.

**Knowledge from uploads:** a profile's documents live in the SQLite file `users/<profile>/wissen.db` (documents, text pieces, FTS5 full-text index over rough word stems, meaning vectors, pages still to be read). Older documents (`docs/<id>.json`) are taken over when first opened, also after restoring an old backup. The backup copies the file with SQLite's backup function, so it is complete even while being written. Under Me → Documents "View" shows the stored text with pages (and a kept original: pictures and PDFs in the browser, everything else as a download), every document has a switch "the assistant searches it", "Try the search" shows hits without the language model, and sources below an answer can be clicked. Three switches below "Own documents", off by default, only the admin under Features; once released they work for every profile (admin defaults never switch them on, never guests):
- *Read pictures and scans:* photos (JPEG, PNG, WebP) and PDF pages without text (the page's biggest picture) are cleaned like chat pictures (format check, at most 40 megapixels, upright, 1600 pixels, no location or camera data) and wait in the database. The language model reads page by page and briefly describes what it shows, with no tools and only after a minute without anyone talking to the assistant (`wissen.quiet()`, where the speech priority rule hooks in); at most 30 pages per document, 200 waiting and 100 read pages per profile and day, two tries per page. Text in a picture stays information, never a command. In the assistant, "Picture to My documents" below the answer keeps an attached picture; in Telegram `/merken` as an answer to a photo or as its caption (also needs "Photos over Telegram").
- *Meaning search:* multilingual-e5-small (quantized ONNX, about 120 MB) runs on the CPU in its own low-priority process (`docembed.py`, `embed_worker.py`; at most half of the cores, as `SCHED_IDLE` only in otherwise unused time, so speech and the panel come first; a partial index finds pieces without a vector), starts only with at least 8.5 GB of free memory, takes about 300 MB and ends after 10 minutes without work. Work means only new pieces and questions to the document search; "Search locally first" uses the model only when it runs anyway and does not extend the 10 minutes. Status → Summary shows a green "working" only while computing, otherwise a green "standby" (loaded with its memory, or not loaded), plus what last needed it and in how many minutes it stops; the check also runs during a Notaus. Pieces get a vector in quiet moments; the search merges full-text and meaning ranks. A question waits at most 8 seconds for its vector, otherwise full text only. The model comes once from Hugging Face; checksums are then kept in `state/doc-embed.json`, other files are refused. Fixed packages: onnxruntime 1.22.1, tokenizers 0.22.1.
- *Keep originals:* the file stays under `users/<profile>/docs/files/` (pictures cleaned), up to the space per profile (`chat.doc_quota_mb`, 50 to 5000, default 500); when full only the text is kept. Opening only as a download (`Content-Disposition: attachment`, HTML as `application/octet-stream`), never as a panel page.
Security: document text is outside text (`READS_OUTSIDE`): wrapped as data, actions and memory locked, never learned. After `document_search` the web search is locked for the rest of that answer too (a document could otherwise carry content away). File names go to the model only quoted, shortened and without data markers. API: `GET /api/profile/wissen`, `PUT /api/profile/wissen/{id}` (`use`), `GET /api/profile/wissen/{id}/file` (`?view=1` for pictures and PDFs), `GET /api/profile/wissen/{id}/text` (also for the iPhone app under My things → Documents), `POST /api/profile/wissen/search`, `POST /api/profile/wissen/picture`; all need a profile login, changes only from the own browser, rate limit `doc`. Self-tests: `app/tests/test_wissen.py`.

**Document status:** under Status → Summary the card “Own documents” appears once the admin switches on “Read pictures and scans” or “Meaning search” (`GET /api/admin/wissen`, admin login only). It shows the meaning model's state (off, starting, running with its memory in MiB, waiting for 8.5 GiB free memory, error with reason), pieces with a vector, waiting pages, pages read today and when last. Only sums over all profiles, never profile or document names or contents.

**Progress:** under Me → Documents every document being read shows "page 3 of 12 read" with a bar and why it is not moving: "reading page 4 now", "waits for a quiet minute" (someone talks to the assistant), "daily limit reached, continues tomorrow" or "Read pictures and scans is off". With meaning search "meaning 40 of 120" follows (and whether the model waits for free memory). While something runs and the list is open, it refreshes every 10 seconds. Everyone sees only their own documents. The iPhone app shows "page x of y read".

**Shared documents:** with the admin switch *Features → Own documents → Shared documents* (off by default, released for every profile) every finished document has "For everyone". A ticked document stays in its owner's database and is not read again; every other allowed profile finds it in its search (source "…, geteilt von name") and under "Shared for everyone by others" with "View". Only the owner takes the sharing back, changes or deletes; the admin sees under Status → Summary what is shared and can take it back. Guests, Telegram without "personal" and voices a shared speaker does not recognize as its owner get no shared documents. Their text stays outside text. Agents search only their own documents.

**Sharing with profiles:** admin switch *Features → Sharing with profiles* (`chat.share`, off by default; once released for every profile, guests never). Every finished own document has „Share“: a dialog with a person search, *Off* or *See* per profile, plus „All profiles“ (like „For everyone“, with *Shared documents*). Saving needs a fresh code. The other profile finds the document under Me → Documents (source „From others“), in its search and under **Me → Shared**; for its assistant it is outside text. Me → Shared shows everything in one place in two tabs: *Shared with me* (documents, calendars and lists of others, „new“ since the last visit, Open) and *Shared by me* (per person „Take back“, for documents „Change“; works at once). The data stays with the owner, no copy; calendars and lists are shared on their own page, they hang on `geteilt.py` as adapters. *Note on shared things* (Me → Shared, on by default): a note „Name shared something with you“ to the recipient's devices, at most one every 10 minutes, more are collected. Limits: 50 people per document, 500 singly shared documents per profile. Stored in `users/<profile>/geteilt.json`. Live (tile „Shared“) and logs (area messages, `geteilt: …`) show numbers only.

**Complete scans and reading again:** scanned PDF pages (under 40 characters of text) are rendered as whole pages with pypdfium2 (fixed version 4.30.0, about 30 MB of memory for a moment per page) and handed to the language model; so pages stored as strips or JBIG2 are not lost. Up to 300 scanned pages per document are read, at most 100 per profile and day; the rest follows on the next days. Pages from knowledge folders (NAS) and own pages (upload, app, Telegram, notebooks) count apart, up to 600 waiting each, own pages are read first so a big knowledge folder does not block an upload. If the own queue is full, the message says how many pages wait and what helps (upload later or delete a document still being read). The document names any limit that applies ("only the first 300 of 412 pages without text", "2 pages without a readable picture skipped", "pages without text not read (Read pictures and scans is off)"). "Read again" under Me → Documents reads a document once more from its kept original (owner only, only with "Keep originals"); place, "For everyone" and "The assistant searches it" stay. A document attached to a question in the iPhone app takes along only its first 20,000 characters; the answer then says it is only the beginning and that under "My documents" the whole text is searched.

**Read long documents at night:** admin switch under *Features → Own documents → Read pictures and scans* (`chat.doc_night`, off) with a night window (`doc_night_from`/`doc_night_to`, default 01:00 to 06:00, may cross midnight). By day the Spark reads in quiet moments only documents with at most 10 waiting pages, longer ones only after 30 minutes without a question or in the night window. At night pages are counted apart, up to 400 per profile and night on top of the daily limit of 100. Speech comes first (vorrang.py). Me → Documents names the reason: "long document, read at night from 01:00" or "daily limit reached, continues at night from 01:00".

**Docling server:** sub-switch "Docling server" under *Features → Own documents* (`chat.docling`, off by default; works only with an address). A [docling-serve](https://github.com/docling-project/docling-serve) on another machine in the home network reads uploaded PDFs, Office files and pictures a second time in quiet moments, with tables, columns and OCR; its Markdown replaces the text read by the Spark (pieces, meaning vectors and the summary card are made again, the document says "mit Docling gelesen"). Scanned pages go to Docling first: pages with at least 40 characters found are no longer read by the language model, the rest waits for the language model. Set up by the main admin only, with a fresh code: address `https://…` or `http://…` (http only to a home network address, with a warning; checked before every call, also after name resolution), API key (`DOCLING_SERVE_API_KEY`, stored sealed, never shown; a new address asks for it again), with its own certificate "Check certificate" as for the offsite backup (compare the fingerprint, accept exactly that one). "Read sample" sends a built-in sample page with a table and shows time and text, never a profile's file. The server gets only the file, named `dokument.<ext>`, never profile, file name or question. Applies to all profiles once the admin switches it on; each profile can switch "Read with Docling" off under Me → Documents (`doc_docling`, on by default, no admin preset), guests never. Until Docling has read it the file stays in `users/<profile>/docling-wait/` (also without kept originals); if the server does not answer the Spark asks again 10 minutes later, after 2 days the language model reads. Switching off (admin or profile) lets waiting files go at once. Live shows "Docling-Server" with waiting and today's files (numbers only), the service watch asks `/health`, logs under Knowledge (`docling: …`), protection level "Data" for saving. Starting the server (other machine, Docker): `docker run -d --name docling -p 5001:5001 -e DOCLING_SERVE_API_KEY=<long random key> quay.io/docling-project/docling-serve-cpu:v1.37.0`; with a GPU the image `docling-serve-cu128`. For https put a Caddy or nginx in front.

**Size and space:** PDFs may be up to 100 MB, set from 20 to 300 MB under *Features → Own documents → Largest PDF file* (`chat.doc_max_mb`); other kinds of files up to 20 MB. Uploads that are too big are refused before reading, the page says so before uploading. The "space per profile" (`chat.doc_quota_mb`, default 500 MB) counts all uploads of a profile together (database with text, vectors and waiting pages plus originals). At most 1000 own documents per profile (`documents.MAX_DOCS`, before 200; documents of the NAS source count against their own limit). When it is full, the Spark takes nothing new; originals are kept only while there is room. Me → Documents shows "Used: 120 MB of 500 MB" with a bar. Under Status → Summary the admin sees the use per profile and gives single profiles their own value (50 to 50,000 MB, `state/doc-quotas.json`, in the backup).

**Read on:** "View" shows the text in parts of about 200,000 characters; "Read on" fetches the next (`GET /api/profile/wissen/{id}/text?start=`). Only the view is split, the search uses the whole text (up to 2 million characters per document).

**Document card and tags:** admin switch *Features → Own documents → Document card and tags* (`chat.doc_brief`, off; released for every profile). In quiet minutes (speech priority applies) the language model reads the beginning of each finished document once (up to 3,000 characters, wrapped as outside text) and notes title, kind (contract, invoice, insurance, manual …), sender, date, deadline, number and 3 to 6 tags; at most 200 a day, existing documents bit by bit without being read again. The fields are checked (length, characters, date YYYY-MM[-DD], kind from a fixed list). Instead of file names the assistant sees one line per document (with many, the 30 that fit best, plus all tags) and can narrow `document_search` with `tags` or `art`; without a matching passage it gets the fitting cards. Under *Me → Documents* title, kind, deadline and tags show at the document; tags filter the list (also others' shared ones), "Tags" sets own ones (empty = automatic again; owner only). "View" shows "Belongs with": documents with the same number, the same sender or two tags in common. With the profile switch *Fristen anbieten* (`doc_due`, off) a deadline gets a "Remind" button (six weeks before at 9:00, else the day before); the assistant never sets a reminder from a deadline by itself. `document_search` stays offered in a narrowed tool set, and words like insurance, policy, warranty, manual, tax notice, cancellation period, tax, doctor's letter, findings, receipt, certificate count as a question about documents.

**Search check:** admin switch *Features → Own documents → Search check* (`chat.search_check`, off), below it *Test questions by the Spark* (`chat.search_check_auto`, off) and *Test questions per day and profile* (`chat.search_check_day`, 5 to 100, default 20); no profile switch, never for guests. Each profile keeps up to 300 test questions with a known right place (`users/<id>/suchpruefung.json`, in the backup): added by hand under *Me → Documents → Search check → Add a test question*, from an answer with "Name the right place" below its sources line, or by the language model in quiet minutes (`vorrang`, at most 3 per document). A model question is outside text and must keep fixed rules (one line, 8 to 160 characters, a question mark, no links, no command words, never four words in a row from the piece, at most 75 % of its words from the piece), else it is dropped. Once a night (2 to 5 o'clock, from 5 questions) or with "Check now" (at most every 10 minutes) every question goes through the search the assistant uses (`documents.search`): words only, meaning only (with meaning search) and mixed; noted are the place of the right document among the first 30, whether the right page was among the first 5, and the time (median and slowest 10 %). The profile sees the shares among the first 5 and first, the history of the last runs and "Found badly" with "Try" (the first 5 hits now). The admin sees only sums over all profiles under *Status → Checks → Check the search*, the live overview the number of questions and the share; the journal fixed words and numbers only. The fixed test bank `app/tests/data/suchbank.json` (20 invented papers, single and 10 multi-document questions) keeps the word search at no less than 85 % among the first 5 in `test_suchpruefung`.

**Context in the sections (no switch):** documents are cut at their headings: Markdown headings (Docling, .md), "§ 3 …", "TOP 2 …", "Artikel 4", numbered lines like "2.1 Kosten", bold lines of their own ("**Wichtige Hebel:**"), a line of its own ending in a colon over the next paragraph, and short lines of their own over a paragraph, also with a bracket at the end ("Stufe 1 – Briefing (lesend, ca. 3 Monate)") or a short word before a colon ("KI-Readiness: Wie ich …") (fixed rules in `documents.heading`, never a model). Such a line also counts in the middle of a paragraph without an empty line before it (`documents.paragraphs`, as in pasted chats and notes): when the line before ends a sentence or is a list or table line, the line below is long and does not go on in lower case, and the line is clearly shorter than its neighbours; so a text broken at the page's width is never cut, a line of a numbered list and the head of a table pasted with tabs never become a heading. This holds for documents read again (select them under Me → Documents and "Read again"). Inside a section about 900 characters with 150 overlap; small sections go with the next one; a Markdown table stays one piece up to 2,400 characters. Text without headings is cut continuously. Every piece gets a context line `chunks.head` from title (the card's, else the file name), kind and the headings above it (`chunks.part`). The word search looks at the context lines in a second index (`ftshead`) with weight 0.4 next to the piece's text; the meaning search embeds context line and piece together. When the line changes (a new card), the vector is computed again in quiet moments. Existing documents get their lines in quiet minutes (20 documents per step, no model, log line `wissen: kontext gebaut | N dokumente`, an event in the live overview); Me → Documents shows how many wait. The model sees the section in the source line ("Vertrag.txt, Seite 2, Abschnitt § 4 Kaution"), cut to 60 characters, without brackets and quotes, because it is outside text. `test_suchpruefung` asks for at least 85 % among the first 5.

**Knowledge question instead of Spark help, only fitting passages:** "what can you tell me about/on X …" ("Was kannst du mir über/zu/zur/von X …") counts as a question about X (`hilfe.ABOUT`), such as "Was kannst du mir zur Frauenkirche in Dresden erzählen?", unless X names the Spark, its panel or one of its functions; the list of example sentences only comes with "What can you do?" (`hilfe.can_ask`). With "look in the archive or Kiwix" the question goes to the own archive. `document_search` only passes on passages holding at least 60 % of the search words (the same rule as "Look locally first", `lokal.cover`; filler words like "information", "look", "archive", "Kiwix" do not count). If all drop out so, the model gets a fixed sentence: nothing fitting, say so, do not present other passages as an answer and look in the archive, Wikipedia or the web if offered (`chat_tools.NOTHING_FITS`); log line `wissen: dokumentsuche | N stellen ohne die suchwörter weggelassen`. Both sentences are in the Weiche's test bench; self-test `test_wissensfrage`.

**Tables in PDFs row by row (`pdftabellen.py`, no switch):** pypdf reads tables cell by cell; so that pairs like complaint ↔ card ("Which Sanjeevini card can I take for diarrhoea?") survive, the panel also looks at every PDF page with pdfium (only drawn lines and boxes, about a tenth of pypdf's time): horizontal lines, cell frames or shaded rows give the rows, vertical lines or gaps in the text the columns, and each cell gets the text from its own box, whether at the top, middle or bottom. Each row becomes one line `| Durchfall | SR 18 … | Hinweis |`. A table up to 2,400 characters stays one piece; longer ones are cut only between rows, every piece starts with the head row (`documents._rows`). Pages without a drawn table stay as pypdf read them; after 20 seconds per document the remaining pages too. Stored PDFs are checked one by one in quiet minutes (`documents.due_tables`, column `docs.tablev`): if the original is kept (*Features → Own documents → Keep originals*), the PDF was read as text (not page by page by the language model, not by Docling, which keeps tables itself) and is at most 20 MB, it is read again when it has a table; log line `wissen: tabellen geprüft | neu gelesen`, `keine tabelle` or `original fehlt`, event "Tabellen neu lesen" in the live overview; Me → Documents shows how many wait. Without the original only uploading again helps. A PDF is outside text; the limits (at most 4,000 drawn parts and 600 lines per page, 20 seconds per document) keep a malicious PDF small. Self-test `test_pdftabellen`.

**Re-rank hits:** admin switch *Features → Own documents → Re-rank hits* (`chat.rerank`, off; no profile switch, never for guests), below it *Model* (`chat.rerank_model`: "klein" = multilingual MiniLM from mMARCO, about 120 MB, recommended; "gross" = bge-reranker-v2-m3, about 570 MB) and *Wait at most* (`chat.rerank_ms`, 300 to 2000, default 800). `wissen.search` then takes 30 candidates from the mix; a cross-encoder (`nachsortieren.py`, its own process `embed_worker.py rank klein|gross`, CPU only, low priority, starts from 4 GB or 6 GB of free memory, ends after 10 minutes without work) reads question and context line with passage together and orders them; passages below a fixed floor (score −4, about 2 %) are left out, and when nothing is left it honestly says "nothing found". A search never waits for the model to start (it then keeps the mix's order), never longer than the time budget (late answers are dropped) and never for another re-ranking. "Look locally first" only re-ranks while the model runs anyway. The model files come once from Hugging Face; their SHA-256 are then kept in `state/doc-rank.json` (per size), other files are refused. Status → Summary → Own documents shows the model's state (memory, last duration, budget), the live overview "Nachsortieren" as background work, Logs → Requests the step "Nachsortieren" with numbers, the journal only `wissen: nachsortieren | 30 -> 5 | 250 ms` or `wissen: nachsortieren langsam | 30 treffer | reihenfolge der mischung`. With the switch the search check measures a fourth line "Re-ranked", so the gain shows on the own documents.

**Multi-document questions:** a test question can name up to three more right documents (`mehr`) when its answer needs several documents. It is measured apart: how many of its documents are among the first 5 passages of one search (*share*) and whether all are (*all found*); the other numbers count questions with one document only. Add one under *Me → Documents → Search check → Add a test question* with "More right documents" (a page then does not apply). With *Test questions by the Spark* every third attempt of the day tries a multi-document question first: the panel picks two documents with the same sender or a common tag in their document card by a fixed rule (never the model), the language model writes a question from one passage of each that needs both; the same fixed checks apply against both passages, at most 60 such questions per profile and one per pair of documents. Shown under Me → Documents → Search check ("Multi-document questions", "Found badly" with "x of y among the first 5"), as sums under *Status → Checks → Check the search* and in the live overview as the share "all found"; journal `wissen: sammelfrage gemacht | 2 dokumente`, `… verworfen | <reason>`, a run with `N sammelfragen alle A teil T`. The test bank has 20 documents and 10 multi-document questions; `test_suchpruefung` asks for at least 75 %.

**Knowledge book:** admin switch *Features → Own documents → Knowledge book* (`chat.knowledge_book`, off by default; "Documents per day and profile" `chat.knowledge_book_day` 5 to 200, default 40). In quiet minutes (after the card step in `wissen.due_once`; with "Document card and tags" a document waits for its card) the language model reads one own document (at most 9000 characters) with up to 5 fitting pages and proposes, as JSON, pages (topic, person, company, thing, place) with short sentences, each with its passage number. The panel decides by fixed rules: a sentence needs a real passage of this document as source, 8 to 400 characters, at least two words, no links, markup or command words; every number and date must stand in the source (number probe). When a new sentence contradicts an old one in value, both stay marked as a contradiction, never overwritten. At most 5 pages and 8 sentences per document, 60 sentences per page, 5000 pages per profile. Under *Me → Documents → Knowledge book* the profile reads its pages by kind, jumps to the source, removes a sentence with "Wrong" (it never comes back from the same document), has a page rewritten or deletes it. `document_search` brings the best page first (shared words, title counted three times) with sources, then the passages; the system prompt names up to 40 fitting page titles. Pages are outside text (`chat.wrap_outside`, read lock as for documents), never go into the memory and hold only own documents, never "for everyone" ones of other profiles; guests never. The search check also measures multi-document questions in the mode "wissensbuch". Status → Live shows only pages, sentences, waiting documents and profiles, the journal only numbers (`wissen: wissensbuch eingearbeitet | …`). Tables `wb_pages`, `wb_sent`, `wb_docs`, `wb_block`, `wb_log` in `users/<profile>/wissen.db`.

**Checking the knowledge book:** once a night per profile (search check's night window, `suchpruefung.night`) the panel checks by fixed rules: a document with changed passages (fingerprint `wb_docs.sig`, e.g. after "Read again" or Docling) loses its sentences and is worked in again; sentences of vanished documents go. `gedaechtnis.check` marks a sentence "out of date?" when a date in it has passed (R1) or a time word like "currently" is too old (R2). Then the language model checks at most `chat.knowledge_book_sample` sentences a night (0 to 100, default 50; 0 = fixed rules only) only for "does the passage say so?"; a no removes the sentence like "Wrong", unclear leaves it. Deleted documents (Me → Documents) and left-out folders take their sentences at once (`documents.on_gone`, subscriber `wissensbuch.forget`). At the top of *Me → Documents → Knowledge book* are contradictions (each value with its source; "Right" keeps that one, the others never come back) and out-of-date sentences ("Still right" counts as said from then on, "Wrong" removes). Answers from the book carry "(maybe out of date)". History, Status → Live (number of contradictions and out-of-date sentences) and journal (`wissen: wissensbuch geprueft | …`, `… stichprobe bestaetigt|entfernt|unklar`, `… quelle weg | …`) only with numbers.

**Filing answers, Kiwix, Obsidian and NAS:** when an answer rests on at least two sources (own documents from `document_search` or Kiwix articles from `archive_search`/`article_more`), the chat shows "To the knowledge book". The panel keeps the answer one hour in memory only (at most 200, bound to the profile); only the owner's click files it, never the model. Every sentence needs a passage from exactly these sources that holds all its numbers and shares at least 40 % of its words (`wissensbuch.match`), plus the same checks as when working in; sentences without a passage are dropped. The page has the kind "Answer"; Kiwix articles stand as source "Kiwix: title" (no link, table `wb_kiwix`) and are checked at night like documents. *Download as an Obsidian folder* (`GET /api/profile/wissensbuch/export`) returns a ZIP with one note per page, `[[links]]`, sources as footnotes and `Wissensbuch.md` as index. When the profile has a filing folder (*Me → Storage*, "File here") with automatic filing, a copy "Wissensbuch YYYY-MM-DD.zip" goes to `spark_ablage/Erstellt/` at night after the check, only when the book changed. Journal: `wissen: wissensbuch abgelegt | …`, `… ablegen abgelehnt | …`, `… export | 1 datei`, `… nas abgelegt | 1 datei`.

**Knowledge map:** admin switch *Features → Own documents → Knowledge book → Knowledge map* (`chat.knowledge_map`, off by default, child of `wissensbuch`; no profile switch, never guests). The knowledge book has its own menu entry (*Knowledge book*, a full page with the tabs *Pages* and *Map*; the Me page `wbbox` stays the target of every way). After the knowledge book step the language model gets the same document with at most 12 pages the panel picks (the document's pages, then pages whose whole title stands in the text) and proposes connections as JSON, each with its passage number and a kind from the fixed list `wissenskarte.ARTEN` (belongs to, part of, owns, paid to, insured with, contract with, works at, lives in, located in, concerns, deadline for, replaces, depends on; causes: led to, causes, raises, lowers). The panel decides by fixed rules (`check_edge`): both ends offered and named in the passage, the detail one line of up to 40 characters without link or command and with the number probe, a cause only with a word of reason in the passage (`SIGNALE`), at most 30 connections per document and 25,000 per profile, "Wrong" (`wk_block`) for good. Existing documents are worked in bit by bit (fingerprint `wb_docs.sig`), deleted ones take their connections at once (`documents.on_gone`). Browser (own SVG code with `textContent`) and iPhone (SwiftUI) draw the map themselves from the JSON of `GET /api/profile/wissenskarte` (at most 150 points and 400 connections, around a page, of a document or the best connected ones); the model never delivers SVG, Mermaid or DOT. Export as Mermaid or Graphviz text from fixed code (names cleaned, no click, style or link lines), as SVG and PNG from the browser, in the Obsidian folder every connection as a `[[link]]` with its kind. Drag points, drag a document onto the map, "To the middle", zoom with the wheel and two fingers. *Work in first* (stage 2): on the left under *Waiting* stand up to 12 documents the map does not have yet; dragging a waiting document onto the map or tapping *First* (`POST /api/profile/wissenskarte/vorziehen/{doc}`, iPhone: *Not worked in yet* → *First*) puts it in `wb_vor`, and the knowledge book, the map and the points take it before all others (`wissensbuch.VOR_ORDER`); the daily limits stay, at most 20 wishes per profile, each for at most 7 days. Only own, searched, ready documents; one the map has already or one the knowledge book could not work in is refused. *Keep places:* a dragged point is pinned (table `wk_lage`, `POST /api/profile/wissenskarte/lage`, at most 50 per request and 3000 per profile, only points of the map, numbers within ±100,000): it stands there in every part of the map, the others arrange themselves around it; a small dot top right shows it. *Unpin* in the selection or *Unpin all* (`DELETE /api/profile/wissenskarte/lage[/{id}]`) frees it. The iPhone keeps its ring picture, shows the pin and can unpin. *Time:* above the map stand *New since last week* and *New in 30 days* (`?neu=7|30`, connections by when they were made, `wk_edges.made`) and *From*/*To* as a month (`?von=&bis=` as YYYY-MM or YYYY-MM-DD, the document's date from its Steckbrief, else the day it came); without a point or document the map shows the points of those connections, else only their connections, the export follows the filter. *Timeline* shows the documents of the part on a line by date, the Steckbrief's deadline as a red diamond; a tap shows the document's connections on the map. iPhone: menu *Time* with the same filters and *Timeline* as a list. *Topic islands:* a fixed rule (label propagation over the connections, at most 12 rounds, order by connections, `wissenskarte.inseln`, no model) groups closely connected points into islands, anew on every change of the map, always the same for the same map. When the map has more points than one answer shows (150), it starts with the overview (`?inseln=1`): per island a bubble with a name (its best connected point) and a count, lines as thick as the connections between islands, at most 80 islands. A tap opens the island (`?insel=`), then as usual *To the middle* for the neighbourhood; the switch *Topic islands* on top changes it. iPhone: menu *Topic islands*. *Contradictions in red:* when a page of the knowledge book has sentences marked "widerspruch", its point has a red dashed border, the selection names the number, "Open page" leads to settling it. Status → Live only connections, points, waiting and wished-first documents, pages with a contradiction and profiles; the journal only fixed words and numbers (`wissen: wissenskarte verbunden | n verbindungen | m verworfen | …`). Tables `wk_edges`, `wk_docs`, `wk_block` in `users/<profile>/wissen.db`. Measured under Status → Checks → Measure knowledge.

**Knowledge store faster:** no feature of its own, no switch. The vectors of the meaning search are kept in memory as float16; `documents._similar` reckons in float32 block by block, 4096 passages at a time, so the ranking stays the same. The knowledge book finds its pages through an own search index (SQLite FTS5, table `wb_fts` with title and sentences of each page); fixed SQL triggers note every changed, new or deleted page in `wb_ftsdirty`, and before each save the panel writes its row again. A missing index is built once on first opening (`wissen: wissensbuch suchindex aufgebaut | n seiten`). The count is shared words, title three times; without FTS5 in SQLite the panel counts the words of all pages (`wissen: wissensbuch suchindex fehlt | wortzaehlung`). The knowledge map picks its visible part in SQLite: a fixed query counts connections per page and document, around a page `WITH RECURSIVE` walks at most two steps, UNION takes each page only once per step (no endless ring); limit 150 points and 400 connections. Two fixed queries with values only as parameters, never from the model's text: `wissenskarte.rund_um` (pages around a page with their distance) and `wissenskarte.weg` (shortest way between two pages, at most 4 steps, always the same one on a tie). For other parts of the panel there is `wissensbuch.liste(uid, worte, k)`: read only, bound to the profile (empty when the knowledge book is not allowed for the profile, never for guests), it returns `[(page id, rank from 1)]` from the same FTS5 search, at most 50, never titles or sentences. Tables `wb_fts` and `wb_ftsdirty` in `users/<profile>/wissen.db`.

**Knowledge map daily limit:** *Features → Own documents → Knowledge book → Knowledge map → Map: documents per day and profile* (`chat.knowledge_map_day`, 5 to 500, default 200). A map step is short, so the backlog of a full knowledge book (about 150 documents) is worked in within about a day. Only in quiet minutes, when the knowledge book has nothing to do, one document per step behind speech.

**Find in all data:** admin switch *Features → Find in all data* (`chat.fund`, off by default; no profile switch, the admin blocks single profiles, never guests or foreign voices). Menu entry *Find* next to the knowledge book with the tabs *Search*, *Tags* and *Settings* (Me page `fundbox`). Per profile a file `users/<id>/fund.db` (SQLite, FTS5): `fund_item` (source, reference, date, title, excerpt, fields), `fund_fts` (words of title, fields and tags, never a document's body), `fund_blind` (for sealed sources), `fund_tag` (tags with an HMAC mark per profile, `vorrat.marke`, key by HKDF from the vault key and the profile id), `fund_hand` (own tags), `fund_stand`. Collecting contract `fund.QUELLEN` (name, feature, protection, kind, `neues_seit`, `noch_da`, `oeffnen`): documents, NAS knowledge folders, reMarkable and knowledge book as references; Find searches their text through `documents.search` (rank fusion RRF k = 60). Every five minutes per profile a background round (`vorrang.in_thread("Suchindex")`, at most 200 entries per source and round), only when documents changed. **Fields and categories only by fixed rules:** amount after "Gesamt/Summe/Rechnungsbetrag", number after its fixed word, kind from fixed words or the document card, company only when the sender stands word for word in the text; categories only from `fund.KATEGORIEN` (kinds of the document card without "Sonstiges", with "Bestellung" and "Buchung", plus 13 areas). **Own tags come first:** ✓ keeps, × removes one for good, + adds one; they survive "Rebuild", free tags of a document go to its document card. **In the assistant:** tool `fund_suche` (Weiche group "finden": a search word and something of one's own like an invoice, a contract, a mail or "my …", never with internet or archive), at most five hits of 400 characters each as outside text; filters (category, company, person, source, one year) only when the person's own words of the message hold them. Below the answer the card "Show in Find" (browser, apps through the panel view, companion). Ctrl K offers "Find in my data: …". Endpoints `POST /api/profile/fund/suche`, `GET /api/profile/fund/eintrag/{id}`, `PUT …/schlagworte`, `GET /api/profile/fund/stand`, `POST /api/profile/fund/neu`, `DELETE /api/profile/fund` (login, own profile, CSRF, size and rate limit), `GET /api/admin/fund` (numbers only). Log area *Find* (`fund: suche | 3 treffer | 41 ms | P1`, `fund: runde dokument | 12 neu | 0 weg`), live line *Find* with numbers only. Self-tests `tests/test_fund.py`. **A collection as the filter:** beside source and category stands a chooser "All collections" / an own collection, and `fund_suche` takes a collection from the own words or the conversation's binding; then only documents and knowledge book pages of the collection count (table `mp_eintrag`, never `fund_tag`), other sources fall away, another profile's or an unknown collection finds nothing (see *Collections*).

**People, companies and deadlines:** admin switch *Features → Own documents → Knowledge book → Knowledge map → People, companies and deadlines* (`chat.knowledge_ents`, off by default, child of `wissenskarte`; no profile switch, never guests), daily limit *Points: documents per day and profile* (`chat.knowledge_ents_day`, 5 to 500, default 200). After the map's connections the language model gets each document (passages [S1] … and offered pages [K1] …) and proposes only JSON: `dinge` with kind, name and passage number, plus `verbindungen` between things (D1 …) and pages. The panel decides by fixed rules (`wissenskarte.check_ent`): a kind from `wissenskarte.ENT_ARTEN` (person, firma, ort, vertrag, konto, frist), the name stands in the passage (compared lower case, without accents, with spaces drawn together, `ent_norm`), 2 to 80 characters without link, markup or command word, a deadline only with a date or a number with a unit of time in its name, an account only as an IBAN or a number with at least five digits; at most 40 points and 200 passages per document, 15,000 points per profile. Connections only from `ARTEN` with the same check as `check_edge`; connections between two pages are the map step's job. Stored in the profile's `wissen.db`: `wk_ent(id, art, name, norm, alias)` with a unique index on (art, norm), ids are never given out again; `wk_ment(ent, doc, chunk)` with the passages; the same name of the same kind (also an earlier name in `alias`) gets the same point. On the map the points are called `k<number>` and carry the colour of their kind; buttons above the map show one kind with its neighbours; `view`, `rund_um` and `weg` take them along (limits 150 points and 400 connections). Other parts only read: `wissenskarte.finde_knoten(uid, name, art=None)` (exactly by name and other names) and `wissenskarte.knoten_liste(uid, art, anfang="", most=50)` (sorted by name), through a read-only connection and only while the switch counts for the profile. Renaming (the old name becomes another name), merging (same kind only; passages and connections move to the target) and deleting (never comes back from these documents, also not in a spelling that is "gleich" by `wissensdoppelt.aehnlich`: another form of address, title, order, ß/ss; a mere suggestion never blocks, `wissensdoppelt.gesperrt`) happen only by the owner's click through `POST /api/profile/wissenskarte/knoten/{id}/name`, `…/zusammen` and `DELETE …/knoten/{id}`, with sign-in, profile binding, CSRF, size limit, rate limit and the code of "Confirm" (`stufen.LIST`); the model never decides. Every change calls the subscribers of `wissenskarte.GEAENDERT` as `fn(uid, alt_id, neu_id, was)` (`zusammen`, `weg` with `neu_id` None, `name` with `neu_id` equal to `alt_id`); an error there is logged and disturbs nothing. When a document goes (`documents.GONE`), its passages go at once; a point without a passage disappears and reports `weg`. Live shows only the number per kind, the log only fixed words and numbers (`wissen: wissenskarte knoten erkannt | n knoten | n neu | n verworfen | n verbindungen | n weg`). iPhone: points in the colour of their kind, "By kind" and "Delete"; renaming and merging in the panel. Self-test `test_wissensknoten`.
**Find: mail and attachments:** sub-switch *Features → Find in all data → Find mail and attachments* (`chat.fund_mail`, off by default, a child of "fund", needs Reading e-mail; never guests). `fundmail.py` reads **all** mail, the newest first, with no upper limit: per mailbox inbox, archive and sent (role through `postfach.role`: the server's SPECIAL-USE flags first, else the usual names), never spam, trash, drafts or Gmail's views; only reading (`tidy.Box`, EXAMINE, BODY.PEEK: mail stays unread) and only to the configured mailboxes (`mail.IMAP` = `netguard.IMAP4_SSL`). Per folder `fund_stand` keeps the UIDVALIDITY, the highest UID read and the lowest read backwards: each round first fetches new mail (`UID n+1:*`), in quiet minutes (`wissen.quiet`: a minute without a question, nobody speaking) and at night (01–06 longer) older ones backwards; another UIDVALIDITY reads the folder anew, once a day a reconciliation (`UID SEARCH ALL`) takes out what was deleted. Slices of at most 0.2 s reading and 20 mails, one transaction each, `vorrang.hold` in between (a pause while someone speaks); the round runs in a low-priority thread (`vorrang.in_thread("Suchindex")`), started apart from `fund.due_once` and never awaited: every five minutes for profiles that read their mail, else hourly, at once after a new mail (`mailidle.on_new`). **Protection by space:** below a fixed free reserve of 2048 MB reading stops with a log line. **Sealed:** subject, excerpt, fields (sender address, amount, number, folder role, attachment names), tag values and own tags only AES-GCM (`vorrat.seal`, bound to profile, source, entry and field), the words only as HMAC marks in `fund_blind` (per word stem, plus its first four letters and the sender's words); in plain text only source, entry key (mailbox id, a mark of the folder, UID) and date; without a key nothing is written. **Attachments** (PDF, Word, OpenDocument, text …, at most 10 MB, text up to 30,000 characters) through `documents.extract_pages`: text and fields (amount, invoice number, company by the fixed rules) go only into `fund`, never as a document into `wissen.db`; a scan without text keeps its file name. An attachment hit lifts its mail (one card with "Attachment: …"). "Open" shows the mail on the E-mail page in its folder (archive and sent, as there, only with "Order mail"). The search no longer finds a removed mailbox's mail at once; the rows go with the next look. With mail released `fund_suche` counts as a mail reader (`offer()["mail"]`): the locks after mail (`LOCKED_AFTER_MAIL`) apply. Logs `fund: runde mail | 120 neu | 0 weg | 4800 offen | 12.3 s | P1`, `fund: mail platz knapp | … MB frei`, `fund: mail uidvalidity gewechselt | ordner neu`, `fund: mail konto entfernt | n weg`; live *Find*: "Mails: heute N eingelesen, M offen", "Platz knapp". Find → Settings shows "Mails not read yet". Self-tests `tests/test_fund_mail.py` (a fake mailbox).

**Find: appointments, contacts, conversations, lists, reminders, pictures, songs and "from the surgery":** five sub-switches under *Features → Find in all data*, off by default, children of "fund"; each source also follows its function (`fundquellen.frei`); never guests. `fundquellen.py` holds the sources, `fund.runde` collects them with the documents (at most every five minutes, only when a file changed, `fundquellen.stempel`; one that reaches the limit of 200 entries per source goes on in the next round). *Find appointments* (`chat.fund_termine`, needs Calendar): own calendars only from the copy on the Spark (`kalenderspiegel`, a Spark calendar from `sparkkalender`), never a fetch of its own; calendars others share stay out. Title, place and description sealed like mail (AES-GCM through `fund.put_sealed`, words as HMAC marks), per object the ETag as its state (as a mark); a removed calendar leaves the search at once. *Find contacts* (`chat.fund_kontakte`, needs Contacts): name, company, position and town sealed, never numbers, mail addresses or streets; name, company and town become tags (person, company, place). *Find conversations* (`chat.fund_chats`, needs Past conversations): per conversation only date, channel (Telegram, Siri, watch …) and number of questions; the words are searched by `recall.liste` (BM25 of the history, answers from mail stay out), the title is read when shown, nothing of it is in `fund.db`. *Find lists and reminders* (`chat.fund_listen`, needs Lists or Timers and reminders): own lists on the Spark, lists of others only through `tasks.shared_to` (Share lists on for both, the share still there), plus every reminder; clear like their source. *Find pictures and songs* (`chat.fund_medien`, needs Painting pictures or Making music): wish, description and lyrics. A hit opens its page (the calendar on the appointment's day, the contact card, the list, the conversation in the chat, notifications, pictures, music). **Periods:** `frisch.spanne(text, now, zone)` is the one rule for "heute", "gestern", "vorgestern", "diese/letzte Woche", "diesen/letzten Monat", "dieses/letztes Jahr", "im März", "März 2024", "2023", "vor drei Wochen" (also in English); two different periods of one kind are none. `wissen.recent_window` and the filter of `fund_suche` use it. **"vom OP" (from the surgery):** `fund.bezug_aus` reads from the person's own words "vom/vor dem/nach dem/seit dem <word>" (sure) or "beim/zum/zur/von der <word>" (only when an appointment fits), never a time ("vom letzten Jahr", "vom Montag"). The panel looks in the own calendars for the newest past appointment (at most 3 years) with all word stems through its marks; entries 14 days before to 60 days after get a fixed rank bonus (1/60), nothing is dropped. Without an appointment a fixed sentence says "Einen Termin „OP“ habe ich im Kalender nicht gefunden." and the search runs without a window; the word is then no search word. On the page Find a line above the hits shows period and appointment. Logs `fund: runde termin | 3 neu | 0 weg | …`, `fund: bezug | termin gefunden`, `fund: bezug | kein termin` (never the word); live *Find*: "Termine N · Kontakte N …", "Bezug heute N gefunden, M ohne Termin". Self-tests `tests/test_fund_quellen.py` (a fixed time, never the clock).

**Find: tags by the model, points of the knowledge map, one ranking and the Sofort-Karte:** sub-switch *Features → Find in all data → Tags by the model* (`chat.fund_schlagworte`, off by default, a child of "fund"; never guests), below it *Tags: entries per day and profile* (`chat.fund_tag_day`, 5 to 500, default 200). In quiet minutes (`wissen.due_once` after the knowledge map, only when `wissen.quiet()`) the language model reads one entry after the other through `llm.complete`, the newest first: title, excerpt, fixed fields, for documents the start of their text, at most 1500 characters, wrapped with `chat.wrap_outside`. Conversations and knowledge book pages have no text in the index and stay out. The answer only as JSON `{"kategorien", "personen", "firmen", "orte", "frei"}`, data, never an order. `fund.pruefe` keeps a category only from `fund.KATEGORIEN` (at most 3), a person, company or place only when the name stands in the entry word for word (`wissenskarte.stands`, name rules `wissenskarte.clean_name`, at most 5 each), a free tag only through `documents.clean_tags` and never with a command word (at most 5); what the person removed (`fund_hand` minus) never comes back. A model tag never overwrites one of the rules, shows dashed with "by the model" and is never a filter of the tool. Under *Find → Tags*, "New from the model" shows the proposals of the last 7 days: ✓ keeps one as your own, × removes it for good. At most two tries per entry; a changed entry is read again. **Points of the knowledge map:** people, companies and places (from rules, contacts, the model and by hand) get the point of the same name (`fund_tag.ent` through `wissenskarte.finde_knoten`, read only; `fund` never makes a point), also when the point comes later (`fund.verknuepfe` per round, at most 500). `fund.knoten_geaendert` listens to `wissenskarte.GEAENDERT`: merged → the tag follows the new point, deleted → it stays a name without a point. Knowledge book pages do not stand in for people, companies and places. A point counts in the ranking like an own tag (+1/60). **Ranking:** every list of the form `liste(uid, worte, k) -> [(key, rank)]` goes into the mix through `fund._fuse` with k = 60: `recall.liste` (conversations), `wissensbuch.liste` and, where present, `wissenskarte.liste` (pieces, their document is the hit); a broken list leaves the others standing. **Sofort-Karte:** when the person asks to find something (`fund.FIND_WORDS`) and one hit lies clearly ahead (the only one or at least 1.5 times the score of the second, `fund.klar_vorn`), the panel answers itself with a fixed sentence (`fund.sofort_satz`: "Gefunden: Mail von Thomann vom 14. März 2024 mit dem Anhang „Rechnung 4711“, Betrag 129,90 €."), values without markup or link signs, at most 60 characters each; the model is not asked and rewords nothing (`chat_tools` hands `ctx["fest"]`, `chat_modell` says the sentence and ends). Panel chat and Android: card "Open the original" and "Show in Find"; Begleiter: "Open the original", then "Show in Find"; Telegram: button "Original öffnen" to `<panel>/#fund=<no>` (only with the panel's address entered, only navigation); spoken (speaker, watch, Siri, CarPlay) the short form without the attachment. When the appointment of a "vom …" is missing, the fixed sentence says so first and the model answers. **Local first:** `lokal.SOURCES["fund"]` looks in the quick look into the clear sources without an index of their own there (knowledge book, lists, reminders, pictures, songs; never mail, appointments, contacts). Logs `fund: schlagworte modell | 3 ok | 1 verworfen | … | nicht_im_text=1`, `fund: schlagworte modell fehler | ValueError | …`, `fund: knoten zusammen | 2 schlagworte | …`, `fund: knoten verknuepft | 4 schlagworte | …`, `fund: sofort | 1 treffer klar vorn | …` (never a word); live *Find*: "Schlagworte heute N Einträge, M übernommen, K verworfen, L offen", "Sofort-Karten heute N". Self-tests `tests/test_fund_schlagworte.py` (an obeying model, a fixed time, browser test).

**Find: measurement, iPhone native and companion jump:** no switches of its own. **Measurement** is the measuring part "fund" of *Measure knowledge* (`sudo speech-spark wissen messen synth 5000` or Status → Checks → Measure knowledge, 10 mails per document; see [Technical](technical.md#measuring-knowledge)): it fills an index with synthetic data from a fixed seed in the run's throwaway folder (never a profile, removed afterwards, also after an error) and measures search time p50/p95/maximum over 200 searches (one to three words, category, period, sender), reading in per 1000 mails (the first 1000 as real MIME messages through `fundmail.eintraege`, every fifth with an attachment), writing per 1000, the size of `fund.db` and the process's RAM. Mails and attachments are sealed like real ones (`fundmail.schreiben`, AES-GCM and HMAC marks); the Spark's key is only read, without it the part measures nothing (`fund: messung abgelehnt | kein schluessel`). The result as numbers only: under Status → Checks → Measure knowledge ("Find: search over mails and documents", "Find: mails · attachments · size of the index"), in the log `wissen: messung teil fund | dokumente 5000 | suche p95 61 ms | ram … mb` and in the box "Wissen messen" under Status → Live. Goal: 95 % of the searches below 150 ms. Measured in the container: p50 24 ms, p95 61 ms, reading in 3.3 s per 1000 mails, 314 MB for 50,000 mails, 10,000 attachments and 5,000 documents (about 5 KB per entry), 89 MB RAM. `test_fund_messung` measures the same with generous limits for GitHub. **Fast search:** `fund._Erlaubt` asks SQL only for the candidates of the lists (by row id), `fund._ext_ids` looks up only the keys a list brings, `fund._klar_absender` checks the sender filter in SQL first (a necessary condition, the exact check stays), `fund._marke` keeps HMAC marks per profile and key in memory (at most 50,000). **iPhone native:** *My things → Find* (with the release of Find, `iphone.THINGS["fund"]`): search field, filter chips source, category and period (last 30 days, this year, last year), hits with source, date, sender, attachment and tags; a hit shows every field, the tags with their origin (✓ keeps as your own, × removes for good, "Add a tag" with its kind), "Open the original" and for a mail its attachments; *Settings* shows count and state per source, "Rebuild" and "Delete the index" (keep the own tags or delete them too). Nothing of the index stays on the iPhone. Under an answer from the own data stands "Show in Find", with the Sofort-Karte first "Open the original" (the panel inside the app at `#fund=<no>`). Android shows the panel's page Find. **Companion (Windows and Mac):** under the Sofort-Karte first "Original öffnen" (`chat.DESK_ORIGINAL`, `#fund=<no>`), beside it "In Finden ansehen"; besides the pages of `chat.DESK_GOTO` the programs open only exactly this form (`#fund=` and at most 12 digits), the panel checks on opening that the entry is the profile's own. **Attachments:** a mail's hit (panel and app) names its attachments (names only, at most 20). Self-tests `tests/test_fund_messung.py`, `tests/test_fund_apps.py` (browser test of the attachments).

**Find: size of the index:** no switch, only a value. *Find → Settings* shows "Size of the index" as a sum (below 1 GB in MB, else GB with one decimal); opened it lists Find's file (`users/<id>/fund.db` with `-wal` and `-shm`), its split by source (mail, attachment, document, appointment …, estimated from rows and text length, the parts add up to the file size exactly) and "Documents and knowledge" (`users/<id>/wissen.db` with `-wal` and `-shm`: text, vectors, knowledge book, knowledge map). One source: `fund.groesse(uid)` gives numbers in bytes only, keeps them 60 seconds (`fund.GROESSE_CACHE`) and never runs on the event loop in the routes; `/api/profile/fund/stand` carries it as the field `groesse`. The admin sees the same number under *People and devices* at the profile in the section Data (`/api/admin/profiles/<id>` field `index`), Status → Live in the box Find the sum of all profiles ("Index 1,2 GB (Finden 300 MB, Dokumente und Wissen 900 MB)"). iPhone under Find → Settings from the same endpoint, Android through the panel view. Written by `sizeText` in `base.js` (also for Kiwix books), in Live `fund.groesse_text`. "Delete the index" and "Rebuild" give the space back at once (VACUUM).

**Map helps the search:** admin switch *Features → Own documents → Knowledge book → Knowledge map → People, companies and deadlines → Map helps the search* (`chat.knowledge_search`, off by default, child of `wissensknoten`; no profile switch, the admin blocks single profiles, never guests). The knowledge map gives the document search a third list of hits besides words and meaning: `wissenskarte.liste(uid, worte, k)` returns `[(piece id, rank from 1)]`, at most `KARTE_MOST` (50), read only (SQLite `mode=ro`), only for the own profile; empty without release. Fixed rules, never the model: (1) search words hit points by name and other names (`wissenskarte.trifft`): whole, compared as `ent_norm` does (lower case, without accents, spaces drawn together, an account also without spaces), or by one name word of at least 4 letters that is no number and no legal form (`KARTE_ALLGEMEIN`: GmbH, AG, KG …), where the question's word may be up to two letters longer ("Stadtwerken"); at most `KARTE_KNOTEN` (20) points; (2) their neighbours one step away over connections that count (the same query as `rund_um`); (3) their places from `wk_ment`, only in used, read own documents; (4) a piece with a named point before one with only neighbours, more points before fewer, then the smaller piece id. `documents.search(..., karte=True)` mixes the list with 60 plus place, weighed `documents.KARTE_WEIGHT` (0.5), only for pieces the search lets through anyway (in use, narrowed documents); never for documents of other profiles (`shared_only`). The weight is below 60/89: a piece only from the map stands behind every one words or meaning found among their 30; it adds, never replaces. Such a hit carries `karte`, below the answer it says "via the knowledge map" (kept as `w` of the source, `profiles._att`). Only `wissen.search` uses it (document_search, "Look locally first"); per search one line `wissen: kartensuche | n von m stellen über die karte`, on a failure `wissen: kartensuche fehler | kind`, then on without the map. Status → Live, box Knowledge map: "Suche über die Karte an: n Suchen, m Stellen" (numbers only). The search check shows its own bar "With knowledge map". With the test bank (`tests/data/suchbank.json`, words only, points as a model without mistakes names them): single questions without and with the map 87 % among the first five, multi-document questions 83 % → 89 % of their documents, all documents 62 % → 75 %; floor `KARTE_SAMMEL_FLOOR` 89 %, plus `FLOOR` and `SAMMEL_FLOOR`. Security: read only, bound to the profile, the same filters as the search; the passages stay outside text. For other parts there is `wissenskarte.knoten_von_dok(uid, doc, arten=None)`: the points of one document through `wk_ment` as `[{id, art, name}]`, sorted by kind and name, at most 200, `arten` filters (unknown kinds drop out), read only, bound to the profile, empty for guests and without the release of "People, companies and deadlines".
**Collections:** admin switch *Features → Collections* (`chat.mappen`, off by default; no profile switch, the admin blocks single profiles, never guests), below it *Collection: tokens to the model* (`chat.mappe_token`, 4000 to 100000, default 24000). Menu entry *Collections* (Me page `mpbox`) with the list and the open collection. A collection bundles own documents, knowledge book pages and notes for one undertaking, with name, goal and symbol. Tables `mp_mappe` and `mp_eintrag` in `users/<id>/wissen.db`, at most 50 collections per profile and 100 entries per collection. A note becomes a document of its own (source "notiz", up to 20 000 characters) and lies whole in the collection. **Levels by a fixed rule (`mappen.plan`), never by the model:** *off* does not go, *short* gives the profile card (`wissen.card_line`) or a page's title and three sentences, *whole* the whole text while the sum fits the budget (characters by `verbrauch.CHARS_PER_TOKEN`); first every short one, then the whole ones in the collection's order; a whole document that no longer fits is only searched (`documents.search` over exactly these documents, at most 8 passages). A bar shows "≈ X of Y tokens". **In the assistant:** "Ask in the assistant" binds the next questions to the collection (chip above the conversation, field `mappe` in the chat); the panel checks the id against the own profile and gives the entries with marks [M1] … as outside text (`chat.wrap_outside`), the turn counts as outside text (changing tools locked). Tools `mappe_liste`, `mappe_stand` (reads names from documents, locks afterwards) and `mappe_dazu` (changes; collection and document only when the person's own words of the message name them), Weiche group "mappe". When a document goes, its entry goes with it (`documents.GONE` and on reading); deleting a collection only removes the assignment, never a document. Endpoints `GET/POST /api/profile/mappen`, `GET/PUT/DELETE /api/profile/mappen/{id}`, `POST/PUT/DELETE …/eintrag`, `POST …/notiz` (sign-in, own profile, CSRF, 64 KB, rate limit "mappe"). Logs `mappen: chat | 3 eintraege | 1 kurz 2 ganz 0 suche 0 stellen | 5200 token | P1`, never a name; live line *Collections* with numbers only. Self-tests `tests/test_mappen.py`.

**Collections in search, Find and on the iPhone:** no switches of its own. **A collection as the search's filter:** `document_search` and `fund_suche` have the parameter `mappe` (name). The panel takes it only when the person's own words of the message hold the name, else the collection the conversation is bound to counts (an own id, checked by `mappen.for_turn`); a different one the model names is dropped and counted (`mappen.fuer_werkzeug`). Only entries set to whole are searched (short already stands in the context as the document card, off never); documents other profiles share fall away, the knowledge book brings only pages of the collection. Without a whole entry a fixed sentence says why nothing comes. `fund.suche(…, nur=…)` takes the collection's documents and pages from `mp_eintrag` (never over `fund_tag`) and leaves out every other source: with a collection no mail, attachment, appointment, contact, conversation or list ever comes. **Find:** beside source and category a chooser "All collections" / an own collection (`mappen.js` `mpFundWahl`); the page filters with every entry of the collection whatever its level (`mappen.fuer_seite`); another profile's or an unknown id finds nothing, never everything. **iPhone native:** My things → Collections (`MappenView.swift`): the list, a new collection, a collection with its entries and the level as a segmented control, "≈ X of Y tokens", take an entry out, delete a collection (with a question; the documents stay); putting documents in, notes and renaming in the panel. Android through the panel view. Companion: `features.DESK_SKIP`. Logs `mappen: filter | fund | chat | 1 dokumente 0 seiten | P1`, `mappen: filter verworfen | dokumente | nicht in den eigenen worten`; Live *Collections* counts searches within collections. Router: three sentences in `intent_bench.py`. Self-tests `tests/test_mappen.py` (class Filter).

**Analyses per document:** admin switch *Features → Own documents → Analyses* (`chat.doc_auswertung`, off by default; no profile switch, the admin blocks single profiles, guests never), below it *Analyses per day and profile* (`chat.doc_auswertung_day`, 10 to 500, default 100). Under Me → Documents every own, read document has the sheet **Analyses** in its details with the fixed kinds summary (at most 6 sentences), key points (8), questions on the text (6), contents, deadlines and people. By default summary and key points are made by themselves in quiet minutes (`auswertung.AUTO`), the others per click "Make" or "Show". **Check:** the model gets the document in numbered places [S1] … and answers only JSON `{"punkte": [{"text", "quelle"}]}`; `auswertung.pruefe` keeps a point only when its place was offered, `wissensbuch.check_sentence` passes against it (length, no links, no markup, no command words, every number stands in the place) and, for summary and key points, `wissensbuch.match` finds enough words of the place; a question ends with "?", the panel shows its answer as a quote of the place. Doubles and points marked "Not right" (table `aw_block`) go; dropped ones are counted, never shown. **Without the model:** contents from the headings of the pieces (`chunks.part`), deadlines from the profile card's deadline and knowledge map points of the kinds deadline and contract, people from people, companies and places (`wissenskarte.knoten_von_dok`, read only; place by `wissenskarte.stands`); without "People, companies and deadlines" the sheet shows only the card's date and says what is missing. **Run:** the quiet chain is the list `wissen.KETTE` (one source for the order); `auswertung.due_once` stands after the knowledge map and `fund.schlagworte_due`. One kind of one document per step, every pass through `vorrang.run("Auswertung")` (stops on speech, the kind waits again), long documents in at most 5 passes of 12 000 characters and only after `wissen.long_ok`; a document read again waits again, a broken answer at most three times. Table `aw_ergebnis` in the own `wissen.db`. **Tool** `dokument_auswertung(dokument, art)` (read only, switch group dokument) hands over only checked points with their page as outside text. Endpoints `GET /api/profile/wissen/{doc}/auswertung`, `POST …/auswertung/{art}`, `DELETE …/auswertung/{art}/{n}` with login, own profile, CSRF, 16 KB and rate limit "auswertung". Status → Live: box Analyses (done, waiting, dropped today); Logs area Wissen: "auswertung: fertig | kurz 3 punkte 1 verworfen | …".

**Analyses: by themselves, templates, remind, collections, iPhone:** Under *Features → Own documents → Analyses* the admin chooses which kinds are made **by themselves** in quiet minutes (`chat.doc_auswertung_auto`, default summary and key points; a kind without the model such as the contents then stands in the sheet without a click). He also sets up to 10 **templates**, own kinds with a name (German and English), a task (8 to 400 characters) and "by itself"; they lie in `STATE/auswertung-vorlagen.json` (only through `common.STATE`, in the backup) and hold at once for every profile. `auswertung.check_vorlage` lets name and task through only without links, markup and command words, also when the file is read. The task is **a topic for the model, no rule**: it stands quoted in the fixed frame of the prompt, the model answers in the same JSON form, `auswertung.pruefe` checks as for the key points (place offered, number check, enough words of the place, at most 8). In the journal a template is only `vorlage`, never by name. A deleted template takes its results in every profile with it. **Remind:** a knowledge map deadline with a full date in its name (31.12.2027 or 2027-12-31) gets "Remind" in the sheet (`POST /api/profile/wissen/{doc}/auswertung/fristen/{n}/erinnern`, sign-in, profile binding, CSRF, rate limit `auswertung`); the reminder comes as for the profile card six weeks before at 9:00 or the day before, only while the deadline reminder is on under Me → Documents. A duration ("three months") or a date only nearby gets no button: the panel never guesses. **Collections:** the level "short" brings along with the profile card the made summary, key points and templates of a document, every point with its page, at most 1500 characters (`auswertung.fuer_mappe`, `mappen.kurz_text`), as outside text; the entry names which are included. Questions on the text do not go along. Live shows the number of templates. **iPhone:** in the document details under Open, "Analyses" leads to the sheet: every kind with its state, "Make", "Show" or "Make again", every point with page and heading, for questions the place as the answer, "Not right" by swiping (the point does not come back), "Remind" for deadlines with a full date; the same endpoints as in the panel (`iphone.THINGS["documents"]`), English in `Localizable.xcstrings`. The admin sets templates and "by themselves" in the panel; Android shows the panel view.

**Transcribe recordings:** admin switch *Features → Own documents → Transcribe recordings* (`chat.doc_audio`, off by default; no profile switch, the admin blocks single profiles, guests never), below it *Largest recording (MB)* (`chat.doc_audio_mb`, 10 to 1000, default 300), *Longest recording (minutes)* (`chat.doc_audio_min`, 5 to 600, default 180) and *Minutes of transcription per day and profile* (`chat.doc_audio_day_min`, 10 to 1440, default 240). Under Me → Documents "Add" then takes audio and video files (mp3, m4a, aac, wav, ogg, opus, flac, webm, mp4, mov, mkv) and sends them to `POST /api/profile/docs/audio` (module `abschrift.py`, login, own profile, CSRF, size limit before reading through `BodyLimit`, rate limit "docaudio"). **Check on upload:** the first bytes must fit the ending (`abschrift.fits`: RIFF/WAVE, OggS, fLaC, Matroska, ftyp, ADTS, MPEG/ID3); the file is copied in parts with a limit, never all into memory, and lies as `users/<id>/docs/audio-wait/<document id>.<ending>` (0600, never under the uploaded name); `ffprobe` reads the length, a file without a sound track or over the limit goes at once. At most 5 recordings wait per profile. The document has kind `audio`, source `audio` and state `hoeren`; the row shows a wave symbol, "12 of 94 minutes transcribed" with the reason it waits, and in the details "Stop" (`POST /api/profile/wissen/{doc}/abschrift/stop`: what is written stays; without any text the document goes). **Transcription:** `abschrift.due_once` stands in `wissen.KETTE` after the page pictures and before the profile card, so it only runs in quiet minutes. Per step `ffmpeg` cuts a piece of 30 seconds (from the second on with 1 second of overlap in front) as 16 kHz mono WAV, only from the local file (`-protocol_whitelist file,pipe`, fixed arguments, no shell); the own speech recognition writes it down through `vorrang.run("Abschrift")`; when somebody speaks the piece is cancelled and heard again in the next pause. The request carries `stufe.headers("hinten")`; with the priority key it waits in the speech service behind every person and **does not count as speech**. Words the overlap heard twice are taken out by `abschrift.trim_edge` with a fixed rule. Recordings over 20 minutes only after `wissen.long_ok` (with "Read long documents at night" at night or after half an hour without a question), at most the minutes per day (`documents.count_today`, key `abschrift`); when speech recognition is gone (`dienstwacht.down("asr")`) or a request fails the transcription waits 5 minutes; a piece refused three times is left out. **Time as the place:** every piece becomes a section of the document with its start second as the page; `documents.stelle` says "bei 12:30", `wissen.where` gives the model "Aufnahme: talk, bei 12:30", text view, content search and analyses show the time. At the end *Keep originals* keeps only the **sound track** (Opus 32 kbit/s mono as `docs/files/<id>.ogg`, within the space), ▶ plays it at the place (range answers as Safari needs them); without that switch the file is deleted. After that everything works as with any document: card, analyses, meaning search, collections. The transcript does not tell speakers apart. It is **outside text**: what is said in a recording is data, never an instruction, and never goes into the memory. **Priority stays with people (`common.SpeechMark`):** a POST to `/v1/audio/…` counts as "somebody speaks" unless it carries the stage "hinten" **and** the valid priority key from this computer (`common.signed_low`); without the key, with a wrong key or from another computer it counts as speech. Real speech always marks and cancels the transcription at once (self-tests `test_vorrang`). When the panel runs on its own computer (Pi) the speech service does not know the key: the transcription then runs at the normal stage. Status → Live: box *Transcribe recordings* ("Hintergrund: 1 Aufnahme · 82 min offen · heute 24 Stücke"), the background step is called "Abschrift"; Logs area Wissen: `abschrift: stueck | 30 s 412 zeichen`, `abschrift: fertig | 94 min 189 stuecke | tonspur ja`, `abschrift: asr fehlt`, `abschrift: grenze | 240 min`, `abschrift: abgebrochen | sprache`. iPhone: My things → Documents shows "transcribing", uploading, listening and stopping in the panel view (`iphone.THINGS["documents"]`); Android through the panel view. Self-tests `tests/test_abschrift.py`.

**Transcribe YouTube links:** admin switch *Features → Own documents → Transcribe recordings → Transcribe YouTube links* (`chat.doc_youtube`, off by default; also needs *Links as documents* and *Transcribe recordings*; no profile switch, the admin blocks single profiles, guests never). **In only through Links as documents:** the field "Add link" under Me → Documents or the tool `link_als_dokument` with an address that stands word for word in the own message, never from a page, mail or search result. When `ytquelle.video_id` finds a video number (watch?v=, youtu.be/, shorts/, live/, embed/; only youtube.com, www., m., music. and youtu.be, without login data, no playlist page), `doklink` keeps only `https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=<number>` (`ytquelle.kanonisch`; the same video in another spelling is a double). **yt-dlp:** license Unlicense, exactly version 2026.10.26 with the SHA-256 of its wheel in `app/requirements-ytdlp.lock` (without optional packages), installed only with `sudo ./install.sh --youtube` on the panel computer into `/opt/speech-spark/venv-ytdlp` (`pip --no-deps --require-hashes --only-binary :all:`, once per version of the file; an update keeps it current). `ytquelle.da` checks the version at run time too; without yt-dlp or with another version the Spark tries nothing and the row says "yt-dlp is not installed on the panel computer (sudo ./install.sh --youtube, guide: Features → Transcribe YouTube links)". Started without a shell (`asyncio.create_subprocess_exec`), isolated (`python -I -m yt_dlp`, only PATH, HOME, TMPDIR, LANG and XDG folders in its own empty folder, no proxy variables) and only with the fixed options `ytquelle.FEST`: `--ignore-config --no-plugin-dirs --no-js-runtimes --no-remote-components --no-cookies --no-cookies-from-browser --no-cache-dir --no-playlist --use-extractors youtube --no-exec --fixup never --downloader native`, `-f bestaudio[protocol^=http]`, `--max-filesize` = `chat.doc_audio_mb`, `--ffmpeg-location` pointing at an empty folder. **netguard proxy** (`netproxy.py`): per run a CONNECT proxy on 127.0.0.1 with a random port and a one-time key (Proxy-Authorization, compared in constant time); only CONNECT to port 443, only names of the fixed list `ytquelle.erlaubt` (youtube.com, www.youtube.com, m.youtube.com, youtu.be, consent.youtube.com, youtubei.googleapis.com, i.ytimg.com and exactly one label before .googlevideo.com), never an IP; every name through `netguard.resolve(host, 443, PUBLIC)` (public addresses only, kill switch, Live), the tunnel goes to exactly that address; at most 8 tunnels at once, `chat.doc_audio_mb` + 30 MB from the servers per run, else every tunnel closes. Refusals are fixed words (`netproxy.WORTE`: methode, anmeldung, port, host, privat, notaus, netz, viele, groesse). `netguard.DIRECT_OK` lists `netproxy` as "geprueft". **Flow** (`ytquelle.holen`, from the run of Links as documents): first a look without download (`-J`, 90 s): the number matches, no live stream, no premiere, length at most `chat.doc_audio_min`, known size at most `chat.doc_audio_mb`; then only the sound track (at most 30 minutes) into a fresh folder `docs/audio-wait/yt-…/` (0700, deleted afterwards). The file goes through `abschrift.aufnehmen` like an upload (first bytes, size, length with ffprobe, queue, storage, second sign-in step) and is transcribed in quiet minutes with time marks. Title and channel are only data: without control and markup signs, cut, as the name "Title · Channel.m4a" (`ytquelle.name_of`); origin `yt:<number>`, every video once; there is no "Fetch again" for the transcript. yt-dlp's error text never reaches the log; fixed patterns make one word of it (video, live, laenge, format, groesse, host, zeit, netz). Journal `ytquelle: …` (area wissen), Live: box Links with "YouTube: today n sound tracks, kB, refused, foreign host blocked" or "yt-dlp fehlt". Weiche: transcription words with an address ("Transcribe https://youtu.be/…") go like "Save … as a document" to Links as documents. Without a JavaScript runtime (on purpose not installed) YouTube gives some videos no sound track; the row then says "no sound track to fetch". Self-test `test_ytquelle` (another host and a private address refused at the proxy, kill switch, key, method, port, byte limit, fixed options, version and hash, without yt-dlp the sentence with the way, live and too long refused before the download, no link from outside text, logs without address or title).

**Audio overview of a collection:** admin switch *Features → Own documents → Collections → Audio overview* (`chat.hoerstueck`, off by default; builds on *Collections*; no profile switch, the admin blocks single profiles, guests never), below it *Audio overviews per day and profile* (`chat.hoerstueck_day`, 1 to 10, default 3) and *The three voices* (`chat.hoerstueck_stimmen`, per role A host, B expert, C questions a voice of the speech output or `-` for the standard voice; without a choice the standard voice, with VoiceDesign a fixed description per role, `hoerstueck.KLANG`). At the bottom of a collection stands the part *Audio overview*: length 5, 10 or 20 minutes, 2 or 3 voices, "Make" (`POST /api/profile/mappen/{id}/hoerstuecke`, module `hoerstueck.py`, login, own profile, CSRF, 8 KB, rate limit "hoerstueck"). **What the model gets:** only the collection's documents at "short" and "whole", never knowledge book pages (model text), as numbered places [S1] … from the originals, at most 16 000 characters, in turns from each document; pieces the summary and key points of the analyses rest on first (only their place, never their text).
