Everything as outside text (`chat.wrap_outside`). **What the model delivers:** only a script as JSON `{"titel", "zeilen": [{"sprecher", "text", "quelle"}]}`. **Fixed rules** (`hoerstueck.pruefe`): speakers only from `SPRECHER` (A, B, C; with 2 voices only A and B), roles and names come from the panel; a line with a source needs an offered place and passes `wissensbuch.check_sentence` against it (length, no links, no markup, no command words, every number stands in the place), at most 300 characters; a transition without a source has at most 120 characters, no digit and only words from `hoerstueck.WORTE` (so never a name or a number), transitions at most 30 % of the lines and never the last; double lines go; at most 120 lines and the characters of the length (4 500, 9 000, 18 000), cut at a line's end; below 10 valid lines: "No audio overview can be made of it". A fixed opening ("Ein Hörstück des Spark aus deiner Mappe … Es kann Fehler enthalten; die Quellen stehen im Panel.") and a fixed closing come from the panel. What drops out is counted per reason (speaker, place, number, form, transition, share, length, double), never shown as text. **Speaking:** `hoerstueck.due_once` is a link of `wissen.KETTE` after the analyses: first the script (one model call through `vorrang.run("Hörstück")`), then per step exactly one line through the shared helper `tonteile.sprich` with the stage "last" and the priority key (`common.signed_low`: never counts as speech, waits behind every person); real speech cancels the line and it comes again in the next pause. Always only one request at a time (the slot of `vorrang`; the speech output is weak with several requests at once). Without the speech output (5xx, no connection or the service watch sees it gone) it waits 5 minutes without losing a line. The lines wait as WAV in `users/<id>/hoerstuecke/<id>/` (folder 0700, files 0600); at the end `ffmpeg` (no shell, `-protocol_whitelist file,pipe`) writes one Opus file at 48 kbit/s with 300 ms pause between two voices, and the parts go. Seconds of the speech output count in the usage as "Hörstück". `dungeon.ask_tts` uses the same helper (`tonteile.sprich`, in the foreground without a stage). **Looking at it:** the list with its state ("waiting", "speaking · 34 of 61 lines", "done", "too little"), a progress bar and "The collection has changed since" (fingerprint of the documents, new only per click); a finished one with a player (`GET /api/profile/hoerstueck/{id}/ton`, ranges, only the own profile), download, delete and the script: every line with its voice, for a statement a chip with document, page and heading that opens the document; opening and closing as "fixed", transitions as "transition". **Limits:** at most 10 stored per profile, `chat.hoerstueck_day` new ones a day, at most 20 minutes each. **Never a source:** an audio overview never becomes a document and never goes into the knowledge book, the search, Find or the memory; a deleted collection takes its audio overviews along, never a document; deleting works also while the admin blocked it, and under Me with "Delete data" with the collections. **Tool** `hoerstueck_machen` (routing group "Hörstück": hörstück, podcast, zum anhören, audio overview): only queues an audio overview with 2 voices, the collection only when its name stands in the own message; locked after outside text (changing), never over MCP. Live: box *Hörstück* (audio overviews, waiting, speaking, open lines, today new, scripts, lines spoken, lines dropped, cancels, missing speech output; numbers only). Logs `hoerstueck: drehbuch | 61 zeilen 7 verworfen …`, `hoerstueck: zeile | 12 von 61 …`, `hoerstueck: fertig | 612 s …` (area knowledge), only fixed words, numbers and `profiles.log_name`.

**Audio overview: apps, Telegram, notice, English:** no switch of its own, everything hangs on *Audio overview*. **The line while playing:** `hoerstueck.zusammen` keeps the start of every line in the sound (`ab`, seconds); while playing, the panel marks the line being spoken in the script (building block `.ld-row.on`), the time in front of a line plays from there. Audio overviews without time marks have no marking. **AAC copy:** AVFoundation (iPhone) and Telegram's `sendAudio` take no Ogg; beside the Opus file `ffmpeg` (no shell, the same rules) writes an AAC copy `.m4a` at 64 kbit/s (`hoerstueck.m4a`), for older ones on the first ask; `GET /api/profile/hoerstueck/{id}/ton?form=m4a` delivers it (`audio/mp4`, ranges, only the own profile). **iPhone:** My things → Collections → a collection → Audio overview (only when the admin released it; `iphone.THINGS` "hoerstueck"): length and voices, "Make", the list with progress, a finished one with its own player (the AAC copy only in memory, never stored on the iPhone), playing in the background and on the lock screen (Now Playing, play, pause, seek), the script with the marked line, "To Telegram" and "Delete". Android shows the part in the panel view of the collection. **Telegram:** "To Telegram" (`POST /api/profile/hoerstueck/{id}/telegram`, login, own profile, CSRF, 8 KB, rate limit "hoerstuecktg" 5 a minute) sends the AAC copy by `sendAudio` through `telegram.send_file` only to the chat linked to the profile, never another, only when personal data is allowed there (as for made documents), not during the Notaus, at most 45 MB; its title is the collection's name, never model text. Without a link the button is missing and a hint with a jump link names Me → Telegram. **Notice:** when done, "Hörstück fertig" ("Audio overview ready") comes through `push.send` (kind "Hörstück", personal) with the way "Collections → <collection> → Audio overview" as a jump link on every device; only the collection's name, never the model's title or a line. **English:** the script is made in the language the person uses the panel or the app in (`sprache` de or en when making it; the tool takes the language of the own words): an English instruction, a fixed English opening and closing, English transition words (`hoerstueck.WORDS_EN`), otherwise the same rules (speaker, place, number check, length, share); the speech output gets the language (`tonteile.sprich` `sprache`). **Delete data under Me:** its own card "Audio overview" (`features.OWN`, `account.WHO`): when the admin blocks audio overviews for a profile, the page Collections shows the number and "Delete data". Companion: on purpose not (`features.DESK_SKIP`; listening in the panel, the app and Telegram). Live: box *Hörstück* also "auf Englisch", "AAC-Kopien", "Mitteilungen", "Telegram: N gesendet, M nicht". Logs `hoerstueck: telegram | ok` (or `aus`, `notaus`, `gross`, `fehler`), `telegram: audio sent | … KB`.

**Make an audio overview now:** no switch of its own, part of the audio overview. Only the admin sees under Collections → a collection → Audio overview next to "Make" the button "Make now", and for one that waits or speaks "Make it now" (pull forward); the iPhone has the same buttons. Whether it is the admin is decided by the server (`coadmin.ist_admin`): the panel password's login (only while a password is set) or the profile with the role admin in its browser login with the second step or its proved iPhone app; never a manager, a guest or a device key. Endpoints `POST /api/profile/mappen/{id}/hoerstuecke/sofort` (length, voices, language as for making) and `POST /api/profile/hoerstueck/{id}/sofort`: sign-in, own profile, release, admin right, CSRF, 8 KB, rate limit "hoerstuecksofort" 5 a minute. A forced one (column `sofort`) skips the quiet minutes (`wissen.quiet`), the quiet chain before it and the day limit (not counted there); the ten stored and the check of the script stay. Real speech goes first: `hoerstueck.sofort_lauf` sends every request through `vorrang.run` (waits while anybody speaks, is cancelled when speech starts, the line comes again) and to the speech output with the stage "last" and its key, so the own lines do not count as speech (`common.SpeechMark`). At most one forced one at a time over all profiles (`hoerstueck._sofort_offen`, else 409); while it runs the quiet chain leaves audio overviews alone, afterwards it takes a marked one first. Live: box *Audio overview* "erzwungen: writes the script / speaks / waits", "today N× made now, M× refused". Logs `hoerstueck: erzwungen | neu` (or `vorgezogen`), `hoerstueck: erzwungen abgelehnt | admin` (or `laeuft`, `keins`, `form`, `voll`, `leer`), `hoerstueck: erzwungen | ende | fertig` (or `leer`, `x`, `aus`, `weg`, `tts`, `schritte`).
**What an audio overview waits for:** a `vorrang.run` inside another one (such as the script's model call `llm.complete` inside `vorrang.run("Hörstück")`, the same for the analyses) runs at once under its slot (`vorrang._inside`); the outer one waits for a pause and is cancelled as soon as somebody speaks. Real speech goes first, only one background request runs at a time. The list says with a fixed word what an audio overview waits for (`hoerstueck._warten` from `vorrang.grund`): "waits: somebody speaks (real speech goes first)", "language model maintenance", "other background work runs" or "speech output missing"; without a reason "script being written". A failed one says why ("the language model gave no usable script", "putting it together failed", "the collection is gone"). If a forced one cannot start, the place is freed at once (503, log `hoerstueck: erzwungen | ende | start`); after a restart of the panel an open forced one runs on by itself (`hoerstueck.weiter`, log `hoerstueck: erzwungen | weiter`). Live: box *Audio overview* "wartet auf: Gespräch / Wartung des Sprachmodells / andere Hintergrundarbeit". The iPhone app shows the same texts.

**Audio overview in CarPlay:** no switch of its own; the row "Audio overviews" stands in the car only with *Audio overview* (hello area "hoerstueck"). `GET /api/profile/hoerstuecke` (`hoerstueck.fertige`, login, own profile, rate limit "hoerstueck", read only; `iphone.THINGS` "hoerstueck" only with GET) gives the finished audio overviews of all own collections with title, the collection's name, length and time, the newest first, at most 12 (`hoerstueck.AUTO_MAX`), never the script or a path. Nothing is made in the car. The app plays through one player for the collection and the car (`ios/Spark/HoerstueckPlayer.swift`, `HsPlayer`): the AAC copy only in memory (its own session without a cache), Now Playing with title and collection, remote buttons (play, pause, 15 seconds forward and back, next and previous, position), on where it stopped (on the iPhone only id and seconds, at most 20). Before it plays a conversation ends and the speech engine hands over the sound (`Conversation.giveSound`; the engine sets "play and record" again at its next start); the wake word rests while an audio overview plays; a conversation pauses the audio overview, afterwards it plays on. When the car disconnects it pauses. Sound log only with fixed words ("Hörstück: spielt", "Pause für Gespräch", "zu Ende"). Live: no value of its own (the playing happens on the iPhone).

**Layout of the Collections page:** display only, no switches, endpoints or logs of its own. The page *Collections* has no tabs: on the left the list in 300 px with the head "Collections 3 of 50" and **＋** (opens "New collection"), per row line symbol, name, goal and on the right the number of entries; on the right the open collection. Its head: symbol, name, "goal · N entries", **Ask** (binds the next questions in the assistant to the collection, the only blue button) and **⋯** with *Rename* and *Delete collection* (asks first). Below it a thin bar "177 / 24,000 tokens", for entries that are too big "· N only in parts", red only "· N no longer fit the budget", and **ⓘ** with the explanation (off, short, whole, budget, deleting removes only the assignment) and, while audio overviews are released, how one is made. A segment divides the collection into **Entries · Analyses · Audio overview** with counts; *Analyses* only while analyses are released (`mappen.mappe` gives `auswertungen` for it), *Audio overview* only with audio overviews. **Entries:** "6 of 100 entries" and the menu **Add** (document, page of the knowledge book, note; the choice or the note field below, with *Cancel*); per row symbol, the **whole title** (wraps, never cut off), "PDF · ≈ 59 tokens" (only the number of the chosen level; else "too big: only fitting parts", "budget full" or "knowledge book blocked"), the level as a **small menu** off/short/whole and **⋯** with *Open* and *Take out of the collection*; tapping opens the document. **Analyses:** per document "Summary · Key points" or "no analysis yet", "done" and ›; tapping opens the document with its analysis (made by themselves, no button). **Audio overview:** one row *Length* · *Voices* · *Make* (a normal button; for the admin *Make now* beside it, *Make it now* under ⋯ for a waiting one) · "today 2 of 3", below it the chosen or newest finished audio overview as a card (title, "date · length · voices", player, ⋯ with *Download*, *To Telegram*, *Delete*, *Script · N lines* folded with the roles and what was dropped), then the others as rows with state and progress; tapping shows one in the card. On a phone (below 660 px) first the list, tapping shows the collection with "‹ Collections", the head in two rows (name, then "Ask" wide and ⋯), the audio overview row stacked. Collections stands in `features.OWN_NO_SETTINGS` (in the panel `kalender.js OWN_NO_SET`): jump links, guides and the list of all features never lead to a tab *Settings* of Collections. Browser test `test_mappen_seite`: list narrower than the collection, at most one blue button per part, no cut-off title, no sideways scrolling at 1440 and 390 px, level through the menu, ⋯ → *Take out of the collection*, make an audio overview from the part *Audio overview*.

**Admin without a day limit for audio overviews and documents:** the day limits of *Audio overview* (`chat.hoerstueck_day`), *Analyses* (`chat.doc_auswertung_day`), *Transcribe recordings* with YouTube links (`chat.doc_audio_day_min`) and *Links as documents* (`chat.doc_link_day`, also "Fetch again") follow the same rule as pictures, music and the allowance: `coadmin.unbegrenzt`. No limit for a profile with an admin role that counts (second step) and for every profile with the tick "No daily limit" (People and devices, at the profile under Rights). For an audio overview made on a click the admin himself counts too, decided by the server as for "Make now": `coadmin.unbegrenzt(uid, request)` then asks `coadmin.ist_admin` (one source in `coadmin.py`), so the panel password's login as well. Background work (analyses, transcripts, fetching the links) knows no request and follows only the role and the tick. Space, queues, the ten stored audio overviews, size and length of a recording and the priority of speech stay. The page then shows "today N · no daily limit" instead of "today N of 3" and never "The limit for today is reached." (`frei` in `GET /api/profile/mappen/{id}/hoerstuecke`, the same on the iPhone), Links as documents "N fetches today, no daily limit" (`frei` in `doklink.info`), a recording never "daily limit reached" (`frei` in `abschrift.info`). It is still counted, only never refused. Self-tests `test_hoerstueck`, `test_auswertung`, `test_abschrift`, `test_doklink`.

**Audio overview as a discussion:** admin switch *Features → Own documents → Collections → Audio overview → Audio overview as a discussion* (`chat.hoerstueck_diskussion`, off by default; builds on *Audio overview*; no profile switch, never guests). Off, one script with `hoerstueck.pruefe` applies. On, every new audio overview is made in two steps (`hoerstueck_diskussion.py`), at most one model call per quiet step: **Talk plan**: the model only delivers chapters with place numbers and a kind from the fixed list (opening, claim, point of dispute, example, turning point, conclusion); the panel keeps only offered places, each once, per chapter at most four times its dialog characters of places (at most 8,000 characters), 3 chapters for 5 minutes, 4 for 10, 5 for 20; the panel sets the bend from the places (opening, conclusion, "What has changed", see *round* below); a too thin plan is replaced by the panel's fixed plan (the places split in order). The places are neighbouring pieces in blocks (up to 40,000 characters). **Dialog per chapter**: one call gets only this chapter's original places, the fixed word of its kind and the fixed role cards (A host: asks, leads on, answers no expert question, at most one sentence with a place per contribution, sums up only in the conclusion; B expert: explains calmly with examples and carries the facts; C sceptic: objects, may name risks with a place, never answers its own question; with two voices A is the sceptic too), never text of step 1. A line is a **contribution** of at most four sentences (at most 900 characters; an answer has two to four, a question may be short). Every sentence with a source passes the number check against its place, also for written-out numbers: a number word stands there as the same word or as the same whole number in digits ("drei bis sechs Monate" for "3–6 Monate", `hoerstueck_diskussion.zahl_ok`), never another number and never a part of "3,8". **Talk sentences without a source** (question, objection, reaction): at most 160 characters, no digit, no number word, no links or command words, every word stands in the chapter's places or in the fixed list of talk words (`hoerstueck_worte.json`, about 2,400 words, plus the transition list); at most 40 % of a chapter's sentences (above that, talk sentences without a question go first, then later questions). In the script they carry the note "Talk", facts keep their source chip; chapters stand as headings above. **Measure**: after each chapter the panel measures the length of the answers (average at least 180 characters; short questions do not count), the share of questions (15 to 40 %), at least one objection of the sceptic (not in the conclusion), no voice more than three times in a row, at least 40 % of the places used, at least half of the sentences with a place from B, no list like bullet points, and from two repairs of one kind (self-answer, open question, repeat of a chapter before) or a greeting after chapter 1 their fixed hint; a flat chapter is written exactly once more with fixed hints, the better one stays (first one without a list like bullet points). At most 11 model calls per piece. The list says "Script being written · chapter 2 of 4". Live (Status → Live, box Hörstück) numbers only: chapters planned, written, rewritten, average contribution length, talk sentences dropped, sentences moved to another voice, open questions dropped, plans with versions, average roundness. Journal with fixed words: `hoerstueck: plan | 4 kapitel fassungen`, `hoerstueck: kapitel | 2 von 4 | 9 beitraege 12 verworfen 3 repariert | …`, `hoerstueck: flach | nochmal | kapitel 2`, `hoerstueck: drehbuch | … diskussion | rund 9 von 10`. Limit: the word check stops invented names, numbers and terms, not every invented claim made of allowed words; so the prompt asks for questions and reactions instead of statements, and "Talk" shows what is not backed.

**Audio overview as a discussion: way of speaking, voices, pauses:** no switch of its own, everything hangs on *Audio overview as a discussion*. **Way of speaking per contribution**: the panel sets a word of a fixed list (curious, thoughtful, enthusiastic, sceptical, laughing, serious, calm) by the kind of contribution (`hoerstueck_diskussion.sprechweise`: objection sceptical, question curious, in the conclusion calm, an explanation with a risk or liability word serious, else B calm and the others thoughtful; a word of the model never counts); that word never goes to the speech output, only the panel's fixed English addition from `hoerstueck_stimmen.json` (`hoerstueck_diskussion.zusatz`; an unknown word gives no addition). `tonteile.sprich` has the parameter `art` for it (empty by default, calls without it stay unchanged, also in the Dungeon): with VoiceDesign it is added to the role's description, with a chosen or the standard voice it goes as `instructions`, which 1.7B-CustomVoice follows; 0.6B ignores it. **Voices**: below the switch the admin chooses a fixed description per role (six each, entry 0 is the standard voice; `chat.hoerstueck_klang`) and listens with "▶ Sample" (`GET /api/admin/hoerstueck/probe`, admin only, a fixed sentence, kept in memory; `GET /api/admin/hoerstueck/stimmen` gives the names only). It needs VoiceDesign: *Settings → Speech output → "Also provide VoiceDesign"* (loads on demand, see below). While it is on, in a discussion every role speaks with its description, also when a fixed voice is chosen under "The three voices"; the fixed voice only stands in when VoiceDesign does not come (`hoerstueck.stimme_von`; the audio overview of before keeps the chosen voice first). "▶ Sample" waits while VoiceDesign loads: the endpoint answers 503 "VoiceDesign lädt …" with `Retry-After` (`PROBE_NOCHMAL_S`, 10 s), the page asks again and shows "VoiceDesign is loading … N s", at most `LADEN_MAX_S`; when VoiceDesign cannot start (not enough memory, stopped, error) it says so in a fixed sentence (`PROBE_SAGT`); log `hoerstueck: probe | neu|behalten|laedt|fehler …`. Before, the first sample after unloading only said "busy or missing" (Dominik 2026-11-12). While VoiceDesign is off, the settings and for the admin the audio overview page of a collection say so with this way; then the fixed voices speak. **Pauses** by fixed rules: answer after a question 200 ms, objection 120 ms, the same voice 250 ms, new chapter 900 ms, before the conclusion 700 ms. **Reactions** as short lines of their own by fixed rules (`hoerstueck_diskussion.rueckmeldungen`, words in `hoerstueck_stimmen.json`): only inside a long explanation (at least 180 characters with a place) between it and the next contribution of the same voice or between the halves of a contribution of three sentences or more, never inside a list, never at a chapter's start or end, at most 10 % of the lines, at least four lines apart; the voice is listening (the one who asked, else the first other role, never the one that goes on); the word by rule: "Ach so." after an answer to a question, "Stimmt." after an objection, else "Mhm." (in English "I see.", "True.", "Mhm."); the same word of the same voice is spoken once and then reused as a part. In the script the way of speaking and "Reaction" stand as notes. Live counts lines with a way of speaking and reactions (spoken, kept).

**Audio overview: described voices while VoiceDesign is still loading:** no switch of its own. VoiceDesign runs on demand and is unloaded after 5 minutes without a request (`tts.voicedesign_leerlauf_min`); so the first line of an audio overview gets the answer "loading" (or "busy"). The line then waits 30 seconds (`hoerstueck.LADEN_S`) and comes again, every try keeps VoiceDesign loaded; log line `hoerstueck: stimmen laden | zeile N | naechster versuch in 30 s | Profil …`, the audio overview's row shows "waits: VoiceDesign is loading", Live "waits for: VoiceDesign loading"; "Make it now" does not count this wait as an outage. Only when VoiceDesign is not there after 10 minutes (`LADEN_MAX_S`, the first load takes minutes), or at once when it cannot start (not enough memory, stopped, error), the fixed voices speak, for the rest of this audio overview (no change of voice in the middle). The script says so: "Fixed voices from line N: VoiceDesign did not come in time" (or "… could not start, not enough free memory", "… was stopped or in error", "… is off in the speech output"; fields `ersatz`, `ersatz_ab`, fixed words `hoerstueck.ERSATZ`, also in the iPhone app), log `hoerstueck: stimmen fehlen | feste stimme | laedt|speicher|fehlt|aus | zeile N | Profil …`. The switch stays on: before, the panel then took VoiceDesign as off for 10 minutes, the settings said "VoiceDesign is off" and the sample refused. An ask to the speech output without answer keeps the last word and asks again after 30 seconds (`STIMMEN_FEHL_S`). The speech output (`tts_proxy.design_bereit`) starts VoiceDesign on demand again also when an earlier start failed for memory or an error (at most every 10 minutes); before, it never came back. Live counts waiting lines, audio overviews with fixed voices and the voice samples (new, loading, failed). Before, the standard voice stood in at once for 10 minutes, VoiceDesign was unloaded unused in that time, and no audio overview ever got its described voices (code review 2026-11-11; Dominik 2026-11-12 "audio plays only with the built-in voices"). (🧪 test_hoerstueck, test_hoerstueck_diskussion)

**Audio overview as a discussion: sample and iPhone:** in a collection the admin has a short discussion made at once with "Sample (2 min)": only the opening and one point of dispute (two chapters, at most 1800 characters), like "Make now" (`POST /api/profile/mappen/{id}/hoerstuecke/probe`, admin only, rate limit as "Make now", without the switch *Audio overview as a discussion* 409 with the way). It never counts towards the daily limit, shows in the list as "Sample · …" and is gone with its sound file after one day (`hoerstueck.proben_weg`, hourly). The iPhone app shows chapter headings, "Conversation", the way of speaking, "Reaction" and the sample in the script. Live counts samples (made, gone), logs only "hoerstueck: hoerprobe | fertig" and "… | weg | number".

**Audio overview as a discussion: round** (plan "Hörstück rund", Dominik 2026-11-11, no switch of its own): the panel makes the talk hang together by fixed rules, without an extra model call (`hoerstueck_diskussion.paare`). **Pairs**: when a voice answers its own question with sentences with a place, it is split: the question stays with the asker, the sentences go to the expert B (when B asks itself, the asking role gets the question). A question without the answer of another voice with a place right after it goes, so no chapter and no piece ends on "?". A contribution only of talk sentences without a question ("Genau.") goes. Outside the conclusion the host A keeps one sentence with a place per contribution, the others go to B. The piece is cut only at a contribution's end, each chapter with its share of the length, then the chapter's end is repaired again; the conclusion is never cut away. **Thread**: every chapter gets fixed facts (chapter i of n, the kind before and after; only chapter 1 greets, from chapter 2 the panel drops sentences with "Willkommen" or "Hallo"; an interim conclusion only in the conclusion, else the panel drops talk sentences with "zusammenfassend") and as data the headings of the documents already talked about ("Already discussed"), never text of the model. A fact said in a chapter before goes (key from `wissensbuch.norm`, kept in the piece's state only as a checksum). **Bend from the places**: the opening is a hook of two places (the summary of a text, else the pieces of the Kurzfassung and Kernaussagen, else the first of the first chapter); the conclusion gets no new places, only ones used before (the hook first, then the most used, at most three), marked "already discussed"; more chapters than the length allows are merged instead of cut. **Versions**: fixed words in a place ("Hier ist die Zusammenfassung", "Erste Ausgabe", "erste Fassung", "Angepasster Plan", "nach Beantwortung der Rückfragen", "Version 2" …; `hoerstueck_diskussion.FASSUNGEN`) mark the summary, an earlier and the current version of a text, also for the places after it. When a collection has an earlier and a current version, the piece gets the chapter **"What has changed"**: only there stand the places of the earlier version, with their counterparts of the current one; the same statement with another number stands as "[Contradiction] S12 ↔ S21" with the documents' sentences above, and the model is to say "Before it said …, now …"; the newest version counts. Summary places stand only in the opening and the conclusion, never besides their detail places (unless the collection is almost only them). In the script a source chip says "earlier version". **Lists**: the prompt asks for whole sentences with linking words; at least three sentences built alike ("Stufe 1 …", "Erstens …") or a sentence with a place of fewer than four words count as a list (hint "liste" for the one rewrite). **Roundness**: over the finished script the panel counts questions answered, self-answers, open threads at the end, greetings after chapter 1, repeats, lists and reactions in the wrong place (`hoerstueck_diskussion.rundheit`) and shows "round 9/10" with the points missed in the script (panel and iPhone app); moved sentences stand there under "Moved". The model is never the source of a fact, the number check stays as strict.

**Links as documents:** admin switch *Features → Own documents → Links as documents* (`chat.doc_link`, off by default; no profile switch, the admin blocks single profiles, guests never), below it *Largest page or file (MB)* (`chat.doc_link_mb`, 1 to 100, default 20) and *Fetches per day and profile* (`chat.doc_link_day`, 5 to 500, default 50, "Fetch again" included). Under Me → Documents → *Add link* (at the top beside *＋ Add*, only while the switch is released; opens the field below the head) one enters up to 10 addresses at once (one per line); the page sends them to `POST /api/profile/docs/link` (module `doklink.py`, login, own profile, CSRF, size limit 32 KB before reading, rate limit "doklink"). **Fixed rules before the fetch** (`doklink.pruefe`): http or https, a host, no login data (`user:pass@` → refused), port 80 or 443, at most 2000 signs, no IP address and no name of the home network (`localhost`, `.local`, `fritz.box` …); every address gets a fixed word back (accepted, private, login data, port, duplicate …). Accepted ones wait in `dl_link` in `wissen.db` (at most 30 per profile), one runner fetches them one after another, without a model. **One fetch helper:** `netguard.hole_oeffentlich` fetches every page or file from an address, also pages of search results (`chat.page_text`) and the web page to the reMarkable (`rmplus.web_page` through `page_text`): first the kill switch, before every request `netguard.oeffentlich` (every address of the name public), at the connection once more the level *internet only*, at most 4 redirects, each checked again, a fixed browser name, content type from a fixed list (HTML, PDF, text, Markdown, DOCX, ODT; for PDF, DOCX and ODT also the first bytes), size and 30 seconds for all of it. **As a document:** `wissen.aufnehmen(uid, name, data, source, herkunft)` takes the page in with every rule of an upload (second login step, the admin's block, storage room, the most documents, the queue) and notes the origin (`link:` with the SHA-256 of the address); the same address comes in once. HTML goes as `<title>.html` into `documents.add` (headings and tables stay, with a Docling server like every HTML file). The same helper also takes "Add to documents" from Find. **Fetch again** only on a click in the details of a link document (`POST /api/profile/wissen/{doc}/abrufen`): a conditional request with `If-None-Match`/`If-Modified-Since`; 304 or the same SHA-256 means "unchanged", else `documents.renew` reads the new bytes into the same document (knowledge book and analyses see the new text by themselves). ✕ takes waiting or failed addresses out (`DELETE /api/profile/docs/link/{id}`). **Tool** `link_als_dokument`: only offered when an address stands in the own message (Weiche group "Links as documents": an address and a word like "save", "store", "as a document"; "to the reMarkable" stays with the reMarkable), takes only addresses that stand word for word in the person's message, at most 10, is locked after outside text and belongs to the kill switch's internet tools. The page content is **outside text**: what a page says is never an instruction. Status → Live: box *Links* (waiting, link documents, fetched today, read again, unchanged, kB and seconds, never an address); logs area knowledge: `doklink: geholt | html 182 kb`, `doklink: unveraendert`, `doklink: abgelehnt | privat`, `doklink: grenze | 50`. iPhone: My things → Documents in the panel view (`iphone.THINGS["documents"]`); Android through the panel view. Self-tests `tests/test_doklink.py`.

**Find: add an attachment to the documents:** no switch of its own: the button follows *Find mail and attachments* and *Own documents* (`fundmail.dok_frei`; the admin blocks single profiles, guests never). On the page Find (Android too, through the panel view) and in the iPhone app (My things → Find) an attachment hit in its detail and every attachment in a mail's list has the button **Add to documents** when the fixed rule `fundmail.dok_passt` lets it through: a kind from `documents.TYPES` (PDF, Word, OpenDocument, text, spreadsheets, pictures …) and at most 10 MB by its size in the index (`ANHANG_MAX`), otherwise no button. `POST /api/profile/fund/eintrag/{id}/dokument` (browser login or the iPhone app through `iphone.THINGS` "fund", rate limit `funddok`) checks everything again: only an attachment entry of the own index (`fund.eintrag`: own sources and mailboxes); the panel fetches the bytes itself from the own mailbox (`mail.file_of`, EXAMINE and PEEK, the mail stays unread), requires the same file name as in the index and at most 10 MB and hands the file to `wissen.aufnehmen` (source "mail", origin "mail:" + the entry key; every rule of an upload: second login step, block, space, queue). If the attachment is in the documents already, the panel does not ask the mailbox and answers "Already in the documents", otherwise "Added". Both answers name Me → Documents as a jump link, plus "Open the document". A refusal is a fixed sentence (kind, size, no longer in the mailbox, mailbox not reachable, queue full or the documents' own sentence). The Begleiter shows no buttons at hits; a click leads into the panel. Live (box Find): "attachments to documents today n, n already there, n refused"; log `fund: dokument | uebernommen|doppelt` and `fund: dokument abgelehnt | <wort>`.

**Page and point, type rules, deadlines:** no switch of its own, everything hangs on *People, companies and deadlines* (`chat.knowledge_ents`) and the *Knowledge map* (`chat.knowledge_map`). (1) **Page and point:** when a knowledge book page (not of the kind "Antwort") has the same name as a point of the kind person, firma or ort (compared as `ent_norm` does, over the name, the point's other names and the page's earlier titles), both are one point: the link stands in `wk_entpage(ent, page)` (one page, one point; first the point of the page's own kind, then name before other name, then person, firma, ort, then the smaller id). It is made only by this fixed rule (`wissenskarte._verbinde`) when the points are read, in the nightly check and once when the table is made, never on the model's wish. On the map the point stays square with "with page" and "Open page"; the page's connections count as its own (`view`, `rund_um`, `weg` and `liste`; a connection between the two drops out, doubles stay once). When the person renames the point, the page gets the same title in the same step (`wissensbuch.retitle`, under the same code); if that is not possible (title too long or taken), nothing changes. The old title stays in `wb_alttitel`: when the model proposes a page under the old title while working in or after "Write again", it lands on the renamed page (`wissensbuch.current_title`), no double page; the page shows it as "Before". *Rename page* for pages without a point: `POST /api/profile/wissensbuch/{id}/name` with sign-in, profile binding, CSRF, size limit 512 bytes, rate limit and the code of "Confirm" (`stufen.LIST`); it refuses a page with a point (409, rename on the map). A deleted page drops its link in the same step (`wissensbuch.SEITEN_WEG`). `GEAENDERT` reports points only, never pages. (2) **Type rules:** `wissenskarte.TYPEN` fixes per kind of connection from which kind to which it may go, for example "works at" only from person to firma or ort, "lives in" only to ort, "deadline for" only from frist; a page counts as "seite", a page with a point as that point's kind. `check_edge` refuses violations with the reason `typ` (journal `typ=n`). (3) **Deadlines:** a deadline needs a date or a number with a unit of time in its name ("31.12.2026", "14 Tage"); number words before a unit of time become digits by `ZAHLWORTE` ("einem Monat" → "1 Monat", "zwei Wochen" → "2 Wochen"), the name must stand in the passage as said or in digits, else `no date`. (4) **Nightly check** (`wissenskarte.nachtpruefung`, once per night and profile in the search check's night window): links pages and points, removes stored connections against the type rules and deadlines without a number or date (with `GEAENDERT` "weg") and writes `wissen: wissenskarte geprueft | n verbindungen gegen typregeln | n fristen ohne zahl | n mit seite`. Live, box Knowledge map: the number of points with a page and what the night removed. **Correcting a name:** Knowledge book → Map, tap the point, enter the new name, "Rename" with the code (the page changes too); two points for the same thing: "Merge with …"; a wrong point: "Delete"; a page without a point: Knowledge book → Pages, tap the page, "Rename page". Round is a knowledge book page, square a point from the documents; the old name always stays as a second name. Self-test `test_wissensseite`.

**Find doubles and a shorter choice:** no switch of its own, it belongs to *People, companies and deadlines* (`chat.knowledge_ents`) and follows its release; guests never. Merging by itself is the rule by which points are made (as the same name gets the same point); it reports and sends nothing and only changes the own map. Whether two points are the same is decided only by `wissensdoppelt.aehnlich(art, a, b) -> (stufe, grund)`, never by the model: `stufe` is `gleich`, `vorschlag` or None, `grund` a fixed word (`schreibweise`, `anrede`, `reihenfolge`, `initiale`, `rechtsform`, `tippfehler`). The normal form per kind is compared (`wissensdoppelt.normalform`): ß/ss, ä/ae, ö/oe, ü/ue, accents, signs and spaces do not count; people without form of address and title (`wissenskarte.ANREDEN`: Herr, Frau, Hr., Fr., Dr., Prof., Dipl.-Ing. …) and "last name, first name" as "first name last name"; companies without legal form (`wissenskarte.RECHTSFORMEN`: GmbH, AG, KG, GmbH & Co. KG, UG, e. V., e. K., OHG, SE, Ltd, Inc …), without "Firma" in front and with "&" as "und"; places with "Str." as "Straße". **The same** are only people with first and last name and places that differ in nothing but a form of address, a title, the spelling or the order of last and first name ("Herr Dominik Bornhäuser", "Dr. Dominik Bornhäuser", "Bornhäuser, Dominik"; never "Herr" and "Frau" together), and accounts (without spaces), contracts and deadlines with the same normal form. A company is never the same by itself: "Müller GmbH" and "Müller AG" are only a suggestion. **A suggestion** are small typing errors (Damerau-Levenshtein at most 2 for normal forms from 6 letters, 1 for 4 to 5, never below; "Dominik Bornhäußer"), an initial ("D. Bornhäuser") only when exactly one person fits, and companies with the same core without legal form. Different numbers are never the same point; accounts, contracts and deadlines never by typing errors. **While reading** (`wissenskarte._match` → `wissensdoppelt.gleich_id`) a name that is the same becomes an other name of the existing point instead of a new one; a key index (`wk_entkey`, fingerprint in `wk_entsig`) keeps that fast. **In quiet minutes** (`wissen.due_once` after the map, `wissensdoppelt.due_once`) one run per profile merges existing points that are the same through `wissenskarte.zusammen` (the same function as the click, `GEAENDERT` says `zusammen`) and fills `wk_paar(a, b, art, grund, stand, na, nb, at)` with suggestions (`stand` offen). Comparisons happen only in blocks (the same first or last three letters, the same last name, the same core of a company) with neighbours of similar length, at most 200,000 comparisons per run, in slices of at most 0.2 s (the next goes on where the last stood), 100 new and 300 open suggestions per profile; with 5000 points never all with all. A run comes only when the points changed, at most six times a day per profile. **On the map** "Maybe double" with its number stands on top: the pairs with their reason and "Merge" (through `POST /api/profile/wissenskarte/knoten/{id}/zusammen`, with the code of "Confirm") or "Different" (`POST /api/profile/wissenskarte/paare/verschieden`, without a code, nothing is lost): kept by the names (`stand` verschieden), the pair never comes back and is never merged by itself. At the chosen point "Maybe the same person: …" (company, place …) stands with the same two buttons. "Merge with …" is a search field: the 8 most similar points of the same kind on top, while typing at most 20 hits by name and other names (`GET /api/profile/wissenskarte/knoten/{id}/aehnlich?q=`, `wissensdoppelt.auswahl`). All these ways with login, profile binding, CSRF, size limit (256 bytes) and rate limit. Logs fixed words and numbers only: `wissen: wissenskarte doppelt einlesen | 1 knoten | anrede`, `wissen: wissenskarte doppelt geprueft | n zusammengefuehrt | n vorschlaege neu | n offen | n weg | n vergleiche | fertig` (a slice with more to do ends with `| weiter`, a limit with `| grenze`), `wissen: wissenskarte doppelt verschieden | 1 paar` and `… abgelehnt | grund`. Live, box Knowledge map: "Doppelte: n Vorschläge offen, m automatisch zusammengeführt". iPhone: "Maybe double" below the map and at the point, swipe left for "Different", delete; merging with a hint to the panel. Self-test `test_wissensdoppelt`.

**Document list for many files:** Me → Documents shows every document as one compact row (icon, title, kind · sender · source, date and size); badges only when something matters ("due 20/12", "reading 3/12", "not readable", "for everyone", "search off"). The list scrolls with the page, shows 50 rows and then "Show more", and is grouped into Today, Last 7 days, months and years when sorted by date. At the top a search field filters at once by title, file name, sender, kind, number and tags, Enter also searches the content (hits open the document); source (uploaded, iPhone app, companion, reMarkable, jobs, from others), sort order (newest, name, deadline, size; source and sort order stay in the browser) and quick filters with counts (deadline open, being read, not readable, for everyone, search off) plus tags as a list. A click opens the details, beside the list on wide pages, as their own page with "Back to the list" on narrow ones: card, state with bar, tags edited in place, switches "The assistant searches it" and "For everyone", open original, read text (with "Belongs with"), remind, read again, delete. Documents others share are in the same list (source "From others"). "Select" ticks documents and acts on all ticked own ones: search on/off, for everyone on/off, add a tag, read again (at most 20), delete (with a question first), via `POST /api/profile/wissen/batch` (`{"do", "ids"}`, the profile's browser login only, CSRF, one step of the "doc" rate limit per request, at most 200 ids, 8 KB body, only documents of the own database, "for everyone" and tags only with their switches). The six switches and the note on file kinds sit folded under "Settings for documents". The layout "list with details" uses the CSS building blocks `ld-…` in app.css.

**Layout of the Me pages:** memory, mail, reMarkable and the other Me pages follow one pattern: sub-tabs with counts (an orange count needs you), a status card with "⋯" for rare actions, compact rows with small buttons, no scroll boxes (the first 10, then "Show more"), "add" as a button, anything irreversible at the bottom in the danger zone. One tap answers a mail question, "Always for this sender" makes a rule; the preview is grouped by target folder. reMarkable: folder tree with ticks and its content beside it, saved with the bar at the bottom. Memory: search, filter (from you / automatic), change entries in place and delete several at once.

**Creating documents:** on request the Spark writes letters, reports, minutes, notes, summaries, invitations, presentations and spreadsheets as a file: Word (.docx), PDF, PowerPoint (.pptx), Excel (.xlsx), Markdown, text or CSV. Switches: *Features → Own documents → Creating documents* (`chat.doc_make`, off) and below it *Own templates* (`chat.doc_make_templates`, off); once released both work for every profile; never guests or unknown voices, never over MCP. Flow: the Weiche group "erstellen" offers `document_plan`, `document_change`, `document_status` (and `document_send` for sending). The model only proposes an outline (kind, format, style, title, at most 30 parts, facts from this conversation, up to 3 own documents as sources); in the chat it shows as a card to change (title, parts, format, look). Only a plain "yes" in the same conversation or "Looks good, create" starts a background job (the outline waits 30 minutes). One model call per part with a fixed JSON schema and no tools; facts and document texts are wrapped as data (`<<<DATEN … DATEN>>>`), never as a command. Speech priority applies (the job waits while someone talks); one job runs, at most 3 wait, 10 per profile and day, 20 minutes per job; the emergency stop halts new jobs and sending. The Spark builds the file itself (`docrender.py`: python-docx, python-pptx, XlsxWriter, fpdf2, pinned with hashes in `requirements-panel.lock`): no macros, no links to the outside, in spreadsheets only `SUM` formulas, CSV cells never start with `= + - @`, charts as bar, line or pie. Every content is checked before building (lengths, characters, parts, cells, pictures only from own documents). Result: under *Me → Documents → Made by the Spark* with preview, download in every fitting format, up to 20 versions ("Bring back"), parts "Edit" (by hand, no model), "Rewrite" with a wish, "Leave out", "Have a part added", switch format and look for good; also listed as a document with source "spark" (searched only with its own switch). By a sentence: "slide 4 shorter", "make a PDF of it", "send it to my PC" (understood without a document word for two hours after the last job). When the Spark asks back ("What should it be about?"), the answer stays with the file wish for 15 minutes in the same conversation (Weiche group "erstellen"). Sending only to own targets: PC (Spark companion, inbox), reMarkable (PDF), Telegram, mail draft with attachment (never sent). Sender for letters under *Me → Documents → Sender for letters*. Own templates (.docx/.dotx/.pptx/.potx, at most 10 MB, 10 of them) are checked on upload: no macros, ActiveX, embedded objects or external links, no zip trick. Storage: `users/<p>/erstellt/` (at most 100 documents). End of a job: push "document ready". Status → Live shows the target "Dokumente erstellen" with counts (running, waiting, done today), never contents. Logs: `docmake: Auftrag praesentation pptx 6 Teile (neu)`, `docmake: fertig … Version 1 in 84 s`. API (profile): `GET /api/make`, `POST|DELETE /api/make/plan/{id}`, `GET /api/make/job/{id}`, `GET /api/make/doc/{id}[/file]`, `POST /api/make/doc/{id}/edit|change|restore|send`, `/api/make/templates`. When the function is off, the model gets a fixed sentence on where to switch it on instead of the tools and does not write the content as text; Me → Documents then shows "Made by the Spark" with a "Switch on" button. Once done, the card in the chat shows a small preview of the first slide, page or sheet (`peek` in `GET /api/make`), plus download, view and send. Where the browser has no login (the Android app hands downloads to the phone's browser, another device) there is a download link: `POST /api/make/doc/{id}/link` returns `/api/make/dl?t=…`, valid 10 minutes and at most 5 downloads, only for this file and version; the Spark keeps only the key's hash; at most 5 open links per profile; when the function is off no link works. "Copy link" in the preview. By mail two ways, both only with a mailbox that has "Allow reply drafts" on (E-mail → Settings → Inbox tidying), and never sent: "As mail draft" (to the address entered, empty: to the mailbox's own) and "File into the mailbox" (a mail from you to you with the file, unread in the folder "Spark/Dokumente", created when needed). By a sentence both go only to yourself (`document_send` with `mail` or `box`, no recipient). "Finished documents also" under Me → Documents → Made by the Spark (`doc_deliver`: PC, reMarkable, Telegram, mail draft or mailbox; only a target that is set up counts) delivers every finished document right after your yes; the push says where. iPhone app: Me → Made by the Spark (when the function is on) with preview, Apple's viewer for Word, PowerPoint, Excel and PDF and "Share" (save to Files, send on). Looks without an own template: presentations with a title slide, an overview slide (from 4 parts), numbered boxes, key-number cards (`stats`, up to 4), a quote box (`quote`), styled tables and charts; Word and PDF with a colored title block, numbered headings with a line, cards, quote box, zebra tables and page numbers; Excel with a title band without gridlines, a colored head row, zebra rows, a set-off total row and a chart with values. Looks also include "Frisch" (fresh) and "Warm"; "Spark dark" prints light in Word, PDF and Excel. With an own template its look applies.

**Professional presentations:** the language model picks one of ten types per slide and fills only its fields: section divider, key statement (one big sentence), icon grid (3 to 6 points with a symbol), big numbers, timeline or process, comparison in two columns, picture and text, chart with a message, conclusion and bullet points. Fixed rules (`folien.py`) keep the variety: never the same type twice in a row, at most a third lists. A slide has at most 4 points of at most 8 words; the heading is a statement. When a part is too long the Spark asks the model once more, then shortens at a natural break and puts the full text into the speaker notes. Charts only with at least 3 different values that all have a source; the source stands below and the key value is highlighted, otherwise they become big numbers. The symbols (161 from Lucide, ISC license) are part of the repository. With the admin switch *Pictures from the Kiwix* (off; then for every profile) the Spark fetches a picture for the title slide and "picture and text" slides from the Wikipedia in the own Kiwix, checks it and saves it anew (at most 3 MB), with source and license note on the slide. Before writing the Spark looks the title up in your Kiwix and your documents; every number in the text must be there, in your words or in the facts of the conversation, otherwise it is left out (small counts up to 10 without a unit excepted). The ready note says how much was left out. Word and PDF show icons, timeline and comparison as blocks of their own, Markdown and own templates as bullet points. Everything works without the internet.

**Picture recognition (off by default):** a profile attaches a photo in the assistant (picture button next to the text field, paste or drop; up to three per question) and asks about it, typed or spoken. The language model looks at it itself (if it cannot see, the LLM-Tor takes the eye model by a fixed rule). Switches: the admin under Features → Picture recognition, where "Check picture recognition" sends a test picture to see whether the running lane accepts pictures; once released it works for every profile, for the iPhone app also "Let the model look at photos from the app", for Telegram "Photos over Telegram" (caption = question, reply to the photo = follow-up). Never guests, speakers or other device keys. The browser makes the picture smaller; the Spark reads it again (Pillow, at most 8 MB and 40 megapixels, JPEG/PNG/WebP only, with a time limit), turns it upright, scales it to 1280 pixels and writes it again as JPEG, so location (GPS) and camera data are gone. It is kept in memory only, bound to profile and device, 10 minutes after the last question (30 minutes at most, 3 per profile, 30 per hour), never on disk, in the backup or the log, unless the person keeps it with "Picture to My documents" (see knowledge from uploads). In an answer with a picture the model gets no tools; the answer counts as outside text, the next turn is locked like after a web page, and nothing is learned from it. Below the answer, "Store under My documents" keeps the answer text. API: `POST /api/chat/image` (raw picture) → `{"id"}`, then `POST /api/chat` with `"images": [id]`; `GET /api/chat/image` says whether the caller may.

**Painting pictures (off by default):** the Spark paints pictures itself with FLUX.2 [klein], without the internet. The model only comes with the installation: `sudo ./install.sh --bild 4b-q8` (4B with Q8, Apache 2.0, recommended), `4b-q4` (less memory), `9b-q8` or `9b-q4` (FLUX Non-Commercial, private use only); the first install asks, the default is none. The engine is stable-diffusion.cpp (`sd-server`, pinned to one commit, built for the GB10 with CUDA, no Docker); the model files are pinned by revision and SHA-256 (`bildmodell.py`). The service `speech-spark-bild` (`bild_server.py`, only `127.0.0.1:31005`, API key) loads the engine at the first picture (`127.0.0.1:31015`, after checking the free memory) and stops it after *Unload after minutes without a picture* (default 10). Switch: admin under Features → Painting pictures (`chat.bilder`); once released it applies to every profile, never to guests, never over MCP. A profile paints under *Me → Pictures* (wish, format square, landscape, portrait or wide) or in the conversation ("Paint me a cat on a skateboard", also with the verb at the end, as in German "Kannst du mir ein Bild von … erstellen?", also caricature, comic and cartoon: "Create a funny caricature for me"): the router group "bild" offers `bild_malen`, the model writes an English prompt that is shown under the picture. The tool counts as a change (locked after outside text, emergency stop stage 2 holds it). The job runs in the background and waits for a pause in the conversation (speech goes first); one picture at a time and never beside a song, at most 10 pictures and songs waiting together (see Heavy jobs), 12 minutes per job. Fixed rules that cannot be switched off: a word list (nothing sexual, no violence, no hate symbols), no people the Spark knows (profile names and contacts), then an image check (ViT classifier, ONNX on the CPU; above 50 % the picture is discarded, without the check nothing is shown); every picture is marked as AI-made (PNG text "KI-erzeugt", IPTC `trainedAlgorithmicMedia`) and re-encoded. Storage: `users/<p>/bilder/` with `index.json`, *Pictures per profile and day* (default 30, rolling 24 hours), *Space per profile* (default 500 MB), at most 300 pictures; *Longest side* 512 to 1408 pixels (default 1024). In the chat a card shows the progress, then the picture with Download and "All pictures"; the answer keeps "Picture: <prompt>" and the job, so the chat shows the picture again after a reload, in every saved conversation and on every device. The card only asks once it stands in the answer. When the assistant asks back after a picture wish (say "I do not paint real people, fictional ones instead?"), a short "yes" or "fine" is enough: up to 10 minutes after the own wish painting stays offered, without a web search. The iPhone app shows the picture right under the answer (touch and hold: save or share); for that its key may only read the job and the picture file of its own profile; list, painting and deleting under My things → Pictures (with the release of Painting pictures). *Me → Pictures* shows them as a gallery with wish, prompt, time, size, duration, download and delete, "Delete all pictures" on top. Finished through the iPhone app or Telegram: push "Bild". "Bild testen" below the switch paints a test picture and shows load time, paint time and memory. State → Live: target "Painting pictures" with counts (running, waiting, done, failed and refused today), never wishes or prompts; service guard part "Painting pictures"; logs (area "Bild"): `bilder: auftrag 1024x1024 quadrat chat fuer …`, `bilder: gemalt …`, `bilder: nicht gemalt <reason> fuer …`, `bild: engine gestartet 38 s 11.2 gib`, `bild: engine beendet leerlauf`. API (profile): `GET|POST|DELETE /api/bilder`, `GET /api/bilder/job/{id}`, `GET /api/bilder/{id}/datei[?dl=1]`, `DELETE /api/bilder/{id}`; admin: `GET|POST /api/admin/bild-test`.

**Changing pictures (off by default):** the Spark changes an own photo or a painted picture after the person's sentence, with the same FLUX.2 [klein] (reference picture to `bild_server.py`, at most 2 MP), without the internet and without a model of its own. Switch: admin under Features → Changing pictures (`chat.bilder_aendern`, below Painting pictures); once released it applies to every profile, never to guests, never over MCP, only in the own browser or the own app. Which picture is decided by the panel with a fixed rule before the answer (`bilder.from_turn`), never by the language model, and there is no tool for it (a turn with a photo has no tools anyway): (1) the first photo of this message when the text has a change word ("make … blue", "change", "remove … from the picture", "take the beard off" (German „mach den Bart weg“), "as a watercolour") and is no question about the picture ("What is in the picture?" stays with Recognising pictures); (2) without a photo: a short message (at most 200 characters) with a change or again word up to 10 minutes after a change takes its result ("brighter", "again"); (3) under *Me → Pictures* "Change" on a picture with an own sentence, plus "Upload an own photo" (JPEG, PNG, WebP, at most 8 MB, cleaned, without metadata, kept as PNG at most 2048 pixels). Pictures from mail, web, documents, other people's chats or Telegram groups never. Fixed rules: the word and name list from painting, plus for changes ID cards, signatures, stamps, number plates, bank notes, undressing, injuries and swapping faces; then the image check (fail closed). Every change is a new picture, the original stays ("Show the original"); the result is marked (PNG text "KI-bearbeitet", IPTC `compositeWithTrainedAlgorithmicMedia`). Day count, space and queue from painting apply; the model's answer only says that the picture is being changed, the card in the chat is called "Changing the picture". State → Live: the target "Painting pictures" also shows "changed" (count); logs (area "Bild"): `bilder: auftrag aendern 1024x768 …`, `bilder: geaendert 1024x768 21.4 s fuer …`, `bilder: nicht gestartet aendern aus|quelle fuer …`, `bilder: foto gespeichert|nicht gespeichert voll|kein bild fuer …`. Quick buttons under *Me → Pictures* on a picture, without a model, at once on the panel with Pillow: rotate left or right, mirror, brighter, darker, more contrast, black and white, crop to a square, half the size; again a new picture, a painted picture keeps its AI marks, an own photo gets none; does not count towards pictures per day; Live "schnell bearbeitet", log `bilder: schnell heller 1024x768 fuer …`. API (profile, also the app under My things → Pictures): `POST /api/bilder/{id}/aendern` (`{wunsch}`), `POST /api/bilder/{id}/schnell` (`{was}`), `POST /api/bilder/foto` (the picture as the body).

**Making music:** every released profile makes its own songs. Admin switch Features → Making music (off, needs the installed model); once released it holds for every profile, the admin blocks single ones. Guests, MCP and outside text (after mail, web or a document, Notaus from stage 2) never get the tool. In the conversation: "Make me a song about our cat", "Compose a piece for piano"; the Weiche (group `musik`, tool `musik_machen`) keeps that apart from "Play music in the living room" (Home Assistant); a short "yes, with singing" within 10 minutes after the wish still counts. "Musik stück", "House Track", "Musiktrack" and "Beat" count as wishes too. A follow-up on the last song (style, genre, "again", "can it be reggae style") gets the tool for 10 minutes after the wish, the start or the end of a song, and always right after an answer about a song; when the person only talks about the song, the model gets no tool and the rule never to promise one. If an answer promises a song anyway without `musik_machen` having run, the action check holds the sentence back (kind `musik`). After an answer with a web search (e.g. chords for the OP-XY) the person's own music wish still makes the song (`own` in `extras.offer`), and that answer stays along as data up to 7 messages back (`musik.BACK`) so key and chords are right. Mail and the Notaus lock the tool. Under Me → Music (laid out like Documents and E-mail): on top the count, space and songs today with the "⋯" menu (Delete all songs), below it the card for a new song (wish, length as chips, switch "With singing" with language and lyrics). Below that a search (wish, description, lyrics) and quick filters All, With singing, Instrumental from six songs on, then the songs by day (Being made, Today, Yesterday, Last 7 days, Older): one row per song with ▶ in front, length and singing below, the time or day on the right. A click opens the details (beside the list on a computer, as a page of their own with "‹ All songs" on a phone): player with a slider, made, length, size, time and model, Download, "Make it again" (only fills the form with wish, length, singing and lyrics; nothing is sent before "Make the song") and Delete; the description by the model and the lyrics folded. One player for the rows and the details, so two songs never play at once; while a song plays whose details are not open, a bar at the bottom shows its title and time. Fixed rules (`musik.refuse_text`): without singing instrumental, without a length 2 minutes (10 s up to `music.max_s`, default 300 s); no word list (sexual, violence, hate), no "in the style of" an artist, no passwords, PINs or account numbers; names are fine (unlike pictures). Limits per profile: `music.day_limit` songs a day (10), `music.store_mb` MB of space (500), at most 200 songs; one song at a time per profile, at most 10 pictures and songs waiting together on the Spark (see Heavy jobs); a song waits for a pause in the conversation (`vorrang.py`) and may take 20 minutes. Picking the model: Features → Making music → Model ("As installed", 2B, 2B small, XL); models not installed are greyed out. Measured on tars: 2B 16.6 s for 30 s with a 13.4 GB peak, XL 17.6 s with 19.2 GB; another model ends the running engine first. Storage `users/<id>/musik/` (MP3 with the AI mark and `index.json`), Delete data of the release counts it. The panel's chat shows a song card with a player, the apps find the song under Me → Music and get a notice when it is done. A song waiting or being made shows in State → Live under "In the background" and on the target "Making music" (numbers only); the music service shows on the model computer's card (Status → Summary and the control centre). Logs with numbers and fixed words only: `musik: auftrag 120 s instrumental chat fuer …`, `musik: gemacht 120 s gesang 9.5 s fuer …`, `musik: nicht gestartet kuenstler fuer …`, never the wish or the lyrics. API: `GET|POST|DELETE /api/musik`, `GET /api/musik/job/{id}`, `GET /api/musik/{id}/datei`, `DELETE /api/musik/{id}`.

**Editing music (off by default):** The Spark edits a song under *Me → Music* without the internet on a page of its own over the whole width ("Edit" in the details opens it): on top ▶ with the time, below the wave with the range, the tools on the left, their fields in the middle, the versions on the right with ▶ (a tap edits that one), at the bottom a fixed bar with the button and the job state; on a phone one column, "‹ Songs" goes back; the iPhone app has the same order. The model is ACE-Step (acestep.cpp: `repaint`, `cover`, `cover-nofsq`; splitting into stems is not available yet). Admin switch Features → Editing music (`chat.musik_bearbeiten`, below Making music); holds for every profile, never for guests, never over MCP. Kinds (`musik.ARTS`, `musik.plan_edit` checks numbers with fixed limits): **Part anew** (range and a sentence), **Extend** (front or end, 5 to 120 s), **Genre** (Pop, Rock, Reggae, Jazz, Classical, Electronic, Hip-hop, Schlager, Big band, Acoustic or own words, slider "free ↔ close to the original" in five steps, BPM), **Remix**, **Change lyrics** (parts verse, chorus, pre-chorus, bridge, intro, outro, instrumental: change, add, remove, move by the handle ⠿, ↑/↓ or in the iPhone app by holding and dragging; remade close to the old sound or with "Make it completely anew" in its own length); without a model at once **Cut & fade**, **Tempo & key** (75 to 125 %, ±4 half tones; ffmpeg on the model computer, fixed filters only, one run at a time, 120 s limit) and **Detect tempo and key** (`/understand`). With the model up to three suggestions per run; outside the range the original sound stays sample for sample. Every change is a **version** at the original (`root`, `von`, `label`), at most 10 per song; the list shows each song once with "· N versions", the details show the versions with ▶, "As a song of its own", Download and Delete; quick filters Uploaded and Edited. AI versions carry "AI-edited". **Upload an own file** ("⋯" menu): MP3, WAV, FLAC, M4A, OGG up to 30 MB and 10 minutes, 10 a day, only with the tick "This is my own recording or I own the rights to it" (`rechte=1`); the panel checks the first bytes, the model computer reads via ffmpeg from a pipe (only `file,pipe`) and returns its own MP3 without foreign tags and without the AI mark until edited. In the conversation the tool `musik_bearbeiten` (Weiche group `musik`, e.g. "Make my last song 30 seconds longer"): the panel picks the song (always the newest own one), never the model; new lyrics only on the page. Songs per day, space, queue and Notaus of Making music apply. State → Live: target "Making music" also edited, uploaded, detected (counts). Logs (area "Musik"): `musik: bearbeitung richtung 2 vorschlaege seite fuer …`, `musik: bearbeitet richtung 2 fassungen 31.0 s fuer …`, `musik: bearbeitet tempo ohne modell fuer …`, `musik: hochgeladen mp3 182 s fuer …`, `musik: hochladen nicht gemacht rechte|gross|format|dienst fuer …`, `musik: erkannt 3 felder fuer …`, `musik: fassung eigenes lied fuer …`; never the wish, text or file name. API (profile, also the app): `POST /api/musik/hochladen?name=&rechte=1` (file as the body), `POST /api/musik/{id}/bearbeiten` (`{art, …}`), `POST /api/musik/{id}/erkennen`, `POST /api/musik/{id}/eigenes`; model computer `POST /v1/musik/quelle|bearbeiten|ton|erkennen`.

**Writing lyrics for songs (part of Making music):** Under *Me → Music* with "With singing", the button **Write lyrics** stands next to "Lyrics"; with empty lyrics "Make the song" starts it as well. The language model writes verses and chorus from the description with `[verse]`/`[chorus]`/`[bridge]` in the chosen language, fitting the length (`musik.text_pairs`: about one verse-chorus pair per 45 s, 1 to 4). The lyrics appear for editing; the song is made only with the next click. The description goes to the language model as data in the block `<<<WUNSCH … WUNSCH>>>`. **Artist names** never reach the music model: the language model names found artists and describes their style in style words; the panel cuts "im Stil von …" with the names out by a fixed rule (`musik.CLAUSE`), adds "Stil: …" and refuses if names stay in the lyrics or description. `musik.ARTIST` also catches "im stiel …" with and without "von", not "so wie"; the rule holds for wish and description, not for own lyrics. In the conversation (`musik_machen`): when `beschreibung_en` names an artist, the result demands in fixed words a description with style and sound words only (`musik.ARTIST_AGAIN`), and "im Stil von …" is dropped before the song title. The panel checks word list and secrets before the language model and on the lyrics. Works with the switch Making music; Notaus (jobs) and the language model's maintenance pause hold it off. Also in the iPhone app. State → Live: target "Making music" also "N lyrics written". Logs (area "Musik"): `musik: liedtext geschrieben 2 teile stil statt namen fuer …`, `musik: liedtext nicht geschrieben aus|notaus|wartung|leer|wortliste|geheim|kuenstler|sprachmodell <error kind> fuer …`; never the description or lyrics. API (profile, also the app): `POST /api/musik/liedtext` (`{wunsch, sekunden, sprache}` → `{liedtext, wunsch, teile, namen, beschreibung}`, 16 KB, 10 per minute).

**Long description as a template:** The own description under *Me → Music* (and in the iPhone app) may be up to **2000 characters** (`musik.MAX_WISH`, as much as `docmake.MAX_WISH`), e.g. a firm's text as a template. The music model reads at most 600 characters (`musik.MAX_CAPTION`); so "Write lyrics" also returns a short English music description (`beschreibung`: style, mood, instruments, tempo, voice, no names; dropped with "in the style of"), which page and app send with "Make the song" as long as the description was not changed by hand; otherwise the first 600 characters go to the music model. The panel checks it like the wish (word list, secrets, artists). The 16 KB body limit and rate limits apply. API: `POST /api/musik` also takes `beschreibung` (at most 600 characters).

**AI Dungeon Master:** admin switch *Features → My data → AI Dungeon Master* (`chat.dungeon`, off by default; released for every profile not blocked; never for the assistant's guests). A role-playing game for up to five players in the menu **Dungeon Master** with the tabs *Rounds* and *Heroes*. A new round has title, world (fantasy, horror, science fiction, pirates, own), opening, tone (from 8, 12 or 16 with its own word list), turn time (1, 2 or 5 minutes), duration (6 hours, 1 day, 1 week) and a hero (name, people, class); the Dungeon Master prompt can be changed, the panel's fixed rules always apply. **Invite** makes a seat link with a QR code per player (`https://<panel>/#spiel=<code>`), played as a guest without a profile or with an own profile. **Flow:** everyone writes what their character does (at most 300 characters); once all have acted, the time is up or the host taps "Go on now", the own language model answers in two steps in a fixed JSON schema: first the checks needed, then narration with consequences, loot, successes, NPC lines, scene and whom it asks. **The panel decides** (`dungeon.py`, rules and catalogue in `dungeon_regeln.json`, checked by `check_rules`): d20 plus value against difficulty 5 to 20 (20 always succeeds, 1 always fails), damage and healing 1 to 6 (minus armour), only group names and kinds from fixed lists, words of the tone and the list "nie" refused; a player silent for two turns is addressed by name. A broken answer gets a second try, then a fixed sentence stands and the turn stays open. **Loot and levels:** the model only reports the occasion (foe, chest, reward, boss) with a name and a sentence; rarity (d100 by occasion and group level, common to legendary, epic from level 2, legendary from level 5, pity after 6 finds), item fitting the class, bonus, effect, receiver (who went longest without a find) and gold come from the panel. Experience by fixed numbers (check passed, foes by rank, scene goal for all), levels 1 to 10, at most one per game evening, credited at the end of the round or chapter; every level brings life and a value point, levels 2, 4, 6, 8, 10 an ability out of two offers. On the sheet: equip, unequip, drink a potion, give. **Trading:** "Offer a trade" with things and gold per side (up to 4 per side, at most 3 open offers per player); on "Accept" the panel checks ownership and bag room (12) and swaps in one step; "Decline" or "Withdraw" takes it back, a traded thing drops out of all other offers. **Taking heroes along:** heroes of profiles stay stored with level, values, equipment and gold (`users/<profile>/dungeon-helden.json`, up to 8), each in only one running round. A guest taps "Get a hero code": shown once, valid 30 days, redeemable once, a newer one replaces the older; with it they play on in a new round or take the hero into a profile. **Admin:** *Features → AI Dungeon Master*, button "Adjust rules": worlds, tones, peoples, classes with abilities, items, effects, rarities, curves for experience and loot, limits and the default prompt as JSON (`dungeon-regeln.json` in the state folder); same id replaces, `"aus": true` removes, new ids are added; the panel refuses faulty rule sets with a list of errors and uses the shipped rules when the file is broken. **Protection:** the code stands after the `#`, the Spark keeps only its hash; other Origins are refused, own rate limit, wrong codes lock like a wrong PIN; actions and narration are data (block `<<< … >>>`, markers defused), unlock nothing and never go into memory; a guest only sees their round, never the assistant, a tool or a setting. Status → Live (target "Dungeon Master") only shows rounds, players, guests, turns, loot, levels, fallbacks and refusals; Logs (area "Dungeon Master") only fixed words and numbers (`dungeon: runde neu | fantasy | ab 12 | Profil …`, `dungeon: zug | ok | 2 proben | 3100 ms`, `dungeon: beute | boss | rar`, `dungeon: stufe | 4`, `dungeon: tausch | angenommen`). *Delete all game data* under Heroes also works while the game is blocked. API: `GET /api/profile/dungeon`, `POST /api/profile/dungeon/runde`, `GET /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}`, `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/aktion|held|weiter|pause|platz`, `DELETE /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}`, `POST /api/profile/dungeon/beitreten`, `DELETE /api/profile/dungeon/held/{hid}`, `DELETE /api/profile/dungeon/daten`; guests `POST /api/dungeon/platz|beitreten|lage|aktion|held|figur` (`{code, …}`); admin `GET|PUT|DELETE /api/admin/dungeon`.

**AI Dungeon Master: rule depth and six switches:** One table (`dungeon_tiefe.py`) says what each part of the game hangs on; no module asks `features` with a part, only `dungeon_tiefe.an`, `fuer`, `admin_an` and `fehlt`. **Core** (story progress, lines and X-card, asking back, narrator faults, guide the players, NPC memory, people catalog, writings, quest items, time of day) is on with the game. **Full** (crit tables, honours, near miss, helping, rest, travel, tongues, calendar, witnesses, thoughts, coming along, errands, wandering, gestures, surroundings, companions, heart's wish, alarm and torches, whispering, stance, scars, heroic courage, factions, gold with a purpose, runes and sockets, game master dial) works only in a round at the rule depth "Full"; the runes' effect on items holds Spark-wide, per round only falling, setting, trader and smith. The admin sets the standard for new rounds under *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth* (Simple or Full, `chat.dungeon_tiefe`, Simple from the start); the host chooses per round when making it and in the game master console under "Difficulty" (`erzaehler` with `tiefe`), at Full there also "Scars only" (`nur_narben`: every wound stays a look, never a consequence). When the host keeps the standard when making a round, the round follows the admin's standard, also when it changes later. **Game master console** (giving, budget stages, own random rules, oracle, real dice) has no switch: the tools only act when the host uses them. Six parts have a switch of their own under *Features → AI Dungeon Master*, off from the start: *Pictures and hero song* (pictures, faces, arms, album, hero song), *Second check by the model* (judge, scribe, heroes comment, `chat.dungeon_pruefung`), *Guests with e-mail*, *Chronicle and hero book* (`chat.dungeon_buecher`), *Own stories and import*, *Test benches* (fight and narrator bench). **Take-over:** at the start the panel reads old single switches once (`dungeon_tiefe.uebernehmen`): when at least half of the Full parts were on, the standard becomes Full; a new switch is on when one of its old parts was on, never the pictures by themselves; then the old keys are gone from the config. Log `dungeon: regeltiefe | uebernommen | voll | 12`, at the table `dungeon: regeltiefe | einfach` and `dungeon: narbe | nur aussehen an`. Live: open rounds per rule depth and with "Scars only", numbers only. Self-tests in `test_dungeon_tiefe` (table, no module asks `features` with a part, Simple and Full, take-over, endpoint, live).

**AI Dungeon Master stage 2:** **Campaigns:** the host's "End chapter" ends the round; narrations, the profiles' heroes and copies of the guests' heroes go into a campaign (`users/<profile>/dungeon-kampagnen.json`, up to 10). In the background the language model writes "The story so far" (fixed JSON schema, at most 800 characters, sources only as game text in the block `<<< … >>>`); the panel checks it like every narration (no markup, no links, no words of the tone and the list "nie"). If the answer does not fit or the model is paused, the last narrations stand as a *draft*. In the tab *Rounds*, section *Campaigns*, the host can change the text (same check), have it rewritten, delete it or "Start chapter N": a new round with the same settings and their hero, "The story so far" goes to the model as game text. Guests get new seat links and pick "Your character from the last chapter"; each character only once. **Reading aloud:** a switch when starting and the host's "🔊 Read aloud/Reading off". After each turn speech output speaks the narration in the narrator voice and NPC lines in the NPC voice (the admin picks both under "Adjust rules", `stimmen`); one reading job at a time, at most 1800 characters per turn. The sound is a WAV in the state folder (`dungeon-ton/`, last 3 turns per round, goes with the round), one file for all seats: it plays by itself on the host's device, the others tap "🔊 Listen". **Speak instead of typing:** 🎤 records up to 30 seconds, the browser makes a 16 kHz WAV, the Spark's speech recognition writes the text into the field; sending stays a tap. Guests too (a transcript is not a tool), with their own rate limit `dungeonohr` and at most 1 MB per recording. **Apps:** iPhone *My things → Dungeon Master* shows rounds, heroes and campaigns with "The story so far", swiping deletes a hero or campaign; "Play" gets a code for the own seat (shown once, only the hash stays, a newer one replaces it, no hero code for a profile's hero) and opens the table with microphone and reading aloud. New rounds and chapters only in the browser. Android shows the panel page. Status → Live counts campaigns, chapters ended, turns read aloud and spoken; Logs: `dungeon: kapitel ende | 2`, `dungeon: bisher | ok | 4200 ms`, `dungeon: vorlesen | ok | 3 teile | 9000 ms`, `dungeon: hoeren | ok | 48 zeichen | 900 ms`, `dungeon: sitzcode | app`. API: `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/kapitel|vorlesen|hoeren|sitzcode`, `GET /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/ton/{n}`, `PUT|DELETE /api/profile/dungeon/kampagne/{kid}`, `POST /api/profile/dungeon/kampagne/{kid}/neu|weiter`; guests `POST /api/dungeon/ton|hoeren`. **Variety:** the catalogue has 44 weapons (up to 11 per class), 18 armours, 17 pieces of jewellery, 18 potions, 22 spells, 12 other things and 28 effects. Potions heal and/or give a **blessing** (a value +1 to +4 for 1 to 5 turns). **Spells** (kind `zauber`, some only for certain classes) are cast with "Cast" from the bag: once, on oneself or the group, with healing and/or a blessing; the log shows "✦ … casts …", the model only learns who cast what.
