A blessing counts in every check and wears off after narrated turns, at most 4 at once; the sheet shows "Strength +2 · 2 turns left". The admin adds own potions and spells under "Adjust rules" (`heilung` 0 to 30, `segen` {wert, bonus, zuege}, for spells `ziel` selbst or gruppe and `satz`); the panel checks them.

**AI Dungeon Master at the table, wave A:** **Multiattack:** a foe can have `angriffe` (2 to `ANGRIFFE_MAX` = 5, checked by `check_rules`); a boss always attacks at least twice (`dungeon_kampf.blows`). The panel rolls every attack on its own against a standing hero; the foe window shows "2 × 1d8+2", the rule page "Attacks per turn"; encounter strength (`BUDGET`) keeps the test bench at 70 to 90 % wins. **A foe's life as a word:** players see the state instead of numbers (`dungeon_kampf.lage`): unhurt, bruised (above half), badly hurt (above a quarter), nearly beaten. Only the host sees the numbers, also on the encounter card. In the car: "The ogre is badly hurt". A state on a hero shows the turns left ("poisoned, 2 turns left"). **Names for listening:** spoken actions go to speech recognition with up to `HOER_WORTE` (60) names of the round (`dungeon.heard_words`): heroes, people and places of the map, foes, things in one's own bag, never anything from the hidden truth. While a profile plays in the car, these names apply there too (`dungeon_auto.words`, `WORTE_S` 600 s). **Dice receipt:** every roll gets a number (W-1, W-2 …, `dungeon_erzaehler.receipts`), small in the feed and with all numbers in the model. If the narration names a number without a receipt of this turn, the panel repairs it by fixed rules (fault `beleg`, hint "The dice receipts hold: …"). **Read along:** while reading aloud, every device marks the sentence being spoken; the reading state brings the words of every finished piece (`lesung.texte`), found also across highlights and quote marks. **Live:** Status → Live counts foes with multiattack, per day narrations repaired for numbers without a receipt and recordings with the round's names. Self-tests in `test_dungeon`: the boss test with multiattack, `test_a_foes_life_is_a_word_for_the_players`, `test_dice_receipts_hold_the_numbers`, the speaking test with names, `test_reading_aloud_piece_by_piece_on_this_device`; `test_angriffe_zwei_bis_fuenf` in `test_dungeon_srd`.

**AI Dungeon Master: oracle (tables from Datasworn, CC-BY-4.0):** A tool of the game master console, no switch of its own (*Features → AI Dungeon Master*; `dungeon_orakel.py`). The host (e.g. in solo play) asks in the game master console under "🔮 Oracle" (`POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/orakel`, host only): a yes-or-no question with a likelihood (certain 90, likely 70, even 50, unlikely 30, hardly 10 %) or up to `TABELLEN_MAX` (3) tables of the oracle group (action, theme, place, descriptor, twist, complication, clue, peril, opportunity, trouble) or own tables. The round's chaos grade (1 to 9, start 5, slider) moves the chance by `CHAOS_SCHRITT` (5) percentage points per step, within 5 to 95. The panel rolls a d100: lowest fifth of the yes "Yes, and …", highest fifth of the no "No, and …"; a double (11, 22 …) up to the chaos grade brings an event from action and theme. At most `ZUG_MAX` (6) questions a turn. Every answer stands in the feed with roll and chance; the model gets them only as data (`orakel`) with the fixed rule to hold to them. **Own tables:** tab "Rules", part "Tables": "Own copy" and "Edit" add a table (group `orakel` or `eigen`, 2 to 300 rows `[German, English]`, optionally only for single worlds), `dungeon_tabellen.check_rules` checks it. **Live:** Status → Live counts questions per day and rounds with an own chaos grade. Self-test `test_the_oracle_answers_by_the_panels_dice` in `test_dungeon`.

**AI Dungeon Master: narrator bench:** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Test benches* (`chat.dungeon_pruefstand`, off from the start; `dungeon_erzaehlstand.py`); checks the running model in the panel, fixed rules decide. In the tab "Rules" at "Narrator bench", "Check now" (`POST /api/admin/dungeon/erzaehlstand`, admin only, at most every `KNOPF_S` (120) seconds, one run at a time) plays eight fixed scenes through the same steps as a turn at the table (`dungeon.ask_checks`, `roll_facts`, `dungeon.tell`; one source) on a round only in memory: nothing saved, no seat, no profile, no day counter. Scenes: a failed check must not succeed, two heroes appear, a fight names the foes by name and no number without a receipt, the children's tone without a word of its list, a person of the truth answers by name, an English round in English (at most `ENGLISCH_MAX` German words), a risky action asks for a check, a harmless one does not. Per scene 2 points on the first try, 1 after a rewrite, 0 when refused or only the panel's repair is left. With "Classification with probabilities" on, the model's own view stands beside it (`systemone`, use `erzaehler`), without points. Result per model name (`llm.known_model`) in `STATE/dungeon-erzaehlstand.json`, the last `MODELLE_MAX` (8) models side by side, only points, reasons and times, never narration text. When changing models: measure, switch, "Check now". **Live:** Status → Live names the models measured and the best score. Self-tests in `test_dungeon_erzaehlstand`.

**AI Dungeon Master: narrator errors from a 👎 with a reason, taken over as bench scenes:** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Test benches* (`chat.dungeon_pruefstand`, off from the start). While on, the table asks after a 👎 on one of the last three turns "What was wrong?" with fixed reasons (`dungeon_erzaehlstand.GRUENDE`: failed check done anyway or something revealed, decided for another character, item or spell missing, fight does not match, gold or loot only told, wrong place, a person looks different or is elsewhere, repeats itself, something else; never free text). The reason goes with the 👎 (`POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/daumen` or `/api/dungeon/daumen` with `grund`); the panel keeps the turn as a case (`dungeon_erzaehlstand.case`): actions, dice with outcome and narration, heroes with bench names (Arvid, Bela, Cora, Dario, Ella, also in the genitive), without profile, seat or round id, only a hash of round and turn (a second 👎 replaces the case). The newest `FAELLE_MAX` (40) stand in `STATE/dungeon-erzaehlfehler.json`. In the tab "Rules" at "Narrator errors" the admin sees every case (`GET /api/admin/dungeon/erzaehlfehler`), deletes it or uses "Take over as a bench scene" (`POST` with `{fall, aktion: szene|loeschen}` or `{szene, aktion: loeschen}`, at most `EIGENE_MAX` (12) scenes, deleting also with the switch off). Every bench run plays these scenes after the fixed ones: the same actions on a fresh round of the same world, tone and language, every check with the same outcome (`same_dice`: 20 passed, 2 failed, if need be the difficulty one above the total); map, fight and people are not carried. Points as for fixed scenes. The result file stays without narration text. **Live:** Status → Live names cases and scenes taken over. Log lines "dungeon: erzaehlstand | fall | neu/ersetzt/leer/aus/uebernommen/geloescht/fehlt/voll/ungueltig". Self-tests `NarratorCases` in `test_dungeon`.

**AI Dungeon Master: fix narrator errors, the narration learns from reports:** On with the game, no switch of its own (*Features → AI Dungeon Master*, counts for every profile not blocked; `dungeon_erzaehlstand.FIX`). With it (or the narrator bench) the table offers the fixed reasons after a 👎, including "Action misunderstood (question, direction)"; every reason has a fixed hint (`GRUENDE`, German and English). **1 Tell again:** a 👎 with a reason on the last narration has it told again once (`dungeon_erzaehler.retell_fixed`, at most once per turn, not while a turn, an X-card or another retelling runs; a stuck one frees after `NEU_S` (300) seconds). The model gets the hint as its task, the old scene, actions and each check's outcome only as data; the new narration must pass word lists, veils and the same fixed checks (`erz.judge`, at most after the panel's rescue), else the old one stays. Dice, life, finds and place never change; pictures and sound files go only with a new text (`dungeon_grenzen.replace`, the same source as the X-card). The table shows "being told again …", then "told again". **2 A world's hints:** every report counts with time, world and reason (numbers only, `meldungen`, at most `MELDUNGEN_MAX`); when a reason comes `WELT_SCHWELLE` (3) times within `WELT_TAGE` (30) days in a world, that world's narrator gets its fixed hint in its task for good (`world_task`, never words of a report). **3 Every night:** while the narrator bench is on and has scenes taken over, it plays them once a night (2 to 5 o'clock, `due`). **4 Weeks:** the page "Narrator errors" shows reports per week and reason of the last `WOCHEN` (6) weeks and the reasons that became a world's hint. **5 New rules:** clusters show on the page; new fixed rules are built by a developer, never by the system itself. **Live:** Status → Live names scenes told again today and those being told again now. Log lines "dungeon: erzaehlfehler | neu erzaehlt | ok/bleibt/ausfall" and "| neu erzaehlen | aus/nein", "dungeon: erzaehlstand | nacht | start". Self-tests `NarratorCases` in `test_dungeon`.

**AI Dungeon Master: adventure import:** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Own stories and import* (`chat.dungeon_geschichten`, off from the start; `dungeon_import.py`). In the story planner (tab "Rules" → "Stories" → "New story") "📄 Adventure from a file" stands at the top: an adventure as PDF, Word, OpenDocument, Markdown, text or HTML (`TYPEN`, at most `MAX_BYTES` = 10 MB) becomes a draft (`POST /api/admin/dungeon/geschichte/import`, a form with file, world, tone, acts, taboos and language; admin only, rate limit `dungeonplan`). The panel reads the text with `documents.extract` (the file is not kept and does not go into the knowledge), cuts it at headings like the document search (`documents.heading`) and gives the model a digest: every heading with the start of its section, at most `ABSCHNITTE_MAX` (60) sections and `DIGEST_MAX` (12000) characters, as data in the block `<<< … >>>` (markers defused). First heading = title, the others = chapters; without a chosen number as many acts as chapters (3 to 5). The model joins chapters in order into acts and keeps places and foes, never game values, rules, loot or instructions from the file; only the planner's fields come back. The panel checks the answer in the fixed schema (`SCHEMA_PLAN`) like a typed story (`check_plan`: lengths, number of acts, tone, "never", taboos), two tries; only "Check and save" keeps it. Fixed refusals with a log line: `aus`, `dateiart`, `zu gross`, `unlesbar` (such as a PDF without a text layer), `leer` (less than `TEXT_MIN` characters), `eingabe`, `sprachmodell pausiert`, `kein vorschlag`. Logs only fixed words and numbers (`dungeon: import | ok | 3 kapitel | 3 akte`, never the file's name or text). **Live:** Status → Live counts adventures taken and refused today. Self-tests in `test_dungeon_import`.

**AI Dungeon Master: quest items:** On with the game, no switch of its own (*Features → AI Dungeon Master*; `dungeon_quest.py`). A quest item is a named thing the story needs (key, map, seal): sign ✦, no weight, never for sale, can be passed on. It comes three ways by fixed rules: an act of the story planner names it (field "Special thing", 2 to 40 characters, checked with tone, "never" and taboos) and it lies in the scene while the act runs (`q_akt1` …); the map lays it at a place or in a vault (`dungeon.quest_legen(rd, ort_id, ding)`, the same id twice changes nothing); or the narration reports a find (`quest_fund`, at most one a turn), which the panel takes only when the narration names it, the name has 2 to 40 characters, passes the word lists, is new and the round has fewer than `QUEST_MAX` (12) things; it goes to the finder, else it lies here. When an action names a thing where the group stands, the hero takes it (before the narration, the model hears `quest_genommen`); what the group carries (`quest_gruppe`) and what lies here (`quest_hier`) the model hears only as data. In the hero sheet, tab "Bag", "✦ Special things" lists own things with "Give …" and "Put down" and what others carry; under "Lying here" named things can be picked up (`POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/held` with `questgeben`, `questablegen`, `questaufheben`, guests `/api/dungeon/held`). Doors and ways open only when an action names the thing and the group carries it (`dungeon.quest_genannt`, `dungeon.quest_haben`); a used thing vanishes with a line in the feed (`dungeon.quest_verbrauchen`) and never returns. When a carrier dies or leaves, the thing lies where the group stands. Carried things go into the next chapter with the hero, things left lying do not. Players see a thing only once named or carried; the host sees all with their place (`quest_geheim`, host only; view filter `quest_versteckt` and `quest_anderswo`). Journal lines `dungeon: quest | …` with fixed words only; the live overview counts things, carried ones and today's taken, found, passed on and used ones.

**AI Dungeon Master: attacking several foes of one kind:** An attack happens only when a check in a fight has a target naming a foe. Fixed rules (`dungeon_kampf._named`): first the whole name, then its start, then the kind (name without number, also as a plural or only its last part: “Riesenratten”, “die Ratten”, “giant rats”); it hits the first one still standing this turn. A check without a target still attacks when the action holds a word of attacking (`HIEB_WORTE`: greifen, schlagen, stechen, schießen, attack …) and names a foe that way; “I listen at the door” stays a check. When the action names magic (`MAGIE_WORTE`) but no listed spell and the class can cast, it is a cantrip: 1d6 plus the best of Intelligence, Wisdom, Charisma, kind force, magical; otherwise a weapon attack. The model only gives data; target and damage come from the panel.

**AI Dungeon Master: tables in full:** In the tab "Rules", part "Tables", a table's detail shows every row with its number, above it the die (d and the number of rows, every row as often; in a world only its rows) and for world-bound rows those worlds, in a box of its own that scrolls. In the list every table has a sign (such as 👁️ Looks, 🔮 an oracle table without its own, ✎ an own one; `dungeon_regelseite.TAB_ZEICHEN`). Only a view for the admin (`GET /api/admin/dungeon/katalog/tabellen`, every row with `tafel`).

**AI Dungeon Master: faster on the Pi:** Every round is a file of its own (`dungeon_ablage.py`, state folder `dungeon-runden/<rid>.json`); `dungeon-runden.json` keeps only shared values (pictures of the day, thumbs per world). An old combined file is split once on the first read. A cache keeps every file's text by its change stamp (time, size, file): only changed files are read and brought up to date, every caller gets a fresh copy; only changed rounds are written, an ended round's file is removed. Running rounds are not in the backup (they go hours after their end; heroes and campaigns live in the profile and are backed up). The poll every 3 seconds (`GET /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}?stand=…`, guests `POST /api/dungeon/lage` with `stand`) gets `{"neu": false}` while the stand holds (change stamp, who is there, reading aloud, pictures on their way, who types); while the Dungeon Master answers and at the latest every `STAND_S` (10) seconds the whole view comes. It checks seat and due turns on that round only. The page loads only `dungeonlader.js`; the five game scripts (`DM_SKRIPTE`) come on the first opening of the Dungeon Master or a seat link, with the running version as `?v=`. The live overview counts the "nothing new" answers.

**AI Dungeon Master: game master console as a window and a draggable column:** The table's right column shows the quest, the own hero with its buttons, for the host “▶ Go on now” (with “waiting for …”) and “🎲 Open the game master console”, below them the tabs Map, Threads and Date. The game master console is a big window with cards in three columns: “This turn” (pause, market, behind “⋯” end chapter or round), “At the table” (read aloud, scene picture, invite with the link, last sentence in the mail) and “Narrator” (way of telling, narrator's voice, combat stage, game master dial); below them the story, random rules, oracle, truth of this chapter and the things. “Go on now” also stands on top of the console and closes it; ✕, Esc or a tap beside it close it too. On the phone everything stands one below the other. The edge between the story and the right column can be dragged or moved with the arrow keys (260 to 560 pixels, 320 from the start); the width is kept per device (`dm-breite`), a double click resets it. With only one column there is no edge. Every button sends the usual request, the panel checks it.

**AI Dungeon Master: end or delete every round:** In “Your rounds” every round has its buttons next to “Open”: the host can “End” or “Delete” a running round and “Delete” an ended one; a player hides an ended round. The first tap asks “Really end?”, “Really delete?” or “Really hide?”, only the second does it. “Delete” on a running round first ends it (experience is paid, the profiles' heroes stay) and then deletes it with its sounds and pictures (`DELETE /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}?loeschen=1`, host only; a player gets 404). A player cannot end another host's running round.

**AI Dungeon Master: a question stays a question, down stays down:** Three fixed rules. An action that only asks or talks (`dungeon_erzaehler.asks`: a word of `FRAGT`, not "frage mich", no word of `dungeon.RISIKO`, which also knows "unauffällig" and "ablenk") gets no check or at most one on `FRAGE_WERT` (Charisma), never sneaking or distracting (`check_proben`, line "dungeon: probe | verworfen | absicht"; the model's task says so too). When an action names a way down (`UNTEN`: cellar, hinab, hinunter …) and the narration only one up (`OBEN`: hinauf, oben angekommen, attic …), or the other way round, that is the fault "richtung" (`wrong_way`); the rescue strikes the sentences with the wrong direction. When the narration of a question tells neither question nor answer (no word of `ANTWORT`, no quote of at least `ZITAT_WOERTER` (3) words), that is the fault "frage" (`not_asked`); the rescue adds "… asks the question and waits for the answer.". Both with a fixed hint for the second try, day counters `regel_richtung`, `regel_frage` in the live line of narrations improved. Self-test `test_a_question_stays_a_question_and_the_way_down_stays_down`.

**AI Dungeon Master: the new place is in the narration:** When `ort_id` leads the group to a neighbouring place the narration does not name (`dungeon_karte.ort_fehlt`), that is the fault "neuer_ort" (`dungeon_erzaehler.judge`). The model writes once more with the hint "with ort_id the group goes to …, but your narration does not name the place" (name from the map, as data). If it fails again, the group stays where it is (`ort_id` empty, log `dungeon: nachbessern | fest | neuer_ort`). Live counts it with the repaired narrations.

**AI Dungeon Master: Dungeon judge:** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Second check by the model* (`chat.dungeon_pruefung`, off from the start, needs *Classification with probabilities*). When the first narration of a turn passes every fixed check, `dungeon_erzaehler.richter` asks over `systemone` (use "richter", with *Helper model* the small model first) one fixed question: does the narration contradict the actions or the outcome of the dice? Actions, checks (only succeeded or failed) and narration go along as data. The model only gives a probability; from 90 % (`RICHTER_MIN`) it writes once more with a fixed hint. If the new version fails a fixed check, the first one stays (`dungeon: richter | bleibt`). Without an answer within 8 seconds or when unsure, the turn goes on without the judge. Log `dungeon: richter | ok | n %`, `| neu | n %`, `| ausfall | …`; Live: judge today ok, written again, without answer (numbers only).

**AI Dungeon Master: pause shown everywhere:** When the host pauses a round (game master console, card "Turn", "⏸ Pause"), it shows everywhere: in the round list in the browser and the Android app "⏸ paused" instead of "running", in the iPhone app in the round's row, in CarPlay after the turn. At the table a bar "⏸ Paused" stands above the narration for every seat and guest (the clock stands still, actions can be written); the host has "▶ Go on" right there. Other seats with a profile get one note each on pausing and going on ("… is paused", "… goes on") over app, companion, watch, only on a change and only if notes are not switched off. In the car the Spark says "The round is paused right now" instead of "your turn". Live counts paused rounds. The list carries `pause` (`GET /api/profile/dungeon`), log `dungeon: pause | an` or `| aus` and `dungeon: melden | pause` or `| weiter`.

**AI Dungeon Master: critical and fumble tables:** Part of the rule depth "Full" (*Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth*, the host chooses per round). On a natural 20 in a fight the panel rolls a d12 on the critical table of the hit's kind of damage, on a natural 1 on the mild fumble table for melee, ranged or spell (`ideen.krit`, `ideen.patzer` in `dungeon_srd.json`). The line stands at the attack in the log (💥 or 🙃, starting with "Critical:" or "Fumble:") and goes to the narrator as data (`kritisch`, `patzer`); the model never picks it. A state from the line holds for its turns: after a critical on the foe, after a fumble on the hero. On a foe by fixed rules: no turn (stunned, paralysed, unconscious …), disadvantage on its attacks (blinded, poisoned, prone …), advantage for the heroes, burning or bleeding costs 1d4 life each turn. The fight bar shows its states; all end with their turns or the fight. The catalog also checks the content of the D&D ideas round 2 by fixed rules (`dungeon_ideen.check_rules`): prices of a near miss (`knapp`, d20 without gaps, mild prices with `kinder`), props (`requisiten` with places and rooms and an effect out of schaden_gruppe, umwerfen, deckung, vorteil, fluchtweg), heart's wishes (`wuensche`), companions (`gefaehrte_werte` level 1 to 10, `gefaehrten`, `gefaehrten_kinder`) and `licht` on tools (−1 always burns, like the Everburning torch, else 1 to 20 rooms). Live: critical and fumble tables rolled today. Log `dungeon: kampf | krit tabelle | w`, `| patzer tabelle | w`, `dungeon: kampf | gegner setzt aus`, `dungeon: kampf | gegner zustand`. Self-tests `Fight.test_critical_and_fumble_tables` and `Fight.test_the_round_two_tables_by_fixed_rules`.

**AI Dungeon Master: honours of the evening:** Part of the rule depth "Full" (*Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth*, the host chooses per round; `dungeon_ehre.py`). While the round runs, the panel counts per seat from its own dice: hits, critical hits, damage, 20s and 1s on attacks and checks, checks passed, Dexterity checks passed, potions for friends and life given (`rd["ehre"]`, never from the model). At the end of the round (also at a chapter's end) fixed titles go to the seat with the most: Blade of the evening (damage), Rescuer of the evening (help), Lucky one (20s), Quietest shadow (from 2 Dexterity checks), Hard-luck hero (from 3 ones); on a tie the seat taken first. At the children's table: Bravest heart, Best friend, Lucky one, Quietest fox, without hard-luck hero and ones. The log shows "🏅 Honours of the evening" with the titles and one certificate per hero with their numbers (log art `ehrung`, with a fanfare); the round list in the browser and the iPhone app show the titles at the ended round (`ehrungen` in `GET /api/profile/dungeon`). The next evening counts anew. Live: honours given today. Log `dungeon: ehrung | n titel | m urkunden` or `| keine`. Self-test `Fight.test_honours_of_the_evening`.

**AI Dungeon Master: near miss:** Part of the rule depth "Full" (*Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth*, the host chooses per round; `dungeon_knapp.py`; the idea "Pay the Price" of Ironsworn and Starforged by Shawn Tomkin, via datasworn, CC BY 4.0, own texts). When a check misses its difficulty by 1 or 2 (never on a natural 1, never on attacks), the Spark asks the seat once a turn: "Near miss! Give up, or make it after all at a rolled price?" with "Give up" and "Pay"; the others see who is deciding. No choice after 20 seconds (`WARTE_S`) counts as giving up. Who pays passes; the panel rolls the price with a d20 on `ideen.knapp` in `dungeon_srd.json` (at the children's table only rows with `kinder`), the dice receipt says "knapp" and `preis` (fixed id), the narration gets the line as data (`knapp_mit_preis`). Effects by fixed rules: noise on the map (`dungeon_karte.laerm`), disadvantage on the next turn's checks, 1d4 damage (never below 1 life), a tool breaks (the smith mends it), up to 10 gold (never more than the hero has); time and trust (`zeit`, `nsc`) stay in the story. The Spark does not ask in the car. Doors `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/knapp` `{zug, wahl}` and for guests `POST /api/dungeon/knapp` `{code, zug, wahl}` with login or guest code, profile binding (only the own seat, only the turn asked, only once, only "zahlen" or "aufgeben"), CSRF, a size limit of 256 bytes and an own rate limit `dungeonknapp`. Live: asked, with a price, given up, without an answer, refused. Log `dungeon: knapp | frage | n plaetze`, `| gezahlt | art`, `| aufgegeben`, `| ohne antwort`, `| preis | art`, `| gewaehlt | wahl`, `| abgelehnt | …`. Self-test `Fight.test_a_near_miss_gives_up_or_pays_a_price`.

**AI Dungeon Master: helping and group checks:** Part of the rule depth "Full" (*Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth*, the host chooses per round; `dungeon_helfen.py`; after "Working Together" and "Group Checks" of the SRD 5.1, CC BY 4.0). **Helping:** when an action has a word of help (`WORTE`: helfe, hilf, unterstütze, help, assist …) and another hero's name (a part of the name from 3 letters as a whole word), the panel rolls that hero's first check of the turn with advantage; disadvantage cancels it. Never for oneself, never on attacks, only who can act, one helper per hero. The roll shows "🤝 with help from …", the narrator gets `geholfen_von`. **Group check:** when two or more seats roll the same value against the same difficulty in one turn, all count as passed if at least half pass (`gruppe` on the roll, "👥 group check", for the narrator `gruppenprobe`). Both are fixed rules on the panel's rolls (`dungeon.roll`); the model only tells. No endpoint of its own. Live: helped today, group checks passed and not. Log `dungeon: helfen | vorteil` and `dungeon: helfen | gruppe | n von m | geschafft` or `| nicht`. Self-test `Fight.test_helping_and_group_checks`.

**AI Dungeon Master: writings:** On with the game, no switch of its own (*Features → AI Dungeon Master*; `dungeon_schrift.py`, own code). When the group finds something written, the narration reports it in the field `schriftstueck` (at most one a turn): title, text, kind (letter, wanted poster, verse, diary, note, inscription) and sender. The panel checks it by fixed rules (plain text, 20 to 600 characters, the round's limits, the list "never") and keeps it in the round (`rd["schrift"]`, at most 40). At the table it appears at its turn as parchment with a seal, a handwriting font and the sender and stays in the tab "Writings" of the right column. The host decides with "All · Nobody · names" who sees it (`POST …/erzaehler` `{schrift: {id, sicht}}`); a secret one shows "only for you" at its receiver. Other seats never get a secret one (sight filter, secret `schrift_geheim`, with a leak test); the narrator knows the last three in its facts but never decides who sees them. The app shows them at the game table. Live: found, dropped, for one seat only today. Log `dungeon: schrift | gefunden | art`, `| verworfen`, `| sicht | alle|niemand|ein platz`, `| sicht | abgelehnt`. Self-test `Fight.test_writings_as_parchment_for_whom_the_host_chooses`.

**AI Dungeon Master: monster album:** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Pictures and hero song* (`chat.dungeon_bilder`, off from the start; `dungeon_album.py`; the foes' sayings `sage` in `dungeon_srd.json`). When the panel's fight defeats a foe, every hero there at the fight's start and still at the table counts one win over this kind (`h["album"]`, kept with the hero, at most 400 kinds). The tab "Album" of the right column shows the entries with stars per stage: after the first win name, picture, description and saying, after the third the values (level, life, armour, attack, damage), after the fifth weaknesses, resistances, immunities and the states its blows cause. Only the panel counts, from its own dice; the model gives nothing. When loaded only catalog foes with whole numbers stay (`album.fix`). The app shows the album at the game table. Live: new entries and stages reached today. Log `dungeon: album | stufe N`. Self-test `Fight.test_the_monster_album_knows_more_after_each_stage`.

**AI Dungeon Master: scars:** Part of the rule depth "Full" (*Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth*, the host chooses per round), plus per round in the game master console *Scars only* (`nur_narben`, off); `dungeon_narben.py`, own code; tables `ideen.narben`, `narben_kinder` and `narben_sprueche` in `dungeon_srd.json`. When a hero stands up after lying down (Gnade too) or a foe hits it critically, the panel rolls a d20 on the wound table: mostly only a scar, on a few rows a mild consequence, −1 on a value (in `dungeon.total`), until a healer in a settlement takes it. At most one wound per hero and turn, at most 8 per hero (`h["narben"]`, checked when loaded). *Scars only* never gives a consequence, the children's table only plasters. The healer is in the settlement's window (`dungeon_gold`, 8 gold per group level) while the switch is on or the hero has a consequence; it takes all consequences, scars stay. In a settlement a fixed line of `narben_sprueche` asks about a scar. The hero sheet shows scars, consequences in red; the log one line per wound; the narrator reads the last three per hero and never rolls itself. The app shows it all at the game table. Live: wounds, with a consequence, plasters, at the healer today. Log `dungeon: narbe | narbe|folge|pflaster | auf|gnade|krit`, `dungeon: dienst | heiler | N gold`, `| heiler | nein`. Self-test `Fight.test_scars_and_lasting_wounds_with_a_healer`.

**AI Dungeon Master: content of the D&D ideas round 2:** `dungeon_srd.json` holds fixed tables under "ideen", checked by `dungeon_ideen.check_rules`. **Near miss** (`knapp`): 20 prices on a d20, each with a fixed id (goes into the dice receipt as "preis") and an effect out of noise, time, disadvantage, damage, tool, gold and NPC; at the children's table only noise and time (`kinder`). Idea "Pay the Price" from Ironsworn and Starforged (Shawn Tomkin, via datasworn, CC BY 4.0, credited in "lizenz"), rows rewritten. **Props** (`requisiten`): 58 with a sign, kinds of places and rooms and exactly one effect (schaden_gruppe, umwerfen, deckung, vorteil, fluchtweg); every kind of place and room of the map has at least 3. **Light:** torch (`holzfackel`, 1 gold) and glow stick (sci-fi), each `licht` 4 rooms. **Heart's wishes** (`wuensche`): 12 for all worlds and 8 each for fantasy, horror, sci-fi and pirates, at most 160 characters each. **Companions:** `gefaehrte_werte` for levels 1 to 10 (life about 80 % of a hero, armour, attack, damage, pay per chapter), 4 templates per world in `gefaehrten`, 3 animal friends for the children's table in `gefaehrten_kinder`. All texts are our own. Self-test `test_runde2_fuer_jede_szene_und_welt`.

**AI Dungeon Master: campfire and rest:** Part of the rule depth "Full" (*Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth*, the host chooses per round; `dungeon_rast.py`, short and long rest after the SRD 5.1, CC BY 4.0; tables `ideen.landschaften`, `nachtwachen`, `fragekarten` in `dungeon_srd.json`). Outside a fight the host calls "Short rest" or "Long rest" in the game master console (`POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/rast` `{art}`, only the host, rate limit `dungeon`). Short: every standing hero gets a quarter of its life back (at least 1), at most twice between two long rests. Long: the panel rolls three night watches with a d20 on the landscape's table (the map place's kind, a vault = "Underground", else "On the road"): quiet, noise, visit or raid. Without a raid full life and every spell, with a raid half the life and no spells; at the children's table a raid is only a noise. At most one long rest in 8 turns. Every seat gets a question at the campfire. Until the narration `rd["rast"]` stands (the music plays "camp"); the narrator gets kind, landscape, night watches and questions as facts and only tells, the next narrated turn ends the rest. Above the story a box shows the night watches and the own question, also in the app. Live: short and long rests, disturbed nights, refused today. Log `dungeon: rast | kurz`, `| lang | 3 wachen | ruhig|gestoert`, `| abgelehnt | Error`. Self-test `Fight.test_rest_at_the_campfire_by_fixed_rules`.

**AI Dungeon Master: travel stages:** Part of the rule depth "Full" (*Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth*, the host chooses per round; `dungeon_reise.py`; tables `ideen.reise_rollen` and `reise_ereignisse` in `dungeon_srd.json`, stages from `dungeon_karte.etappen`). When the map leads along a way of at least 2 stages (not in a vault), the panel rolls a journey: every seat with a standing hero takes a role (scout, hunter, watch, pathfinder; own names in science fiction), the roles change with the turn. Every role is a d20 on its value against 10 plus the stages (a 20 always passes, a 1 always fails). The first failed role decides the consequence: caught off guard (disadvantage for all next turn), hungry (1 life less, never below 1) or lost (a day more); if all pass, the journey is "rested" with a 20 (2 life), else quiet. An event of the landscape's table comes with it. `rd["reise_tage"]` counts the days on the road (for the world's calendar). The next narrator gets stages, landscape, roles with outcome and event as facts and only tells; the log shows "🧭 Journey" with every roll. Live: journeys, roles passed and failed today. Log `dungeon: reise | 3 etappen | 1 von 2 | hungrig`. Self-test `Equipment.test_a_long_way_is_a_journey`.

**AI Dungeon Master: tongues of the peoples:** Part of the rule depth "Full" (*Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth*, the host chooses per round; `dungeon_sprachen.py`, own code; sounds from the name syllables `namensilben` in `dungeon_srd.json`). The narration may let one person of the scene say a short greeting or cry in its people's tongue (`fremd` `{name, volk, sagt}`, sagt is the meaning). Fixed rules: only tongues with name syllables (Elvish, Dwarvish, Halfling, Orcish, Gnomish, Draconic, Infernal), at most 60 characters and 8 words, no refused word, never when the meaning already stands in the narration's quotes. The sounds come fixed from the meaning (syllables by each word's hash, the same sentence always sounds the same). Who understands is fixed: a hero of that people (a half-elf understands Elvish), a bard or wizard every tongue. Everyone sees and hears the sounds (in the person's voice); the meaning stands only in `rd["fremd"]` and goes only to seats that understand and the host, never to the car, never to the model (visibility filter `fremd_bedeutung` with a leak test). The others see "(not understood, Elvish)". Live: foreign lines, understood, refused today. Log `dungeon: sprache | elf | 1 verstehen`, `| verworfen | sprache|name|laenge|wort|im text`. Self-test `Equipment.test_a_peoples_tongue_only_for_who_knows_it`.

**AI Dungeon Master: calendar of the world:** Part of the rule depth "Full" (*Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth*, the host chooses per round; `dungeon_kalender.py`, own code and own lists). Every world has twelve months of 30 days, one or two moons and festivals (own lists per world, the children's table takes the general ones). A round's first day follows fixed from start time and title (`rd["kalender"]` `{start, halbe}`). Time runs only by fixed rules: half a day per stage of a way (`dungeon_karte.etappen`), a journey gone astray a day more, a long rest or a night at the inn a day, at most 20 days at once, nothing in a vault. On top of the table stand the date with moon phases, a festival today or within 7 days and, in a settlement, the market day every 7th day; on a festival a settlement's services cost 20 percent more (`dungeon_gold.price`). The narrator gets date, festival and market day only as data and may not change them. Live: half days passed and festivals reached today. Log `dungeon: kalender | 2 halbe tage | weg|reise|rast|gasthaus`, with `| fest` at a festival. Self-test `Equipment.test_the_worlds_calendar_runs_only_by_fixed_rules`.

**AI Dungeon Master: chronicle book:** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Chronicle and hero book* (`chat.dungeon_buecher`, off from the start; `dungeon_chronik.py`, own code and layout). When the host closes a chapter, the panel writes a page into the campaign's book (`buch`, at most 30 pages) only from the round's data: number and title, turns, cover (last scene picture), heroes with people, class, level and scars, honours of the evening, writings for everyone (one for a single seat stays out) and the map's places; "The story so far" comes onto it once written. The model writes nothing for it. Only the campaign's owner sees the book (`GET /api/profile/dungeon/kampagne/{kid}/chronik`); it can be turned, made as a PDF on the Spark (`…/chronik.pdf`, fpdf2 with the panel's fonts, rate limit `dungeonpdf`) and deleted page by page (`POST …/chronik/loeschen` `{kapitel}`). Every chapter's end stands in the log as `kapitel_ende` `{kapitel}`, also without the switch. Live: pages, PDF, deleted, refused today. Log `dungeon: chronik | seite | 2 helden | 1 schriften`, `| ansehen | 3 seiten`, `| pdf | 40 kb`, `| geloescht`, `| abgelehnt | Error`. Self-test `Campaign.test_a_chronicle_page_for_every_chapter`.

**AI Dungeon Master: hero book to the reMarkable:** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Chronicle and hero book* (`chat.dungeon_buecher`, off from the start, needs "Sending to the reMarkable"; `dungeon_buch.py`, own layout "Old grimoire"). Every hero of a profile gets a PDF in the page format of the own reMarkable (`rmpapier.MODELS`): contents with "New since the last edition", hero sheet (values, life, armour, gold, load, six scores, what it wears, abilities, bag, protection, scars), chronicle of encounters (per chapter evenings, fights, people met, levels; fellow heroes only with name, people, class), people (only what was unlocked, never wish, secret, role or goal, "x of y"), bestiary (by the monster album's stages, "x of y kinds") and items. Tabs at the edge (a bar at the bottom on the Move) and bookmarks jump to the parts. Three layouts: reMarkable 2 in two columns in greyscale, Paper Pro in two columns in strong colours, Paper Pro Move in one column with larger type, broken tables and a hero sheet over several pages; every text is measured, nothing reaches over the page. Only the panel's own pictures (portrait, faces, beasts, items), opened again and brought to the device's pixels: colour on Pro and Move, else 16 grey steps with error diffusion; without a picture a drawn symbol. Colour is never the only sign (rarity written, stages as dots). The choice "Colour" under *Me → reMarkable* (`rm_color`, "In colour" or "In greyscale", only for Paper Pro and Move) applies to everything laid out for the reMarkable, from the next sending. The book comes by itself half an hour after the end of a round or chapter (several chapters in one evening = one sending, at most once a day per hero, never again unchanged), on "Send now" under *Dungeon Master → Heroes* (there also "Preview (PDF)") or with "Send my hero book to the reMarkable". Without handwriting the new edition replaces the old one; with handwriting a new one comes and the old one goes to the archive. Once "Notes – NAME" comes along, lined with the same tabs, never replaced. The hero's chronicle (`chronik`, at most 300 entries) holds only fixed kinds with catalog ids, whole numbers and fellow heroes' names, peoples and classes, never model text; written only for profiles with the switch and checked on every read. Guests, figure codes and guest accounts get no book; the balance bench writes nothing. Sendings count in the reMarkable's daily limits. `GET /api/profile/dungeon/buch`, `GET …/buch/{hid}.pdf` (rate limit `dungeonpdf`), `POST …/buch/{hid}/senden`. State `users/<id>/dungeon-buch.json`. Book and notes lie in their own folder "Dungeon-Master" (Dungeon Master) below "Spark" (or the chosen folder), made when needed, also without "Folders by kind" (`rmpapier.OWN_FOLDER`); a loose book in "Spark" moves there with its next edition (the same document, without handwriting), one the person moved stays, notes already sent stay put. "Send now" answers at once and makes and sends in the background; the page shows per hero "being made and sent …", then "done" or "failed" with step and reason (took too long, no connection, cloud refused, too large, daily limit); a second press starts nothing new. Pictures are made once per book (colour JPEG, greyscale PNG with 16 steps), fonts only as subsets; with 60 pictures the book stays below 2 MB. When uploading, the wait grows with the file size (`remarkable.upload_timeout`: connect briefly, read 60 s plus time for a slow line, at most 15 min), a timeout is retried once after 5 s; this applies to everything sent to the reMarkable. Live: sent today, unchanged, not sent, in progress. Log `dungeon: buch | gestartet | knopf`, `| gebaut | seiten 9 | bilder 7 | kb 610`, `| gesendet | seiten 9 | bilder 7 | auto`, `| fehler | hochladen | ReadTimeout` (step `bauen`, `bild`, `liste`, `hochladen` or `wurzel`, then the reason in fixed words), `| uebersprungen | unveraendert`, `| uebersprungen | laeuft`, `| chronik | kapitel | 2 helden`, `| notizen | gesendet`, `| abgelehnt | aus`; before it possibly `remarkable: cloud ReadTimeout, trying once more`. Self-tests `test_dungeon_buch`.

**AI Dungeon Master: foe announcements and stance:** Part of the rule depth "Full" (*Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth*, the host chooses per round; `dungeon_haltung.py`). After every fought turn each standing foe announces its next blow at a standing hero (the panel's dice, `rd["kampf"]["ansage"]`, log card `ansage`): "takes aim" +2 to hit, "winds up" −2 to hit and double damage dice on a hit, "waits for an opening" with advantage. In the foes' next turn its first blow goes at that hero; if the hero fell, the foe attacks normally. The stance stands in the action with fixed words, also by voice and in the car (buttons 🛡 and 💨): "Defend" gives +2 armour, the own attack is at a disadvantage; "Dodge" gives advantage on saves against states. The narrator gets the announcements only as data (`ansagen`) and the receipts `wie_angesagt` and `haltung`. Live: announcements, defend, dodge today. Log `dungeon: ansage | 2 gegner`, `dungeon: haltung | abwehr|ausweichen | 1`. Self-test `Fight.test_foes_announce_and_heroes_defend_or_dodge`.

**AI Dungeon Master: use the surroundings:** Part of the rule depth "Full" (*Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth*, the host chooses per round; `dungeon_umgebung.py`). At the place of a fight 2 to 3 things of `ideen.requisiten` lie (`dungeon_karte.requisiten`, rolled once per place, `rd["kampf"]["requisiten"]`), at the table as buttons under the input. When an action names one (its name or its last word, also by voice and in the car), that is the hero's deed: one check of the panel instead of the ones the model wanted, d20 plus the value of its kind (dexterity, strength or wisdom) against 12, at the children's table 10, at the real table with the own die; each thing once a turn. On a success it works at once by a fixed rule: `schaden_gruppe` 1d6 bludgeoning to every foe (resistances count), `umwerfen` a foe lies down (the one named, else the one with the most life), `deckung` +2 armour, `vorteil` the group's attacks with advantage, `fluchtweg` the foes cannot reach the hero. Then it is used up (`dungeon_karte.requisit_benutzt`); on a failure it stays. The narrator gets the things only as data (`umgebung`) and the check as a receipt. Live: used, worked, missed today. Log `dungeon: umgebung | probe | 2`, `dungeon: umgebung | schaden_gruppe | ok|daneben`. Self-test `Fight.test_props_of_the_fight_work_by_fixed_rules`.

**AI Dungeon Master: whisper to a person:** Part of the rule depth "Full" (*Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth*, the host chooses per round; `dungeon_fluestern.py`). Once a turn a seat whispers something to a person of the scene (people of the NPC memory in the scene and whoever spoke in the last two turns), at most 200 characters, never from the car. Doors `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/fluestern` (login, own seat, CSRF, 2 KB, rate limit `dungeonfluester`) and `POST /api/dungeon/fluestern` (seat link, same door as `/api/dungeon/platz`). The model answers as that person (at most 400 characters) with its card, attitude towards the hero, place and last narration; the whisper stands only as game text between <<< and >>>. The panel checks the answer (plain text, no word of the word list) and keeps it only for that seat (`rd["fluester"]`, view filter `gefluestert` in the leak test); the host only sees that and with whom someone whispered. Nothing is read aloud at the table, 🔊 only on the own device. Fixed rules: attitude at most one step (NPC memory), a gift of at most 10 gold only from a warm person and once a round, a d20 up to 3 minus the attitude means betrayal: the table shows "… passes on what … whispered", and the next narration gets the whisper as game text. Live: whispered, betrayed, gifts, without an answer, refused today. Log `dungeon: fluestern | gefluestert | w14`, `… | verraten`, `… | geschenk`, `… | stumm`, `… | abgelehnt | ValueError`. Self-test `Turns.test_whisper_to_a_person_only_for_the_own_seat`.

**AI Dungeon Master: heart's wish:** Part of the rule depth "Full" (*Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth*, the host chooses per round; `dungeon_wunsch.py`, list of wishes per world `ideen.wuensche`). Every hero may carry a heart's wish (`held["wunsch"]`): one of its world's list or own words of at most 160 characters. Doors `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/wunsch` (login, own seat, CSRF, 1 KB, rate limit `dungeonwunsch`) and `POST /api/dungeon/wunsch` (seat link, same door as `/api/dungeon/platz`); both empty takes the wish away. Only the own seat, the host and the model see the wish (view filter `herzenswunsch` in the leak test). Per act (without a story every 8 turns) the panel gives the narrator at most one wish, in turn by seats: in the facts only the name (`herzenswunsch_von`), the wish only as game text between <<< and >>>. When the narrator reports the hero in `wunsch_erfuellt`, it counts only by fixed rules: this act's hero, acted this turn, named, wish still open. Then there is the experience of an act (`ep.akt`), and the table shows "💖 …'s heart's wish has come true.", never the wish. Live: set, fulfilled, refused reports, refused today. Log `dungeon: wunsch | gesetzt | liste|eigen`, `… | erfuellt`, `… | verworfen`, `… | weg`, `… | abgelehnt | ValueError`. Self-test `Turns.test_hearts_wish_secret_one_per_act_in_turn`.

**AI Dungeon Master: hire companions:** Part of the rule depth "Full" (*Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth*, the host chooses per round; `dungeon_gefaehrte.py`; tables `ideen.gefaehrten`, `ideen.gefaehrten_kinder`, `ideen.gefaehrte_werte`). In a settlement "🤝 Hire a companion" is a service of "Gold with a purpose" (`dungeon_gold.serve`, no door of its own, through `/held` with `{was: "dienst", id: "gefaehrte", i}`): only while fewer than four heroes play, up to a group of four, at most two. Life, armour, attack, damage and pay come from the fixed table by group level; the pay is due each chapter from the hero's gold, the first on hiring. In a fight the panel rolls a blow per companion (d20 + attack against the armour of the foe with the least life); foes can strike it like a hero (without states). At half its life a courage check once a fight (d20 ≥ 10, else it flees this fight), at 0 it goes down and stands again after the fight (never lost for good). A new chapter without pay: a courage check, else it leaves. No loot, no experience. In the encounter budget it counts as half a hero (`dungeon_kampf.budget`). The model gets companions only as data and their blows as receipts, it never decides. A campaign keeps them. Live: hired, blows, down, fled, left, pay today. Log `dungeon: gefaehrte | angeheuert`, `… | voll`, `… | unbekannt`, `… | sold | N gold`, `… | ohne sold | bleibt`, `… | gegangen`, `… | am boden`, `… | moral | geflohen|bleibt`. Self-test `Turns.test_companions_hired_fight_by_the_panels_dice`.

**AI Dungeon Master: fight turn check:** fixed rules of the panel, no switch. 1. Whoever fights a foe in a fight attacks it: the attack words also know "kämpfen", "bekämpfen", "hauen", "prügeln", "fight" (`dungeon_kampf.HIEB_WORTE`); when the model's check names a foe to hit, it becomes an attack against the armour with a damage roll; when the model asks no check, the panel adds the attack (`dungeon: probe | kampf | angriff ergaenzt`). 2. Only the player chooses a spell: naming it in the action costs its charge; when only the model names a spell in the check's label, the player's action stands there (`dungeon.spell_named`, `dungeon: probe | zauber nicht gewaehlt`). 3. When the narration reports a goal reached or an act's end during a fight, the panel waits until the fight is over (`ziel_wartet`, `akt_wartet`, `dungeon: ep | ziel | wartet kampf`, `dungeon: akt | wartet kampf`); the fight's experience comes from its foes. 4. Every XP entry says in brackets what for (check, new place, fight, goal, heart's wish, act); when a check in a fight names a foe and still stays a check, the log shows `dungeon: probe | kampf | kein angriff`. 5. Self-tests `Fight.test_a_fight_turn_keeps_to_the_panels_rules` and `Powers.test_known_spell_uses_a_charge_when_told`, plus the scene "Fighting becomes an attack against the armour" in the narrator bench.

**AI Dungeon Master: people catalog:** Admin switch *Features → My data → "People catalog"* (off; builds on "NPC memory and chronicle", applies to the round's host). 1. At a round's end every person met goes into the catalog (`dungeon_personen.collect`): card (looks, manner, occupation, wish, secret), traits, role and goal from the world's truth, voice, portrait and attitude towards each hero by name; each name once per world, at most 300 people (the oldest from rounds go first, the admin's own stay). 2. The admin sees, changes, deletes and adds them under *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Adjust rules → "People (NPC)"*; fixed rules check every input (name 2 to 40 characters, one of the worlds, only card fields, traits −3 to 3, no word of "nie", no name twice per world). Wish and secret are seen only by the admin there and the host in the round. 3. Per act (without a story per chapter) the panel offers the narrator up to three known people of the world only as data (`bekannte_personen`, rolled with round, chapter and act), never one with a word of the table's lists; when the narration names one with exactly its name, it comes with its stored card, traits, voice and face and recognises the heroes (chronicle "X recognises Y."); any other new name is a new person. After the round its entry is updated: attitudes, number of meetings, face. 4. When a person fights, it takes fixed values of a bestiary template by occupation (`dungeon_personen.VORLAGE`: knight → knight, veteran, guard; mercenary → veteran, thug, guard; guard → guard; scout → scout; thief → bandit; spy → spy; priest → acolyte, cultist; noble → noble; else guard), the strongest that plays in the world and fits the budget; the model never names values. Up to two people of the scene stand with their name in the foes' offer (`p:<name>`), never one friendly to a hero here (attitude 2 or more), never one beaten or fled; each only once. Beaten or fled goes into the chronicle afterwards. 5. **Balancing:** a person counts in budget and experience with its template's experience times a measured weight (`GEWICHT`, e.g. thug 1.65, noble 0.66). The bench plays encounters of people only: on average the group wins 70 to 90 % at "medium", every cell lies between 50 and 98 % (`SPANNE`). 6. Log `dungeon: personen | gesammelt | N neu`, `| wieder | N helden`, `| kampf | besiegt` or `| geflohen`, `| setzen`, `| zurueck`, `| abgelehnt | …`; Live: people in the catalog and today new, met again, recognised, fought, changed, deleted. State `dungeon-personen.json`. 7. Self-tests `Fight.test_people_catalog_keeps_the_people_met_and_brings_them_back`, `Fight.test_a_person_who_fights_takes_a_fixed_template`, `Bench.test_people_who_fight_keep_the_aim` and `Page.test_the_people_of_the_catalog`.

**AI Dungeon Master: album page "People":** No switch of its own: the page is there when "Monster album" and "People catalog" are on for the host. When the catalog collects the people met at a round's end (`dungeon_personen.collect`), each counts one meeting for every living hero at the table (`h["personen"] = {catalog id: meetings}`, `dungeon_album.met`). The tab "Album" has the pages "Monsters" and "People". After the first meeting the page shows name, face, looks and world, after the second occupation and manner, after the fourth traits and attitude towards exactly this hero (`PERSONEN_STUFEN = (1, 2, 4)`). Wish, secret, role and goal never show there; a person with a word of the lists of the round's tone is left out. Only a seat of a round in which a hero met the person loads the face (`dungeon_gesicht.may_see` with `album.knows_face`). The hero's file keeps only catalog ids and whole numbers (`album.fix`, at most 300). Log `dungeon: album | person stufe N`, Live "N people stages".

**AI Dungeon Master: own stories:** Admin switch *Features → My data → "Own stories"* (`dungeongeschichten`, off by default), module `dungeon_geschichten.py`. 1. **⋯ on every story card:** when starting a round every card has a "⋯" with Edit, Copy and Delete. Edit opens the story planner's form (title, idea, tone, goal, foe, places, taboos, acts with "↻ Write again") in a window of its own. A story of the admin or a shipped one becomes the host's own copy on saving, the original is hidden only for that host. Delete hides such stories only for this host ("Show N hidden again" brings all back), own ones it deletes for good. Copy makes "(copy)". 2. **"＋ New story" first:** the card comes first. Pick a tone, keywords if you like, "Create": the language model writes title, idea, goal, foe, places and acts in the chosen world (`dungeon.propose_plan`), the panel checks the draft like a typed one (`dungeon.check_plan`: lengths, tone, "nie") and keeps it as an own story. "Write it yourself" opens the empty form. 3b. **Every host its own:** up to 20 per host (`EIGENE_MAX`); no other host sees, changes or plays them (404). The admin sees all under Features → AI Dungeon Master → Adjust rules → "Stories" with the host's name, changes them (they stay the host's) and counts only the own for the admin's limit. A round plays only an allowed story (`dungeon_geschichten.find`), in the car the Spark offers the own ones first. `dungeon-geschichten.json` keeps the own ones with `"von"`, `dungeon-geschichten-aus.json` the hidden ids per host. Endpoints under `/api/profile/dungeon/geschichten/…` (view, save, copy, delete, show, create, act) with login, profile binding, CSRF, size and rate limit (`dungeongeschichte`, the planner `dungeonplan` per host). Switch off: no ⋯ and no ＋, own stories are missing from the choice, hidden ones are back, nothing is deleted. Logs `dungeon: geschichten | gespeichert / kopiert / geloescht / ausgeblendet / wieder gezeigt / erstellt / abgelehnt`, Live "N own stories, today …".

**AI Dungeon Master: people of the catalog in stories:** No switch of its own: the people catalog counts (`dungeonpersonen`, with "NPC memory and chronicle"). In the story planner (Features → AI Dungeon Master → Adjust rules → "Stories", for a host in the window of the own story) below the taboos stands "Known people from the catalog": the chosen world's people with name and occupation to tick, up to three (`dungeon_personen.PLAN_MAX`); wish and secret never. When the world changes, its people show. The panel keeps only catalog ids from the story's world, each once (`dungeon_personen.plan_ids` in `dungeon.check_plan`). "Create a proposal" and "＋ New story" give the language model up to 12 people of the world only as data (name and occupation, `bekannte_personen`); whoever the draft names with exactly their name comes ticked (`dungeon_personen.pick`). A round of that story puts the people with role and goal from the catalog first into the chapter's truth, never twice (`dungeon_personen.into_truth`), and offers them to the narrator first in every act, with fixed card, voice and attitude. Catalog off: no list, nothing in the truth. Log `dungeon: personen | geschichte | N in der wahrheit`.

**AI Dungeon Master: choose a person's voice:** Under *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Adjust rules → "People (NPC)"* every person shows its voice, its own or "(by the name)" (`dungeon_stimmen.of_name`). Below "Voice when the person speaks" the admin picks age, gender, sound, manner and pace from the fixed lists of `dungeon_stimmen.json` ("npc", words `dungeon_stimmen.WORTE`). "▶ Sample" (`POST /api/admin/dungeon/personen/probe`, admin only, only with the people catalog on, rate limit "dungeonprobe") speaks the fixed sample sentence in the description built from fixed English words (`dungeon_stimmen.describe`), without VoiceDesign in the rules' fixed NPC voice. "Save voice" (`was` "stimme" at the catalog endpoint) sets only the voice, the person otherwise stays unchanged, also "from a round"; "By the name" takes the own voice away. `dungeon_stimmen.clean` refuses a wrong voice and nothing is written; the JSON of "Edit" gets the same check. When a round meets the person, it speaks with that voice (`adopt` puts it into `npc_stimmen`). The portrait comes from `dungeon_dingbild`. Log "dungeon: personen | stimme" and "dungeon: personen | probe", Live counts "Stimmen gesetzt".

**AI Dungeon Master: ask back on unclear actions:** On with the game, no switch of its own (*Features → AI Dungeon Master*), released for every profile; guests at the table get it the same way (part of the game). When an action is sent the panel checks it by fixed rules (`dungeon_rueckfrage.unclear`): too short (fewer than 2 words or 8 letters, "ok", "hm"), gibberish (one character four times, half the words without a vowel), only a pronoun as the target while several fit ("I attack him" with two foes, "take it"), or no doing (no word of `TUN_WORTE` and the fight's word lists, no subject with a verb, no name of the scene, no quoted speech). Then the action stays as written but marked: below it stands "❓ What exactly does Morpheus do?" with the reason, up to three suggestions and "Keep it". The panel builds the suggestions from fixed patterns and the scene's data (foes still up, things in a fight, own attack spell, people here, ways of the map), never from model text; each is clear itself. Tapping one puts it into the input for editing. While the question is open the seat does not count as "has acted" (`dungeon.due`), but the turn waits at most `WARTE_S` (30) seconds, then the action counts as written. The panel asks once a turn for the same text, "Keep it" (`so_lassen`) takes the mark off. In the car (`dungeon_auto`) the action goes in at once and the question is read with "First …, second …"; the CarPlay list "What exactly?" shows suggestions and "Keep it" as buttons, "the first" or "keep it" answers by voice, only the panel's suggestions count. The model never sees the question. Log `dungeon: rueckfrage | regel | kurz` (`salat`, `fuerwort`, `kein tun`), `| vorschlag genommen`, `| so lassen`, `| klar`; Live counts questions, suggestions taken and "kept". Self-tests `Rueckfrage.test_the_table_of_clear_and_unclear_actions`, `test_asked_at_the_table_never_blocking`, `test_asked_once_for_the_same_text`, `test_never_from_the_model_and_only_the_own_seat`, `InTheCar.test_an_unclear_action_is_asked_back`.

**AI Dungeon Master: the hero's gender:** No switch of its own. When making a hero (host, seat link, guest) one picks "male", "female" or "non-binary" below the name; on the hero sheet (tab "Spend points") it can be changed any time. Heroes without it stay unset until one picks. The panel takes only these three values (`dungeon.GESCHLECHT`, `dungeon.gender`), else fault "geschlecht". The model gets per hero `geschlecht` with the pronoun ("female, call her she"; non-binary "they or the name") and the fixed rule in `KANN_REGEL`. A fixed check (`dungeon_erzaehler.wrong_pronoun`, fault "fuerwort", day counter `regel_fuerwort`) finds a wrong possessive right after a hero's name (at most three words between, no comma, no article, no other person) before an own body part or thing ("Lia raises his blade"): first a second try with a fixed hint, then the panel sets the right word (his ↔ her, sein ↔ ihr, for non-binary the article or "their"; without a sure word the sentence stays). Log `dungeon: nachbessern | fest | fuerwort` or `| bleibt | fuerwort`. The proposed voice comes from the voices of the gender (`STIMMEN_W` for female, the others for male, non-binary all; with none free, any). The portrait names "a man", "a woman" or "an androgynous person"; after a change it may be painted anew at the same level (`bild_neu`), the old one stays until then. Log `dungeon: held | geschlecht | geaendert`. Self-test `Voice.test_a_hero_has_a_gender_for_words_voice_and_portrait`.

**AI Dungeon Master: combat audit:** Fixed combat rules without a switch of their own. 1. **Attack from the action:** in a fight the panel decides by fixed word lists (`dungeon_kampf.intent`) what an action does, never the model's check. `ANGRIFF_WORTE` (greife … an, schlage, steche, haue, kämpfe, töte, ramme, attack, fight, stab …, also with prefixes such as er-, be-, zu-) always attack, with no foe named the first standing; `BEZUG_WORTE` (ziele, werfe, feuere, stürme … los, schneide, aim, throw, charge …) only with a foe named; `NICHT_ANGRIFF` (greife nach, treffe mich mit, stoße die Tür auf, betrete, campfire …) never. Foe names with a hyphen (Meervolk-Späher) are recognised. The model's check for an attacker is dropped (log `dungeon: kampf | angriff | aus aktion` or `| modell ohne probe`); advantage only from fixed sources (states, surroundings, stance, a foe on the ground), never from the model. 2. **Target fallen:** when the foe named already fell this turn, the attack takes the next one standing (`| ziel neu`); with none standing it lapses without a check and experience (`| verfaellt`). 3. **Damage spells:** a spell with a damage kind always attacks and costs exactly one charge, only when its attack came; a spell on foes without damage (`KONTROLLE`: sleep, hold person, fear …) costs no charge in a fight. The seat reads "… does not work on foes in a fight yet, the charge stays." or "… had no target left, the charge stays." (log `dungeon: kraft | keine ladung | kontrolle` or `| kein angriff`). 4. **No healing by the model in a fight:** the panel drops `heilung` in the consequences during a fight (`dungeon: folgen | heilung | kampf verworfen`); healing only from potions, spells and abilities. 5. **Peace only with a check:** when an action asks for peace (`FRIEDEN_WORTE`: ergebt euch, verhandle, lasst uns reden, surrender, parley …) without attacking, the panel rolls Charisma against the higher of 12 and 10 + the strongest foe's level (`FRIEDEN_SG`); only a success ends the fight with "frieden" and half the experience. A `kampf_ende` "frieden" of the model without this check does not count (`kampf | frieden | ohne probe`). 6. **Rule 6, weapons by the hero sheet:** the model gets per hero the list `waffen` (equipment and bag) and the fixed rule to name only these. When the narration gives a hero a weapon it does not carry with a possessive (sein, ihre, his …), that is the fault "dabei" (`dungeon_erzaehler.not_carried`): first a second try with a fixed hint, then the panel strikes the sentence. A short sword counts as a sword, "blade" and "weapon" always fit, another person's weapon never. Day counter `regel_dabei`. 7. **Balance:** `BUDGET` for levels 1, 2, 5, 6, 9 and 10 and `GRUPPE` (0.7 for one hero, 0.4 for each further one) are measured with the bench, every cell lies at 70 to 90 % at "medium". 8. Self-tests in `Fight`: `test_kampf_angriffswoerter_tabelle`, `test_kampf_angriff_aus_aktion`, `test_kampf_ziel_gefallen_neues_ziel`, `test_kampf_kein_vorteil_vom_modell`, `test_kampf_schadenszauber_immer_angriff_und_keine_ladung_ohne_wirkung`, `test_kampf_keine_modellheilung`, `test_kampf_frieden_nur_mit_panelprobe`, `test_narration_gives_no_weapon_the_hero_does_not_carry` and the simulation `test_kampf_invarianten_simulation` (300 fights with a fixed seed through the real steps of a turn: hits only by the rule, life within its bounds, the fallen do not act, one end of the fight, experience only then, a charge only with a spell, death only after three failed saves and never at the children's table, every attacker gets its attack).

**AI Dungeon Master: one sorting for every typed action:** No switch of its own. 1. **One module:** every fixed word list that sorts a typed action (attack, attack on a named foe, flight, peace, everyday, question, selling, buying, rest, going, plus the gentle words, magic, throwing and the table of check values) stands in `dungeon_einordnung.py`. 2. **Exactly one kind:** `einordnung.kind` gives every action exactly one kind by a fixed order: attack, attack on a named foe, flight, peace, everyday, question, selling, buying, rest, going. Fight, checks, the narrator's check, the map, the witness rule, the follow-up question and the protocol checker all ask the same module, no list contradicts another. Asking for peace with a word of talking ("Lasst uns verhandeln") counts as talking too. 3. **Examples:** "Ich frage den Wirt nach dem Weg. Dann gehe ich los." is a question ("gehe … los" attacks only with "auf"), "Wir schlagen hier unser Lager auf und rasten." and "We rest by the fire." are rest, "Ich laufe davon" is only flight, "Ich sage Hallo" is everyday without a check, "Ich schlage vor, wir reden" is talking. 4. **Bench:** `test_dungeon_saetze` checks the one kind of each of the about 300 sentences and that every helper (`fight`, `asks`, `typed`, `everyday`) says the same; the table of check values (`einordnung.values`) serves the checks and the guide's hints.

**AI Dungeon Master: anchor table, page tables and protocol checker:** No switch of its own: fixed rules, a measurement and self-tests. 1. **Anchor table:** `tests/dungeon_anker.py` names for every field of every JSON answer of the model (narration, checks, story, truth, summaries, whisper, X card, scribe; about 110 fields) its kind (words only, fixed list, offered by the panel, a name of the group, named by the narration, the panel decides) and the fixed check that holds it. `test_dungeon_anker` compares it with the schemas: a field without an anchor or a check that is gone turns it red. A thing for the memory (`dinge`) and a writing count only when the narration shows them (`erz.check_things` with `foe_named`, `schrift.check` with `schrift.WORTE` or the title). 2. **Page tables:** `test_dungeon_tabellen` compares the game page's tables (a check's level, rarity, a power's kind, weapon properties, resistance, night watch, a companion's state, experience reason, bench mark, time of day, the settlement's houses and services, damage kinds, the map's room kinds) with the panel's lists; every refusal reason needs its sentence on the page (`DMGRUND` or another game script; general reasons with their why in `ALLGEMEIN`). 3. **Protocol checker** (`dungeon_protokoll.py`): after every told turn all fixed checks run once more over the narration that stays, the repaired one too (`rest`); findings go to the journal ("dungeon: protokoll | rest | …", else "dungeon: protokoll | ok"). It also checks the round's names (`runde`): a word for no name as a foe, person card or person of the truth, and a person card that is a foe too. The Live overview counts checked narrations, those with faults left and wrong names. A round copied from the game page it reads back into its turns (`read`) and finds display faults by fixed rules (`text_faults`: two levels for one number, a placeholder as a foe, a missed spell with its effect sentence, a merchant not shown, a sentence about the panel, a question as "going needs none", a check instead of "cannot do that yet"). `cd app && python3 -m tests.test_dungeon_protokoll copy.txt --fall name` keeps it in `tests/dungeon_protokolle/` with its findings as header, `test_dungeon_protokoll` checks every case against it (among them the round in the Old Library with 16 findings). 4. **Round run:** the narrator bench has the scene "15 turns without faults left" (`dungeon_erzaehlstand.RUNDLAUF`): 15 fixed turns (entering an inn, asking Oskar, a spell not known, opening a book, raising the staff, buying without a merchant, setting up camp …) run through the real model, each narration the next turn's history. Every kept narration goes through the protocol checker, no speaker may be called "keine"; 2 points when every turn passes at the first try, 1 when none keeps a fault. The bench counts nothing into the day's numbers (`ask_checks` with `count=False` too).

**AI Dungeon Master: sentence bench:** No switch of its own. 1. **Fixed sentences:** `tests/dungeon_saetze.py` holds about 300 player sentences in German and English with their kind (attack, attack on a named foe, peace, flight, going, question, buying, selling, rest, everyday, nothing of these). `test_dungeon_saetze` runs every word list (`kampf.intent`, `dungeon.typed`, `erz.asks`, `dungeon.everyday`) over every sentence, the table of attack words included. A sentence sorted wrong at the table comes in with its fix. 2. **No attack:** a staff with an article ("meinen Stab"), Stichwort, Schlagzeile, opening a book, a map or setting up a camp ("aufschlagen"), unpacking, ramming a stake into the ground, beating one's own fear, fighting through undergrowth, setting sail, killing time, "ans Feuer", battle map, kick off, swing open; "Wir ergeben uns" asks for peace; "Ich trete ein" is going. 3. **Abilities not named like spells:** the abilities are called Flammenkunde (Flame lore), Verblassen (Fade), Leuchtende Hand (Glowing hand), Lügen spüren (Sense lies) and Anfeuern (Rally), ids unchanged, so a spell of a similar name correctly gives "cannot do that yet". `check_rules` refuses any name that stands for two kinds (spell, ability, thing, foe; a role word like "guard" only as a foe); so the ability Zäh is called Hardy in English. 4. **Spell names without a verb:** a spell's name counts without "cast" too ("Ich werfe einen Feuerball", "Fireball!"). Names that are also everyday words carry `"alltagswort": true` (spells Light, Haste, Sleep, Darkness …; abilities Camouflage, Evasion, Blessing, Steadfast, Cleanse): such a spell counts only with a verb of casting ("wirke", "zaubere", "beschwöre", cast), and such a missing ability gives no "cannot do that yet" ("Ich nutze die Tarnung der Büsche").

**AI Dungeon Master: faults of the round in the Old Library:** Fixed rules without a switch of their own. 1. **Difficulty:** the dice bar names the panel's word (`sg`, 13 is "medium") like the card; only a number without a level goes by the SRD table, also for own dice. 2. **Damage spells:** the line "casts Fire bolt" stands without its effect sentence, whether it hits is told by the attack line. 3. **Fireball:** the wizard's ability is named differently from the spell (see sentence bench). 4. **Panel in the story:** a sentence in which the panel speaks ("Das Panel würfelt …") leaves the story (`dungeon.panel_says`, in English only with a word of rolling or checking, a door panel stays). 5. **Reason without a check:** who only asks hears "who only asks or talks …" instead of "walking needs none". 6. **Entering:** "betrete", "trete in … ein" and "enter" are walking without a check, whatever the model writes, never an attack (`kampf.intent` with `NICHT_ANGRIFF` for typed actions too). 7. **Goal reached:** reads "Goal reached: name", without inflection. 8. **Merchants:** a merchant of the model opens only when the story shows one (name or a word of `dungeon.MARKT_WORT`), else `dungeon: markt | nicht erzaehlt`. Self-test in `test_dungeon` (Bibliothek).

**AI Dungeon Master: "keine" is no name:** A fixed rule for people, without a switch of its own. 1. **Never a person:** a word for "no name" (`dungeon_nsc.LEERWORTE`: keine, niemand, unbekannt, namenlos, none, nobody, unknown …) or a name without a letter never becomes a person, not in the fixed truth, not from the narration, not through the scribe check (reason `leer` in the log, never the name); a narration line with such a speaker is dropped. 2. **Existing cards:** such a card or person in a running round goes away at the next turn with its place on the map (`dungeon: nsc | leer | N weg`). Self-test in `test_dungeon` (ReviewPersonen).

**AI Dungeon Master: foe names known when bent:** A fixed check without a switch of its own. 1. **Bent and in another order:** a foe of the encounter counts as named when its whole name is there or every word of its name is in one sentence by its stem, in any order (`dungeon_erzaehler.foe_named`): "giant, red-eyed rats" for "Giant rat", "warrior kobolds" for "Kobold warrior", "jungen roten Drachen" for "Junger Roter Drache", plus fixed plural forms (f becomes v as in wolf, wolves; man becomes men; the last vowel with its umlaut as in Wolf, Wölfe). Filler words such as "of", "the", "der" do not count; words in two sentences or another foe never count. 2. **The bench shows the foe:** when a scene fails on "gegner", the reason names the missing catalog foes (catalog names only, never model text). Self-test in `test_dungeon_erzaehlstand`.

**AI Dungeon Master: settlement reordered:** No switch of its own (gold with a purpose, *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth* "Full"). The window "🏘 Settlement": 1. **Houses:** the services belong to a house (`dungeon_gold.HAEUSER`, one source): inn (stay the night, rumour, hire a companion), smithy (mend, improve, drill a socket, take runes out, fuse runes), healer, teacher; the view gives `haeuser` with the services there are here. 2. **List and house:** the houses on the left with a small picture and what works right now ("2 offers", "nothing right now", "🌙 shut until morning"), the chosen house on the right; on the phone first the list, a tap opens the house with "‹ Houses". 3. **Rows:** per offer name, a short sentence and a button with verb and price; too dear, the button is grey with "N gold short"; the price calculation stands once, small, at the bottom of the house. 4. **Empty:** what does not work right now is a thin dashed row with the reason (slept here tonight, nothing broken, no rumour left, shut at night); a house without an offer stands pale at the bottom. 5. **Head:** one slim line with place, time of day, the own gold big and the group's small. 6. **Pictures:** one picture per house and world (`haeuser[].bild`); without one the house shows the first picture of its services, else the place's, else its sign. Self-tests in `test_dungeon` (the view's houses, the window on computer and phone).

**AI Dungeon Master: game master gives things:** A tool of the game master console, no switch of its own (*Features → AI Dungeon Master*). 1. **Where:** in the game master console under "Only for you" the tab "🎁 Give", only for the host and only in the admin view; every request checks the admin again (`dungeon_geben.admin`): the panel's admin login or the host's own login as an admin profile (role admin, not manager), never a guest, another seat or the language model. 2. **What:** an item of the catalog of the round's world with a rarity up to "epic" (a fixed rarity stays, the thing is known at once), stackable things like potions and spells up to 5 pieces; gold up to 1000 at once; runes up to 5 of a kind, only with *Runes and sockets*; experience at most up to the next level. Never a legend, never a fixed legendary thing, never a quest item. The list comes once on opening (`GET /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/geben`), with a search. 3. **Limits:** as in the game: a full or too heavy bag (`dungeon.room`), gold never above 100000 (`GOLD_MAX`), rune bag 40, one level a game evening (`dungeon.gain`), after which a waiting runeword wakes. 4. **At the table:** a fixed line "🎁 The game master gives Arvid: Longsword." (log kind `geben`); the next narration gets it as data (`spielleiter_gibt`) with the task to tell it as a find or a gift, inventing nothing more. Live: things, gold, runes and experience from the game master today, refused (numbers only). Log `dungeon: geben | ding|gold|rune|ep | ok|nein`, `dungeon: geben | ding | voll`, `dungeon: geben | kein admin`. Self-tests `test_dungeon_geben`.

**AI Dungeon Master: runes and sockets, runewords and smith:** Part of the rule depth "Full" (*Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth*, the host chooses per round). 1. **Runewords:** when the runes of a runeword sit in its order in a plain, known thing with exactly as many sockets at its place (weapon, shield, head or body; `dungeon_runen.match`), it wakes as soon as the hero has its level: at the insert or on a new level (`wake`). The panel sets `it["rw"]`, the extra effect runs through the same rules as every rune (effect, value, to hit and damage only in attacks, armour class, healing potions) with its own cap: at most like "legendary" (weapon 10, body 8, the rest 4), two kinds of resistance a thing, to hit at most +3. Worth = base price + rune prices + base price × 8. The preview says "Then the runeword Stormsong wakes: …" or "wakes only from level 5"; the table shows "Runeword Stormsong wakes in Arvid's Longsword: …", the thing "Runeword Stormsong: …". All seven runewords stand in the rune bag under "Runewords" as goals: runes in order (the ones there highlighted), place, sockets, from level, extra and own fitting things. When a hero is read, a runeword stays only where its runes sit. 2. **Smith** (with *Gold with a purpose*, in a settlement or at a merchant, shut at night, never in a fight; `dungeon_gold.serve` with `dungeon_runen.DIENSTE`): *Drill a socket* 25 × group level × new socket count, never fails, at most one drilled socket a thing, never above the base's sockets, never into a runeword; *Take runes out* broken for 5 × group level or saved for half the rune prices back into the bag, a runeword always goes out; *Fuse runes* three of the same into one of the next rank for a quarter of its price, up to rank 9.
