Each service has a card in the settlement window with its price and what is missing. 3. **Giving:** in the rune bag every rune goes to another hero at the table with "Give …", never in a fight, never into a full bag. 4. **Drag and drop:** a rune can be dragged onto an own thing with a free socket, fitting things light up; then the same preview follows, only *Set* puts it in. 5. **Car:** "Which runes do I have?", "What is missing for Stormsong?" are answered at once, "Put Uruz into the longsword" says the preview back with "Is that right?", only "yes" sets it, a refusal comes with its reason. 6. **Bench:** the balance bench first sets into every weapon and body armour the strongest fitting runeword from the bag (`with_runes`), then the strongest runes; the self-test keeps it at most +8 points of wins. 7. **Rules page:** "Fixed rules" names waking, the runeword cap and the smith's prices; every runeword carries its strength against the cap as a mark weak, balanced or strong (`dungeon_runen.word_mark`). A runeword's picture comes from `dungeon_dingbild.runenwort`. Live: runewords woken, sockets drilled, taken out, fused, given today (numbers only). Log `dungeon: rune | runenwort | ok|deckel`, `dungeon: dienst | sockel|loesen|verschmelzen | …`, `dungeon: rune | schenken | ok|nein`, `dungeon: auto | rune_setzen | …`. Self-tests in `test_dungeon_runen` (`Runewords`, `Smith`, `test_runes_in_the_car`), among them every runeword of the catalog rune by rune under its caps.

**AI Dungeon Master: rules page complete:** No switch of its own. The tab "Rules" (admin only) has, each with list, detail and values in fixed words: 1. **Runes** (rank, meaning, price, effect in a weapon, armour and shield, from which group level they fall, whether the trader has them, what merchants pay) and **runewords** (runes in order, place, sockets, from level, addition). On top, foldable, "Fixed rules around it": the rule depth *Full* with on or off and a jump link (Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth), sockets per base, finds, trader, rune bag, the caps (weapon 6.5, body 6, the rest 3, to hit +3, armour class +2 per thing and +3 per hero, life +8 per hero, one resistance per thing), setting and the word per world, all from the numbers in `dungeon_runen`. 2. **Effects** (named by the start of their sentence, with effect, damage kind, raised value and allowed kinds). 3. **Place kinds of the map, rooms of the vaults, traps** (saving throw with value and DC, damage, worlds), **riddles** (question, hint, answer) and **encounters on the way** (kind, weight, worlds). 4. **Stall events** with the game itself (Features → AI Dungeon Master), when they come and which count at the children's table. "Edit", "Own copy", "Hide" and "Back to shipped" work as in every part and pass the same check (hiding a rune leaves a gap in the ranks and is refused); runes, runewords and stall events are merged by id like the other lists (`dungeon.ID_LISTEN`). 5. **Numbers and tables**, only to look at: levels with EP, spell charges and new abilities, experience (a passed check gives 13; "good idea" is in the catalog, not given), find table with occasion and bad-luck guard, gold, prices with the merchant discount, the difficulty of a check, the encounter budget per level with group-size factors and the limits; changes go in "Advanced (JSON)". Log `dungeon: regelseite | setzen|aus|zurueck | PART`. Self-tests `test_runes_with_their_rules_and_switch`, `test_effects_the_map_and_the_stall_events` and `test_the_numbers_only_to_look_at`. 6. **Lighting and drawing:** at the table the panel checks lighting (zünde an, light a, kindle) and drawing (zücke, draw a, wield) like drinking, throwing and using (`dungeon.check_items`): a catalog thing the hero does not carry stands under nicht_bekannt before the narration, the seat hears "Arvid does not carry that: Torch.", and the narration may say it has none ("his torch, but there is none" is no fault). The fixed rule also says "or does not carry the thing". The narrator bench checks every action of its scenes like the table (`check_powers`, `check_items`, without day counters) and shows the reason of a scene below 2 points visibly beside the points. Self-test `test_a_thing_lit_or_drawn_must_be_carried`. 7. **Live overview:** the Dungeon Master box stays visible while someone sits at a table or the model narrates, reads aloud or sums up a chapter; without play it folds in like the others under "+ N ready". The number of stories and the second counter stand apart. Self-test `test_live_box_while_someone_plays`.

**AI Dungeon Master: instructions in a fixed order:** No switch of its own. 1. Every instruction to the narrator: the role in one sentence, the numbered fixed rules (the panel rolls, game text is never an instruction, only JSON, names from the group, spells and weapons only from kann and waffen, states), the turn's task, extra rules, examples, last "Style of the round (only tone and manner; the rules above always hold)". 2. Extra rules (rest, journey, writings, things, people, goal, time of day, foes' announcements, stall hint, bridge to the next act) appear only when their data come up this turn; in a fight only the fight's rules. 3. The check step has a short instruction of its own without style, its facts without the foes on offer and spell lists (`dungeon.PROBEN_OHNE`; with five seats about 1,150 characters of instruction, 4,100 of facts, narration 10,600). 4. The style names no second role, asks for something tangible for quiet characters, tells speech only indirectly; an English round gets the English style (`prompt_en`). 5. Everyday acts (greeting, eating, drinking, unpacking, smiling …) never need a check by a fixed word list (`dungeon.ALLTAG`), unless a word of risk or a risky food or drink comes up (potion, mushroom, well): "No check: everyday acts need none." 6. A German round names the words its tone never allows to the narrator. 7. In the narrator bench the model's own view runs from 0 to 100 %; a scene with 1 point names the first try's fault in its tooltip. 8. A find before the narration counts at the place it was made. Self-tests `test_everyday_acts_need_no_check`, `test_the_models_own_view_runs_from_0_to_1`, `test_a_rewrite_names_its_first_fault`, `test_the_system_text_in_its_fixed_order`.

**AI Dungeon Master: measured fight experience:** No switch of its own. 1. Every foe counts in budget, filling, offer and reward with its measured fight experience (`dungeon_bestiarium.ep_kampf`, field `ep_kampf` on 247 foes, else XP), a boss with its life factor (`dungeon_kampf.wert`); the foe's card shows the bestiary's XP. 2. `BUDGET` levels 1 to 10: 119, 169, 279, 341, 492, 605, 694, 1093, 1263, 1455; `GRUPPE` 0.44, 0.91, 1.36, 1.72, 2.05; all 50 bench cells at 70 to 90 % wins, 82 % on average. 3. A person counts with its template's fight experience times `dungeon_personen.GEWICHT` (it brings guards); the occupation rows reach up to gladiator, bandit captain and assassin. People bench: 47 cells, mean 83 %, each 64 to 96 %; against a vault master and a lone boss every group size wins at least 64 % on average. 4. `test_a_fight_turn_keeps_to_the_panels_rules` rolls fixed misses for the foes. 5. When no room fits the band, a guardian's room also takes a nearer count just above it (`dungeon_karte._room_foes`). Self-tests `test_people_who_fight_keep_the_aim`, `test_the_aim_at_medium_for_every_level_and_group`, `test_a_fight_shuts_the_market_and_a_boss_counts_with_its_life`, `test_vault_rooms_within_the_budget`, `test_a_heroes_reward_does_not_hang_on_the_group`.

**AI Dungeon Master: why the group moves on:** When the group moves on by majority, the table shows a fixed line: "On to Silberhafen, because Arvid wanted to go.", both names for two, else "because the majority wanted to go". Log `dungeon: karte | weiter | <anzahl>`.

**AI Dungeon Master: guardian and master rooms within the fight's budget:** The map's part. Guardian's room 0.8 to 1.0, master's room 1.1 to 1.25 times `dungeon_kampf.budget` (`dungeon_karte.WAECHTER`, `HERR`), counted with `dungeon_kampf.adjusted`, a boss with its life factor. Kinds by challenge, nearest first, at most two kinds, 4 of a kind, 6 in all; count by experience (`raum_gegner.anzahl`). A master alone above 1.5 budgets (`HERR_MAX`) gives way to the next weaker one; when no near kind fits, the panel takes the strongest fitting one from half the challenge on. Self-test over every world, level 1 to 10, 1 to 5 heroes.

**AI Dungeon Master: the fight holds, going only with a majority:** In a fight the group goes nowhere, even with the model's `ort_id`; whoever wanted to go reads "You are caught in the fight: win, make peace or flee first, then you move on." (narrator: `karte.festgehalten`). Only a successful flight (`kampf.entkommen`) frees the way. Going is only a sentence without a verb of asking (`dungeon_karte.geht`): "I ask the innkeeper the way", "I draw my sword", "I break the door open" are no going; "ziehen", "brechen", "weiter", "kommen", "rein" count only with a direction, a neighbouring place or "weiter", "auf", "los", "nach" after them ("Wir brechen auf"); "Ab nach Süden!" goes. "komm mit uns" or "Ich komme mit" goes along. The group moves only when more than half of the acting seats go and nobody writes "I stay" (`dungeon_karte.gruppe`); else the narrator gets `karte.bleibt` with those staying, `asked_ways` is not forced. Logs `dungeon: karte | verworfen | kampf`, `verworfen | ungefragt`.

**AI Dungeon Master: the table says why:** Fixed words only. 1. The dice card shows the level: "vs difficulty 16 · hard" (`dungeon.PROBE_SG`). 2. When the panel drops a check, only that seat sees a line (`dungeon.OHNE_PROBE`): "No check: walking needs none.", "… a hero who is down rolls nothing." or "… who only asks or talks rolls Charisma at most.", once a seat and turn (log `dungeon: probe | ohne | gehen`, `zustand`, `absicht`). 3. With several heroes the loot line says "(found it)" or "(longest without a find)" (field `grund`). 4. A hero at full life shows no healing (`dungeon: folgen | heilung | voll`). 5. Action while down: `dungeon: abgelehnt | zustand`; settlement service in a fight: `dungeon: dienst | … | kampf`. Self-tests `ReviewAnzeige` (`test_dungeon`).

**AI Dungeon Master: master and boss in the bench:** Besides "mittel" the balance bench plays two tables (`dungeon_pruefstand.bosses`): **Master** (a master's room as the map builds it, `dungeon_karte._room_foes`) and **Lone boss** (0.8 to 1.2 budget with life factor, `BOSS_BAND`). Aim: every group size wins at least 60 % on average (`BOSS_ZIEL`). Measured against the master 70 to 92 % per group size; a lone boss exists only from level 5 to 8, winning 75 to 100 % there. Columns "Master" and "Lone boss" per level with the group's mean; Live names group sizes below the aim (log `dungeon: pruefstand | boss | N gruppen unter dem ziel`). Self-test `test_the_master_and_a_lone_boss_are_won_often_enough`.

**AI Dungeon Master: reward per hero:** 1. Experience is not divided: every hero the fight began with gets its level's budget (`dungeon_kampf.BUDGET`) times the encounter's adjusted XP over the group's reference budget (`reference`, without life share, dial, fight stage, children's table), times the share won (flight, peace and `allein` half). So a lone hero needs about as many fights per level as one of five. 2. One find per two heroes (`dungeon.funde`), with an odd count one more half the time; chest, reward and boss round up. Without a find only coins: "+N gold each" (`dungeon: beute | gegner | nur gold`); further finds go to whoever went longest without; a find the model names always comes. 3. Every hero gets the gold whole (`dungeon.coins`, range times level; `dungeon_regeln.json`: foes 1 to 3, chest 2 to 9, reward 3 to 11, boss 8 to 26); a hero earns 1.26 to 1.79 times the cost of play (aim 1.2 to 2, `dungeon_pruefstand.economy`). Self-tests `test_a_heroes_reward_does_not_hang_on_the_group` (at most 25 % apart), `test_the_economy_keeps_its_aim`.

**AI Dungeon Master: foe count by the panel, people with guards:** 1. The model names at most two kinds in `begegnung`, by `id` only (`dungeon_kampf.ARTEN`). `fill` sets the count: every kind once, then one more of the kind that brings the adjusted XP nearest the budget, until at least 0.9 and at most 1.1 budget (`BAND`), 6 foes at most, 4 of a kind; too much, the strongest goes (boss and person last). The host's fight stage counts. 2. `allein: true` brings exactly one foe for half the reward (`ALLEIN`, log `dungeon: begegnung | allein`). 3. A person fights with its occupation row's template nearest half the budget (`dungeon_personen.nearest`, `HALB`) and brings the weakest template as guards (`guards`); it comes exactly once. 4. The bench counts "mittel" with `fill`, "schwer" up to 1.5 budget, people as at the table (`people`). Self-tests `Fight.test_the_panel_counts_the_foes_into_the_band`, `test_a_person_who_fights_takes_a_fixed_template`, `test_a_group_an_encounter_and_a_fight_by_the_real_rules`.

**AI Dungeon Master: runes in the bench:** While *Runes and sockets* is on, the bench also plays every cell with runes (`dungeon_pruefstand.with_runes`): sockets as with finds, the runes of every find up to the level by the real find rule (`dungeon_runen.sockets`, `draw`, `occasions`) plus trader runes of ranks 1 to 4; each hero sets the strongest rune within the caps (`fits`), so it is the upper bound. Measured +2.1 points of wins on average (cells −5 to +13). `test_runes_win_at_most_eight_points_more` keeps the mean at +8 at most (`RUNEN_PLUS`) and every cell at 98 % at most; with runes sold at half price the economy stays in its aim. Column *with runes* on the bench page (`runen` in `dungeon-pruefstand.json`), Live "mit Runen +N Punkte", log `dungeon: pruefstand | runen | N punkte`. Table and bench use the same rules from `dungeon_runen`.

**AI Dungeon Master: invented rune:** The panel rolls runes only after the narration (`put_find`). The check "find" (`found_untold`) catches a rune in a sentence with a word of `FINDET` and none of `NEIN` ("You find a Tiwaz rune." becomes "Group: Tiwaz"): a catalog rune's name (four letters or more, never a person's name), "runeword" or the world's word in the singular (rune, chip, pearl, seal; `_rune_terms`). A rune the hero already has does not count, nor do runeword names and "socket". Then hint, counter `regel_fund`, sentence struck. Self-test `Table.test_a_rune_told_as_found_is_struck`.

**AI Dungeon Master: runes and sockets:** Part of the rule depth "Full" (*Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth*, the host chooses per round), for every round with a profile as host. 1. **Sockets:** weapons and armour up to "rare" come from finds with sockets 30 times in 100 (`it["so"]`): weapon d8 or more 3, else 2; body 3; shield and head 2; hands and feet 1 (`dungeon_runen.sockel_max`). 2. **Runes** (13, ranks 1 to 13): fight find 5, chest 15, reward 20 in 100, boss always; after 8 fight finds without one a rune is sure (`PECH`). Highest rank is the group's level + 2, low ones more often. The rune goes to the hero with the fewest, into its rune bag (`h["runen"]`, no bag place, at most 40). The trader sells ranks 1 to 4, merchants buy at half price. 3. **Setting:** in the hero sheet under *Bag* pick an own thing with a free socket; the preview names effect and new strength ("Kenaz into socket 1 of Longsword: … Strength 0 → 3.5 (at most 6.5).") or why not; only *Set* puts it in for good (`POST …/held` with `{was: "rune", tun: "pruefen"|"einsetzen", rune, i}`). Never in a fight. 4. **Effect** through the existing rules (`item_effects`, `worn_bonuses`, `life`, `resists`, `rabatt`, `dungeon_kampf.rk`, attack): weapon element, value +1 (only the strongest per value), life, armour class, protection (twice on the body), resistance, discount, healing potions +2, gold per foe. A weapon rune's to-hit only for its attacks. Line *Sockets 3: Kenaz (…) · free · free* on the thing. 5. **Caps:** at most as strong as the base in "epic" (weapon 6.5, body 6, else 3; a "rare" weapon takes no element rune), to hit at most +3, armour class +2 a thing and +3 a hero, life +8 a hero, one resistance kind and each element once a thing. 6. World words: rune, chip (science fiction), pearl (pirates), seal (horror). 7. Switch off: runes stay but do nothing and fall no more. The smith never betters a thing with runes. Live: found, set, refused, bought, sold, finds with sockets (numbers only). Log `dungeon: rune | fund | rang N`, `| pruefen | …`, `| einsetzen | ok|deckel|voll|sockel|kampf|aus`, `| kauf|verkauf | rang N`, `| sockel | N`, `| gold | N`. Self-tests `test_dungeon_runen` (every chain of up to three runes on every base: under every cap or refused).

**AI Dungeon Master: abilities when used, only the strongest bonus:** 1. An ability's bonus counts only when it always works (`immer`) or is used in the action ("I use Sweeping blow", the *Use* button; `h["einsatz"]` from `check_powers`); the tile says "when used". 2. Per value only the strongest bonus of jewel, gear and ability counts (`dungeon.worn_bonus`, `ability_bonus`, `total`); an outdone piece says "Does not count for Strength: something else gives more there, only the strongest counts." (`outdone`, `worn_view`). 3. Fixed effects (`KRAFT`): *Weapon master* +1 damage per weapon hit, *Hardy* +3 life per level (`relife`), *Laying on hands* once a scene 1d6+2 life for a friend or oneself, *Revive* once a scene a friend who is down back with 3 life (`use_abilities`, never the dead). 4. Other ability sentences promise nothing the panel does not compute (`dungeon.VERSPRECHEN`). 5. Peaceful creatures (`dungeon_bestiarium.friedlich`) are never foes, not in quests either. 6. "rare" only from level 3, "epic" from level 5, at the merchant too. 7. The bench plays as at the table: every class in turn, heroes raised level by level (`dungeon_pruefstand.raise_to`), gear of the median find's rarity (`gear_rarity`), no peaceful foes (`encounter`); from it come `dungeon_kampf.BUDGET` and `GRUPPE` (`group_factor`, beyond 5 heroes +0.33 per hero). Self-tests `test_an_ability_counts_when_used_and_only_the_strongest`, `test_abilities_the_panel_computes`, `test_an_ability_promises_only_what_the_panel_computes`, `test_peaceful_foes_are_never_offered`, `test_the_find_table_keeps_its_promises`, `test_heroes_like_at_the_table`.

**AI Dungeon Master: honest sentences of the SRD classes:** The 7 SRD classes (barbarian, bard, druid, monk, paladin, sorcerer, warlock in `app/panel/dungeon_srd.json`) promise nothing, for abilities without a fixed effect, that the panel does not compute (`dungeon.VERSPRECHEN`: numbers, damage, life, healing, targets, hits, dodging, checks), such as Dodge "Stays calm and is hard to catch."; their value bonus counts when used. The paladin's Lay on hands heals once a scene by 1d6+2 (`wirkung` `heilen`). `test_an_ability_promises_only_what_the_panel_computes` checks all 12 classes.

**AI Dungeon Master: measured combat XP per foe:** 247 foes in `app/panel/dungeon_srd.json` carry `ep_kampf`, measured on the bench (a group of four against 1 to 4 of them, 60 fights each, `ep` ÷ the ratio at 80 % wins, a quarter to four times), such as archmage 2800 instead of XP 8400. `ep` stays for display and the D&D reference; 24 foes without a measurement count with `ep`. Read through `dungeon_bestiarium.ep_kampf(g)`, `check_rules` requires a whole number within a quarter to four times `ep`. Self-test `test_dungeon_srd` (`test_kampf_ep`).

**AI Dungeon Master: English foe names as in the SRD:** The English names in `app/panel/dungeon_srd.json` follow SRD 5.1 or 5.2.1 (such as Stirge, Animated armor, Swarm of piranhas, Warg), so card, narration and foe check see the same name. Self-test `test_dungeon_srd` (`test_englische_namen_wie_im_srd`).

**AI Dungeon Master: unique spell names, everyday words marked:** No spell in `app/panel/dungeon_srd.json` is named like an ability or an item: `schildzauber` is "Shield spell", `tiersprache_d` "Animal whisper", `spottlied` "Mocking song" in English (ids stay). 26 spells and 4 abilities named by an everyday word (Darkness, Silence, Sleep, Fly …) carry `"alltagswort": true`, so "I use the darkness" gives no wrong "cannot do that yet". Self-test `test_dungeon_srd` (`test_zaubernamen_eindeutig_und_alltagswort`, `test_satz_pruefstand_zauber`).

**AI Dungeon Master: choose the gender, voice and pronoun follow:** 1. A new hero needs male, female or non-binary (`dungeon.made_hero`, else "Please pick male, female or non-binary."); bench and test heroes do not. 2. The voice list has "Female voices" and "Male voices", the own ones first (`stimmen_w`, `dmVoiceGroups`). 3. When the voice no longer fits after a change (`dungeon.voice_fits`) and was not described by the player, a fitting one comes (`voice_for`, log `dungeon: held | stimme | nach geschlecht`). 4. People with a card get the same pronoun check as heroes (`dungeon_erzaehler._figures`): "Grimma hebt seine Hand" becomes "ihre". Self-test `test_gender_is_chosen_and_the_voice_follows`.

**AI Dungeon Master: age and fixed looks per person:** Under "NPC memory and chronicle". 1. Every person gets an age once (`rd["nsc"][k]["alter"]`: kind, jung, mittel, alt; `dungeon_nsc.age`) from its looks (`ALTER_AUSSEHEN`), else by the name; with an adult occupation (any but `KIND_BERUF`) never a child. Voice, portrait and facts (`nsc_szene[].alter`) read it. 2. The gender comes from title, role or name; age and gender from the model are dropped (`dungeon: nsc | stimme | verworfen`). 3. The panel rolls hair, eyes, beard, build from `nsc_haar`, `nsc_augen`, `nsc_bart`, `nsc_statur` (`dungeon_nsc.looks`, `MERKMALE`; grey or white only when old, beard only for men from young on); the narration is checked against them (`dungeon_erzaehler.looks_off`). 4. A child never fights (`dungeon_personen.fighters`). 5. When the role names an occupation of the table, the panel sets it (`dungeon: nsc | beruf | nach rolle`), never for catalog people. 6. The catalog keeps gender, age and voice (`collect`, `adopt`); the rules page "People (NPC)" shows them. Self-test `test_one_age_and_fixed_looks_per_person`.

**AI Dungeon Master: story progress:** The stall counter, the wink in three stages and the clues to the turning point are on with the game, no switch of their own (*Features → AI Dungeon Master*, no model call; `dungeon_anleiten.kern` for `progress`, `seen`, `clue_facts`, `facts`, `WINK_TASK`, `SPUR_TASK`). While "Guide the players" is off, level Normal counts (wink after two turns without progress). Narration form, buttons, questions to quiet players, the check "vage" and the way out belong to "Guide the players" (off from the start). Live: part "Fortschritt der Geschichte" (`KERN_ZAEHLER`). Self-test `test_story_progress_is_core_and_on_from_the_start`.

**AI Dungeon Master: the panel decides:** 1. **Checks:** the model names only the level (`leicht`, `mittel`, `schwer`, `sehr_schwer`), the panel the number (`PROBE_SG`: 10, 13, 16, 19). The value must fit the fixed activity table (`dungeon_anleiten.WURF_WORTE`, climbing = Dexterity), else the table's counts (`fitting_value`, `dungeon: probe | wert | tabelle`); check XP does not depend on the level (`PROBE_EP_SG`). 2. **Loot:** a passed search or open check (`FUND_TAT`, not traps or tracks, `FUND_NICHT`) draws the find before the narration (`first_find`, fact `fund`) for the finder. A place gives one find only (`fundorte`, `dungeon: beute | ort schon`). A model name naming another thing's head word is dropped (`name_fits`). 3. **Healing and damage:** the panel always drops healing in `folgen` (`dungeon: folgen | heilung | verworfen`); damage outside a fight it rolls itself (1d4, after a hard check 1d6, `SCHADEN_W`). 4. **Act end and goal:** `akt_fertig` counts only when the narration names `WENDE_WORTE` (2) words of the turning point (`dungeon_anleiten.named`, `dungeon: akt | modell | ohne wende`); when the panel moves on by time, the next turn tells the turning point as `bruecke`. A goal gives XP only in the last act with goal words in the text (`goal_told`). Live counts all these cases. Self-tests `ReviewPanel`, `Stories.test_round_follows_the_act_model_decides_panel_limits`.

**AI Dungeon Master: people are people:** Under *NPC memory and chronicle*. 1. `dungeon_nsc.ist_person` says whether a name may be a person: not a hero, companion, foe of the fight, bestiary name (plural too), group of the truth or role word (`ROLLENWORTE`, `ROLLEN_ENDE`). It holds for truth, narration, merchant and catalog; log `dungeon: wahrheit | person | abgelehnt | <reason> | <where>` without the name. 2. The merchant's kind comes from fixed words (`HAENDLER_WORT`: „Alchemistin Mira“ sells potions), else drawn; a name that is no person gives the default name (`HAENDLER_IN`). A merchant with a name gets a card (`dungeon_nsc.merchant`, `dungeon: markt | person | neu`). A treasure is only bought (`NIE_ANGEBOT`). 3. Occupations without a fight row never fight as a „guard“ (`dungeon_personen.NICHT_KAEMPFER`); a person in a fight shows its portrait (`dungeon_kampf._picture`) and does not count in the album. Live: „today N names refused as a person, M merchants with a card“. Self-tests `ReviewPersonen`.

**AI Dungeon Master: going not first:** When others have acted this turn and all stay, the ask-back and *guide the players* (`dungeon_rueckfrage.points`) put „I look around carefully.“ before the map's way, so no button leads the group away. Self-test `Rueckfrage.test_going_is_not_offered_first_when_the_others_stay`.

**AI Dungeon Master: pronoun, place, secret, track, action:** Fixed checks of the narration. 1. **Pronoun** („fuerwort“, `ER`): „Er“, „He“ or „She“ right after a sentence about only one hero of another gender is wrong (any for divers); the repair puts the name. German „Sie“ never counts. 2. **Invented place** („ort_erfunden“, `place_untold`): a word of `ANKUNFT` with a settlement word and name (`ORTSWORT`) or a name ending in `ORT_ENDE` that neither map nor person knows, with no `ort_id`, not in a vault; the sentence is struck. 3. **Secret** („geheim“, `secret_told`): a sentence with `GEHEIM_ANTEIL` (60 %) of a secret's longer words without a passed check is struck. 4. **Track after a failed search:** the check's thing plus a word of `SPUR` or a map place counts as „probe“. 5. **Action passed over** („ignoriert“, `ignored`): when the narration names the hero but none of `TAT_WORTE` (2) words of the action, a rewrite needed anyway gets a hint; alone never a reason to fail (`NUR_HINWEIS`). Counters `regel_ort_erfunden`, `regel_geheim`, `regel_ignoriert`. States in the thing memory are not checked. Self-test `ReviewPruefung.test_pronouns_places_secrets_tracks_and_ignored_actions`.

**AI Dungeon Master: nothing invented:** Fixed checks (`dungeon_erzaehler`) with a hint and, if need be, a struck sentence. 1. **„dabei“:** hero, a word of `ZIEHT`, head noun of a catalog weapon, armour, shield or potion (`SCHUTZ_KOPF`) the hero does not carry; not when the hero's action names the thing. Gear never goes into the thing memory (`check_things`). 2. **„fund“** (`found_untold`): hero, a word of `FINDET`, a catalog thing (`FUND_ARTEN`) that is neither loot, quest find nor in the bag. 3. Gold in words (`GOLD_WORT`) counts like figures. 4. **„gegner_neu“** (`foe_untold`): a bestiary name with a word of `ERSCHEINT` that neither encounter, fight nor action has; peaceful beings, humans, people of the round and `GEGNER_MEHRDEUTIG` never count, names only as a whole word. 5. **„zeitsprung“** (`ZEITSPRUNG`): „three days later“ with no rest and no travel (`ort_id`). Ambiguous head nouns (`MEHRDEUTIG`) never count. Counters `regel_fund`, `regel_gegner_neu`, `regel_zeitsprung` under the narration rules. Self-test `ReviewPruefung.test_invented_gear_finds_foes_and_time_jumps_are_caught`.

**AI Dungeon Master: the check catches everything:** 1. `dungeon_erzaehler.faults` returns every fault of a try (`judge` the first), `hints` names all, the mildest try is kept (`milder`). 2. When every try fails, `repair_all` works through all faults (strikes first, then fixed sentences) and checks again: a missing hero gets a sentence from their roll („Silas tries: open the lock, it fails.“) or „… waits“; an `ort_id` to a place not named keeps the group (`dungeon: nachbessern | nachgeprueft | namen` or `| neuer_ort`). 3. A failed check told as done is caught and struck; the turn's loot is lost (`| beute verfallen`). 4. When a hero asks, an `npc` line with at least `ZITAT_WOERTER` words counts as an answer. Nothing to repair: `| bleibt | <fault>`. 5. Retelling after 👎: with a hard fault (`HART`) the old text stays. The narrator bench counts no day counters (`faults(…, count=False)`). Self-tests `ReviewPruefung`.

**AI Dungeon Master: life only with a reason:** `dungeon.grounded`: damage outside a fight counts only after a failed check this turn on `KOERPER_WERTE` (strength, dexterity, constitution), such as a jump, a climb, a lock that snaps; the line names „check „…“ failed“. Other damage is dropped (`dungeon: folgen | schaden | ohne grund`, counter `schaden_ohne_grund`). Healing and foes' hits stay. Self-test `LifeLossReason`.

**AI Dungeon Master: the game table stays calm:** `dmMorph` (static/js/dungeon.js) puts new HTML into the existing nodes (same tag, id, key or text start, up to `DMMORPH_VOR` (6) places ahead); only what changed is set, pictures keep their address, input, focus, open sections and scroll stay. The feed jumps to its end only when you were there or an entry comes. Browser self-test `Windows.test_the_table_stays_put_across_polls`.

**AI Dungeon Master: voices from a description visible:** Own voices per person need VoiceDesign (Einstellungen → Sprachausgabe → „VoiceDesign zusätzlich bereitstellen“); otherwise every person speaks in the fixed voice (women in the female one). While it counts as off, catalog and sample ask at most every `DESIGN_NEU_S` (30) seconds (`dungeon.design_up`). When it does not run, catalog and game master console show „Voices from a description are not running right now: every person speaks in the fixed voice“ with a jump link. A failure in play: log „dungeon: stimmen | voicedesign … | feste stimme“. Self-test in `test_dungeon_regelseite`.

**AI Dungeon Master: understanding the input:** **Ask back:** „No doing“ asks only without a verb: a subject (ich, wir, du, ihr, er, sie, a hero's name) plus a verb-like word (-e, -n, -t; any word in English) counts as doing (`dungeon_rueckfrage.subject_verb`), never `KEIN_VERB` („Ich bin müde“). `TUN_WORTE` also knows durchsuchen, rasten, bestellen, plündern, kaufen and more. An ask-back holds the turn at most `WARTE_S` (30) seconds. **Checks:** plain going, buying, selling and resting need no check (`dungeon.typed`, `dungeon: probe | verworfen | kauf`). **Typed mechanics:** buying and selling only over „⚖ Merchant“, a rest through the game master console; „Ich kaufe …“ or „Wir rasten“ gives that seat a fixed line with a button („⚖ Merchant ›“, „🎲 Game master console ›“), the narrator hears `nicht_erfolgt` and tells only the intention. „Ich gebe Grimma 10 Gold“ really takes the gold („⚖ Arvid gives Grimma · 10 gold“, `gegeben`), never more than there is; things over an errand „bringen“. **Small things:** „Heiltrank“, „Heiligtum“, „heilig“ are no help (`dungeon_zeugen.TATEN`); a verb checks only things of its kind (`dungeon.DING_ART`). **Live:** Status → Live counts typed hints and gold given. Log „dungeon: getippt | kauf | hinweis“ (verkauf, rast, gold), „dungeon: getippt | geben | gold“. Self-tests `ReviewEingabe`.

**AI Dungeon Master: recognising attacks and healing:** **Attack:** in a fight an action naming a standing foe with no word of healing, protecting, talking, looking, hiding, giving, waiting, fleeing or peace (`dungeon_kampf.SANFT_WORTE`); also „greife … an“, „stürze mich auf …“, „lasse … fliegen“, „gehe … los“ (up to 8 words), „verpasse … eins“, „packe …“. „Ork“ names the Ork-Krieger (part words from 3 letters, split at hyphens too). A check naming a foe that is no attack gives no check XP; the seat reads „No attack recognised. Write for example: "I attack Goblin."“. **Healing:** „Ich heile Bela“ (heal, tend, bandage, cure) casts a healing spell with a charge in a fixed order (`dungeon.heal_named`); without a charge a fixed line; the spell line names the healing („· Bela +7 life“). Log „dungeon: kann | heilen | zauber“, „| leer“, „| kein zauber“, „dungeon: probe | kampf | kein angriff | hinweis“. Self-test `Fight.test_review_attacks_are_recognised_and_healers_heal`.

**AI Dungeon Master: fights hold and are less deadly:** **Holding:** in a fight no merchant (`open_market`), no buying, selling or naming („First the fight …“, `markt kampf`); a scroll of knowing works. Flight rolls count before the map's step, so a successful flight can reach a neighbouring place in the same turn. **Boss:** counts with its life factor (`dungeon_kampf.wert`, `faktor`), at most one per encounter; map rooms bring their fixed number (`raum_gegner.anzahl`, at most `ANZAHL_MAX`). **All down:** when every hero is down and one lay already, the fight ends in mercy: each wakes with 1 life, loses half its gold and one thing, no XP (`dungeon.down`, „dungeon: zustand | gnade | gruppe | n“). While others still fight, death stays possible. **Stable:** three successful rescue rolls = „stable but unconscious“; healing gets it up, with no fight the hero wakes with 1 life; a 20 gets it up at once. **Breather:** after every win a quarter of its life for each hero standing (`dungeon.breather`, `VERSCHNAUFEN`). **Follow-up fights:** the budget grows with the group's life share (at least `ANTEIL_MIN` 0.4), the danger display too. Live counts stable heroes and breathers. Self-tests `Permadeath.test_the_whole_group_down_is_spared_and_a_won_fight_gives_breath`, `test_rescue_rolls_get_a_hero_up`, `Fight.test_a_fight_shuts_the_market_and_a_boss_counts_with_its_life`, `test_kampf_flucht_nur_mit_mehrheit_und_panelprobe`.

**AI Dungeon Master: nothing breaks:** **Prompt:** the style examples stand at the task's end between „[Examples]“ and „[End of the examples]“ (`dungeon_erzaehler.STIL_BLOCK`), so no rule counts as an example. **Word lists:** harmless words holding a list word („knackt“, „Blutegel“, „Schlachtross“ …) are taken out before the check by `dungeon.HARMLOS` (`dungeon.word_hit` for round, hero name, catalog); other word parts still count („splitternackt“). **Outage brake:** without a usable narration a fixed sentence stands in the log as a system line, never as narration (`dungeon.outage`); the next try after `DM_S` (75) seconds at the earliest, after `AUSFALL_HALT` (2) outages the turn waits with „The Dungeon Master cannot get through right now …“ for the host's "Go on now". **Children's table:** a refused word that can be softened (töten, ermorden, enthaupten, abschlachten, umbringen) becomes „Ich besiege den Goblin.“, the seat reads once „Here foes are defeated, not killed. Your action reads:“ (`dungeon_rueckfrage.milder`, never blocking); what cannot be softened stays refused. „Besiegen“ is an attack (`dungeon_kampf.ANGRIFF_WORTE`). Live counts waiting turns and softened actions. Log „dungeon: ausfall | neu | 1“, „| halt | 2“, „dungeon: aktion | wortliste | milder“, „dungeon: rueckfrage | milder“. Self-tests `ReviewSofort`.

**AI Dungeon Master: one gender per person:** Under *NPC memory and chronicle*. The gender is fixed once on the card (`dungeon_stimmen.fix`, `rd["nsc"][…]["geschlecht"]`: weiblich, maennlich, divers), from title or role („Schattenwächterin“), else from the name. Portrait (`dungeon_gesicht.who`), voice, occupation word (`m`/`w` of `nsc_beruf`, `dungeon_nsc.beruf_wort`) and the narrator's pronouns (`geschlecht` in `nsc_szene`) follow it. When name or role names an occupation („Schattenwächter“ → guard), that one counts (`dungeon_nsc.beruf_of`).

**AI Dungeon Master: persuade to come along:** Part of the rule depth "Full" (*Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth*, the host chooses per round; needs a map). Without it „come along“ takes a person of the map along at once. With it the panel decides (`dungeon_mitkommen.py`, question through `dungeon_karte.fragen`): not at the place, without a card or attitude below +1 stays without a check; „devoted“ (+3) comes without a check; in between the panel's Charisma check (friendly 13, warm 11). The model only writes the person's sentence (fact `mitkommen`). The map takes the person along (`dungeon_karte.mitnehmen`), feed „Grimma comes along with you.“ or „Grimma stays here.“, the chronicle notes it. Those who come travel and speak but do not fight like companions. Log „dungeon: mitkommen | probe/ja/nein/abgelehnt/treu/weg“; Live counts questions, those who came, those who stayed.

**AI Dungeon Master: going to a neighbouring place:** When an action names going and exactly one neighbouring place by name or direction („go to the old library“), it is a walk (`dungeon_karte.asked_ways`): the model's check goes (unless a risky word like sneak, climb), the facts name it (`karte.gewollt`); when the model tells elsewhere, the check „gehen“ sends it back once, then the panel takes the way itself („You set off, your destination: …“). A fight or new foes hold the group. Under *Spieler anleiten* „vague“ wants at least one point from the panel's data, „What you can do here“ shows up to three ways. The dice card shows the receipt number („W-2“) only as a tooltip with real dice.

**AI Dungeon Master: wandering people and small gestures:** Part of the rule depth "Full" (*Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth*, the host chooses per round). Wandering: after a long rest (`dungeon_rast.rest`, `dungeon_wandern.rest`, `dungeon_karte.wandern(…, "lange_rast")`) every person outside the group moves one way on with `WANDERN` percent towards `dungeon_nsc.ziel_ort` (a place of its goals, else by `BERUF_ORT`). Every move stands as a rumour in the feed, the next narration gets them (`gewandert`) and tells only a meeting again. Not between chapters. Gestures: `dungeon_nsc.geste(rd, rng)` picks a person of the scene with a trait of ±2 and a fixed line from `GESTEN`, without the model; `dungeon_warten` decides when (never in the car). Logs `dungeon: wandern | 1 personen`, `dungeon: karte | wandern | lange_rast | 1`, `dungeon: nsc | geste`. Live: long rests, people moved, gestures.

**AI Dungeon Master: when a small gesture comes:** `dungeon_warten.geste_faellig` (from `dungeon.maybe_close`): the turn is open, nothing at the table for 2 minutes (`GESTE_S`), no fight, no pause, the narrator silent, no dice question open, at most 2 gestures a turn (`GESTE_ZUG`). Then `dungeon_warten.geste` writes a line of kind "geste" (🤲, fields `nsc`, `geste`, `text` de and en). If nobody fits, the next try after another 2 minutes. Never while a seat plays in the car (`dungeon_auto.im_auto`). An error becomes a journal line, never a break. Live: gestures, nobody fitting, left out for the car; log `dungeon: geste | gezeigt`, `| keine person`, `| auto`, `| fehler | kind`. Self-tests `test_dungeon_gesten`.

**AI Dungeon Master: errands from people:** Part of the rule depth "Full" (*Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth*, the host chooses per round). `dungeon_auftraege.seen` reads actions with fixed word lists (`ARBEIT`, `GEBEN`, `ZURUECK`) and the person of the scene named. A new errand (person not hostile, no open errand, at most `OFFEN_MAX` = 3) comes from `roll`: kind from `ARTEN` (bring a thing, talk to a person, defeat a foe up to the group's level), reward `LOHN` times the group's level, `EP` for everyone, two clues from fixed data, the reason is the person's wish. Done only with a receipt: thing given and carried (it goes), other person in the scene addressed, foe felled this turn. Giving it back cools the attitude one step. The narration gets `auftraege` and `auftrag_jetzt` and only tells reason and thanks; the panel gives and closes (`auftraege.apply`: gold, XP `ep_tisch`, attitude +1, feed, chronicle). Every seat sees the errands at the table's side, in the apps too. Logs `dungeon: auftraege | neu | bringen`, `| erfuellt | reden`, `| zurueck`, `| kein neuer`. Live: given, done, given back.

**AI Dungeon Master: read thoughts:** Part of the rule depth "Full" (*Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth*, the host chooses per round). `dungeon_gedanken.checks`: when an action casts `gedanken_lesen` (`kraft` from `check_powers`) or the hero has the blessing of the spell or `gedankentrank` (`QUELLEN`, `dungeon.bless`) plus a word of `LESEN`, and it names a person of the scene, the panel rolls an Intelligence check (`SG`: hostile 15, neutral 13, fond 11). The narration tells only the listening, never the content, so the secret cannot reach the table through the model. Then `gedanken.apply` writes a fixed line from the card (wish, secret, attitude) into `rd["gedanken"][pid]`, failed "… shuts their thoughts away from you.". Only this seat sees it (`gedanken.view`), the host only who read whose thoughts; secret `gedanken` in `dungeon_sicht.GEHEIM`. Never read aloud in the car. Logs `dungeon: gedanken | probe`, `| gelesen`, `| verschlossen`, `| kein ziel`. Live: tries, read, shut away.

**AI Dungeon Master: witness rule:** Part of the rule depth "Full" (*Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth*, the host chooses per round). `dungeon_zeugen.seen` recognises attack (`dungeon_kampf.intent`), theft, gift, help (`TATEN`) towards a person of the scene. Table `FOLGE`: attack and theft target −2, everyone present −1; gift and help target +1. A theft with a passed Dexterity check stays unseen; an attack on a fight's foe is no deed. The narration gets `zeugen` and tells only the reaction; `zeugen.apply` sets the attitude (once per pair and turn, −3 to +3), the feed shows every change, the chronicle "in front of witnesses"; the model's own report for the same pair is dropped (`zeugen.drop`). Logs `dungeon: zeugen | diebstahl | 1 zeugen`, `dungeon: zeugen | diebstahl | unbemerkt`. Live: deeds, witnesses, thefts unseen.

**AI Dungeon Master: guide the players, check "vage", stall hint, way out and clues:** On with the game, no switch of its own (*Features → AI Dungeon Master*). **"vage"** (`dungeon_anleiten.vague` in `erz.judge`): fewer than two points of the scene (people, things, foes, props, ways; fewer when the scene has fewer) or an empty closing question of `HOHL` ("What do you do?") has it told once again with a hint naming up to three names (`erz.repair`, "nachbessern | bleibt | vage"). 👎 can carry the reason "Did not know what we could do" (`dungeon_erzaehlstand.GRUENDE`). Bench scene "Sackgasse". Logs `dungeon: anleitung | vage | zu wenig`, `| vage | hohl`. **Stall hint** (`anleiten.progress`): progress = a new place, a thread new or closed, a new thing, an act done, a fight begins or ends. Without progress after `WINK` turns (Beginner 1, Normal 2, Experienced never) stage 1 (`wink` 1: show something tangible), then stage 2 (an event of the table `stillstand`, mild at the children's table, rolled by the panel), then a fixed line "Tipp: Ihr könnt mit Grimma sprechen oder nach Norden gehen."; then from the start. **Way out:** after a failed knowledge check the buttons start with another way to the same goal. Logs `dungeon: anleitung | wink | 2`, `| ausweg | 1`. **Clues:** every act has up to three clues to the turning point (`spuren`, 5 to 100 characters, `check_plan`; planner field "Clues to the turning point", the 50 shipped stories two each). The next clue not shown is in the facts (`anleiten.clue_facts`); when the narration names most of its words it counts as shown (`anleiten.seen`, `rd["spuren"]`) and as progress. Clues not shown are secret, a way naming one is dropped. The host sees ✓ or 🔎. Log `dungeon: anleitung | spur | 1/2`. Live: vague, hints per stage, ways out, clues.

**AI Dungeon Master: guide the players, narration form and buttons:** On with the game, no switch of its own (*Features → AI Dungeon Master*). **Narration form** (`dungeon_anleiten.py`): first the deeds' effects, then what is to be seen, at least two named points (person, thing, way) from facts or narration, at the end a concrete choice for one figure, never "What do you do?"; a model narration per world (`welten[].anleiten`). Whoever is silent for `STILL` turns gets a question from the scene's data, never the model's words ("Bela, do you talk to Grimma, or do you go north?"). **Buttons** "What you can do here" for every seat without an action, from data only (`anleiten.buttons`): the narrator's checked ways, points from `dungeon_rueckfrage.points`, healing below half life, a thing, an open thread; a tap fills the action field. From the model (`wege`) the panel takes at most `MODELL_WEGE` = 2 short ways with a doing word, no refused word and no knowledge of later acts, the turning point or secrets (`anleiten.check_ways`). **Level** per round in the game master console under Narrator (`erzaehler` with `anleitung`): Beginner four buttons with the likely value (`WURF`), read aloud in the car; Normal three (default); Experienced none. In the car: "What can I do?", the CarPlay list "What you can do here", "the first". Logs `dungeon: anleitung | frage | 2`, `| frage | zu wenig`, `| zeile | 2`, `| knopf genommen`, `| weg | 2`, `| weg verworfen | geheim 1`, `| stufe | einsteiger`. Live: questions, lines, buttons taken, ways.

**AI Dungeon Master: time of day by the panel:** On with the game, no switch of its own (*Features → AI Dungeon Master*; idea from LlamaTale, LGPL-3.0, own code). The panel sets the time of day (`dungeon_tageszeit.py`): an even half calendar day is morning and day, an odd one evening and night, `ZUEGE` = 6 turns each; after twelve turns the calendar runs half a day on (`kalender.weiter` "zeit"), silently if need be. A long rest and the inn end at the next morning. At night: smith, lesson, improving, healer and merchant shut (`tageszeit.LADEN_ZU`, "🌙 Shut at night"), inn, rumour, companions open; outdoors (not vault, not settlement) checks at a disadvantage without darkvision and without a light in the group; night foes preferred (`nacht: true`). The narrator gets the time of day and after a change `tageszeit_neu`; its `szene.tageszeit` does not count. Display "🌇 Evening · 4 turns left". Logs `dungeon: tageszeit | nacht | panel`, `| zu | lehre`, `| dunkel`. Live: changes, dark checks, shops shut. Without the switch the narrator reports the time of day (only forward).

**AI Dungeon Master: night foes in the SRD catalog:** 76 foes in `app/panel/dungeon_srd.json` carry `"nacht": true` (undead, lycanthropes, wolves, spiders, light-shy peoples, nightmare, plus bandits and spies and own night creatures of horror, sci-fi and pirates), on every level from 0 to 15. In every world at least 5 fit the budget of one hero of level 1 to 3. Self-test `test_dungeon_srd` (`test_nacht_gegner_je_welt_und_stufe`).

**AI Dungeon Master: runes and runewords in the catalog:** In play they work under "Runes and sockets". `app/panel/dungeon_srd.json` carries 13 runes (`runen`, rank 1 to 13, historical names Uruz to Tiwaz) with meaning, price (20 to 1500 gold) and effect per place `waffe`, `ruestung`, `schild` (`null` = not possible) and 7 runewords (`runenwoerter`, such as Ember, Daybreak) with a fixed rune order, place (`waffe`, `koerper`, `nebenhand`, `kopf`), number of sockets, minimum level and extra effect. Allowed keys: `treffer`, `schaden` (1 to 2, weapon only), `effekt`, `werte` with `bonus` 1, `leben`, `widerstand`, `rabatt`, `gold`, `heiltrank`, `rk`, `schutz`. `app/panel/dungeon_runen.py` checks both in `dungeon.check_rules` (ids, ranks without a gap, prices up to "epic", runewords possible, sockets = number of runes, no sequence twice). Self-test `test_dungeon_srd` (`test_runen_und_runenwoerter`).

**AI Dungeon Master: occupations in both forms, looks only outward:** Each of the 61 occupations in `nsc_beruf` names besides `t` the forms `m` and `w` (Heiler/Heilerin, Bergmann/Bergfrau …), fitting the fixed gender; "Bord-KI" (ship AI) is no person's occupation. `nsc_aussehen` describes only the outside, behaviour stands in `nsc_art` (absent-minded, nervous, eccentric …). `dungeon_tabellen.check_rules` requires `m` and `w` only on occupations (`FORMEN`) and refuses in "Looks" the words of `AUSSEHEN_NICHT`. Self-test `test_dungeon_srd` (`test_berufe_mit_formen_und_aussehen_nur_aeusseres`).

**AI Dungeon Master: what a foe is, companions with gender and age, fixed looks:** Each of the 268 foes in `app/panel/dungeon_srd.json` carries `wesen`: `mensch`, `humanoid`, `riese`, `tier`, `untot`, `konstrukt`, `drache`, `unhold`, `elementar`, `fee`, `himmlisch`, `pflanze`, `schleim`, `aberration` or `monstrum` – the one source for it (`app/panel/dungeon_bestiarium.py`: `WESEN`, `wesen(g)`, check in `dungeon.check_rules`; an admin's own foes may leave it out). Companions (`ideen.gefaehrten`, `gefaehrten_kinder`) carry `geschlecht`, `alter` (jung, mittel, alt; never a child) and `art` (mensch, tier, maschine), required by `dungeon_ideen.check_rules`. Look tables `nsc_haar` (15), `nsc_augen` (7), `nsc_bart` (10, men from young on only) and `nsc_statur` (12); hair, eyes and beard name their colour as a fixed word (`farbe`: rot, braun, schwarz, grau, weiss, blau, gruen), so the look check recognises it (`FARBE`, allowed only there). Five foes are `"friedlich": true` (commoner, unicorn, couatl, young silver dragon, giant seahorse) and never foes (`dungeon_bestiarium.friedlich`). Self-test `test_dungeon_srd` (`test_wesen_und_gefaehrten`).

**AI Dungeon Master: the foes' saving throws:** 1. Spells with a saving throw (`rettung`, 44, such as Fireball, Sleep): in a fight the panel rolls for the foe d20 + its bestiary value bonus against 10 + proficiency bonus + the caster's value + the spell's bonus above the blessing (`kampf.ZAUBER_SG`, `kampf.spell_save`; 10 instead of D&D's 8, since a save knows no crit and no advantage); a 20 always passes, a 1 never. Failed: full damage and the condition (`zustand`) for its turns; passed: with `halb` half damage, else nothing. Log `dungeon: kampf | rettung gegner | geschafft / nicht geschafft`, row "saving throw Wisdom = 3 vs DC 15 · failed". 2. Banishing spells without damage, with a save and a condition (`kampf.bann`, 13: Sleep, Entangle, Hold Person, Command, Fear …) act on a foe and cost a charge, with no blessing for the caster; control spells without a save (Counterspell, Bane, Slow, Polymorph, Confusion) do not work in a fight and cost nothing. 3. Spells with the target "verbuendeter" (10, such as Healing Word) act on the hero named exactly (`dungeon.friend`), else on the caster, never on the dead or unconscious. Rules page: "Foe's saving throw", "Condition on the foe". 4. When a foe's hit causes a condition, the hero rolls against the bestiary's `sg` (10 to 19), without one against 10 + a quarter of the foe's level.

**AI Dungeon Master: scrolls, order and targets in a fight:** 1. A damage scroll (Fireball, Ice Lance, Chain Lightning) makes the next attack in this or the next turn that spell, once (`h["rolle"]`, logs `dungeon: rolle | bereit`, `dungeon: kampf | rolle | angriff`); unused it lapses. 2. First all heroes act, then all foes, no initiative; healing and blessings (`cast_powers`) come before the hits (`consequences`). 3. Several fitting foes: whole name, then kind (end of the name: "Ratte" is the giant rat), then start; among the same kind the one with the least life. 4. Experience: see "reward per hero". 5. Milder than 5e on purpose: no "massive damage", damage while down is no failed death save; foes do not attack heroes on the ground.

**AI Dungeon Master: saving throws, conditions and DCs in the SRD catalog:** Data in `app/panel/dungeon_srd.json` following SRD 5.2.1 (CC-BY-4.0). 1. 18 damageless spells against foes carry `rettung: {"wert": …}`, 13 of them a `zustand` (Sleep unconscious, Hold person paralysed, Fear frightened, Command prone …); Bane, Slow, Confusion, Polymorph, Faerie fire only the save. 2. 22 damage spells carry `rettung`, 17 of them `"halb": true`; without `halb` (Sacred flame, Acid splash …) a success means no damage; spells with an attack roll and smites have no save. 3. Revivify, Raise dead, Cure wounds, Healing word, Restoration, Death ward, Remove curse, Heroism carry `ziel: verbuendeter` (`dungeon.ZIELE`). 4. Each of the 268 foes carries `sg`: 89 from the SRD stat block, 179 by 8 + proficiency bonus + best modifier (mental conditions with Int, Wis, Cha). 5. Guard: `schaden_art: stich`. Self-test `test_dungeon_srd` (`test_rettung_zustand_halb_und_sg`).

**AI Dungeon Master: blessings, armour and flight:** 1. Per value only the highest blessing counts (`dungeon.blessing`); the same blessing again only renews its turns. Concentration: a new blessing spell ends the same caster's last blessing on everyone (`dungeon._cast`, `dungeon: segen | konzentration | N beendet`); others' blessings, potions and altars stay. In a spell attack the spell's bonus counts only above the blessing present. 2. `dungeon.body` gives the body's value: score bonus and blessing less a wound, if asked with worn items (never weapons), without abilities and lessons (those count only for checks, `dungeon.total`). Armour class: Dexterity with items up to the armour's cap, a blessing on Dexterity beyond it too. Damage: score bonus and blessing without item values. 3. Flight: fixed words (`kampf.FLUCHT_WORTE`) make an action a flight, an attack word wins. When more than half of those able to act flee, each rolls the panel's Dexterity check against max(10, 8 + the strongest foe's level) (`FLUCHT_SG`, `kampf.escape`); if at least half pass, the fight ends with "flucht" (`kampf | flucht | entkommen / gestellt`). Else they stay words (`| keine mehrheit`); the model's `kampf_ende` "flucht" alone ends nothing (`| ohne probe`). No check XP.

**AI Dungeon Master: combat word boundaries:** 1. Every word in `NICHT_ANGRIFF` ends at a word boundary, "Ich greife Nachtmahr an" hits exactly that foe; a self-test attacks every bestiary foe without an article (eight German, five English verbs). 2. Attacks also include "schlage dich nieder", "trete ein paar Mal nach", "Gebt Feuer auf die Wache"; no attacks: "Ich haue ab", "schlage Alarm", "trete die Tür ein", "Ich trete ein", "I hit the road", "I strike a deal". 3. The panel drops checks for heroes at 0 life, unconscious or dead (`dungeon: probe | verworfen | zustand`), no check XP.

**AI Dungeon Master: attack, peace and bench in a fight:** 1. `NICHT_ANGRIFF` also knows "trete zur Seite / zurück …", "schlage vor", "werfe der Wache einen Blick zu", "step back", "retreat", "suggest" and more; "Ich trete die Wache" stays an attack. 2. Only the action's words decide whether it attacks; the model's `ziel` only picks the foe of a recognised attack. 3. Peace: one Charisma check per fight (`kampf.frieden`, else `dungeon: kampf | frieden | schon versucht`), no check EP, success gives half the fight EP. 4. With a spell from `KONTROLLE` the panel drops the model's check (`dungeon: probe | kampf | kontrolle verworfen`). 5. Whoever is down, unconscious or dead never attacks (`attacks` checks `zustand`). 6. The bench attacks with an attack action and starts every turn without old actions. Self-tests `Fight.test_kampf_angriffswoerter_tabelle`, `test_kampf_modellziel_allein_kein_angriff`, `test_kampf_frieden_einmal_je_kampf_ohne_proben_ep`.

**AI Dungeon Master: telling richly:** The task names a minimum length per way of telling (`dungeon.WEISE_MIN`: brief none, normal 500, rich 1000 characters, always 300 below the upper limit, `erz_min`) and a style sentence (`WEISE_STIL`: rich several paragraphs with senses and a moment per hero, brief a few clear sentences). For "rich" a narration too short is told once again (`dungeon_erzaehler.judge`, fault `knapp`, log `dungeon: nachbessern | knapp`, `| bleibt | knapp`). Self-test `Fight.test_longer_at_the_moments_and_the_rounds_way_of_telling`.

**AI Dungeon Master: heroes comment:** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Second check by the model* (`chat.dungeon_pruefung`, off from the start; `dungeon_kommentar.py`). In `helden_sagen` the narrator gives every acting hero a short line in its own words about its deed. Fixed check: only heroes who acted (not ooc, not down), one line per hero, up to 120 characters, no number, no word-list word, no first-person deed, no deciding word. Shown as "💬 name" under the narration (`helden` in the log), read aloud in the hero's voice after the narration, after the players' quotes. What a hero does only its player writes. In the app too. Log `dungeon: kommentar | N gesagt | M verworfen`. Self-test `Turns.test_heroes_comment_on_their_own_deeds`.

**AI Dungeon Master: fixed checks of the narration, each with a rescue:** After every turn the panel checks with fixed word lists, never the model, besides names, checks, foes, receipts, veils, places, things and repetition also: fallen foes do not act, standing ones do not die without a hit (`gefallen`); what the character does not have or cannot do does not work (“… does not carry that”, `fehlt`); only the panel sets amounts of gold and loot (`gold`); who did not act decides nothing (`fremd`); hair, eye, beard colour follow the NPC card (`aussehen`); the time of day only goes forward (“tageszeit | verworfen”). On a fault: a new try with a hint; when the last fails too, the panel strikes sentences, takes speech from people elsewhere or sets fixed sentences (“waits to see”). Log “nachbessern | fest” or “| bleibt”; Live counts rescues, missing things, dropped times of day.

**AI Dungeon Master: game master console as cards:** On top “▶ Go on now” with one chip per hero (✓ acted). Below four cards: “Turn” (pause, market, behind “⋯” end chapter or round, asking again), “At the table” (read aloud, scene picture, invite, switch “Last sentence in the mail”), “Narrator” (way of telling brief, normal, rich; voice with sample and change), “Difficulty” (combat stage easy to deadly, game master dial “hold” with a danger bar). At the bottom “Only for you” with tabs story, truth, things, random rules, oracle. On the phone one below the other. Every button sends a request the panel checks. The D&D idea tables under “ideen” in `dungeon_srd.json` are checked by `dungeon_ideen.check_rules`: critical tables, fumbles (d12, states from the list, 1 to 3 turns), landscapes with night watches (d20) and travel events, question cards, travel roles, scars (d20, at most −1) and the saying per foe (`sage`).

**AI Dungeon Master: tables of the D&D ideas:** Under "ideen" in `dungeon_srd.json`, checked by `dungeon_ideen.check_rules`, all our own texts (templates darker-dungeons CC BY-NC-ND, VoloBot, rpg-card only as ideas). **Critical:** 12 rows per damage kind (d12), 1 to 4 narration only, else a condition for 1 to 3 turns (incapacitated, paralysed, stunned, unconscious only 1). **Fumbles:** melee, ranged, spell, 12 mild rows each, a condition only for the fumbler. **Landscapes:** 13 (fallback "weg", "huegel", "gras") with night watches d20 and 8 travel events. **Campfire:** 12 general questions and 12 per world. **Travel roles:** scout, hunter, watch, pathfinder. **Scars:** d20, 1 to 4 with −1 on a value until a healer; at the children's table only plasters; 8 tavern lines. **Legend:** every one of the 268 foes, at most 200 characters per language, for the monster album. Self-tests `test_jeder_gegner_hat_eine_sage`, `test_ideen_tabellen_und_sagen` (no children's-tone word).

**AI Dungeon Master: the table on the phone:** Head in one row (title, turn, 🔊, “⋯”); under “⋯” “← Rounds”, “How to play”, light/dark, “Full screen”, music and sounds, for the host the game master console; the table uses the whole width. Fight as a box with a row per foe, the encounter as a line, a tap shows every value. The action field stands in Story, Group, Hero and Map above the bottom bar with “● Your turn”. Group as a list with life bars; the “Hero” pane with armour class, row “Quick” (potion, abilities, spells, weapon; the panel checks the sentence as if typed) and bag. The guide starts as a card “How it works”. In the iPhone app there is no “Full screen”, guests without a profile see no Spark row. A failed knowledge check gives nothing away: when the narration still names a direction, trail or find, it is retold or struck. Plain going has no check. Repeated scene sentences are struck by the panel. Security: display only, every action through the panel's checks; “&app=1” only changes the view.

**AI Dungeon Master: factions with clocks:** Part of the rule depth "Full" (*Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth*, the host chooses per round; `dungeon_fraktionen.py`). Every group of the truth (`wahrheit.gruppen`) becomes a faction with a clock of 4, 6 or 8 fields, at most `FRAKTIONEN_MAX` (6). After every turn a d6 per clock, from `UHR_WURF` (5) one field. The narration reports up to 2 groups in `fraktionen` (`hilft`/`stoert`); the panel turns only when the name is in the text, once per group and turn. Full clock = goal reached: feed (and chronicle), `fraktion_voll` once to the narration, the group holds its place on the map (`dungeon_fraktionen.occupied`). **Visibility:** "⏳ Factions" shows the clocks of named groups, never goals; the host sees all with goals (`fraktionen_geheim`, `dungeon_sicht.NUR_GASTGEBER`, secret "fraktion_ziel"), adds, changes, turns (−/+) or removes (`POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/fraktion` with `name`, `will`, `felder`, `schritt`, `weg`; host only, fixed values). Factions go on into the next chapter. **Live:** Status → Live counts factions, full clocks, fields turned. Self-test `test_factions_with_clocks_turned_by_fixed_rules`.

**AI Dungeon Master: combat stages easy to deadly:** A tool of the game master console, no switch of its own (*Features → AI Dungeon Master*; `dungeon_kampf.STUFEN`). The host picks at "⚔ Combat stage" Easy, Medium, Hard or Deadly (`POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/erzaehler` with `kampfstufe`, host only, list value only). Medium = the measured `BUDGET` (70 to 90 wins in 100); Easy and Hard by the ratio of the SRD 5.2.1 table (`budget`, CC-BY-4.0) averaged over the heroes' levels; Deadly = Hard times `TOEDLICH` (1.5); without the table `STUFE_OHNE` (0.67 and 1.4). Works from the next encounter with the children's table and the dial; the danger shown measures against Medium. `dungeon_kampf.check_rules` checks the table. Live counts rounds with an own stage. Self-test `test_combat_stages_by_the_srd_table`.

**AI Dungeon Master: foes, spells and combat budget from the SRD 5.2.1:** The catalog holds 44 foes (such as goblin warrior, pegasus, werebear, young dragons, giants, roc, pirate, scout, guard captain) and 30 spells (such as cure wounds, guiding bolt, moonbeam) from the SRD 5.2.1 (CC-BY-4.0), with damage type, resistance, immunity, boss and multiattack. 21 of them that fight harder than their challenge rating have `ep` one to three steps higher, so "medium" stays at 70 to 90 % wins (`test_the_aim_at_medium_for_every_level_and_group`). The table `budget` comes from the SRD 5.2.1. **Children's tone:** no name or text in the catalog holds a word of the children's tone list (hence e.g. "giant gnat", "Dragon's ember", condition "wounded"). Self-tests `test_budget_aus_dem_srd_521`, `test_kinderton_im_katalog`. Attribution: *This work includes material from the System Reference Document 5.2.1 ("SRD 5.2.1") by Wizards of the Coast LLC, available at https://www.dndbeyond.com/srd. The SRD 5.2.1 is licensed under the Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 International License, available at https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/legalcode.*

**AI Dungeon Master: NPC goals:** On with the game, no switch of its own (*Features → AI Dungeon Master*, `dungeon_nsc.py`; idea from NarrativeEngine-P, MIT). Every person has six traits (`WESEN`: brave/fearful, honest/sly, kind/gruff, generous/stingy, calm/hot-headed, loyal/fickle; each 2d4−5, −3 to +3) and goals on three levels (`EBENEN`): today (2 steps), soon (4, from the truth or the wish table), in life (6). After every turn a d6 per person: from `ZIEL_WURF` (5) one step, brave (2 or more) from `ZIEL_MUTIG` (4). Reached: the chronicle says only "Oskar has reached a goal.", the content only as data (`nsc_ziel_erreicht`), then a new goal for today. The model gets traits as words and the open goals; only the host sees them in "👥 People and chronicle" (`nsc_ziele` host and model only). Live counts steps and goals reached. Self-test `test_npc_traits_and_goals_move_between_turns`.

**AI Dungeon Master: NPC memory, chronicle and reputation:** On with the game, no switch of its own (*Features → AI Dungeon Master*; `dungeon_nsc.py`, `dungeon_tabellen.py`; ideas from loreweaver and NarrativeEngine-P, MIT). Every person of the truth gets a rolled card (looks, manner, occupation, wish, secret), an attitude per hero (−3 hostile to +3 devoted) and up to `KANTEN_MAX` (4) relations (owes, fears, likes, knows about). The model only reports changes (`nsc`, at most `AENDERUNGEN_MAX` = 3); an attitude counts only when person and hero are in the text, one step per hero and turn. Feed: "🤝 Oskar is now friendly towards Arvid."; relations only the host sees. Only the scene's people go to the model in full (`nsc_szene`: at the place, travelling along or named in the last `SZENE_ZUEGE` (2) turns), others by name only (`nsc_fern`), plus `NAMEN_VORSCHLAG` (3) free names (`freie_namen`). **Chronicle:** fixed events (meetings, wins, fallen, levels, threads, acts, chapter end, attitude ±3), at most `CHRONIK_MAX` (40) lines, the last `CHRONIK_FAKTEN` (6) for the model. **Reputation** = mean attitude (notorious to celebrated). Everything goes on into the next chapter. "👥 People and chronicle" shows people met, reputation and chronicle; the host also wish, secret, relations and those not met (`nsc_geheim`, host and model only). Live counts cards, chronicle lines, changes. Self-tests `test_npc_memory_cards_attitudes_and_chronicle`, `test_every_secret_stays_with_its_viewers`.

**AI Dungeon Master: the same face for NPCs:** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Pictures and hero song* (`chat.dungeon_bilder`, off from the start; `dungeon_gesicht.py`; idea from Project_Infinity, MIT); works only with "NPC memory and chronicle" and the host's "Painting pictures". Every person met with a card gets a portrait once, painted by the minute loop (`dungeon_dingbild.due`) within the daily limit, the voice comes first. The prompt has only fixed parts (world style, age and gender from `dungeon_stimmen`, the card, fixed lists), never model text or the name (`dungeon.picture_text`); whoever has not spoken waits `WARTE_ZUEGE` (2) turns. Scene pictures (`dungeon.queue_scene`) take up to `VORLAGEN` (2) portraits as references (`bilder.paint` with `refs`): first people of the last narration, then an acting hero; a little slower on tars. "👥 People and chronicle" shows the faces, the feed small next to speech lines. "↻ Paint the face again" (`POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/gesicht` with `name`, host only, rate limit `dungeonbild`, daily limit); the old picture stays until the new one. The portrait goes on into the next chapter (`rd["nsc"][name]["gesicht"]`), orphans go with the hourly sweep. Fetch (`nsc-<hash>`) only for seats of a round that names it (`dungeon_gesicht.may_see`). The apps show it through the panel view. Live: faces, painted, not come, scenes with a reference. Logs `dungeon: gesicht | ok | 41000 ms`, `| abgelehnt | tag`, `| neu malen`, `| aufgeraeumt | 2 bilder`. Self-tests `test_dungeon_gesicht`.

**AI Dungeon Master: arms for group and factions:** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Pictures and hero song* (`chat.dungeon_bilder`, off from the start; `dungeon_wappen.py`, `dungeonwappen.js`). The panel draws every coat of arms itself as SVG without a picture model from a start value: shield shape (5), division (10), two tinctures by the rule metal next to colour, a charge of 16 own drawings. The group's arms hang on the campaign (else the round); the host chooses in the "Factions" window through "Choose colour and charge" ("?" = chance). Kept in `users/<id>/dungeon-wappen.json` (at most 50, orphans go with the hourly sweep). Every faction has arms from its name. **Security:** codes only from fixed lists; `svg(code)` builds and checks (`check`, `defusedxml`): only `ERLAUBT`, no scripts, links, `style`, only internal references; shown only as a picture with a `data:` address. **Visibility:** every seat gets in `wappen` the group's arms and the factions it sees, the host all and the choice. Helpers `gruppe(rd)`, `fraktion(rd, name)`, `svg(code)`, in the page `dmWappen`, `dmWappenFr`, `dmWappenGruppe`, `dmWappenHref`. Endpoint `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/wappen` (login, `browser_profile` with CSRF, size limit `dungeon.BODY`, rate limit `dungeon`, host of a running round only). Logs `dungeon: wappen | gewaehlt | eigen/zufall`, `| abgelehnt | aus/wert`, `| aufgeraeumt | N wahlen`; Live: rounds with arms, chosen, refused. **Licence:** structure after Azgaar/Armoria (MIT, https://github.com/Azgaar/Armoria); code and drawings our own, nothing from WappenWiki (CC BY-NC). Self-tests `test_dungeon_wappen`.

**AI Dungeon Master: NPC tables, name syllables and oracle in the catalog (data):** 15 fixed tables (`tabellen`) give randomness by fixed rules: for side characters looks, manner, wish, secret and occupation, for the oracle action, theme, place, descriptor, plot twist, complication, clue, peril, opportunity and settlement trouble. Rows can apply to single worlds only (eye patch for pirates, implants for sci-fi); each table has at least 6 rows per world. Name syllables (`namensilben`, not `silben`: those are the map's place names) per people and for sci-fi and pirates build names from start, middle and end (method like Syllabore, own syllables). The oracle rows and many NPC rows are translated from Datasworn, only from tables under CC-BY-4.0, reworded to be family-friendly. Attribution: *This work is based on Ironsworn, Ironsworn: Delve and Ironsworn: Starforged (found at www.ironswornrpg.com), created by Shawn Tomkin, and licensed for our use under the Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 International license.* Self-test `test_dungeon_srd`: at least 6 rows per world, syllables for every people, attribution in file and docs.

**AI Dungeon Master: the host's random rules (idea from AnyWorld, MIT, own code):** A tool of the game master console, no switch of its own (*Features → AI Dungeon Master*; `dungeon_zufall.py`). In the game master console under "🎲 Random rules" only the host gives the round up to `ZUFALL_MAX` (5) rules (`POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/zufall`): a name, a chance in percent, a trigger (every told turn, a fight starts, a new place), an effect (damage or healing 1 to 6, gold won or lost 1 to 50, a hint for the narration) and one roll for all or one per hero. The round's word list checks name and hint. On the trigger the panel rolls a d100 (a hit at or below the chance), applies the effect by its own rules (damage and healing like every consequence, gold never below 0 and never above the most) and writes every roll into the feed. The model only hears a hit's hint as data (`zufall`), never the rules; only the host sees them (`dungeon_sicht`: `zufall` host only, `zufall_hinweis` model only). **Live:** Status → Live counts random rules and per day hits and misses. Self-test `test_the_hosts_random_rules` in `test_dungeon`.

**AI Dungeon Master: lines, veils and the X-card:** On with the game, no switch of its own (*Features → AI Dungeon Master*, `dungeon_grenzen.py`), on from the start as protection (`features.DEFAULT_ON`). **Lines and veils:** under "🛡 Lines" next to the hero sheet every seat sets what never appears (lines) and what is only hinted at (veils), up to `GRENZEN_MAX` (8) short words each, comma-separated (`POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/grenzen`, guests `POST /api/dungeon/grenzen`). The table sees all words together, never whose they are. A line counts like a word of the word list (`dungeon.bad_words`): the panel refuses an action with it; a narration, NPC line or name with it is thrown away and told again. A veil may stand only once in a narration; more often is the fault "schleier" (`dungeon_erzaehler.judge`), the model retells with a fixed hint, on the last try the panel strikes further sentences with the veil (`dungeon_grenzen.cut`). The model gets both lists only as data (`grenzen` with `nie` and `nur_andeuten`) plus the fixed rule `GRENZEN_REGEL`, never as an instruction; the repair hint does not name them either. **X-card:** "✖ X-card" says with one tap, without reason or name: that was too much. The feed only says "Someone laid the X-card."; reading of this turn stops on every device, the model tells the last scene again more calmly (`dungeon_grenzen.retell`, the old scene only as data), checked by the same word lists, else a fixed sentence fades out; the scene's pictures and sound pieces go, and the next `X_ZUEGE` (3) narrations lead away from it. At most one X-card a minute per round, not while a narration is being made. **Live:** Status → Live counts seats with lines or veils, retellings running and per day lines set, X-cards laid and repairs for a veil. "How to play" has a page "Lines and X-card". Self-test `test_lines_veils_and_the_x_card` in `test_dungeon`. **View filter (idea from loreweaver, MIT, own code):** every secret of a round stands once in `dungeon_sicht.GEHEIM` with its viewers: the host, another seat (guest, TV and app show a seat's view), the car and the language model. The hidden truth, the story's plan, the things' features and a foe's life as a number are seen only by host and model; a seat's open action only by itself and the model; a question outside the game only by its own seat; the table talk only by the seats, never the model; a seat's key and device by nobody. `sicht(doc, betrachter)` is the last step of every view of the table (`dungeon.view`). The self-test `test_every_secret_stays_with_its_viewers` plants a marker in each secret and looks for it in every viewer's view (`dungeon_sicht.docs`): it stands exactly where `GEHEIM` allows. A new secret joins in the same commit. **Scribe check (idea from loreweaver, MIT, own code):** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Second check by the model* (`chat.dungeon_pruefung`, off from the start: one more model call each turn; `dungeon_schreiber.py`). After every told turn a small second call reads the narration (only as data) and proposes up to `VORSCHLAG_MAX` (6) changes not yet counted. The panel takes by fixed limits at most `NEHMEN_MAX` (4) a turn, once a turn: gold only when number and a gold word stand in the narration, at most `GOLD_JE_STUFE` (10) times the level and not when the panel already gave this hero gold; a thing only from the ground, when it stands in the narration and fits the bag; life never in a fight (the dice count there), at most `LEBEN_MAX` (3) and never down to 0; the place only without a map; a person only new and with the round's truth. The hero must stand by name in the narration. Every proposal writes a log line ("angenommen" or "verworfen" with its reason), every one taken a line "📜 Noted" in the feed. Status → Live counts those taken and dropped per day. Self-test `test_the_scribe_proposes_and_the_panel_takes_by_fixed_limits`.

**AI Dungeon Master: a settlement with life:** The "🏘 Settlement" window shows the place's picture on top (day or night) with its name, the time of day and gold: one's own large, the group's only to look at, since everyone pays from their own gold. Every service is a card with its picture (its sign until painted), a line of life from fixed lists per world (never the model's), the calculation small above (for example "10 × level 2 × 4 heroes = 80 gold") and a button with a verb: stay the night, mend, listen, improve. What one's own gold does not cover is grey with "N gold short"; a service not possible right now (already slept, nothing broken) is pale. The lesson shows the six values as a 3 × 2 grid with before → after (for example STR +3 → +4), the price once below. The view (`dungeon_gold.view`) brings `tageszeit`, `gold_gruppe`, `stufe`, `helden` and per service `je` (gold per level). Self-tests `test_gold_with_a_purpose_in_a_settlement` and `test_hero_sheet_merchant_and_item_windows` (three widths, grey without gold). Without "Gold with a purpose" everything stays off.

**AI Dungeon Master: economy (bench, smith, oils):** **Economy on the bench:** the balance bench (Features → AI Dungeon Master → Test benches) runs 200 runs of a group of four per level with the real finds, prices and services (`dungeon_pruefstand.economy`): fights up to the next level from the budget, a chest every three fights, a reward, a boss in every second run, a merchant every four fights with its purse. It shows a hero's income (median and worst tenth), spending (inn, healing potions, rumours, smith), the ratio and the dearest single find. The aim is a ratio of 1.2 to 2 (`WIRTSCHAFT`); when a level lies outside, the log writes "stufen wirtschaft ausserhalb". **Gold keeps its worth:** the inn costs 10 gold times the level per hero. In a settlement the smith improves a weapon or armour without a fixed rarity by one step up to "epic" (`dungeon_gold.better`), never a legend; the price is the piece's full worth afterwards (`better_price`), from "rare" on with an effect. **Oils:** a flask with `oel` (a damage kind other than slashing, piercing and bludgeoning) goes from the bag with "On the weapon" onto the main-hand weapon: the next 3 hits (`OEL_TREFFER`) do +1d6 of that kind; without a weapon nothing is used up, an oil is never drunk. The item shows "Oil: … N hits left". **Effects:** thundering (thunder), corroding (acid) and withering (necrotic) burn like fire, each with its own kind. Self-tests `test_the_economy_keeps_its_aim`, `test_gold_with_a_purpose_in_a_settlement`, `test_an_oil_on_the_weapon_for_three_hits`, `test_weapon_effects_for_thunder_acid_and_withering`, `test_every_common_kind_of_attack_has_its_protection` (in every world each kind other than slashing, piercing and bludgeoning that at least five foes attack with has a protection thing there).

**AI Dungeon Master: oils in the SRD catalogue:** five flasks with `oel` in `dungeon_srd.json`: thunder oil (thunder), lightning oil (lightning), fire oil (fire), frost oil (cold) and blessed oil (radiant), 25 gold each, common, in every world. So in every world a hero has a weapon of that kind or an oil against a weakness to thunder, lightning, fire or cold. Self-tests `test_schutz_nur_aus_der_liste` (an oil is a potion with a kind other than slashing, piercing and bludgeoning) and `test_jede_welt_trifft_donner_blitz_feuer_kaelte`.

**AI Dungeon Master: coins, merchants and find table:** **Coins:** a foe gives the group 5 to 15 gold times the level; coins are the main income. **Merchants with a kind:** every merchant is a smith (weapons, armour), an alchemist (potions, spells) or a trader (jewellery, tools, scrolls, treasures) (`HAENDLER`) and sells only its kind. It pays half the worth for its kind, a quarter for anything else and for unknown things (`sell_price`). Its purse holds 50 gold times the group's level per visit (`KASSE`); heroes' purchases fill it; when empty it refuses with its own sentence. The merchant's window names its kind, what it takes for half the worth and the purse. **Find table:** a find's rarity comes from a fixed table per level (`fundtabelle` in the rules, `find_row`, `NIE` = never).
