From a foe no rarity from "uncommon" to "epic" gets rarer with the level; legendary things come from level 5 on only from bosses, at most 10 in 100, not from chests and rewards. Under Rules each rarity shows the level from which it occurs. The balance bench equips its groups by a fixed assumption per level (`GEAR_SELTEN`), so a new find table does not move the aim by itself. Self-tests `test_the_find_table_keeps_its_promises`, `test_a_merchant_of_a_kind_with_a_purse`.

**AI Dungeon Master: prices, treasures, potions:** **One price rule** (`dungeon.price`): the art's base price (or the thing's own) times the rarity's factor; a thing with a fixed rarity and its own price already has its final price and is not multiplied again. Every price lies within the band of its rarity (`PREISBAND`: common up to 50, uncommon up to 250, rare up to 1000, epic up to 4000); legendary things have no price and cannot be sold. Gear without a fixed rarity (plate armour, breastplate, dragon armour, elven bow, greatsword and others) costs at most 50. The "medium" encounters at level 1 and 2 are a little bigger for it (`kampf.BUDGET` 95 and 150); the balance bench holds 70 to 90 per cent. **Treasures by level:** a treasure has a class instead of a price (`klasse`, `SCHATZ`: small 10, medium 25, large 60 gold) times the group's level when found (`it["st"]`); large treasures only from chests and bosses (`SCHATZ_GROSS`). **Potions and scrolls by strength:** a healing potion costs 2 gold per life healed (`HEIL_GOLD`), a spell scroll 25 × (grade + 1) (`ROLLE_GOLD`); a hero starts with 25 gold. Under Rules → Items a fixed rarity shows its final price or "not for sale". Self-test `test_every_price_lies_in_its_band`.

**AI Dungeon Master: prices, treasures, weapons and protection in the SRD catalog (data):** Every own price lies within the band of its rarity (common up to 50, uncommon up to 250, rare up to 1000, epic up to 4000). Legendary things like the vorpal sword or dwarven plate have no price and cannot be sold. Gear without a fixed rarity costs at most 50 (musket, laser weapons, combat suit, hacking module), so it gets no bonus. Each of the 38 treasures has a class (`klasse`: small, medium, large); the panel computes its value from the class and the group's level. 24 SRD spells carry their damage type (fire bolt fire, thunderwave thunder, vampiric touch necrotic). 12 weapons close gaps: thunder hammer and storm spear (fantasy), bell mace (horror), shock baton, arc thrower and sonic cannon (sci-fi), blunderbuss, eel harpoon and storm sabre (pirates), flame sword, frost dagger and pitch torch. 18 protection things (`widerstand`) halve one type, never more: 7 rings (fire, cold, lightning, poison, necrotic, psychic, acid), 6 potions for 10 turns, shield belt against force (sci-fi), amulet of the living light against necrotic (horror, pirates), salamander cloak, heat suit and coral helm; the potion of resistance protects against fire, cold and poison. Self-test `test_dungeon_srd`: prices within the band, treasures with a class, protection only from the list of damage types.

**AI Dungeon Master: damage kinds:** **Spells with their own kind:** 31 damage spells have `schaden_art` (Fire bolt, Fireball, Burning hands … 🔥 fire, Ray of frost, Ice lance, Cone of cold ❄️ cold, Shocking grasp, Lightning bolt, Chain lightning ⚡ lightning, Thunderwave, Thunderous smite 💥 thunder, Sacred flame, Divine favour ☀️ radiant, Poison spray, Stinking cloud, Cloudkill 🐍 poison, Life drain, Vampiric touch 🌑 necrotic, Eldritch blast, Magic missile, Spiritual weapon ✨ force). A spell without damage (Shield, Bless, Sleep …) does not attack, it stays a check. The item card shows the kind. **Magic against resistance:** a weapon with a bonus of +1 or more and every spell go through a resistance to slashing, piercing and bludgeoning (`kampf.against`, `KOERPER`), not through an immunity or other kinds. **Protection for heroes:** armour, jewellery and potions can have `widerstand` (`WIDERSTAND_ARTEN`); worn or drunk it halves a foe's hit of that kind (`resists`, `consequences`), a potion for `SCHUTZ_ZUEGE` = 10 told turns (`h["schutz"]`). In the catalog: Ring of frost ward, Ring of fire ward, Ring of grounding (lightning and thunder), Amulet of vitality (necrotic and poison), Potion of fire resistance, Potion of cold resistance, Antitoxin (poison and acid). The hero sheet shows 🛡 with the kinds, the feed "halved". **Found out first:** at the table a foe's card shows only what the group found out (`kampf.known`, `rd["kennt"]`): a hit with "double", "halved" or "no effect" reveals that kind, a passed Intelligence check in a fight (`KENNER`) everything of the foes in the fight; the rest shows as "❔ still unknown". The bestiary under Rules shows everything. **Coverage:** `test_every_common_weakness_has_its_weapons` checks per world: against every weakness at least three foes there have, there are at least two weapons, spells or a weapon effect of that kind. **Time of day:** the narrator reports `szene.tageszeit` (morgen, tag, abend, nacht); the panel keeps it in `rd["tageszeit"]` and gives it back in the facts, the stage picks the day or night picture by it. **Balance:** the "medium" encounters are a little bigger at level 4, 5, 7 and 8 (`kampf.BUDGET` 380, 450, 950, 1000), the balance bench holds 70 to 90 per cent; its casters take only spells with a damage kind. **Nothing jumps:** on a big screen the hero sheet has a fixed height (at most 900 pixels), the filters under "What I can do" change only its content; the group and the right column keep their scroll place when the table repaints (`dmColKeep`); self-test in `test_hero_sheet_merchant_and_item_windows`. Status → Live counts foes found out and hits halved by resistance. "How to play" explains it under "Damage kinds". Self-tests `test_damage_kinds_of_spells_weapons_and_heroes`, `test_every_common_weakness_has_its_weapons`, `test_the_time_of_day_by_the_narrator`.

**AI Dungeon Master: game mails in the Dungeon style:** The mails to guests with a guest account (`dungeon_gast.message`) have, next to the text, an HTML part like a game page: a frame, a heading with the world's sign, a book-like font, only styles inside the mail, no picture and nothing loaded from outside (no tracking pixel), every value escaped. **Colours per world** (`FARBEN`): fantasy parchment, horror dark red, sci-fi steel blue, pirates sea green, any other world parchment. **Last sentence:** "Your turn" shows under "Last at the table" the narrator's last sentence (`teaser`: at most `VORSCHAU` = 160 characters, without links and addresses). The host switches it off at the table under "✉ Last sentence in the mail" (`rd["mail_vorschau"]`, on by default; the line shows only when a guest with an account sits at the table). **Play on:** the button is a one-time link `https://<panel>/#weiter=<t>` (`WEITER_S` = 12 hours, valid once, only its hash lies in `dungeon-gaeste.json`), never the seat's key. `/api/dungeon/weiter` gives the device a fresh key for exactly that seat (`play_on`, `p["hx"]`, at most `WEITER_KEYS` = 3) while the seat still belongs to the guest account; a new seat link of the host takes all of them back. The panel's address comes from the host's seat link (`rd["base"]`). Self-test `test_turn_mail_looks_like_the_game_with_a_one_time_link`. **Heroes tab:** every hero shows under "🎒 Equipment and bag", folded, what it wears (per place), its bag with where each thing was found, its gold and its spells; changes only at the table. Self-test `test_the_heroes_tab_shows_what_a_hero_has`.

**AI Dungeon Master: joining without cheating:** **Waiting at the edge:** whoever takes a seat during a fight waits at the edge (`p["wartet"]`): no action, no own moves with bag and potions, not in the fight's group, the turn does not wait for them. The feed says "… arrives and waits at the edge until the fight is over.", the group shows them with ⏳, the host can take the seat back. After the fight (or the round's end) they come to the table (`dungeon.arrive`). **First turn:** in their first turn at the table (`p["neu_zug"]`, also after waiting) a hero gives nobody anything: no potion for a friend, no gift, no trade, no spell on the group; the model hears "just arrived", and a healing it tells for a friend does not count when only new heroes acted that turn. **Fleeing:** a fight's group is fixed at its start (`kampf["helden"]`) and shares the experience; whoever leaves the table meanwhile has fled (a line in the feed, their share stays out, they lose the table's experience), whoever came later gets none. **One device, one hero:** a device that takes a seat gets the cookie `dmgeraet` (random, HttpOnly, SameSite=Strict); the seat keeps only its hash. A second seat of the same round on that device is refused, also next to the host's seat. **Live:** Status → Live counts "waiting at the edge" and per day joins during a fight, heroes who left a fight, help blocked in a first turn and refused second heroes. "How to play" explains it under "When it goes on". Self-tests in `test_dungeon` (Fight): `test_join_during_a_fight_waits_at_the_edge`, `test_the_first_turn_gives_nothing_to_others`, `test_leaving_a_fight_is_fleeing_and_the_share_stays_out`, `test_one_device_one_hero`. **Death saves as in D&D:** when a hero is down, from the next told turn the panel rolls a d20 for them each turn (`dungeon.save_roll`, `h["rettung"]` = successes, failures): `RETTUNG` (10) or more succeeds, a 1 counts as two failures, a 20 or three successes get them up with 1 life; after three failures they fall (unconscious at the children's table). Every roll is a line with 🎲 in the feed, the hero sheet shows "saves ✓ … · … failures left". Older states count their turns down as failures. **Mercy:** if they are the only living hero at the table (`dungeon.alone`), they do not fall (`dungeon.spare`): a stranger brings them to safety, they wake with 1 life, a running fight ends without experience ("The fight is lost, but nobody dies."), they lose half their gold (rounded up) and one thing from the bag; the next narration hears of it (`GNADE_FAKT`). Foes attack only standing heroes. Status → Live counts death saves and rescues by mercy. Self-tests `test_rescue_rolls_get_a_hero_up`, `test_the_only_hero_is_spared`, plus `test_down_three_turns_then_dead_and_things_lie_here` with failed rolls.

**AI Dungeon Master: bestiary:** The catalog `app/panel/dungeon_srd.json` has 224 foes (142 from the SRD, 82 own), among them for sci-fi repair bot, cloaked hunter, station core AI, for pirates deck rat, sea witch, deep kraken, for horror dollmaker, plague doctor, the Pale Countess, for fantasy rune guardian, frost giantess, ancient fire dragon, for sci-fi and pirates star buccaneer and comet raider, and for every world highwayman, storm keeper, iron colossus, star whale. Their values follow the balance formula per level (`dungeon_balance`), texts in the tone of the children's table, damage kind, weakness, resistance and immunity from the fixed list; 17 own bosses of levels 10 to 15. Every world has at least three foes on each level 1 to 12. A world without its own foes (the own world, a new one of the admin) meets the fantasy foes and those for every world (`dungeon_kampf.plays_in`, the same rule for encounter and list). In the tab "Regeln", part "Foes (bestiary)", each row shows the damage kind as a symbol (🗡️ slashing, 🏹 piercing, 🔨 bludgeoning, 🔥 fire, ❄️ cold, ⚡ lightning, 🧪 acid, 🐍 poison, 🌑 necrotic, ☀️ radiant, 🧠 psychic, 💥 thunder, ✨ force), then ▲ weakness (double damage), ▼ resistance (half damage), ✕ immunity, the mark "👑 Boss" and "new" (a foe with `seit` in the last 30 versions). The detail adds what a boss does more (more life per hero, phase 2 at half life, two attacks, a better find), the EP per hero for 2 to 5 heroes and the worlds. Filters: world, level 0-4, 5-10, 11+, bosses, new. "Alle Bilder malen" paints foes without a picture. Self-test `test_dungeon_bestiarium`. **A failure stays a failure:** the narration model gets for every check what it was for and the result in words ("failed: it does not happen this turn"). If it tells a failed check as done (a sentence names the check's thing with a word like "gives way", "bursts open", "off its hinges" and without "not", "holds"), it writes again. For this and for foes not named by the card's name there is a third try; if all fail, the panel tells the truth in a fixed sentence after it ("But the check "break the door" failed …", "Before you: 2 × Dunkelmantel."). An encounter does not depend on a check's outcome: the panel offers fitting foes (budget by group and level), the model decides whether a fight comes and picks only from the offer. Self-tests `test_a_failed_check_stays_failed`, `test_foes_not_named_three_times_the_panel_names_them`. **Feed:** going down and getting up show "is down" and "is back on their feet", a potion at full life "already at full life". **Portrait and hero sheet:** the table's answer (such as a refused portrait with its reason) also stands at the top of the hero sheet and at the merchant, which lie over the table; at "Life" the sheet shows "down".

**AI Dungeon Master: fast over the proxy:** **Reading aloud as MP3:** every piece read aloud is kept as MP3 next to the WAV (48 kbit/s, about a tenth; ffmpeg on the panel computer); the browser fetches the MP3 (`?mp3=1`, a guest `"mp3": true`), the iPhone app the WAV; the next piece loads while the current one plays. **Reading counts as speech:** while a turn is read aloud, scene picture, music and all other background work wait (`vorrang`), even when speech output runs on the model computer. **Small pictures:** lists (rules, bag, seats) fetch a small copy (96 pixels at most, made once, kept in `dungeon-bild-klein`, deleted with the picture); only the merchant and the detail window show the large picture. Every picture address carries the picture's version, so the browser keeps it until it is painted again. **Compressed:** the admin's catalog and rules come gzipped (from 4 KB); a round's view never (invite links and players' text, protection against BREACH).

**AI Dungeon Master: legendary pieces:** The file `app/panel/dungeon_legenden.json` holds 20 named pieces per world (fantasy, horror, sci-fi, pirates; the own world takes the fantasy ones): a wearable base item of the catalog, an own name and sentence in both languages and two effects from the list of effects (at most one with damage, only ones that fit the kind). The catalog's items of fixed rarity "legendary" (from the SRD, such as the Vorpal Sword) count as well. A legendary find becomes one of them by fixed rules of the panel, never the model: only from a boss, as a reward or from a chest; a merchant never sells one (at most epic); each only once a campaign (the round remembers the ones found, a new chapter takes over the list, none comes that a hero at the table already carries); first one for the receiver's class; with none free the find is epic. It comes unknown; once known it shows name, sentence, both effects and "✦ One of a kind", and both effects count for checks, damage, armour class and the ▲/▼ comparison. A selftest checks the file against the rules (`dungeon_legenden.check`). Status → Live shows the pieces found that day.

**AI Dungeon Master: tools, prices per kind, jewellery, comparison:** **Tool quality:** a tool helps by its quality (`WERKZEUG`): common "simple" +1, uncommon "good" +2, from rare "masterwork" +3; the tool's line names quality and bonus, the roll carries `werkzeug_plus` and the check bar shows exactly that part. **Prices per kind:** potions, scrolls, spells and tools (`GUENSTIG`) cost half the rarity's factor, weapons, armour and jewellery the full one. **Jewellery:** common jewellery is a treasure without a value; magic jewellery (from uncommon) always raises a value by at least +1. **▲/▼:** in the bag, at the merchant and on the ground every wearable item shows "▲ better than yours", "▼ weaker than yours" or "= like yours" against what the hero wears (the weakest of its places; weapon: mean damage and bonus, armour: protection, twice on the body, and bonus, jewellery: bonus; effects counted, unknown only the base), and "✗ not for your class". The panel computes the numbers (`dungeon.strength`, `compare`, view `vergleich`).

**AI Dungeon Master: items with fixed effects:** **Rarity bonus:** common +0, uncommon +1, rare +2, epic and legendary +3, never more (`BONUS_MAX`, `check_rules` refuses more). **Effects:** from "rare" on (`EFFEKT_AB`) a wearable item gets an effect from `effekte`, each a fixed rule of the panel (`dungeon_kampf.WIRKUNG`): `schaden` (weapons only) +1d6 damage of its kind on every hit, twice on a critical, through the foe's resistance, immunity and weakness like any damage (fire, cold, lightning, poison, radiant); `lebensraub` (weapons) the hero heals 1d4 on every hit; `glueck` a natural 1 on a check or attack is rolled again once ("🍀 Luck" at the roll); `wachsam` (armour and jewellery) armour class +1; otherwise +2 on one of the six values. There are no effects the panel cannot compute; an item with such an effect counts only by its base. An unknown item works only once identified. `check_rules` asks every own effect for a `wirkung` (with `art` for `schaden`) or a bonus and allows `nur` only from the wearable kinds. **Rarity per level:** the find roll (d100 + occasion + 2 per level, `FUND_STUFE`) counts for the rarest kind without the level bonus; so legendary is rarer than epic at every level. A self-test checks both.

**AI Dungeon Master: gold with a purpose:** Part of the rule depth "Full" (*Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth*, the host chooses per round; `dungeon_gold.py`). In a settlement (a map place of the kinds village, town, tavern, station, bazaar or harbour, `SIEDLUNG`, not inside a vault) or where a merchant stands, the hero card shows "🏘 Settlement" with four fixed offers of the panel, never the model, never in a fight. Prices grow with the group's level (`PREIS` per level): **Inn** (one pays for all): full life and all spell charges for every hero not unconscious or fallen, a hero down stands up; once a game evening per round. **Smith** (5 per level): a tool that broke on a natural 1 comes back into the bag; the last 5 are kept, only while the switch is on. **Lesson** (20 per level): +1 on one value until the round or chapter ends regularly, one at a time. **Rumour** (3 per level): the panel names the first unvisited place on the shortest way to the map's goal (else an unvisited neighbour) with its direction, once per place. The hero pays with an own move (`POST …/held` with `{was: "dienst", id, i, wert}`, for guests `/api/dungeon/held`). Security: no new endpoint, only fixed rules and prices of the panel; logs `dungeon: dienst | <offer> | N gold` and a fixed word per refusal; the live overview only counts the offers paid today.

**AI Dungeon Master: heroic courage:** Part of the rule depth "Full" (*Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth*, the host chooses per round; `dungeon_mut.py`). Once a game evening (round and day) every hero may call on heroic courage with an action: tap "⭐ Heroic courage" before "Act", the action carries `mut: true`, the panel notes it only while a charge is free. If a check fails, the panel rolls once more; if an attack hits without being critical, it becomes critical (double damage dice); if it misses, it is rolled once more. If the first roll succeeds, the courage stays; per action it works on one roll at most. The roll shows "⭐ Heroic courage". **From the table:** the other players' actions of the last three turns carry a 👍 (`POST …/daumen` with `{zug, aktion}`, for guests `/api/dungeon/daumen`); when at least half of the other players (rounded up) give a 👍, the hero gets a second charge for the evening (`LOB_MAX` = 1). Never for oneself, not alone at the table. The state lives in the round (`mut`, `mut_lob`); each evening brings a new one. Security: no new endpoint (the existing thumbs endpoint with login, profile binding, CSRF, size and rate limit takes `aktion`), only fixed rules of the panel, never the model; logs `dungeon: heldenmut | bereit|keiner frei|genutzt|lob|geschenkt|lob nein|lob alt|aus`; the live overview only counts today's uses.

**AI Dungeon Master: experience for more than fighting:** The panel, never the model, gives every hero at the table fixed experience: for a **new place of the map** `ep.ort` (10), for an **act of the story done** (`dungeon.step_act`, whether the model reports the turning point or the panel moves on) `ep.akt` (100), for a **fight ended in peace** (`kampf_ende` "frieden") the experience of the foes still standing as for defeated ones, but only when a check passed in the same turn (persuading, say). A solved riddle of the map gives half of `ep.ziel` (50). The numbers are in the rules under `ep` and can be set (`ort` 0 to 200, `akt` 0 to 1000, checked by `check_rules`). The experience gathers as "+X EP still at the table" and comes at the end of the round. Logs `dungeon: ep | ort | N`, `dungeon: ep | akt`; the fight line in the log counts `frieden`. No new endpoint. Self-test `Equipment.test_experience_for_more_than_fighting` in `app/tests/test_dungeon.py`.

**AI Dungeon Master: game master dial:** Part of the rule depth "Full" (*Features → AI Dungeon Master → Rule depth*, the host chooses per round; `dungeon_kampf.turn_dial`). After every fight (once its last damage is with the heroes) the Spark sets the round's next encounters by fixed numbers: won with less than a quarter of the group's life lost 10 % harder; a hero down, a lost fight or a flight 15 % easier; else unchanged; at most 25 % either way, at the children's table never above 0. The dial changes the experience a medium encounter may have (`dungeon_kampf.budget`) and so the offer of foes and the encounter's limit; the danger ("easy", "medium", "hard") is measured against the dialled value, and players see only that. The host sees in the game master console *🎚 Game master dial* with the setting ("danger +10 %") and *hold*. The setting belongs to the round; switch off means the measured strength. The model decides none of it. Live counts the day's steps (harder, easier); logs `dungeon: regler | hoch | N`, `runter | N`, `gleich | N`, `fest`, `festgehalten`, `frei`. API (host): `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/erzaehler` with `{regler_fest: true|false}` (sign-in, CSRF, size and rate limit). Self-test `Fight.test_the_game_master_dial_by_fixed_numbers` in `app/tests/test_dungeon.py`.

**AI Dungeon Master: balance bench:** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Test benches* (`chat.dungeon_pruefstand`, off by default; `dungeon_pruefstand.py`). The Spark plays fights by itself with the real rules and dice of `dungeon_kampf` (attacks, the foes' turn, damage, states, flight) and the catalogue including own rules, without the language model and without a round: per level 1 to 10 and group size 1 to 5, 200 fights of danger "medium" each, once a night between 2 and 5 or at once with *Play it through now* (at most once a minute, never two runs at a time). The group is fixed: the classes in turn, the first people, made like a player's hero and raised to the level, the class's first weapon and the best body armour up to the level's price, both of the rarity of a medium find of that level. Below a third of its life a hero drinks its healing potion, otherwise attacks the foe with the least life (a caster with its grade-0 attack spell). The encounter has one or two kinds of foes from the whole bestiary, exactly of the danger asked. Under *Adjust rules* the part *Balance bench* shows wins per level as bars with the aim of 70 to 90 % as a green band (red outside), plus life lost in won fights and turns, for 1 to 5 heroes. **Measured in:** the experience of a medium encounter per hero and level (`dungeon_kampf.BUDGET`, levels 1 to 10) is measured with the bench and grows with the group (`GRUPPE`: 0.7 for one hero, 0.33 more for each further one). A self-test keeps every cell (level and group size) in the aim. In the real game abilities and healing spells, which the bench does not use, help too. No bench fight counts into the day's numbers, no round is touched. The result is in `dungeon-pruefstand.json` in the state folder. Live: cells, below and above the aim, whether it is playing. Logs `dungeon: pruefstand | start | nacht` (or `knopf`), `fertig | N zellen | M unter dem ziel | S s`, `knopf | ok` (or `aus`, `zu oft`, `laeuft`). API (admin): `GET` and `POST /api/admin/dungeon/pruefstand` (sign-in, CSRF, size and rate limit `dungeonpruef`). Self-tests in `app/tests/test_dungeon_pruefstand.py`.

**AI Dungeon Master: weapon properties, damage kinds, states, bosses:** **Weapon properties:** every weapon carries a damage kind (`schaden_art`: slashing, piercing, bludgeoning, for wands force, radiant or necrotic) and properties (`eigenschaften`) with fixed rules (`dungeon_kampf.EIGENSCHAFT_REGEL`): light is critical from a 19; finesse hits with the better of Strength and Dexterity, a throw (the action says "throw", `WURF_WORTE`) too; heavy attacks at a disadvantage below Strength 13; versatile rolls a die two sides bigger with the off hand free; reach keeps the foe it hit at a distance (its attack on that hero in the same turn at a disadvantage). The item shows kind and rules as a line of its own. **Damage kinds:** every hit has a kind (fists = bludgeoning). `resistenz` halves, `immun` cancels, `schwaeche` doubles; the log shows the kind and "halved", "no effect" or "double!", the foe's window its weaknesses and protections. **States from hits:** when a foe with `zustaende` (poisoned, grappled, frightened, burning …) hits, the hero rolls a Constitution save (Wisdom against charmed and frightened) against 10 + a quarter of the foe's level. Failed, the state lasts its turns from the catalogue: disadvantage on own attacks or checks, advantage for the foes, no attack (paralysed, stunned …), burning or bleeding costs 1d4 life each turn. All states end with the fight. The seat shows them with ☠, the log every save, the model gets them in the facts. **Bosses:** a foe with `boss` has more life per hero (+50 % for each further one), a second phase from half life (+2 to hit, a line in the log), attacks twice a turn and leaves a better find (occasion "boss"). `check_rules` checks all fields. Self-test `Fight.test_weapon_properties_damage_kinds_states_and_bosses`.

**AI Dungeon Master: place in the story so far, characters from elsewhere:** The map gives the way back only when an action names it, `dungeon_karte.leute` (who is here, who elsewhere and where, who travels along) and the note "place not changed". **Story so far with places:** every narration carries its place in the log (`where`: `ort`, `ort_name`; a vault's rooms count as the place above). After a change the model gets under `bisher` only the new place's narrations and the last one as a bridge, each with "[place]" in front (`told_here`); turns without a place stay as they are. The facts carry `personen` from `dungeon_karte.leute`; a fixed rule says: nobody is elsewhere than given, whoever is "anderswo" does not speak here unless travelling along (`ORTE_REGEL`). **Check:** if a character speaks who is elsewhere by the map and does not travel along, the model writes once more (`dungeon_erzaehler.elsewhere`, hint "anderswo", log line `dungeon: nachbessern | anderswo`); when the group is just walking to that place, it is allowed. Self-test `BetterStories.test_a_narration_keeps_its_place_and_others_stay_elsewhere`.

**AI Dungeon Master: story planner:** Under *Features → AI Dungeon Master*, button "Adjust rules", section **Stories**, the admin keeps up to 30 stories: title, world, tone, idea, the heroes' goal, foe, up to 6 places, up to 10 taboos and 3 to 5 acts with title, content, turning point and planned turns (2 to 30). **Create a proposal** lets the own language model draft the story from keywords (at most 300 characters), **↻ Write again** one act with a free hint; keywords stand only as data in the block `<<< … >>>` (markers defused), the answer comes in a fixed JSON schema and is checked like a typed story (`check_plan`: lengths, number of acts, turns, words of the tone, "nie" and the taboos); only "Check and save" keeps it (`dungeon-geschichten.json` in the state folder). When starting a round, the story is picked as a card under *Dungeon Master → Rounds*, together with the shipped ones; world and tone come from the story, the round gets a copy, and a campaign chapter plays on in the same act. **Flow:** the model gets only goal, foe, places and the current act (data block) and reports with `akt_fertig` when the turning point is told; the panel takes that only after 3 turns of the act, moves one act on, never back, and moves on by itself when an act runs one and a half times as long as planned (`step_act`). After the last act "The story is told." stands there. The taboos count in every narration like the list "nie". **Spoilers:** players only see "Act 2/4" and "Act 3 begins.", only admin and host see the plan (to unfold at the table). Status → Live shows stories, acts moved on today and proposals (numbers only); logs only fixed words (`dungeon: vorschlag | geschichte | ok | 4100 ms`, `dungeon: akt | modell | 2`, `dungeon: geschichte gespeichert | 4 akte`). API (admin): `PUT|DELETE /api/admin/dungeon/geschichte/{gid}` (`neu` for a new one), `POST /api/admin/dungeon/geschichte/vorschlag` (`{welt, ton, akte, stichworte, tabus}`), `POST /api/admin/dungeon/geschichte/akt` (`{plan, n, hinweis}`), rate limit `dungeonplan` (10 a minute).

**AI Dungeon Master: advantage, OOC, length:** **Advantage and disadvantage:** the model may report `vorteil` or `nachteil` per check; the panel then rolls two d20 and takes the higher or lower, the story shows both ("Advantage 14/7"); any other value counts as a plain roll. **OOC line:** when an action is unclear or no move at all, the model does not narrate it but writes that character a short line in `ooc` (at most 160 characters, only names of the group, checked like the narration) that only this player sees as "(OOC: …)". **Length:** a narration has at most 900 characters, the opening of a round, chapter or act up to 1500 (`erz_max`). **Prompt:** the default prompt asks for senses (smells, light, sounds, feelings) and stays with world, tone and the story so far; the panel's fixed rules stand behind it.

**AI Dungeon Master: calm table tone:** The game table looks like the rest of the panel with a warm tone of its own: system font, gold as the accent (the world only tints the accent, ground and surfaces are the same), serif only for the narration, no parchment card, initial or small caps; actions stand with a line instead of a speech bubble. Buttons, tabs and the phone's bottom bar have line icons instead of emoji (emoji stay in the game's text). "Your rounds" is a list with the name and one line below, on the phone the buttons stand under the name. Light or dark follows the panel, the button at the table keeps a choice of its own. More in [Technical, design system](technical.md#design-system-tokens).

**AI Dungeon Master: table layout:** Under the narration (with "Turn n") actions stand with the avatar in the seat's colour, rolls as a slim line, act lines highlighted. On the left hero cards of all players with life bar, the six values (with bonus) and protection as a grid, equipped items and the state (acted, thinking, away), the own one highlighted; the head shows title and current place; under the narration "Waiting for: …", the input, the microphone and **Act**. On a phone story, group and own sheet come one below the other. **Colours per world** (`welten[].farben` with `grund`, `karte`, `pergament`, `tinte`, `gold`, only `#rrggbb`, checked by `check_rules`); the admin changes them under "Adjust rules". **Full screen:** "⛶ Full screen" in the header, also for guests, shows only the table (the browser's full screen; where there is none, as in the iPhone browser, the table fills the window); the button, Esc or leaving the browser's full screen end it. **How to play:** "❔ How to play" opens a short sheet above the table for players and guests (what you write, when it goes on, how the panel rolls, life, loot and trading, listening and speaking, the hero code); the first time you take a seat on a device it opens once by itself (remembered only in the browser).

**AI Dungeon Master: six values as in D&D:** every hero has **Strength, Dexterity, Constitution, Intelligence, Wisdom and Charisma** (`werte`, exactly six, `konstitution` required). The **bonus** for rolls is (score − 10) / 2 rounded down: 8 gives −1, 10 gives 0, 16 gives +3 (`mod`). The panel rolls d20 plus bonus plus equipment, abilities and blessings. **When creating** a hero the player lays out the fixed row 15, 14, 13, 12, 10 and 8 (`verteilung`): drag numbers or tap two to swap, preset by the class's `reihe` (warrior strength, mage intelligence, rogue dexterity, healer wisdom, ranger dexterity); the panel takes only exactly that row, otherwise the class's order counts. The **people** add +1 or +2 (human +1 everywhere, dwarf constitution +2 and wisdom +1, elf dexterity +2 and intelligence +1, halfling dexterity +2 and charisma +1, orc strength +2 and constitution +1, each 0 to 2). **Constitution** gives life: the class's start plus the bonus, per level `leben_plus` plus the bonus (at least 1); when the bonus rises, life grows for every level. Every level brings a value point (+1, at most 20, `WERT_MAX`). Heroes with the old three values are carried over when read (`six`): values by the class's order plus the people, points come back to spend, life fitting the level, wits count as intelligence; log "dungeon: held umgestellt | sechs werte". The admin's old own rules keep working (`_alt`): "verstand" becomes "intelligenz", a class's old `werte` become its `reihe`. Abilities, effects and items are spread over the six values (battle cry charisma, iron skin constitution, healer wisdom). Hero card and sheet show score and bonus. **Hero voices:** on the own sheet every player, a guest too, picks a speech-output voice under "Your hero's voice" (list from `/v1/voices`, fetched in the background at most every 10 minutes, `STIMMEN_S`); "▶ Sample" speaks a fixed sentence (made once per voice and kept, rate limit `dungeonprobe`). With reading aloud on, the voice speaks only what the hero says **in quotes** ("…", „…“, »…«, at most 200 characters, `says`), before the narration in the same sound track, one after another; a line with a word the tone, "nie" or the taboos refuse is not spoken. Only voices from the list can be picked; without one only the narrator reads. **Voice from a description:** when Settings → Speech output "Also serve VoiceDesign" is on (a second engine on the model computer with the same memory again; `/v1/voices` reports `voicedesign`), a field "or describe it" appears, for example "deep, rough dwarf voice": only letters, digits, spaces, comma, full stop and hyphen, 3 to 80 characters (`KLANG`), no word the tone, "nie" or taboos refuse; it goes to the speech output as `task_type: VoiceDesign` with `instructions` and counts before the picked voice. The voice is made anew for every line, so it sounds alike, not always the same. With VoiceDesign off the picked voice or the narrator speaks.

**AI Dungeon Master: new round, ready-made stories, light or dark:** The *Dungeon Master* page (rounds, campaigns, new round, heroes) and a guest's invitation window have the table's look in the world's colours. **New round** in four steps: 1 World, 2 Story, 3 The round (title, opening, tone, time per turn, duration), 4 Your hero. World, tone, people and class are chosen as **cards with pictures** (`zeichen` in the rules, only a picture without letters, at most 8 characters, checked by `check_rules`; without one ◆); the people's card names the bonus (for example "DEX +2 · INT +1"), the class card its two main values and life. Tapping a world tints the page at once. **Ready-made stories:** 10 stories per world ship with the Spark (`dungeon_geschichten.json`, German and English, 3 acts each with turning point, goal, foe and places, different tones). They stand in step 2 as cards with title, summary, tone and number of acts, next to the admin's stories ("by the admin") and "Tell freely". The panel checks each when reading like an admin's story (`check_plan`) and leaves out one the rules refuse (for example when the admin takes a world out). A chosen story sets the tone (other tone cards locked) and fills in its title as long as you do not type your own. A guest sees no stories. **Light or dark:** "☀ Light" or "☾ Dark" at the top of the page, at the table and in the guest's window switches; both take the world's colours. At first the game follows panel or device, the choice is kept only in this browser. **Heroes** stand as cards with a life bar, six values with bonus, level, XP and gold. The word "leiche" in the list of the tone "from 8" only matches the start of a word ("schleichen", "Federleicht" are no hit).

**AI Dungeon Master: equipment, carrying and merchants:** **Nine places as in D&D** (`SLOTS`): head, neck, body, main hand, off hand, hands, feet and two rings, on the sheet as a figure of three by three fields; **Equip** or dragging puts an item on, × or dragging into the bag takes it off. Every catalog item has its place (`platz`, otherwise the first of its kind: weapons main hand, armour body, jewellery ring); shield and tower shield off hand, helmet and leather cap head, gauntlets hands, iron boots feet. A **two-handed weapon** (`zweihand`, for example greatsword, bow, staff) takes both hands and displaces the shield, a shield displaces it. Protection adds up everything worn. **Weight and carrying:** every item weighs something (`kg`, otherwise the value of its kind). A hero carries strength times 3.75 kg (`TRAG_KG`); from three quarters the hero is **burdened** (`BELASTET`) and rolls dexterity checks with disadvantage, advantage cancels it (the roll shows "burdened"). The panel takes nothing beyond the carry limit or more than 12 places in the bag: loot without room **stays on the ground** ("Lying here", at most 12, `BODEN_MAX`), anyone in the group can **pick it up** or **put something down**. A new place leaves the ground behind. Potions, spells and scrolls of the same kind **stack** up to 3 (`STAPEL`). Trading and giving check places and carry limit afterwards. **Merchants:** in a settlement the model only reports a merchant's name and one sentence (`haendler`); the host can call one with **⚖ Open market** and close it. The panel always sets goods and prices: 6 to 10 goods (`WARE`), rarity as for loot by the group's level, price by the price rule (`preise` per kind or the item's own `preis` times the rarity's factor 1, 3, 8, 20, 50). **Charisma** gives 5 % off per bonus point, at most 20 % (`RABATT`). Every good exists once. **Selling** fetches gold by the merchant's rule (less while unknown); the merchant adds the item to its goods. A new place closes the market. **Unknown magic:** items of the two rarest kinds (epic, legendary) arrive **unknown** ("Longsword (unknown)"): only the base counts, bonus and effect once known, through a **scroll of knowing** (`erkennt`, used up) or the merchant for a fifth of the worth, at least 5 gold (`ERKENNEN`). Heroes without places get weapon, armour and jewellery onto the places when read (`nine`), the rest goes into the bag. The model only hears that a merchant is there and who bought, sold or named what. Status → Live shows open markets and markets opened, purchases and sales today (numbers only); logs only fixed words (`dungeon: markt | auf | 8 waren`, `dungeon: handel | kauf | selten`, `dungeon: handel | erkannt | rolle`). API: `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/held` with `was` `kaufen`, `verkaufen`, `erkennen` (`mit` for the scroll), `aufheben`, `fallen` and `platz` when equipping and unequipping; `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/markt` (host, `{auf}`). **Prologue:** every new round and chapter opens with a prologue (head "Prologue"): the place with all senses, mood, starting situation, every character of the group appears, at most one NPC speaks, at the end a question to the group. The model gets world, group, the host's opening, "The story so far" and the first act; it is checked like every narration, damage, loot and successes in it do not count. The first turn waits until it stands (at most two tries), then the turn time starts; with reading aloud it is spoken. When the model does not answer, the host's opening or the first act's content stands there. When a round hangs while narrating after a restart, the next request frees it. Log `dungeon: prolog | ok | 4100 ms`. The scroll bars at the table have the world's colours. **In the car:** in CarPlay "Play Dungeon Master" reads the newest narration aloud and takes the spoken action; short fixed questions about the own hero (life, bag, drink a healing potion, who is missing, again; at most 60 characters, `dungeon_auto.FRAGEN`) are answered by the panel with fixed sentences, everything else is the action of the open turn. Every narration has its own number (`k`). `POST /api/profile/dungeon/auto` (`{text, nach, lang}`, answer `{sagen, n, warten, aus}`), also for the iPhone app, rate limit `dungeonauto` (60 a minute); Status → Live counts the steps in the car, log `dungeon: auto | leben`.

**AI Dungeon Master: pictures:** Pictures come only when Features → Painting pictures is released for the host and the picture model is installed; otherwise the game runs without them. **Scene picture:** when the place changes (`szene.ort`, also in the prologue), the model gives `bild` in the same JSON: an English scene description, at most 300 characters. It is only data and is checked like a picture wish: the word list of "Painting pictures", the tone's words, the list "nie", the story's taboos, no names of people the Spark knows. The panel puts the world's fixed style in front (`welten[].bildstil`, the admin may change it, only letters, digits and punctuation), in the 8+ tone always the picture-book style, and "no text, no letters, no watermark" after it. It is painted at 1024×576 through the queue "Heavy jobs" (one model at a time, voice first, maintenance pause "bild"); the picture check throws away NSFW above 0.5, without the check there is no picture. **Display:** the newest picture stands large above the narration without a mark on it (the AI label is in the PNG file), in the log small at its turn; while painting, "The scene picture is being painted …" or the queue place shows. Guests get pictures through their seat code (`POST /api/dungeon/bild`), only of their own round. **Limits:** the host's "🖼 Paint the scene now" takes the last description (else place and mood); at most one picture every 3 turns, 12 per round, 40 a day for the whole game (`grenzen.bilder_tag`, 0 to 500, counted in the rounds file, so across a restart). Otherwise the button is grey and names the reason. **Hero portrait:** a tick when creating or "🖼 Have a portrait painted" on the sheet paints 512×512 from people, class and the sentence about the character (fixed portrait style), painted again once per level; the portrait shows on the hero cards, stays with the hero (profile or hero code) and goes with it. **Map and cover:** the side column shows visited places as a chain (without the model, at most 20, carried into the next chapter); when a chapter ends its last scene picture becomes the chapter's cover (at most 20 per campaign), shown in the campaign list and in the iPhone app under *My things → Dungeon Master*, with the portraits of the own heroes. Stored in `dungeon-bild/` in the state folder: scene pictures go with the round, portraits with the hero, covers with the campaign, "Delete all game data" takes everything; hourly the panel clears pictures nobody owns. Status → Live counts pictures painted, discarded, failed and on their way; logs: `dungeon: bild | ok | szene | 14000 ms`, `dungeon: bild | abgelehnt | abstand`, `dungeon: bild | ohne_pruefung | szene | 900 ms`, `dungeon: portraet | abgelehnt | stufe`. API: `GET /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/bild/{b}`, `GET …/{rid}/portraet/{pid}`, `POST …/{rid}/bild` (host), `POST …/{rid}/portraet`, `GET /api/profile/dungeon/held/{hid}/bild`, `GET /api/profile/dungeon/kampagne/{kid}/bild/{n}`; for guests `POST /api/dungeon/bild|portraet|portraet/malen`; rate limit `dungeonbild`.

**AI Dungeon Master: your turn, game date and look back:** So that a round runs over days, the game reaches out by itself. **Your turn:** when a new turn opens and a profile is not at the table (no request for 15 seconds), "It is your turn in "title" (turn n)." comes once per turn through the profile's own ways (Me → Notifications, quiet hours apply). **Reminder:** when the turn still waits, ""title" is still waiting for your turn n." comes once: after half the turn's time when others acted already, else after 6 hours. Whoever acted or sits at the table gets nothing; nothing during a pause or a narration. The texts are fixed, never a sentence of the game. At the table "🔔 Notes on/off" switches this for the own seat; guests without a profile and profiles the admin blocked get none. Once a minute the panel closes a turn whose time is over, even with no page open; the Dungeon Master starts on the panel's event loop (`dungeon._go`). **Game date:** the host sets the next date under "Game date" (in the future, 60 days at most); 15 minutes before, every profile seat gets ""title" goes on at hh:mm." once. "Add to calendar" downloads an .ics file (two hours, reminder 15 minutes before), guests too through their seat code. **Look back:** when someone comes to the table after more than 12 hours (kept in the browser), "Last time" stands above the narration: the last two narrations, latest loot and levels, from the round's log, without the model; "Play on" closes it. Status → Live counts game dates and notes sent today; logs: `dungeon: melden | dran`, `dungeon: melden | erinnert`, `dungeon: melden | termin`, `dungeon: melden | an`, `dungeon: termin | gesetzt`, `dungeon: termin | kalender`. API: `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/melden` `{an}`, `POST …/{rid}/termin` `{t}` (host, 0 removes it), `GET …/{rid}/termin.ics`; for guests `POST /api/dungeon/termin`.

**AI Dungeon Master: better stories:** Six parts without a switch, because they only concern the own language model. **World truth:** before the prologue the model writes a hidden truth once per round (3 to 6 people with role, goal and way of speaking, up to 3 groups and 3 secrets); the panel checks it (fixed lengths, no hero names, no doubles, word list) and gives it to every turn as facts. New people from narrations are added (at most 12). Only the host sees it under "Truth of this chapter". **Open threads:** with each narration the model reports new tasks, promises or riddles (at most 2) and finished ones (only open ones can be closed); at most 8 stand under "Open threads". **Running summary:** every 5 turns the model sums up the round in at most 900 characters; each turn then gets only the summary and the last two narrations. **Rewrite:** the panel checks every narration: too short (under 80 characters), a hero with an action missing, or repetition (more than 35 % equal word groups). Then it asks once more with a fixed hint; otherwise the first one stays. **Style and tone:** each world has fixed style examples (`welten[].stil`, up to 3 pairs German/English, 20 to 400 characters); the narration asks with more variety (temperature 0.85, repetition penalty), checks and summary stay sober. World truth and threads go on into the next chapter. **Thumbs:** under each narration 👍 and 👎 per seat (guests too); the admin sees the numbers per world and tone under "Adjust rules", never who pressed what. Status → Live counts world truths, summaries, rewrites and thumbs today; logs: `dungeon: wahrheit | ok`, `dungeon: wahrheit | ausfall`, `dungeon: kurz | ok`, `dungeon: kurz | ausfall`, `dungeon: kurz | fehler`, `dungeon: nachbessern | namen`, `dungeon: nachbessern | kurz`, `dungeon: nachbessern | wiederholung`, `dungeon: daumen | hoch`, `dungeon: daumen | runter`. API: `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/daumen` `{zug, gut}`, for guests `POST /api/dungeon/daumen`.

**AI Dungeon Master: big windows for hero sheet, merchant and map:** The side column shows a short card of the own hero (portrait, level, life, gold, bag, hints on a new level, trade offers and things on the ground) with "📜 Hero sheet", "⚖ Merchant" (only with an open market) and "🗺 Map". They open big windows over the narration: the hero sheet with values, abilities and voice on the left, the nine places and the bag as tiles with pictures on the right (drag and drop), below them ground and trading; the merchant with goods as big cards (picture in the rarity's frame, values, price, "Buy"), below them what the bag can sell or have identified; the map as the large map view. A tap on a thing in the bag, equipment, at the merchant or on the ground opens its window with big picture, rarity, weight, description, values and fitting buttons (equip, drink, cast, identify, sell, give, put down, unequip, buy, pick up). ✕, Esc or a tap beside closes it (Esc the item window first); on a phone it fills the screen. If the Dungeon Master narrates on, "New turn …" with "To the table" shows at the top. The window always shows the panel's state, the panel checks every action, there are no new endpoints. Guests get the same windows. Self-test `Windows` in `app/tests/test_dungeon.py` (browser at 1280, 390 and 320 px).

**AI Dungeon Master: a pleasant wait:** While the Dungeon Master narrates, the table shows at the bottom of the story where it stands: the steps reading, dice, narrating with one fixed sentence each (a rewrite has its own), the panel's rolls right away, three lore cards of the world (peoples, places, rules, seafaring, spaceflight …) and a hint "While you wait". All texts are fixed (`app/panel/dungeon_warten.json`: 122 progress sentences per step and world, 160 lore cards, 30 hints), fine from age 8 and checked against the word list of the youngest tone, never from the model; a round does not repeat them soon (a step's sentences after 10 at the earliest, cards after 90). A new narration appears sentence by sentence (without motion with "reduce motion"). When someone else is writing their action, "✎ Name is typing …" shows above the field (memory only, 6 seconds). Below the field lies the "💬 Table talk": short lines only between the round's seats (up to 200 characters, cleaned like actions, the last 40 stay); the Dungeon Master never reads it, it is in no facts and no task for the model (self-test). Status → Live counts rounds waiting for the narration and lines of table talk today; logs: `dungeon: tisch | gesagt`, `dungeon: tisch | abgelehnt`. API: `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/tisch` `{text}` (rate limit `dungeontisch`), `POST …/{rid}/tippt` (rate limit `dungeontippt`); for guests `POST /api/dungeon/tisch` and `POST /api/dungeon/tippt` via the seat code. Self-tests `Waiting` and `Windows.test_waiting_and_table_talk_at_the_table` in `app/tests/test_dungeon.py`.

**AI Dungeon Master: deleting old rounds and narration by the map:** In "Your rounds" an ended round has "Delete" next to "Open" (host: the round with its read-aloud sound and pictures is gone) or "Hide" (player: only off the own list); the second tap ("Really?") confirms. Only the host ends a running round. If "Open" fails, the page scrolls to the message. Logs: `dungeon: runde | geloescht`, `dungeon: runde | ausgeblendet`. When a narration names a map place the group cannot be at (neither its place, a neighbour nor the chosen way), the panel asks once more with the fixed hint to narrate only the map's place and ways (`dungeon: nachbessern | karte`); the scene's place is always the map's.

**AI Dungeon Master: items and values explained, tools that work:** Each of the 137 catalog items has a fixed description in German and English (`info` in `dungeon_regeln.json`, at most 160 characters, fine for the youngest tone): what it is, does and helps with; it shows at the merchant, in the bag and in the item window. On hero card and hero sheet a tap on a value (STR, DEX, CON, INT, WIS, CHA), on protection, life, level, gold or a worn thing explains right below what it is and what it counts for; a second tap closes it. The values explain themselves from `werte[].info`, life, level, protection and gold with fixed sentences. Tools (climbing rope, lockpick, compass, puzzle cube, lantern …, 33 with the SRD catalog) are a kind of their own, `werkzeug`: sign 🧰, picture text "a tool or a piece of gear", filter "Tools" on the rules page, price `preise.werkzeug`; `hilft` is allowed on `werkzeug` and `rolle` (scroll, treasure map and the admin's older own tools stay valid). Fixed effect (`hilft`: values and words): when the action names the tool (name or one of its words) and the check fits one of its values, the panel adds the bonus by quality (`dungeon.WERKZEUG`), one tool per check at most. On a rolled 1 it breaks and leaves the bag; compass, spyglass, everburning torch and treasure map (`fest`) never break. The roll shows "🔧 Lockpick helps" or "🔧 Lockpick broke", the narrator learns both in the checks. Log: `dungeon: werkzeug | kaputt`. The admin's own items may have `info` and `hilft`, both checked by `check_rules`.

**AI Dungeon Master: throwing your own die and the check bar:** When a check for your hero comes during the wait, a big d20 with "?" shows: a tap (or a shake, where the browser gives motion without asking, such as Android) rolls it and it lands on the number the panel rolled before (`dungeon.roll`); the tap does not change the result. The other seats see "X is rolling …" without a number until then (the panel does not send it), after the throw (`POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/wurf`, guest `POST /api/dungeon/wurf`, rate limit `dungeonwurf`) or after 12 seconds at the latest (`dungeon_warten.WURF_S`) it rolls for them too. Below every roll the check bar shows die, bonus and tool as a bar up to the red line of the difficulty, with "just made it!" or "just missed" one point away. A 20 throws sparks, a 1 wobbles; with own sounds for success, failure, the 20 and the 1, made in the browser and only when sounds are on. A thrown die does not roll again in the narration. With "reduce motion" only the number shows. Live: "n rolls thrown today". Log: `dungeon: wurf | geworfen`.

**AI Dungeon Master: time to throw and a throw that bounces:** If your own die is not thrown yet when the narration comes, it stays in the feed at its place with a ring that runs down for 30 seconds; the narration after it is blurred until then ("Throw first, then it goes on"), afterwards it throws itself. Only your own seat sees it waiting, the others read on. The panel rolled the number before, the tap only shows it (`rd["wurf_offen"]` with seat, check and turn, never the number in the view). The throw flies over the card, hits with its shadow, hops, rolls out and always lands on the panel's number, about 1.4 seconds with a click on each impact. Holding before letting go throws further (display only). On landing it glows green when made, grey when missed, gold with sparks on a 20, cracked on a 1; the phone buzzes briefly where the browser allows. An own die announces itself with a soft chime. With "reduce motion" the number shows at once. Everything is drawn in the browser, no library and no files.

**AI Dungeon Master: real dice at the table, fanfare, turning over and campfire:** **Real dice** (a tool of the game master console, no switch of its own): the host chooses which seats use their own dice (`rd["echt"]`). Before the panel rolls a turn's checks, `dungeon_echt.real_ask` asks these seats: in big letters "Throw a d20 for Dexterity against 14" (two d20 with advantage or disadvantage), below a number pad 1 to 20, "↶ Back", "Take it" and 🎤 (the game's speech recognition, number words eins to zwanzig and one to twenty). The others see "Mara is throwing at the table …". The panel takes only whole numbers from 1 to 20, only from the own seat, only for the turn asked, exactly as many as asked and only once (rate limit `dungeonecht`). Then it goes on as always with the given number as this seat's d20 (`Hand.d20`); luck and heroic courage are rolled by the panel. The dice receipt says "real". If no number comes within 30 seconds (`WARTE_S`), the panel rolls. **Never in the car:** if a profile plays from the car (`dungeon_auto.im_auto`), its seat is not asked; a guest has no car. Doors: `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/echt` `{zug, zahlen}` and `POST /api/dungeon/echt` `{code, zug, zahlen}`. Live: asked, real throws, rolled by the panel after 30 s, refused (numbers only); log `dungeon: echt | frage | n plaetze`, `dungeon: echt | alle da …`, `dungeon: echt | rueckfall …`, `dungeon: echt | eingetragen`, `dungeon: echt | abgelehnt | …`. **Fanfare:** when the narrator reads the evening's honours (log kind `ehrung`), a short, soft fanfare sounds once (WebAudio, no file). **Turning over:** when a natural 20 or 1 sets off an entry of the critical or fumble table (`krit_tabelle`, `patzer_tabelle` on the throw, a fixed name of the panel), the die card turns over on landing and shows "Critical: …" or "Fumble: …"; with "reduce motion" without turning. **Campfire:** while the group rests (`rd["rast"]`), the music mood "Campfire" per world plays (calm, 70 beats per minute, the world's major key, guitar, flute, soft hand drum, warm bass), with crackling fire in the background. Only a fight comes first. The store has eight moods per world; the storage defaults to 2400 MB. The table "Music at the table" in the admin window counts its columns from the moods: first the world, then one column per mood (the browser test `test_dungeon_musik.Grid` checks the alignment).

**AI Dungeon Master: real dice in a fight, short wait, difficulty as a word:** In a fight the Spark asks every seat with own dice whose action attacks by the fixed rules (`dungeon_kampf.attackers`, the same choice as the attack itself) "Throw a d20 for the attack on X" instead of its checks; X is the foe named, else the first still standing. The number goes into the attack roll, the attack says "real dice". With advantage or disadvantage the Spark rolls the second d20. A hero a prop of the fight takes rolls the prop's check. If a seat gives no number twice in a row, the turn waits only 10 seconds (`KURZ_S`, else 30); the count is kept in the round (`echt_aus`) and forgets a seat the host deselects. Check bar and question give the difficulty as a word after the SRD table ("target 15 (medium)": 5 very easy, 10 easy, 15 medium, 20 hard, 25 very hard, 30 nearly impossible, numbers between take the step below, `dmStufe`). Live: attacks asked and short waits (numbers only); log `dungeon: echt | frage | n plaetze | n angriff | n kurz`. Self-tests `Rules.test_an_attack_is_one_question_instead_of_the_checks`, `Rules.test_two_turns_without_a_number_wait_short` and `Table.test_an_attack_in_a_fight_takes_the_own_die`.

**AI Dungeon Master: sounds for just missed, the foe's announcement, alarm and torch:** Everything sounds in the browser (`dungeon-ton.js`, WebAudio, no files) and follows only fields the panel sets; the model names no sound. **Just missed:** when a throw in the dice receipt carries `knapp` (the check missed by 1 or 2 and the hero paid the price, `dungeon_knapp` then sets `ok`), the wait takes `knapp`, the fixed price id `preis` and the price's text `preis_t` (`dungeon_warten.step`, for other seats only once the die is open); the die card shows "Just made it, at a price: …" with a short shake (not with "reduce motion") and two slightly falling tones instead of the success tone. **Foe's announcement:** a log entry of kind `ansage` blows a deep warning horn. **Critical and fumble on an attack:** when a log entry `angriff` (or `wurf`) carries `krit_tabelle` or `patzer_tabelle`, a bright run or three falling tones sound. **Vault:** when `karte.alarm` (0 to 6) rises to 3, a bell strikes once, at 6 the storm bell. While `karte.fackel` is a number (rooms of light left, −1 burns always), the torch crackles softly; at 0 it goes out audibly and a dark hiss with drops remains. Outside a vault (`fackel` missing) the place's sound counts. No own switch: the sounds belong to "Sounds" at the table, each device switches them itself.

**AI Dungeon Master: bard's hero song:** Admin switch *Features → AI Dungeon Master → Pictures and hero song* (`chat.dungeon_bilder`, off from the start), plus music at the table ("Prepare music" and the music model). When a chapter ends (log kind `kapitel_ende` with `kapitel`), the own music model sings a song of 60 to 90 seconds (60 and 6 per hero) in German, in the world's sound (`STIL_LIED`: lute in fantasy, cello in horror, guitar and pads in science fiction, accordion with pirates). The text comes only from fixed verses of `dungeon_lied.py`: "Hört, was im zweiten Kapitel geschah:", per hero one fixed deed line after a word list from the chapter's log (hit or critical hit, spell, new level, loot, else "ging mutig stets voran"), one chorus per world and a closing line. A name is sung only if it is plain (letters, space, apostrophe, hyphen, at most 24) and the word list of Make music lets it through, else "ein Held"; brackets like `[chorus]` thus never get into the lyrics. The song is made on the heavy jobs' place after the voice (`dungeon_musik.render`) and before the store's pieces, one try per chapter. At the table it plays once (then the mood's music again); under 🎵 in the music bar the round's songs can be heard again, the host deletes one with 🗑 ("Really?"). Only a seat of its round can hear a song (`GET /api/profile/dungeon/{{rid}}/musik/{{id}}` and `POST /api/dungeon/musik`); delete: `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{{rid}}/lied/{{id}}/weg`, only the host, rate limit `dungeonmusik`. Each round keeps at most 20 songs, at night those of rounds that are gone go. Storage `STATE/dungeon-lieder/` (MP3 with the heroes' names). Live: songs, sung today, did not come (numbers only); log `dungeon: lied | ok | kapitel N | s | ms`, `dungeon: lied | geloescht`, `dungeon: lied | loeschen abgelehnt | …`.

**AI Dungeon Master: real dice in the console:** The host chooses in the game master console under "At the table" at "🎲 Own dice" which seats throw their own d20 (a tap on a name switches, 🚗 shows who is playing in the car and is not asked). Only seats of the round with a hero, each once, only with the admin switch, only the host sees the choice (`dungeon_echt.choose`, `POST /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/erzaehler` `{echt: [seat, …]}`, log `dungeon: echt | plaetze | n` or `… | abgelehnt`). When a turn closes, `run_turn` asks these seats after the checks and before the attacks (`dungeon_echt.real_ask`); `dungeon.roll` and `dungeon_kampf.attacks` take a seat's d20 from its numbers first. Throw and attack carry "real" in the dice receipt (W-n · real), and the die does not wait for a tap at the table. Without a number after 30 seconds the panel rolls.

**AI Dungeon Master: own voices for narrator and characters, clear messages:** When the speech output can make voices from a description (Settings → Speech output "Also serve VoiceDesign"), the Dungeon Master reads with voices from fixed descriptions (`dungeon_stimmen.json`, `dungeon_stimmen.py`). Every new story gets a narrator voice of its world (six per world and four for all, such as "old sea dog, rough" for pirates), never one of the same host's last three rounds; a campaign keeps it. The host picks it under "🎙 Narrator's voice", rolls another with "🎲 New" or listens with "▶ Sample". Fixed mood key words (eerie, battle, tense, sad, cheerful, calm) add a fixed addition, such as quieter and slower when eerie. Every NPC has its own voice: on its first appearance the model only names traits from fixed lists (age, gender, sound, nature), the panel builds the description from fixed English words and keeps it for round and campaign (40 at most); a missing or unfitting trait comes from the name, always the same. The traits are not in the log. Without VoiceDesign the fixed voices of the rules count. Live: number of NPC voices. Log: `dungeon: erzaehler | neu` or `gewaehlt`. Every refusal of the panel says its reason (`dungeon._http` sends the fixed reason word, such as `held spielt`, `runden`, `tasche voll`); the page shows a whole sentence with what to do (`DMGRUND` in `dungeon.js`) in a notice box with ⚠, such as "This hero is playing in another round right now. End it there or pick another hero." `test_dungeon.Refusals` checks every reason word has its sentence. When the second engine is not running (loading, stopped, failed; log `dungeon: stimmen | voicedesign <reason> | feste stimme`), the fixed voice stands in, for the narrator the one from the rules: reading aloud goes on, a sample adds "Voices from a description are not running right now" and is not kept; after 10 minutes the panel asks again. When the speech output is only busy, it says "busy right now".

**AI Dungeon Master: right column and class choice:** At the table the right column shows the own hero on top (the turn stands in the head), below it tabs with only one open at a time; the device remembers the last one. The class cards count what the class can do ("1 power · 20 spells"); below them the chosen class's starting powers and spells by grade stand as small chips. Class names do not break in the middle of a word.

**AI Dungeon Master: a prologue with a hold:** The prologue has a fixed shape: place and mood, a clear quest (who wants what from the group, why, what reward waits), a first lead, at least 700 characters. If it is shorter, has no quest or fewer than two possibilities, the panel sends it back once; the second try counts. Below it each hero gets one sentence why they are here (only names of the group), and **What you can do** with two to four buttons; a tap writes the possibility into the own field, own ideas still go. The **quest** stands in the side column until the narration reports a goal reached (then ticked); the model gets it every turn. Without a story plan the panel picks a kind of quest from a fixed list (rescue, search, protection, mystery, escape); with a plan its goal counts, also without a quest from the model. All of it is the model's text, checked like every narration (tone, "never", taboos). A tap on a worn thing on a hero card opens its big window with picture, rarity, description and values; for the own hero with "Unequip", for others only to look at. Dice (1d6+2, d20) stand out everywhere in the game as a violet box, bonuses green, penalties red: in narrations, rolls, values, items and "What I can do". **Side column:** the four tabs map, host, threads and date are equally wide with the sign above the word; the map is the first tab, large via ⤢. In the tab *Host* "▶ Go on now" stands wide on top with "waiting for …", below it four tiles of the same size with their state (pause, market, read aloud, scene picture), invite and the narrator's voice as one row each, ending the chapter or round behind "⋯ More".

**AI Dungeon Master: painted pictures, the class large, a hero sheet with tabs:** Peoples, classes and every class's abilities have a painted picture in every world's style (the fixed texts name no genre, the world's picture style says it, for bestiary foes too), from a fixed text of the panel (the English name, for abilities their sentence), painted like the item pictures (Features → AI Dungeon Master → Pictures and hero song, at night or on the button, the same check and day limit). Until a picture is there a placeholder stands. When **making a hero** the people and class cards show the picture, bonuses in colour below, on the class "1 power · 10 spells". The chosen class stands large: picture, sentence, main values, life per level and powers as picture tiles by grade (starting powers, cantrips, grade 1); a tap explains a tile right below with picture, kind, sentence and values. Peoples and classes have a short sentence (`satz`, optional, checked by `check_rules`). The **hero sheet** shows the portrait large on the top left (or the colour), beside it life, experience, the six values as tiles with a coloured bonus, and protection, gold and bag; below four tabs: *Equipment* (the slots), *What I can do* (powers as picture tiles, coloured on top by kind: red attack, green healing, blue protection, gold other, with a filter and charges per grade as dots), *Bag* (bag and trading) and *Spend points* (new value points, a new ability, voice, hero code). The last tab stays remembered on the device; "New level: spend it in the hero sheet" opens *Spend points*. A tap on a power opens its window with picture, description, values and "✨ Cast" ("Use" for abilities), which writes the sentence into the own action; the panel checks it like every action. A power's kind comes from fixed catalog words (`dungeon.power_kind`), never the model. No new endpoints. Self-tests `Heroes` in `app/tests/test_dungeon_dingbild.py` and `Windows` in `app/tests/test_dungeon.py`. **Guest mails:** if no mailbox is chosen for guests with e-mail, the page says "No mailbox is chosen for guest mails yet …", and the log names a fixed reason for every guest mail that did not go: `dungeon: gastmail | code nicht gesendet | kein postfach` (or `kein tls`, `nicht erreichbar`, `anmeldung`, `empfaenger`, `abgelehnt`, `abbruch`, `ausgang fehlt`, `notaus`), never the address or the server's text. The mailbox choice also lists mailboxes of known providers without an own outgoing server (iCloud, Gmail, GMX, WEB.DE: the known server counts). Self-tests `GuestMail.test_a_failed_code_mail_says_why` and `test_a_known_provider_mailbox_can_be_picked`. **After "Act":** the own action stands as a green card with ✓ ("Your action for turn 3", the text, "Waiting for Gandalf · at the latest in 1:55"), pops in briefly and a phone vibrates once. "Change" brings the text back into the field; a new "Act" replaces the action while the turn is open. The own name never stands under "Waiting for" after acting. If the panel refuses, the text stays in the field with the reason below. Once everyone has acted, the narration shows the steps reading, dice, narrating. Self-test `Windows.test_acting_shows_the_own_action`. **A seat link fills the window:** a seat link opens the table over the full browser area, without the assistant behind it and without the assistant icon at the bottom right; whoever is signed in with a profile gets back with "Close". A guest without sign-in gets no "Close", everything else of the page (sign-in, emergency access, search) is hidden and not reachable by keyboard; the server lets a seat code reach only `/api/dungeon/*` of its own seat. Self-test `Windows.test_a_seat_link_fills_the_browser`. **Managing fellow players:** as the host every fellow player's hero card has "⋯" with **New link** (guests only: the old link stops at once, the hero stays, the new one shows once with its QR code), **Stop waiting** (the turn no longer waits for them until they act again; "Wait again" undoes it) and **Take off the table** after asking once more (a hero with a profile stays in the profile, with a guest account in the guest account, otherwise it is gone). A free seat whose link nobody used for 72 hours goes by itself. Endpoint `PUT /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/platz/{pid}` (host only, sign-in, CSRF, size and rate limit), logs `dungeon: platz | link`, `dungeon: platz | ruhen`, `dungeon: platz weg | 1 frei abgelaufen`. Self-tests `Seats` and `Windows.test_acting_shows_the_own_action`.

**AI Dungeon Master: pictures in the right places:** The newest scene picture stands once, large, at its place in the log, scrolls with the story and never lies over the text. Older scene pictures stay small with their place, "The scene picture is being painted …" stands as a calm line at the end of the log. Every **action** in the log shows the hero's portrait, otherwise its initial. Where **no picture** is painted (peoples, classes, powers, the portrait in the hero sheet), a placeholder stands: the sign on a calm ground (for powers ⚔ attack, ✚ healing, 🛡 protection, ✦ other, otherwise the initial), the large ones with "No picture yet". No new endpoints. Self-test `Windows.test_pictures_once_in_the_story` in `app/tests/test_dungeon.py`.

**AI Dungeon Master: reading aloud piece by piece, every character with its own voice:** A turn is made **piece by piece** (one or a few sentences, the first very short), each its own file; the devices play them in order while the next ones are made, so the first sound comes after a second or two. Every piece gets a **second try**, then the speech output's own voice speaks; if that fails too, only that piece is left out. Reading aloud runs on **every device** that has it on, guests and the iPhone app too: one tap on "🔊 Sound on" unlocks the page's sound (browsers allow sound only after a tap). 🔈/🔇 switches for this device only, "⏹ Stop reading" stops, "🔊 Listen again" reads the last turn again. Whoever joins mid-turn hears nothing old. While it reads, **the clock waits**: reading and turn time start only after the last piece, at most 4 minutes later ("being read aloud, the clock starts afterwards"). **Voices:** under Features → AI Dungeon Master → Adjust rules, part "Voices", there are three fixed voices, preset narrator "ryan", NPCs (men) "eric", NPCs (women) "serena"; if one is missing, the speech output's own voice speaks. What stands in quotes is spoken **by the character, never by the narrator**: whoever is named right after it ("…", says Grimma) or last before it, an NPC in its voice (women and girls in the women's voice), a hero in its own, otherwise the NPC voice. If the same line also stands in the NPC field, it is read once. The model is told to put quoted speech with the name into the NPC field. With VoiceDesign the described voices count. A **hero** at the table gets a voice proposed that is neither a fixed one nor another hero's at the table; changeable in the hero sheet. Module `dungeon_vorlesen.py`, pieces under `STATE/dungeon-ton/<round>-<turn>-<piece>.wav` (only the last three turns), endpoint `GET /api/profile/dungeon/{rid}/ton/{n}/{k}`, for guests `POST /api/dungeon/ton` {code, zug, k} (sign-in or seat code, size and rate limit). One log line per piece with milliseconds: `dungeon: vorlesen | teil | ok` (or `nochmal`, `ersatz`, `fehler`) `| 812 ms`, at the end `dungeon: vorlesen | ok | 7 teile | 9400 ms` (or `teils`, `ausfall`, `pause`). The live overview shows "reading aloud running", turns read aloud today, pieces with a stand-in voice and failures. Self-tests `ReadAloud`, `Voice` and `Windows.test_reading_aloud_piece_by_piece_on_this_device` in `app/tests/test_dungeon.py`.

**AI Dungeon Master: look at foes and loot closely, voices for side characters:** **Look closely:** a tap on a foe of the encounter card or the fight box opens a big window: big picture, name, count, size, description, in a fight its life as a bar, level, life, armour class, attack, damage and XP as chips, the six scores with bonus and under "Can cause" the conditions its hits can cause (from the bestiary, `dungeon_kampf.card`; the fight box via `dungeon_kampf.view`). ‹ › or the arrow keys page through all foes of the encounter or fight. A tap on a loot line opens the item: in the own bag with "Equip" and the bag's buttons, on the ground with "Pick up", otherwise to look at (`dungeonfenster.js`, keys `g:`, `kg:`, `b:`). Browser test `Windows.test_a_foe_and_a_loot_line_open_a_big_window` at 1280 and 390 pixels. **Voices of side characters:** without VoiceDesign the fixed male and female voice count. With VoiceDesign every side character has its own description with a speaking pace (slow, even, quick, halting) and timbres such as hoarse, nasal, velvety, scratchy (`dungeon_stimmen.json` "npc", `MERKMALE`); characters without a kept pace get it from their name. Self-test: 60 names give at least 50 different voices, every description at most 80 characters. **For the car:** `set_pause(rid, uid, an)` is a function of its own so "pause the round" in the car uses the same rule.

**AI Dungeon Master: checks with their purpose, ways of speaking, values as chips:** **Checks:** every check carries its purpose (`wofuer`, at most 60 characters): the model's words, checked like its text (plain text, word filter), else the start of the player's action. The check card in story and dice view shows "Gandalf rolls Intelligence", the purpose large, below it "14 +2 = 16 vs difficulty 13" and on the right "success" or "failed"; at the own throw the purpose stands large above the die. **Ways of speaking:** with VoiceDesign every character speaks a quote the way the text says: the words right after the quote ("…", flüstert er mit archaischer Stimme) or the clause with a colon before it (Kael zischt: "…") are matched against a fixed list of 20 ways of speaking (`dungeon_stimmen.json` "sprechweise": whispering, shouting, stammering, growling, hissing, laughing, sobbing, murmuring, trembling, angry, ancient, cold, soft, hurried, mocking, commanding, tired, singing, pleading, warning); at most two fixed English additions go onto the voice description (`stimmen.weise`). The model's text never reaches the speech output. Without VoiceDesign the fixed voices count. Log `dungeon: sprechweise | 2` (quotes with a way of speaking in the turn). **Values as chips:** people and class cards when creating show values as small chips that never break: the people's bonus ("KON +2", "WEI +1"), the class's main values, "❤ life", "✦ powers" and "✨ spells"; names stand whole, two cards a row on a phone. No new endpoints. Self-tests `Turns.test_every_check_says_what_it_is_for`, `Voices.test_a_quote_is_spoken_the_way_the_text_says` and browser tests `Windows.test_waiting_and_table_talk_at_the_table`, `Windows.test_side_tabs_and_class_detail`. **Overview in the middle, full screen:** on wide screens the rounds overview and heroes stand in the middle, at most 1240 pixels wide, world cards spread over the row, input fields stay readably narrow; "⛶ Full screen" stands in the overview's head (one source `dmVollKnopf`, also at the table). Browser test `Windows.test_overview_in_the_middle_and_full_screen` at 1920 and 390 pixels. **Only on the fitting body part:** there is a tenth place "belt" (`SLOTS`, `PLATZ`, `PLATZ_ART`) for belt, sash and belt of giant strength. An item dragged to a place it does not belong is refused (`platz falsch`, log line `dungeon: platz | falsch`) with the note that every item belongs on its body part; while dragging, fitting places light up and the others fade. Wrongly worn items in rounds or stored heroes move on reading to a free fitting place, else into the bag (`right_place`). Self-test `Equipment.test_only_on_the_fitting_body_part` also checks the catalogue: whatever is named -gürtel, -stiefel, -handschuhe, -helm, -amulett, -halskette or -schild sits at the fitting place.

**AI Dungeon Master: longer narrations at the moments, way of telling per round:** **Length by moment:** before every narration the panel decides by fixed rules whether the turn is a moment (`moment_of`): "ort" when an action asks for a neighbouring map place (name or direction, `dungeon_karte.named_way`), "kampf" in a fight's first turn or when a foe falls, "wurf" on a natural 20 or 1.
