**Sort every mail here (part of "Mails ordnen", no switch of its own):** in the "⋯" (or right click, a long press on the iPhone) at the inbox, the archive and every own folder, "Sort every mail here" sorts every mail of that folder once, down to the oldest (the newest 50,000), not only 90 days like "old mail". Per mail the first fitting rule decides: first your mail rules (only their folder), then, where inbox tidying is on for the mailbox, its stages 1 to 3 (protected senders, tidying rules, fixed header signs). The language model never decides here: a mail without a fitting rule stays, so does one whose target is the folder itself. First a proposal comes in the background with progress ("1,240 to Rechnungen, 830 to Spark/Newsletter; 2,100 stay"); mails move only after "Move" (with the code like any bulk change), in packages of 100; "Stop" halts after the running step. "Undo" brings every mail of the last run back by its Message-ID; after "Close", "Undo the sorting" stays in the folder's "⋯". Never out of sent, drafts, trash or junk, never deleted, never flagged; if the mail server renumbered the folder, nothing moves. One run per profile, at most two at once on the Spark, speech goes first between the steps. `einordnen.py`, `GET/POST /api/profile/mail/einordnen`, `…/go`, `…/back` (both with the code), `…/stop` (rate limit `postordnen`); the proposal lives in memory only, the last run for "Undo" sealed in `mail-einordnen.bin` (30 days). Log `mail: sort folder …` with numbers only, Live: runs and mails moved today.

**Reading e-mail:** "Any new mail?", "What does Anna write?", "Read me the mail from the bank". Each profile connects up to 4 mailboxes over IMAP in the "Me" window, tab E-mail: iCloud (imap.mail.me.com, your iCloud mail address and a new app-specific password from appleid.apple.com), Gmail (imap.gmail.com, app password, needs 2-step verification), GMX and web.de (allow IMAP there first) or any other IMAP server with TLS. Outlook/Microsoft 365 is not supported (needs OAuth). The assistant only reads the inbox of the last 30 days and changes nothing: the mailbox is opened read only, no mail is sent, deleted, moved or marked as read. It summarizes mails and reads them out word for word only when asked; the daily briefing names the unread ones. Mail text is someone else's words: once the assistant read a mail in an answer, in that answer it cannot switch smart home devices, change memory, cancel reminders, search the web or enter appointments; answers made from mails are not learned into memory. Guests and another person's voice at a different device get no access. The password is stored encrypted on the Spark and never shown again. Times are given in your time zone (else the Spark's), not with the sender's offset. Off until turned on under Features.

**Inbox tidying** (Features → Mail and contacts → "Inbox tidying", off by default; needs reading e-mail; profiles only): the Spark sorts advertising, newsletters, invoices, notifications and social media mail into folders under a main folder (`Spark/` unless renamed, e.g. `Spark/Werbung`; renamed in the "Me" window under E-mail, empty puts the folders at the top; renaming also renames them in the mailbox with IMAP RENAME, the mail moves along; if the new name exists already, the old folder stays) so the inbox keeps only what matters. It only moves; it never deletes, never sends and never marks mail as read. Without IMAP MOVE (iCloud) it copies, checks the copy and only then removes exactly that original (UIDPLUS); no other mail is touched. Each profile picks per mailbox in the "Me" window, tab E-mail, part "Inbox tidying": off, preview (only shows what it would move; a week is recommended to start), safe automatic (only what a rule or clear headers such as List-Unsubscribe prove) or fully automatic (also when the model is at least 85 % sure). Below the switch Off / Preview / Safe / Full one line says what the chosen mode does; pointing at or tapping another shows its line. With the second login step, switching asks for the code. Only new mail is checked, every 5, 15 (default) or 60 minutes. Order: people you wrote to (sent folder), sign-in and security mails and your "always keep" list stay put; then your rules (sender or @domain); then fixed headers; only then the language model, which may only return a category from the fixed list and has no tools. It asks first for invoices, notifications and unclear mail; your answer can become a rule. When no folder fits, "＋ New folder" in the question adds an own one: type a name, "Create and file"; then the folder is made under the main folder, the mail moves there and "Always" turns it into a rule. The name follows a fixed rule (1 to 40 letters or digits, plus space and . , & ( ) + ' -, no `/`, no control characters, never a folder of the mail program such as inbox or trash); if it exists, the Spark takes that one. Own folders (at most 20) show in every choice and under "Folders and exceptions" (add, rename, remove). Only your answers and rules fill them, never the language model; "Remove" only takes them off the list and deletes their rules, folder and mail stay. A mail put back into the inbox is not moved again, and the Spark asks. Brakes: at most 200 mails per run; a run that would move more than half of at least 10 new mails stops and puts everything into the preview. Every move stays in the history for 30 days and can be undone. "Old mail" proposes once where the last 90 days belong; nothing moves until you press "Move". By voice: "Tidy my inbox", "Move everything from Lidl to advertising", "Undo that"; the assistant reads out the proposal and the panel carries it out only after your plain "yes". **Reply drafts** (per mailbox): "Write Anna that I'm coming" saves a draft in the drafts folder after your "yes"; you send it yourself in your mail app. Guests and other people's voices get no access.

**Learn mail style** (`mailstil.py`, Features → Mail and contacts → "Mailstil lernen", `chat.mail_style`, off by default; needs reading e-mail; once released for every profile, never guests): under *E-mail → Settings → My mail style*, "Learn style" reads the "Sent" folder of the chosen mailboxes read-only (EXAMINE and BODY.PEEK), at most the newest 200 mails of the last 12 months. The panel cuts quotes, "On … wrote", forwarded parts, signatures and "Sent from my iPhone" with fixed patterns and puts every mail into a group: "Sehr geehrte", "Guten Tag" or "Sie" → Work, "du" or "Hallo/Hi/Liebe" → Private, the first mail to someone also → Unknown. The panel counts the form of address (du, Sie, mixed) and the length (median of words) itself. The language model gets at most 40 own texts per group between `<<<MAIL` and `MAIL>>>` as data, without tools, in the background with speech first, and only fills a fixed form: greeting (names become `{Name}`), sign-off, tone (casual, friendly, matter-of-fact, formal), up to 8 typical phrases, up to 4 habits. The panel checks every field; addresses, numbers, links and marks are dropped, nothing is repaired. Only this summary is kept in `users/<p>/mailstil.json` (stays on the Spark), recipients only as a salted hash, never a mail. Every field can be adjusted with "Change" and stays when it learns again; "⋯ → Delete mail style" and "Delete data" remove everything. It learns only on the button. It works in mail drafts: by voice and in the chat `mail_draft` reshapes the draft in the style of the recipient's group (on an error the draft stays), "Draft a reply" in the Begleiter (Outlook classic, Mac Mail, the Outlook button "Antwort") gets the style as `<<<STIL … STIL>>>` (form and tone only); the panel finds the group from the "Von:" line, unknown senders get "Unknown" and the form of address of their mail. `"stil": false` in `/api/write/assist` writes once without it. The writing help page, Word, Excel and the browser stay without it. Logs only with fixed words: `mailstil: gelernt … postfaecher=1 mails=180 gruppen=3`, `mailstil: abgebrochen grund=kein_gesendet_ordner`, `mailstil: benutzt weg=begleiter gruppe=beruflich`. API (profile): `GET|PUT|DELETE /api/profile/mailstil`, `POST /api/profile/mailstil/learn`.

**For you from mail** (`fuerdich.py`; Features → Mail and contacts → "Für dich aus Mails", `chat.mail_insight`, off by default; needs reading e-mail; once released for every profile, never guests): under *E-mail → For you* (a tab of its own) the Spark shows what it found in the mails of the last 30 days. Everything is read read-only (EXAMINE and BODY.PEEK): the inbox, the tidying folders Werbung, Newsletter and Rechnungen when they exist, and the headers of "Sent". Fixed rules, no language model: **bill** (amount, deadline from "fällig am", "zahlbar bis" …, or "wird abgebucht"), **contract** (renews on …, cancel by …), **order** (order confirmation with amount), **offer** (advertising or newsletter with coupon/discount ending within 14 days, at most 5), **reply missing** (someone you wrote to before asks something and has had no answer for 2 to 14 days; by In-Reply-To and later mails to the address), **suspicious** (sender or subject names a known brand like Sparkasse, PayPal, DHL but does not come from its domain and wants something confirmed, or the header says DMARC failed; a brand only in the subject does not count when the mail server confirms that the sender's own domain sent the mail (DMARC, DKIM or SPF passed) and the subject itself does not urge to confirm or log in; a brand in the sender's name or a lookalike domain stays suspicious). Amounts and days count only when they parse; every sentence on a card is a fixed sentence with checked values. Sender name and subject are outside text and appear only as text; links from mails are never opened or linked. Per run at most 60 mail texts per mailbox, in the background with speech first (`vorrang.in_thread("Für dich")`), the result kept 10 minutes in memory. ✓ (done) and ✕ (not interesting / is genuine) keep only a checksum of the Message-ID in `users/<p>/fuerdich.json` (stays on the Spark); "⋯ → Forget the marks" and "Delete data" remove them. Under *Me → Overview* the card "For you" only counts how many cards need you (waits at most 3 seconds, else "being read"). Logs only with fixed words: `fuerdich: gelesen funde=3 fehler=0 sekunden=1.2`, `fuerdich: markiert als=done Profil …`. API (profile): `GET /api/profile/fuerdich` (`?fresh=1` reads again), `POST /api/profile/fuerdich/mark` `{"id", "as": "done"|"no"|"open"}`, `DELETE /api/profile/fuerdich`.

**For you, stage 2: buttons, voice, by itself:** every card gets the buttons the panel allows by fixed rules for its kind and the released functions (`fuerdich.acts`): **Remind me** (bill, contract, offer, reply missing; with "Timers and reminders") sets a reminder with a fixed text, e.g. "Rechnung Stadtwerke bezahlen (84,20 €)", at 9 am the day before the deadline, without a deadline tomorrow at 9 am, never sooner than in an hour. **Add to calendar** (cards with a deadline; with "Calendar") opens the calendar with a fixed line such as "31.10.2026 Kündigungsfrist congstar" in "Quick add"; it is saved only with "Add". **Draft a reply** (reply missing from the inbox; with "Send mail") opens the mail on the E-mail page with the reply form and reply suggestions in the own mail style. **Draft a cancellation** (contract; with "Send mail") opens a new mail with a fixed text ("hiermit kündige ich meinen Vertrag bei … fristgerecht zum …") to the sender, except no-reply addresses. Sending only goes through the preview sheet of the E-mail page. By voice: "What do I have to do this week?" reads the cards as numbered fixed sentences (tool `fuer_dich`, at most 8, mail data like any reading from mail), with the mail of every entry (sender, day, subject, folder outside the inbox); "reply missing" reads "… sent you a question 4 days ago and waits for your answer". When reading takes longer than 20 seconds the tool uses the last reading or says "still reading" (log `fuerdich: werkzeug laedt noch` or `… liste aus letzter lesung`). "Number two is done", "… not interesting" or "remind me of number one" acts through `fuer_dich_tun`, only with a number of the list read last (valid 30 minutes) and never in the same answer that read mail text. The daily briefing names at most three cards that need you. **Speak up by itself → Deadlines and suspicion** (`pro_fuerdich`, under E-mail → Settings, on once "Speak up by itself" is on): a bill or cancelling deadline due today or tomorrow and a suspicious mail, once per card, 8 am to 8 pm, at most 3 a day, fixed sentences only. It checks every 30 minutes from the last reading; with "New mail at once" (IMAP IDLE, `mailidle.py`, `chat.mail_idle`) on, right after a new mail (`mailidle.on_new(fuerdich.new_mail)`). The last reading per mailbox stays sealed in the profile (`fuerdich-vorrat.bin`, through `vorrat.Vorrat`, only found entries with sender, subject and time, never mail text): after a restart the tab shows it at once, and when a mailbox does not answer its entries come with "Stand hh:mm" (up to 24 hours old), in the tab, when read aloud and in the card under Today; without a kept reading the assistant says the mailbox cannot be read right now. The file goes with "forget marks", with the profile and after 14 days without a reading; Status → Live counts under E-Mail the answers from the last reading today. Endpoint `POST /api/profile/fuerdich/act` `{"id", "do": "remind"|"cancel"}` (own profile, only cards of the last reading, only allowed buttons, size limit, rate limit "fuerdich"). Logs: `fuerdich: erinnerung gesetzt`, `fuerdich: kuendigung entworfen`, `fuerdich: knopf abgelehnt grund=…`, `fuerdich: werkzeug liste funde=N`, `fuerdich: briefing teile=N`. iPhone: Today → For you with the same cards and buttons (swipe for Done and Not interesting, ⋯ for Read again and Forget the marks).

**What I know about you, stages 3 and 4:** a page of its own *Me → What I know about you* (`ichweiss.py`, part of "For you from mail", `chat.mail_insight`, no switch of its own; never guests). Layout: a title, one line of explanation, tabs "To sort out" (proposals and "Which topic is this?" with word buttons "Right/Not really" and "Take it/Leave it"; opens first while something waits, its count then orange), "Interests" (list on the left, details on the right, on the phone a page of its own with "‹ Interests"), "Recurring" and "Week & kept". **Interests:** a proposal is a topic from a fixed list (`ichweiss.TOPICS`, about 25 topics such as "Music production & samples", "Smart home & IoT", "Food delivery"; per topic fixed words and sender domains, whole words, no model), backed by headers of the last reading of For you (`fuerdich.shops`, never mail text) and keywords of Reddit and feeds. A sender gets the topic whose words stand in at least two and at least a third of its mails (subject and sender name) or whose domain it has. Under the topic its senders show as chips (✕ leaves one out) and up to three subjects as examples, as text only, in memory only, never in a file, log or search. A sender with offers or newsletters but no topic shows "Topic unclear": open its subjects and pick a topic (confirmed interests and fixed topics, with suggestions while typing; a name of the fixed list, German or English, becomes that topic) or type one. Never proposed: own addresses and the profile name, services and accounts (e.g. `notifications@github.com`, `security@`, `account@`) and machine mail (`Auto-Submitted`); a sender with orders only just when it has a topic. An interest counts only after "Right" (the topic with its senders; then offers of new senders with the same words fit too); "Not really" is remembered; own interests can be added ("＋ Add an interest", folded "Not really (n)"). "More of this" on an order or offer under *E-mail → For you* makes the shop's topic (else the shop) an interest; fitting offers come first with "fits …". The details show the senders (✕ takes one out, it does not come back as "Not really"), "✎ Rename or give a topic", the switch "Tips from the web" with the last tip, and "Forget". An old confirmed sender name without a topic shows in orange as "old sender, no topic yet" and is asked once with "Take it" or "Leave it"; with a topic it becomes a sender there; it is no topic to pick; the chips under "own topic" are called "Quick pick". Only name, topic, senders, state and time are kept, in `users/<p>/interessen.json` (at most 60). **From the web:** for an interest with "Tips from the web" a separate run searches at most once a day between 10 am and 7 pm in the own web search (SearXNG) for exactly the topic word, never with mail text or sender; the first hit with an https address becomes the tip (title as text, link only in a new tab without referrer), visible for three days. Needs the web search released. **Recurring:** bills of the same sender in three or more months around the same day (at most 6 days apart), from the headers of the last 200 days (read-only, once a day); "Remind me monthly" sets a monthly reminder "Rechnung … kommt um den 15." at 9 am. **Week & kept:** "Deine Woche: 3 erledigt, 2 offen. Nächste Woche: …" from the marks of the last 7 and the deadlines of the next 7 days; the month's sum of bills and orders only from checked amounts (also under E-mail → For you); tip of the day; the newest facts of the memory and the mail style with the ways to their pages. On Sundays from 6 pm the weekly review comes by itself (By itself: "Weekly review", `pro_rueckblick`, once a week, under E-mail → Settings). "⋯ → Forget every interest" and "Delete data" remove the file. iPhone: *For you → What I know about you* with the same tabs as a segment, a senders menu, "Topic unclear", an interest as its own page (swipe a sender left to take it out), "More of this" on the card, CarPlay row "Read deadlines aloud". State → Live: event "Was ich über dich weiß" (only "Netz-Tipp gefunden"). Logs (area "mail"): `ichweiss: angesehen vorschlaege=2 interessen=1 wiederkehrend=1 …`, `ichweiss: interesse als=yes …`, `ichweiss: erinnerung monatlich gesetzt …`, `ichweiss: netztipp gefunden|ohne treffer|fehler=…`, `ichweiss: kopfzeilen gelesen ordner=2 absender=5`, `fuerdich: mehr davon …`. API (profile): `GET /api/profile/ichweiss`, `POST /api/profile/ichweiss/interest` `{"name", "state": "yes"|"no"|"forget", "src", "web"}` with `topic` and `senders` (at most 20) and the states `adopt` `{"name", "topic"}`, `keep` `{"name"}`, `rename` `{"name", "to"}` and `drop` `{"name", "senders": [one sender]}`, `POST /api/profile/ichweiss/recurring` `{"id"}`, `DELETE /api/profile/ichweiss`; plus `POST /api/profile/fuerdich/act` `{"id", "do": "more"}`.

**Writing help (`schreib.py`):** improve mails and other texts (also from Word, Excel, Pages). Switch: Features → Mail and contacts → "Schreibhilfe" (admin, off); once released it works for every profile, Me → Writing help shows what it can do; never guests. On the page Me → Writing help: paste a text, pick what to do (Correct: only spelling, grammar, punctuation; Smooth; Shorter; Friendlier; More formal; Translate into German, English, French, Spanish, Italian or Dutch; Draft a reply from the mail you received and your own notes, missing details as [square brackets]), optionally a free wish about the form ("informal", sign-off). When correcting, smoothing, shortening and rephrasing the panel shows every change in colour, worked out by itself (word comparison), not by the model. "Copy", "Use as my text" (for a second round). With "Picture recognition", "Read from picture" or a screenshot pasted with Ctrl+V reads the text from a picture (the picture is dropped right after). With "Reading e-mail", "Inbox tidying" and the mailbox switch "Drafts", "Put into the mailbox as a draft" saves the text in the drafts folder; nothing is ever sent. For other programs there is the MCP tool `write_text` ("Improve text") under Me → Spark as MCP server. The text is data only: one call of the language model without tools, between fixed marks, told never to follow requests in the text. Nothing is stored (no history, no memory); the journal, Logs → Requests and Status → Live see only the kind, the number of characters and the time. Limits: 20,000 characters of text and mail, 96 KB per request, one request per profile at a time, two on the whole Spark, 20 per minute. Notaus stage 3 stops the writing help, from stage 2 there are no drafts.

**Writing help in other programs:** sub-switch Features → Mail and contacts → "Writing help in other programs" (admin, off; then for every profile, pairing is under Me → Writing help). Me → Writing help then shows the programs to download and "Pair a program": one click (with confirmation code) gives a setup line like `https://spark.example.de#schreib=ABCD-EFGH`, valid 10 minutes, usable once. The program swaps it for its own device key of kind "write" that can only improve texts (no chat, no mail, no drafts, no history). Paired programs are listed below and can be removed one by one; at most 10 per profile.
- **Mac** (download "Mac quick action"): Services menu "Mit Spark verbessern" in Apple Mail, Pages, Word, Excel and any other program, optionally with a shortcut. Pick what to do, look at the suggestion, "Übernehmen" replaces the selection. Key in the keychain.
- **Windows** (x64 and ARM): part of the Spark companion (below), paired under Me → Companion.
- **Outlook, Word, Excel** (Office add-in, only with the Spark's https address): download the manifest and load it in Outlook under "Get Add-ins" → "My add-ins" → "Custom add-in", or in Word/Excel as a shared add-in. Side pane with kind, suggestion and "Übernehmen" into the mail, document or cell. Works in Outlook for Windows, Mac and the web.
- **Browser** (extension for Chrome, Edge, Firefox): right-click selected text in webmailers (Outlook on the web, Gmail, GMX, web.de and others) → "Mit Spark verbessern". Load as unpacked extension; in Firefox only until restart.
- **iPhone and iPad** (Spark app): Share → Spark → "Improve text" and the shortcut "Improve text with Spark"; uses the app key.
All programs send only the selected text, the Spark does not store it; http only in the home network. Live and logs show program kind, kind and number of characters.

**Spark companion on PC and Mac (`begleiter.py`, `tools/begleiter`):** a small program in the Windows taskbar (x64 and ARM, GitHub Action "Spark-Begleiter Windows", release `begleiter-windows`) and the Mac menu bar (macOS 13 and later, Action "Spark-Begleiter Mac", release `begleiter-mac`). Switch: Features → On the go and devices → "Spark companion on PC and Mac" (admin, off); once released it works for every profile, pairing is under Me → Companion; never guests. Pairing: Me → Companion → "Pair a device" gives a setup line `…#begleiter=ABCD-EFGH` (10 minutes, once, with confirmation code); the program gets its own key of kind "desk" that knows only its own paths. Parts, on or off per device under Me → Companion (new devices: writing help, Ask Spark, notes and state on; dictation and the kill-switch button off):
- **Writing help** (needs the writing help): select text, Ctrl+Alt+S (Mac ⌥⌘S or the Services menu). Defaults per device: kind, language for translating, fixed wish, which kinds show in the menu, show the suggestion first or replace at once.
- **Ask Spark**: Ctrl+Alt+A (⌥⌘A) opens a window for a question, the answer comes as text; follow-ups within 10 minutes keep the context, a day's questions are a conversation "Begleiter" in the history. With cards as in the panel (`chat.desk_cards`): follow-up cards (Yes/No, "A or B?"), the approval card ("Appointment waits for your yes"), "Show in calendar"/"Open mail" (open only `#me=calbox` or `#me=mailbox`) and the line of tools used; a click only sends the fixed word as if typed.
- **Dictation**: Ctrl+Alt+D (⌥⌘D) starts and ends the recording (at most 60 seconds, visible indicator), the recognised text lands at the cursor. Language per device.
- **Notes**: the Spark's notes as system notifications when the companion is the device used last (fetched every 30 seconds, like the Android app).
- **State dot**: icon normal, grey (Spark not reachable) or red (Notaus or my kill switch).
- **My kill switch**: menu item with a confirmation, stops the own profile (as Me → Security). Lifting only in the browser with the PIN.
Hotkeys are set in the program under Settings. Changes in the panel reach the program with its next check. Removing a device under Me → Companion stops its key at once. Windows: not signed, SmartScreen on first start ("More info" → "Run anyway"), key encrypted with DPAPI. Mac: not notarized, first start right-click → Open; for the writing-help hotkey and pasting macOS asks once for "Accessibility", for dictation for the microphone; key in the keychain. Status → Live shows pairing, questions, dictations (character count only) and notes to "Spark-Begleiter".

**Companion: updates, look, coffee:** the programs look like the panel: the Spark face as icon in taskbar and menu bar (dot bottom right: green reachable, grey not reachable, red kill switch, accent colour when a new version is there), a window on clicking the icon with all parts that are on and their hotkeys, light and dark following the system. Updates: the Spark fetches new versions itself from the GitHub releases `begleiter-windows` and `begleiter-mac` (once a day, only when a device of that kind is paired; Features → Spark companion → "Fetch new version now" at once) and checks size and SHA-256 against the version's `begleiter.json`. The program learns from its check of the new version, downloads it only from the paired Spark, checks size and SHA-256 again and installs only after your click on "Update"; a damaged file is deleted and the old version keeps running. In the Companion app: Settings → About shows the version, "Check for updates" and the switch "Download updates automatically, ask before installing". The program and Me → Companion carry the link "☕ Support Spark" (buymeacoffee.com).

**Companion deeper in the computer:** eight more parts, all off, turned on per device under Me → Companion:
- **Right-click on files:** "With Spark" in Explorer (Windows 11: "Show more options") and under Services in Finder: summarize, translate, explain (PDF, Office, text, mail, pictures; at most 20 MB, the first 40,000 characters are read) or add to knowledge (needs own documents).
- **Ask about the screen:** Ctrl+Alt+B (⌥⌘B), drag a region, optionally a question, then explain, read out the text or translate. The language model reads the picture; it is kept nowhere.
- **Know the app:** the writing help learns the kind of program (mail, Word, Excel, chat, browser …) and fits tone and form; program names and window titles do not go to the Spark.
- **Draft a reply:** Ctrl+Alt+M (⌥⌘M) for the open mail in Outlook (classic, Windows) or Apple Mail: the answer is a draft in the reply window; nothing is ever sent. In the new Outlook it is on the clipboard.
- **Spark-Wissen folder:** what lands in the folder (default: Spark-Wissen in the user folder) goes into the own documents; a changed file replaces its older version, at most 50 a day. Sub folders you leave out under *Me → Documents → Left-out folders* are not sent; what was sent from them leaves the search at once.
- **Buttons in notes:** Reply, Later, Open panel. A reply goes to the Spark with the note as context; the note counts as outside text (nothing switches). The buttons depend on the kind of note (fixed in the panel, `begleiter.NOTE_ACTS`, never from its text): a deadline or suspicion from For you has **Done**, **Remind** and **Show** (`POST /api/desk/note/act`; the panel looks up note and card itself, the program only names the note's number and the button). "Open panel" goes to the note's way (`weg`: E-mail → For you, or the first way in its text).
- **Mail in the companion (part `post`, off; follows Features → Reading e-mail):** a section "Post" in the tray window: unread mail and For you as numbers, up to 3 urgent For-you cards with **Done**, **Remind** and **Show** (`GET /api/desk/post`, `POST /api/desk/post/act`; the panel looks up the card itself) and "Open e-mail". An **important new mail** comes as a note as soon as "New mail at once" (IMAP IDLE) reports it (`mailidle.on_new`): only unread, at most 15 minutes old, from a contact or someone you wrote to in the last year (Sent folder, read only), never from lists or no-reply addresses; at most 3 at once, each only once. Sender and subject are only text in it. Buttons: **Draft a reply** (opens Ask Spark with the note and the request in the field, nothing is sent), **Read** (only with "Mails ordnen") and **Show** (E-mail → Inbox). Only devices with this part get such notes; on the Mac the lock screen shows only "Neue Mail" when previews are hidden.
- **Quick capture (part `capture`, off; follows Features → Calendar or Features → Timers and reminders):** Ctrl+Alt+N (⌃⌥N), the tile "Erfassen" or the menu open a small window for one line like "Dentist Fri 10 am at Dr. Weber"; marked text comes along as the line. The panel reads it by fixed rules (`calendars.quick`, no language model) and shows what it found: appointment or reminder, day and time, place (`POST /api/desk/capture`). It is saved only with **Eintragen**; the panel reads the same line again for that, so exactly what was shown lands in the calendar or the reminders. A reminder without a time is for 9:00 (if that has passed, in one hour); an appointment needs an own calendar account. Then **Rückgängig** for 10 minutes (`POST /api/desk/capture/undo`, only the entry this profile just made). At most 200 characters, 60 calls a minute. Logs only with fixed words: `begleiter: erfassen termin|erinnerung ok`, `begleiter: erfassen rueckgaengig … ok`, `begleiter: erfassen abgelehnt grund=eingabe|notaus`.
- **Reply suggestions in the companion and the Outlook add-in (follows reply suggestions and the writing help):** in the window "Draft a reply" (Windows, Mac) and in the Outlook add-in's side pane **3 Vorschläge** fetches three short replies to the mail that was read (`POST /api/write/suggest`, as in the panel: the mail is only data, one call without tools, nothing is stored). A click takes a suggestion; then **Kürzer**, **Freundlicher**, **Förmlicher** and **Englisch** reshape it (`/api/write/assist`). Nothing is ever sent, the draft opens in the reply window.
- **Shared calendars in "Als Nächstes" (part `agenda`):** "Als Nächstes" in the companion window also shows appointments of calendars other profiles share with you (Features → Share calendars), with the profile's name as a chip; a private appointment is only "belegt". A click opens the calendar in the panel on that day (`#kal=YYYY-MM-DD`; the program takes only a plain date).
- **Lists in the companion (part `lists`, off; follows Features → Lists for shopping and tasks):** a section "Listen" in the companion window: shopping, tasks and own lists with open entries, each with a count and the newest entry as a fixed sentence ("Sabine hat „Milch“ ergänzt", else "zuletzt „Milch“"; `GET /api/desk/lists`, at most 6). "Öffnen" goes to the page Lists. **Quick capture** knows the kind **Liste**: "Milch, 2 Eier und Butter auf die Einkaufsliste" puts three entries onto that list (only lists of the profile, else shopping), only with "Eintragen", with you as "who" and "Begleiter" as "from where"; "Rückgängig" takes only the new entries off.
- **Act on the PC** (own admin switch "May act on the PC", off): for a request like "Open tagesschau.de on the PC" the computer asks in a window whether it may (60 s, else no). Allowed only: open a page (http/https), a program released under Me → Companion, music (play, pause, next, previous), volume, lock the screen, show a text. Locked after mail, web pages or other outside text.
- **Wake word on the PC:** "Hey Spark" through the computer's microphone, as in the browser (short pieces to speech recognition, a hit starts the question). Listens only while the part is on; the icon shows it, "Pause wake word" in the window.

**Companion: seven more parts:** all off, turned on per device under Me → Companion. **Read aloud** (Ctrl+Alt+L, ⌥⌘L): marked text in your Spark voice, at most 5000 characters. **From the phone to the PC:** text (into the clipboard), a link (opened only after asking, only http/https) or a file (at most 20 MB, into "Downloads/Vom Spark") from the panel ("Send to the PC" at the device) or by voice ("Send the link to my PC"); waits up to 24 hours. **Appointments in the companion:** the next 24 hours with a note 10 minutes before, "Remind me in 20 min" on notes. **Recognise do not disturb:** while another program uses the microphone or camera or a presentation runs, notes and announcements on your own speakers wait; then "Während du beschäftigt warst" follows. Notaus, security notes and reminders always come. **Write along in meetings** (own admin switch "Write along in meetings", off): only on a click, with a red bar; the microphone and the computer's sound go to speech recognition, the transcript stays in memory only (at most 3 hours). Afterwards a summary and tasks; with "Ausgewählte auf die Liste" chosen tasks go onto a list ("Tasks" by default, selectable; with the date from "due" and the source "from a meeting"), with "Als Erinnerung" into the reminders, the summary on request as a document on the reMarkable (folder "Meetings"), and it is in the history. Everybody taking part must agree. **Smart home buttons:** up to 12 buttons (lights, switches, fans, climate, scenes) of your Home Assistant, set up under Me → Companion; with a code word the companion asks for it. **Start and end of the day:** in the morning the daily overview, in the evening tomorrow's appointments and what is open. For "Act on the PC" the companion sends the list of installed programs; under Me → Companion you pick from it.

**Companion update looks right away:** "Check for updates" in the program and every program start make the Spark look at GitHub at once (at most every 5 minutes per kind of computer). The Mac names the failing step when it refuses an update.

**Companion with the chosen face:** the companion shows the face picked under Settings → Defaults (robot, comic, comic woman or wave) in every window and the icon; a change arrives with the next check. In the companion the comic face is a still picture.

**Acting on the PC right at the companion:** in Ask Spark on the computer itself ("open Safari with Google") the computer need not be named; a verb of doing (open, start, play, louder, quieter, lock, show) is enough. The question at the computer stays.

**Acting on the PC from a speaker or the phone:** a clear order naming the computer ("Open heise.de on my Mac") goes to the computer from there too; the question comes at the computer. When it cannot go (no computer paired, "Act on the PC" off at the device or for the profile), the Spark says exactly that instead of trying other tools. Speech recognition may write "Mac" as "Mäc".

**Remote control of the computer, stage 1: more fixed orders:** "Act on the PC" can also bring a released program to the front ("Switch to Outlook"), close, minimize, maximize or full-screen the front window, press fixed keys (next, previous, space, enter, Esc, Tab, undo, redo, copy, paste, save, reload, find), type your own words ("Type on the PC: be right there"), open a folder (Documents, Downloads, Desktop, Pictures, Music, Videos, Recent), put the computer to sleep, turn the screen off and run a shortcut. Shortcuts (Mac: the Shortcuts app; Windows: scripts, links, programs) are released only at the computer (companion → "Am PC handeln einstellen"); the Spark knows only their names. There are no free key combinations. Only what you said yourself in this sentence is typed; if the model adds words, the panel refuses. The computer asks; in the window "Without asking from now on" can be picked for a kind (not for pages, typing and showing a text), set and undone only at the computer, Me → Companion only shows it. Requests arrive at once: the companion waits for them with an open request.

**Remote control of the computer, stage 2: show and operate the screen:** under Me → Companion tap "View screen" or "Operate" for the device and you see the main screen of your own PC or Mac in the panel or in the iPhone or Android app. The computer asks every time: Decline (preselected), View only or Allow (only when operating was asked for). After "Allow" a tap or click on the picture is a click on the computer, dragging moves the mouse, the wheel scrolls, "Right click" makes the next tap a right click; text and fixed keys (Esc, Tab, Enter, Backspace, arrows, Copy, Paste, Undo) come through the bar below the picture. There is no Windows key, Cmd-Space, Alt+Tab or Ctrl+Alt+Del. While it runs the computer shows a red frame and a bar on top with "Beenden" (End); ending also works in the companion menu (Windows), with My emergency stop and in the panel. Up to four pictures a second, at most 1600 pixels wide; the Spark keeps only the newest picture in memory and forgets it at the end. No recording, no history. At most 30 minutes; when nobody watches any more or the computer stops sending, the session ends after 30 seconds. Own admin switch (Features → Show and operate the screen) and own part per device, both off; only own computers, never guests. On the Mac the companion needs the permissions "Screen Recording" and, for operating, "Accessibility".

**Remote control of the computer, stage 3: Spark operates the computer:** "Spark, write to Anna in Outlook that I'll be there at three" – the Spark does the task on your own PC or Mac step by step: it sees the screen, proposes exactly one step (click, double click, right click, scroll, a fixed key or typing text), the computer shows it with the target marked in red and asks: Stop (preselected), Skip or Allow. Only after "Allow" does the step happen; 60 seconds without an answer count as Stop. Protection against injected orders: the task is fixed from the first sentence and must be in your own words; screen text is only content for the Spark. Only text from your task is typed, anything else is shown as a warning above the step. Only a program released under "Act on the PC" may be in front; terminal, settings, login windows, password managers and the Companion itself are always blocked. If something on the screen looks like an order to the Spark, it pauses and asks (No / Continue). At most 30 steps and 5 minutes; Stop, "End" in the bar and My emergency stop end it at once. The result comes as a notice in fixed words ("On the computer: done (4 steps)"); pictures and screen text never go to memory, history or chat. Own admin switch (Features → Spark operates the computer), off; on the device both "Act on the PC" and "Show and operate the screen" must be on. Needs a language model that sees pictures.

**Describe the screen:** "What do you see on my Mac right now?" or "What is open on my PC?" – the computer asks (Decline preselected, 60 seconds without an answer count as Decline), takes one single picture after "Allow", and the Spark describes it in a few sentences. Screen text stays content, never an order; it never names passwords, codes or account numbers, and in the same answer it changes nothing afterwards. If a protected program is in front (login, password manager, settings, terminal), it describes nothing. Only you see and hear the description: in the history, on other devices and in memory there is only "Here was a description of your screen". Switched on with Features → Spark operates the computer; on the device "Show and operate the screen" must be on. If something is missing, the Spark says exactly what.

**Follow-up requests to the computer:** after "What is running on my Mac?", "Bring the Terminal to the front." or "Again" is enough without naming the computer again: if one of your two messages before named it, the request goes to it (a fixed rule, only with an order word or "again"). "… in den Vordergrund" or "… nach vorne" brings a released program to the front, with the question at the computer. After a screen description the next request is not locked.

**Office add-ins from the companion:** part "Office add-ins" (per device, off; needs the writing help). Then "Office-Add-ins einrichten …" in the companion; it asks first. **Outlook classic (Windows):** a "Spark" button in the ribbon (main window and reading: draft a reply, translate; composing: improve text, translate), also with iCloud or IMAP accounts. The button only sends one fixed word to the running companion, which works as with its hotkeys; nothing is ever sent. **Word and Excel (Windows and Mac):** the companion registers the Office add-in itself; for that open the panel once at its https address and save under Me → Companion; "In other programs" of the writing help must be released. **New Outlook, Outlook for Mac and on the web:** the companion shows the file and opens "Add from file"; adding it is done by the person (Microsoft allows nothing else, only for Microsoft 365, Exchange or Outlook.com). Part off: everything is removed.

**Outlook classic with the add-in:** the Spark add-in declares the Office interface exactly as Microsoft does (the `custom` parameters as a SAFEARRAY), and nothing in it can disturb Outlook with an error. If Outlook classic stops at "Loading add-ins", switch the part "Office add-ins" off and on again in the companion; the setup dialog names this way out.

**Blocked Spark add-in:** after a hang Outlook moves an add-in to "Disabled items", and its tick under COM add-ins stays empty. "Office-Add-ins einrichten …" lifts that block for Spark only (Resiliency\DisabledItems and CrashingAddinList); other add-ins stay as Outlook set them.

**Companion window in order:** in the window on a click on the icon (Windows) or on the face in the menu bar (Mac), the head, "Frag Spark …", the tiles and the foot with "Panel öffnen" and Notaus always stay visible; below are three tabs: **Heute** (Als Nächstes, Post, Listen), **Meldungen** (with a count) and **Rechner** (Zuhause, Dieser Rechner or Dieser Mac). Tabs without content are left out, with only one there is no bar. The window is at most as high as the free screen and only the tab scrolls; all tabs have the same height so nothing jumps. The tiles are small, four in a row, the hotkey is in the tooltip. Als Nächstes and Post show the first three, "Alle n zeigen" opens the rest; lists are one line each with count and newest entry, a click opens the page Lists. When a new note comes, the window opens on "Meldungen" (newest on top, "OK" and "Erledigt" take it away), else on the tab chosen last (kept only on the device). On the Mac "Meldungen" also shows the Spark's notes since the start (at most 10, only in memory) with the same buttons as the notification; "OK" also removes it from the Notification Center. The Mac window always sits exactly as high as its content right under the menu bar. No switch of its own, no change in the panel.

**Notes with "OK":** in the Windows companion's tray window every note has the button "OK" first. It takes the note away without opening anything; the window stays open for further notes. On the Mac notes are macOS notifications and go away with "Close".

**Microphone open/closed:** the companion (Windows: right-click menu and tray window, Mac: menu bar window) has "Mikrofon schließen" or "Mikrofon öffnen" as soon as a part with the microphone is on (dictation, Ask Spark, wake word, meeting). Closed really means closed: the wake word stops, a running recording is discarded and not sent, a meeting ends, and no button opens the microphone until you open it again. The state stays after a restart; the icon then shows a struck-through microphone. Under Me → Companion every device shows "Mikrofon offen" or "Mikrofon zu"; Status → Live only counts the devices with the microphone closed. Only this state goes to the Spark, never sound.

**"Draft a reply" when Outlook asks:** when Outlook asks something while creating the reply (e.g. "Download external content?"), the companion brings Outlook to the front, does not stay on top of it and shows "Outlook öffnet den Entwurf … bitte dort antworten". While the Spark writes, the seconds count up, and "Abbrechen" stops it.

**Speaking into the companion windows:** with "Dictation" on, every free text field in the companion windows has a mic button: "Draft a reply" (notes and mail text), "Improve text" (wish, source text, result), "Ask about the screen" (question) and "Ask Spark". First click starts, the second (■) stops the recording (at most 60 seconds); the recognised text goes to the cursor or the end and is never run. So the Outlook classic buttons "Draft a reply" and "Improve text" can be used by voice too. Never in code word or password fields. The Office add-ins in Word, Excel and new Outlook have no mic button. Button labels never break inside a word; if the Mac panel is too narrow, "Spark unterstützen" moves to its own line.

**Companion window:** clicking the face opens at the top a greeting, device and state as a dot, on the right a gear (settings) and ✕ or power; below the field "Frag Spark …" (Enter asks, the microphone asks by voice), the commands as tiles with their hotkey and sections like in the panel: next up, notes (Windows), home and "this computer" or "this Mac" with switches for microphone and wake word and, when "act on the PC" is on, the row "act on the PC … set up" (a running screen session can be ended there). At the bottom "Open panel" and "Notaus …". The settings have a side menu (overview with the state and the parts switched on in the panel, Ask Spark, hotkeys, start of the day, knowledge folder, below "device" with pairing and About and update; on the Mac also permissions); pages of parts that are off are left out. The coffee button sits quietly in About and update.

**Companion, buttons into the panel:** in the companion's settings the page "Übersicht" has under "Im Panel" a button each for **Meine Daten** (my data), **Gedächtnis** (memory) and, only for a profile with the admin role while remote access is on, **Fernzugang** (remote access), the same on Mac and Windows. Each button only opens the panel in the browser at its place (Me → Security → My data, Me → Memory, Remote access → Systems); the panel checks as always. Whether the remote access button shows, the Spark reports with `fern` in `/api/desk/config` (only yes or no; after a role change the next check applies). Sprung-Links in notifications, Telegram, the apps and the companion also know the own pages Lists, Open, Find and Dungeon Master: "Find → Tags" in a note opens the place. Companion parity: every function is in the companion (`features.DESK`, with the parts that carry it) or outside with a reason (`features.DESK_SKIP`, such as "stays in the panel"); a missing entry turns the self-tests red. No switch of its own; needs the current companion build.

**Companion, Open and Find:** two parts, each turned on per device under Me → Companion, off by default, the same on Mac and Windows. **Offen:** the tab "Heute" has a section "Offen" with the number of open cases and the first three cards (newest first, kind, title, line) with their fixed buttons such as "Erledigt" or "Später". Only the direct buttons that `vorgaenge.knoepfe` also gives a note, never a yes to a proposal; a button runs through `vorgaenge.act` with the card's state (`rev`) and is checked like in the panel (place "Begleiter"), the companion decides nothing. A card without a button and "Alle n ansehen" open Offen in the panel. Only when "Everything open in the assistant" is allowed for the profile. Notes "Gemerkt von selbst" carry "Stimmt" and "Rückgängig". **Finden:** Ctrl+Alt+F (Mac ⌃⌥F, because ⌥⌘F is taken in Notes and Xcode), the tile "Finden" or the menu open a search field; marked text comes along as the search. The Spark searches the profile's index with `fund.suche` and gives up to eight hits (title, source, date); a click opens the entry in the panel (`#fund=`), "In Finden ansehen" the page Finden with the search (`#fundq=`). Nothing stays on the computer, the words are in no log. Only when "Find in all data" is allowed for the profile. Endpoints `GET /api/desk/offen`, `POST /api/desk/offen/act` and `POST /api/desk/fund` only with the companion's device key (`profiles.DESK_PATHS`), profile binding, size and rate limit; log `begleiter: offen angesehen`, `begleiter: offen knopf=… ok|abgelehnt`, `begleiter: finden | n treffer | ms`. No switch under Features; needs the current companion build.

**Companion in system style:** the companion looks like the panel in "Clear 2 · system style" on Mac and Windows. Its colours stand only in `Theme.swift` and `Theme.cs` and match `tokens.css`: neutral greys, one blue accent (light #0071E3, dark #0A84FF), green, yellow and red only for state, light and dark following the system, with high contrast the system colours. No colour bar, no gradients, no shadows inside windows; only floating indicators (recording, dictation) have a light shadow. Radius 6 for buttons and fields, 10 for cards and groups. Tiles show line symbols (SF Symbols on the Mac, own line signs on Windows) in the accent, writing along in red. **Face only on request:** the face shows in the head of the windows, in the companion window and next to Ask Spark's answers only with the switch "Gesicht in den Fenstern zeigen" on the page "Übersicht" (this computer only, off by default); the icon in the menu bar or taskbar and "Über und Update" always show it, which face (robot or comic) the admin picks. The coffee button stands only under "Über und Update", the switches on the Mac are rows in a box. No switch in the panel, nothing goes to the Spark. Guard: `test_design_tokens.Begleiter` (theme equal to the tokens, no colour outside the theme, no gradients, face only on request). Needs the current companion build.

**Room mode** (Features → Home → "Room mode", off by default; profiles only): the switch "Raum" below the face lets the assistant listen to the conversation in the room at this device for a chosen time (15 minutes to 4 hours, it ends by itself); an orange dot shows it. In a pause it helps on fixed cues: an open question (with web search, nothing when in doubt; also without a question mark, after "Sag mal", "Weißt du, wann …", "Stimmt es, dass …"; not questions about the people in the room such as "Wo ist meine Brille"), an appointment ("Shall I enter it?"), "cold", "too warm", "too dark" (the room's real temperature from Home Assistant, offering the heating one degree or the light on), things to buy (Home Assistant shopping list), timers, reminders and unit conversions. A question pointing back is completed only with words that were said. It changes something only after a "yes", for the smart home with the code word when one is set; with a taught voice and speaker identification on, only the profile's own voice can say "yes". Under Me → Room mode each device sets the duration, how much it may say (questions only, also hints, also comments), each kind on its own, the room in Home Assistant and "text only". "Also comments": at most every two minutes, after three seconds of quiet, one casual sentence. What it hears stays in memory for five minutes only and is never stored; it uses neither memory, mail nor earlier conversations. Everyone in the room should know it is listening. A soft tone comes before it speaks; "Stopp" ends the sentence, "Nicht jetzt" keeps it quiet for 15 minutes, "Raummodus aus" (also "Hör auf zuzuhören") switches it off, from any voice. In the profile's quiet hours (Me → By itself, 22 to 7 by default) text only. The answer is looked up while people still talk. Page in the background or phone locked for over two minutes: ends with its summary; tab closed: ends at once. **Speakers:** on all ESP32 boards with "Raummodus an" after the wake word or the switch "Room" under Me → Speakers (when the speaker sleeps, it starts at its next wake word), where duration, how much it may say, the room in Home Assistant and "detect more" are set per speaker. The Spark keeps the connection, cuts sentences (at most 8 seconds a piece), checks each on its own, speaks after 1.5 seconds of quiet from the last loud sound after the soft tone and streams the speech; boards with a display show the sentence. It ends after its time, with "Raummodus aus", the switch, a dropped connection or when the admin or profile switch it off; then the board waits for its wake word. In quiet hours it stays silent; the wake word interrupts it. At most two speakers at once. Me → Speakers → "Stimme hier anlernen" (speaker identification on, browser login, with the second step when set up): three sentences make its own voiceprint for the board microphone; a sentence counts for a profile when it fits any of its voiceprints. Without a voice taught through it, a speaker listens to everybody despite "only known voices" (log: "listens to everybody (no voice taught through it yet)"). "Genauer erkennen": the model looks for missed questions, appointments and shopping, counted only with a word-for-word quote. When it ends, the page shows what it said and did (not what it heard), not stored. Every decision writes a "room:" line to the log (why it spoke or stayed silent, never the heard text).

**Ignoring TV voices** (Features → Room mode → "Fernsehstimmen überhören", off by default): every device picks under Me → Room mode "Who it listens to" (speakers under Me → Speakers): *everybody* (default), *only known voices while the TV is on* (recommended) or *always only known voices*. A voice is known when a profile taught it under Me → Speaker identification; speaker identification must be on and runs on the CPU (about 0.1 s per sentence, no GPU load). Whether the TV is on comes from Home Assistant at most every 30 seconds: a media player on, playing or buffering in the device's room, without a room set anywhere in the home; when Home Assistant does not answer, it listens to everybody. A sentence in a foreign voice triggers nothing and is dropped at once; the model never sees it, not with "Genauer erkennen" or comments either. Always counted: sentences starting with "Spark" (also from visitors), "Stopp", "Nicht jetzt" and "Raummodus aus". A "yes" counts only in the profile's voice. Very short sentences (under about one second) often count as foreign. With *Trial* (on at first) it only counts: summary and log say e.g. "Probelauf: Von 40 Sätzen hätte ich 12 als fremde Stimme überhört.", for speakers also under *Check*. Only this number is kept, never text or voice; log "room: ignored (unknown voice)" or "(unknown voice, tv on)".

**Where it listens right now** (part of room mode, no switch of its own): the Spark keeps, in memory only, a list of every device in room mode (browser pages and speakers) with name, kind, since/until, "who it listens to" and trial, never anything heard. A page signs in and gives a sign of life every minute; missing for three minutes, it drops out; after a restart it signs in again with its next sign of life. Visible (1) as a green strip on top of every page ("Kitchen listens until 21:30", View opens Me → Room mode), (2) at the face ("Also the kitchen is listening"), (3) under Me → Room mode → *Active now* with *+30 min* and *Stop* (waiting speakers with *Do not start*), (4) for the admin under Status → *Listening right now* with profile, device, time, *Stop* and *Stop all*, and as a mark "listening" and the filter "listening right now" under People and devices. Tab title "● Room" and a red dot on its icon. Everybody who sees the list may end a room; extending only in the profile's browser login, never beyond 4 hours from now. A browser's name under Me → Room mode → *Name of this device* (else e.g. "iPad · Safari", with a device key its name). *Reminder tone* (per device, speakers under Me → Speakers, off): the soft tone every 15 minutes, never in quiet hours. *Note when a speaker starts listening* (profile, off): on "Raummodus an" at your speaker a note goes to the device used last, at most once an hour per speaker, never in quiet hours. *Recently*: the last 20 times within 14 days with device, time, how it ended and the count of sentences heard, said and ignored, never text (`users/<profile>/room-history.json`, in the backup). **Home Assistant** (admin: Features → Room mode → *Room mode for Home Assistant*, off): under Me → Room mode → Home Assistant the profile makes a key of its own (with the second step when set up; a new one replaces the old). It may only `GET /api/room/active` (count, first device, until when) and `POST /api/room/end` (`{"all": true}`); the page shows the text for `configuration.yaml` once (REST sensor "Spark Raum-Modus", command `spark_raummodus_aus`). The log only says "room: listed …", "room: … ended (panel|iphone|ha|admin)" and "room: page … gave no sign of life".

**Starting from afar** (admin: Features → Room mode → *Start room mode from afar*, off; then for every profile): "Raummodus im Wohnzimmer an" (also "… für 60 Minuten") in the panel, the iPhone app or at another speaker. The panel recognizes the sentence by fixed rules (`roomfar.py`); the language model gets no tools, Home Assistant is not asked, no code word is wanted. Only the profile's own speakers that are connected right now, found by name or by the room entered under Me → Speakers; when several fit, the answer names them. Only a plain "Ja" in the next message of the same conversation (15 minutes) starts it; the speaker says "Raum-Modus an, gestartet von der iPhone-App" (or the panel, the kitchen speaker). Duration from the sentence (rounded to 15/30/60/120/240 minutes), else the speaker's own. Not via Telegram or Siri, never for guests or a voice recognized as someone else. "Raummodus im Wohnzimmer aus" / "Raummodus überall aus" ends without a question (history: "ended from another device"). With the switch off a fixed answer names the switch. "Starte den Raummodus" without a place stays the device's own room mode. Log: "room: start at speaker … asked from …", "room: on at speaker … (remote from …)".

**Speaking up by itself** (Features → My day → "Speaking up by itself", off by default; profiles only): once released it is on for every profile; under Me → By itself each profile sets each kind on its own or switches it all off: its own smart home rules ("Whirlpool above 37.5", "bathroom window is open and rain is on", "washing machine power below 5 for 3 minutes"; read only, never switching), a short greeting with the next appointment or reminder when the conversation is opened after three hours or more, at most one follow-up a day on a plan mentioned yesterday and a weather note for tomorrow via web search. The mail rows (important mail, mailbox tidying, parcel comes today; sender and subject only) sit under E-mail → Settings, appointments ahead ("In 20 minutes: dentist. Shall I remind you again at 9:55?", a "yes" sets the reminder) under Calendar → Settings; Me → By itself keeps the main switch, quiet hours, the daily limit, learning from feedback and the other kinds. "nicht jetzt" means two hours of quiet, "das interessiert mich nicht" halves how often that kind may speak (can be reset). The panel decides when, by fixed rules from real data; where the model words something (weather), its answer only counts when it quotes the data. The follow-up is a fixed sentence with the person's own words ("Gestern hast du zu mir gesagt: „…“. Hat das geklappt?"); the model only points at a sentence, and the panel takes it only as an own plan in the first person (no question, no request to the assistant, no pasted text, no question about a mail or web page, nothing from briefing, jobs or meetings). With the conversation open the note is spoken, otherwise a push notification comes; only the first device of the profile (page or iPhone app) speaks, like reminders. Every note is in the log with its reason. Asked about a note ("what do you mean?", "where is that from?"), the model gets its basis from the panel and must not make up another source; for mail notes only "sender and subject read", never mail text. Logs → Requests show it as "von selbst: Grundlage", Live shows a refused pick as an event.

**Home Assistant tells the Spark:** each smart home rule under "By itself" can speak *at most every N minutes*, for "changes" *on*, *off* or both, and have an extra condition: *only when I am away* (the person in Home Assistant with your profile name or the one chosen under "Me in Home Assistant"), *only when nobody is home*, *only at night* or *only during the day* (by `sun.sun`, else 9 pm to 6 am). Plus five parts, each with its own admin switch under Features → Speaking up by itself, all off at first, never for guests; once released they work for every profile and can be switched off one by one under Me → By itself. *Instant notes from Home Assistant*: a WebSocket connection (same address and token, only the devices of your rules, `subscribe_entities`), changes arrive in about a second; if it drops, the Spark checks every minute. *House notes on speakers*: a rule also says its own sentence on chosen speakers that take announcements from you, e.g. "Someone rang the bell."; without names, never for person devices, never with a camera picture, not in the speaker's quiet hours, at most 20 an hour. *Home Assistant tells the Spark*: a list of event names with a fixed sentence (e.g. `klingel` → "Es hat geklingelt.") and a key of its own; Home Assistant calls `rest_command.spark_melden` with `event: klingel` (`POST /api/ha/event`). Only names from the list count, nothing else is read or said; at most 30 a minute, a pause per event (default 1 minute). *Smart home rule by voice*: "Sag mir Bescheid, wenn jemand an der Haustür ist": the panel recognizes fixed words, finds the device and asks "Soll ich mich melden, wenn …?"; only a "yes" in the same conversation adds the rule. *Describe a camera picture*: a rule can name a camera; the local language model describes the still in a few words. The picture is never stored, the description is outside text and goes to your devices only, at most 30 an hour. Picking devices: the field suggests all devices from Home Assistant, "Zuletzt geändert" shows the last changed ones, with "Gerät erkennen" you trigger the sensor within 40 seconds and tap what changes (only the change time Home Assistant reports is compared). "Edit" brings a rule back into the form, "Check and save" checks it like a new one and replaces it in place; it speaks only after it was false once. Log lines start with "hamelden:". Status → Live shows the live connection (under channels) and each fired rule and event as a request of "Home Assistant" with camera picture, note (Web Push, Apple Push, Android app, Telegram or the open page) and speaker announcement, everything else said by itself as a request of "Von selbst" (e.g. greeting, reminder, task); never device names, sentences or camera words.

**Natural turn-taking** (on by default, conversation settings): during a short pause the recording is transcribed already; a finished sentence ends the turn at once and its transcript becomes the question. After "and …", "because …", "um" or a comma the assistant waits up to 2 s.

**New conversation every day** (on by default, conversation settings → Display): the first question of a day starts a new conversation; earlier ones stay in the history and can be continued.

**Speaker identification** (off by default, Features): each profile reads three short sentences in the "Me" window. When the assistant clearly recognizes a voice, it answers for that profile (memory, documents, reminders, voice), even on a guest device; the conversation shows "🎙 name". When someone other than the logged-in profile speaks, the assistant gets only that question, and the exchange stays out of the logged-in profile's history and memory. When the voice is unclear, nothing changes. Runs on the Spark's CPU (Resemblyzer, Apache 2.0), no GPU memory; strictness adjustable. A voice is not a password: similar voices or recordings can get into a profile.

**Profiles and memory:** under People and devices the admin creates profiles with a name and PIN; in the chat you log in with the "Guest" button at the top of the conversation. Logged in, the assistant remembers things when told ("remember that I'm vegetarian") or when they will be useful later, and forgets them on request; the Me lists what it knows, for deleting. Memory and conversations belong to the profile and live on the Spark, so the same history shows on every logged-in device. Which profile is asking is decided only by the login (cookie) or the device key, never by the model; guests get no memory. Speakers and your own programs get a device key of one profile under People and devices and send it as the header `X-Speech-Device` to `/api/chat`. A new PIN logs out every browser of that profile; deleting a profile removes its memory and devices. Data under `/var/lib/speech-spark/users`, one folder per profile.

**Home Assistant** (Funktionen → "Home Assistant", off by default): each profile connects its Home Assistant in the "Me" window under "Smart home" with the address (e.g. `http://homeassistant.local:8123`) and a long-lived access token (best of a separate non-admin user). Then "turn on the living room light" or "how warm is it in the bathroom?" work. Commands go to Assist (`/api/conversation/process`), so only entities exposed there (in Home Assistant: Settings → Voice assistants → Expose) are switched; devices Assist does not know are switched directly through their service (e.g. `switch.turn_on`), except locks, alarm systems and updates. With a **code word** ("Me" window → Smart home) it switches only when the code word is in the same message ("lights off, code word sunflower"), else it asks. The panel checks this, not the model; the model and stored conversations see only "[Codewort]", it is stored encrypted and compared with some tolerance (a letter or two off, "Apollo 13" = "Apollo dreizehn"). Questions need no code word. Also: history ("how warm was it in the bathroom yesterday?"), several devices at once (each checked; an unknown room never switches the whole home), typo-tolerant device search and to-do lists such as the shopping list (read, add, tick off; changes need the code word). Values, states, zones and where someone is are read directly (`/api/states`): everything the token's user may see, exposed or not. Home Assistant tells rooms (areas) only to an admin token, otherwise devices are found by name only. Only that profile uses its Home Assistant: never guests, never a recognized voice at someone else's device. The token is checked before saving, stays on the Spark (`users/<id>/homeassistant.json`, mode 0600) and is never sent to the browser. When several devices fit, the one whose name, room or device class fits wins ("TV in the living room" is the TV, not the Apple TV box); if it stays unclear, the Spark switches nothing, names the fitting devices, and the model gets no switching tool.

**Ask instead of guessing** (Funktionen → "Rückfrage statt Raten", off by default; released for every profile): when several devices fit a switching command, the Spark switches nothing at random but asks: "Several fit: Samsung, Samsung The Frame or TV. Which one do you mean?", in the chat with buttons and "None of these". When it cannot find the device exactly it asks "I can't find that exactly. Do you mean …?"; "None of these" or "show all" brings the whole device list with a search field, by room (never locks, alarm panels or remotes). Answer typed, spoken or tapped: name, room or number ("the second"), only in the same conversation, on the same device and for two minutes; the command's code word covers the choice but is never stored or spoken. When the Spark picked one itself, "No, the Apple TV" corrects it: the wrong device goes back to its old state, the right one is switched. Only fixed rules in the panel decide, never the language model.

**Remember the choice** (sub-switch, off by default): after a choice or correction the Spark asks "Shall I remember that "TV in the living room" is Samsung?" ("Yes, remember" / "No"); only a yes stores it. From then on the same sentence switches that device at once; the code word is still needed. Mappings are under Me → Memory → "Mappings" (change words, other target, delete, assign your own with "Give own names"), in the profile's `zuordnungen.json`, with the memory in your own storage and the backup. Live shows numbers only (open questions, asked today, chosen, corrected, remembered, used), the log fixed words only ("klaeren: frage ha 3 kandidaten"), never devices or sentences.

**Asking back for bus and train, weather and messages too** (same switch): when "to the station" fits several stops, the Spark asks with buttons instead of taking the first; likewise for same place names in the weather ("Karlsbad" in Germany and Czechia) and for lists in Home Assistant ("Einkauf" fits "Einkaufsliste" and "Einkauf Baumarkt"; for changes the request's code word covers the choice). The choice runs the same request with the chosen target; after a yes the word stands for it from then on (Me → Memory → Mappings, areas "Bus and train", "Places (weather)" and "Lists"). With "Do you mean Quirin Meier or Quirin Müller?" the names are buttons below. Telegram shows the choices as a keyboard; in the apps and at a speaker the spoken or typed answer does it. A named calendar that does not exist is never silently replaced: the Spark enters nothing and names the calendars there are.

**Ask which way** (Features → "Ask which way", a sub-switch of "Ask instead of guessing", off by default; once released for every profile, the admin blocks single ones under People and devices → Blocked functions): if a wish could go two changing ways, the Spark asks first: "Write Tom a mail that I am late" → "Should I first send it as an e-mail, second send it as a message at home or third just answer?". Likewise for appointment or reminder, call or message, list or reminder, and when a changing way is only weakly recognized (hint words only such as "Meeting Anna tomorrow at 3", a follow-up naming something new such as "And what about Dad?", the model's classification below its threshold). The rules are fixed in the panel (`intent.ask_route`), question and buttons in a fixed table (`intent.ASK_SAYS`), at most two ways and always "Just answer"; the answer is only the question, without language model or tool. The choice (button, number such as "second", word such as "mail") carries out your sentence that way; "Just answer" answers without changing tools. It counts for two minutes in the same conversation, only from the own profile and voice; any other message lets the question lapse. Never asked for reading or knowledge questions, negated sentences, "Never mind" or "Stop", after outside text, for guests, other voices or over MCP, never more than once per turn or twice for the same sentence; if the sentence names the way ("by mail", "Remind me …"), nothing is asked. Panel chat, Telegram (keyboard), watch and iPhone app show buttons; at a speaker, in CarPlay, in the Android app and at the companion the number is enough. The choice counts as a correction for the switch (Features → Routing, groups and numbers only). Status → Live shows numbers only ("Weg: asked, chosen, just answered, lapsed"), the log fixed words ("klaeren: frage weg | 2 kandidaten | nah", "klaeren: auswahl weg | mail").

**Searching conversations**: the magnifier next to the conversation list (on phones in the menu) searches all earlier conversations of the profile, as a guest the browser's; a click opens the spot.

**Earlier conversations** (Funktionen → "Frühere Gespräche", on by default; profiles only): the assistant looks things up in the own profile's saved conversations ("what did you tell me about the grill last week?"). Conversations quiet for ten minutes are read once in the background while nobody talks, and up to three lasting facts per conversation go into memory, marked "automatic" in the "Me" window, deletable. Can be switched off in the conversation settings ("Learn from conversations"). Once a week (or with "Propose cleanup") it proposes merging duplicates and removing outdated entries; nothing changes until you accept under Me → Memory. With notifications on, a note is sent.

**Pronunciation:** Settings → Speech output → Pronunciation takes one rule per line, e.g. `DGX = De Ge Ix`; it applies to whole words and all speech output, Open WebUI included.

**Barge-in:** while the assistant speaks, the microphone keeps listening; about a quarter second of talking interrupts the answer and becomes the next question. This relies on the browser's echo cancellation; if it interrupts itself on loud speakers, use headphones or untick "barge in" below the chat. With "Barge-in: pause first" the answer only pauses, and a "mhm" lets it play on.

**Read answers aloud:** speech output can be switched off. For one device, the "Vorlesen" switch below the face (on a phone the speaker icon at the top right of the chat), in this browser or the Android app only. For all devices of the profile: conversation settings → answer → "Read answers aloud". The Spark then answers as text only, does not call the speech output (saves the GPU) and plays no instant acknowledgement; notes, messages and reminders come with a chime and text. Speakers without a screen and CarPlay keep speaking. "Read again" on an answer works. The Spark-Begleiter then does not read "Frag Spark" answers aloud either.

**Your own voice:** under Voices you can record a reference from the microphone (read the suggested text, about 10 seconds); speech recognition fills in the transcript. Cloning needs a Base model (Settings → Speech output → model `…-Base`), then pick the new voice as the default voice. The default voice is a list fitting the model: CustomVoice shows the built-in speakers, Base your own voices, VoiceDesign locks it (the voice comes from the speaking style); a saved name missing from the list stays as "(not in the list)". A German reference gives steadier German than the built-in speakers. Own voices can be exported one by one or all as a ZIP (reference audio and transcript) and imported on this or another Spark; on a name clash rename, replace or skip.

**Required tools and answer check:** questions about appointments, mail, reminders, timers, weather, parcels, transit, lists, match results and news have to go through the matching tool (table `NEED_TOOLS` in `chat.py`, also used by the quality test). While choosing the tool the "tool choice" temperature (0.1) applies, the normal one afterwards. The answer check (Settings → Language model, on by default) lets no sentence through for such questions that names a time, date, score or amount found in no tool result, the question or the conversation; instead it says once "Genauere Angaben dazu habe ich nicht." If the model answers without looking anything up, the answer is dropped and it is asked once more. Both show in the tool log as "Antwort-Prüfung"; general questions without a tool are not checked. Optionally the admin allows "think while choosing tools" (off), then for every profile, never for guests. A round with thinking holds its text until the thinking ends: when the thinking comes as text up to a lone `</think>` instead of apart (reasoning_content), everything before it is never shown, spoken or kept; a lone `</think>` is never shown. No sentence comes twice: when the model writes the answer before and after a tool call, the next round shows each sentence only when complete and leaves out sentences already said, in chat and voice; Logs → Requests show "Repetition left out" with the count.

**Check deeds and stop error loops:** two protection rules, on by default like the answer check (Settings → Language model, `chat.action_check`, `chat.loop_stop`, reason in `features.DEFAULT_ON`), without a switch per profile (`taten.py`, fixed rules, no model). **Check deeds:** every changing tool has a kind (`taten.KIND`: geraet, erinnerung, termin, mail_entwurf, dokument, remarkable, nachricht, merken, aufgabe, musik and more). Per answer the panel keeps which kind worked (the same failure words as Logs → Requests; "NOT saved yet" counts as a proposal). When a finished sentence claims a deed ("is on now", "I have entered it", "has been sent", "I'll remember", a bare "Done." while something changing was offered) and none of the named kinds worked, it is neither shown nor spoken. At the end comes one fixed sentence: "That did not work: TV." (tool failed) or "I did not do that." (did not run); nothing while a proposal is open. No second try. When a tool of that kind works later in the same answer ("I'm making the song." before `musik_machen`), the sentence is left out silently. In doubt the sentence is said (several deeds, one worked). **Stop error loops:** when the same tool fails twice with the same details (or three times with any), it is not offered again for the rest of the answer, the model hears why. Visible in Logs → Requests ("Taten-Prüfung", "Schleife gestoppt"), in the journal (`chat: action check: held back kind=… reason=…`, `chat: loop stop: tool=… fails=…`) and on the Live page ("Taten-Prüfung": held back and stopped today, numbers only). The interface `taten.check(text, acts, offered)` also serves the settings assistant's rule set (a fixed "built-in" rule).

**Three protection rules from the quality test:** (1) **Announced deed:** a sentence that only announces a tool call or deed ("I'm calling the timer tool …", "I'll set a timer …", `taten.announces`) is neither shown nor spoken while no tool has worked in this answer. When a tool call follows, it is left out silently; otherwise the panel says "I did not do that." at the end. Check deeds also applies when no tool was offered (then every claimed deed is false). Journal: `chat: action check: held back kind=ankuendigung …`, `chat: action check: announcement left out, a tool ran`. (2) **Remember only what there is to remember:** a message that only asks (one sentence with a question mark, no "remember", nothing the person says about themselves like "I am …"; `chat.just_asks`) is not offered `memory_save` (`chat.offer_memory`, in the quality test as well), so "Do you know my brother's name?" never saves a made-up fact; journal `chat: memory_save not offered - the message only asks`. (3) **Account balance:** there is no bank tool, so every honest answer is right ("no access", "no tool", "check your banking app"). The quality test checks only facts here: an amount in figures or words (`quality.MONEY`) or an unsuitable tool fails. The two cases without calendar and mail access accept every "no access" answer (`quality.NO_ACCESS`).

**Appointments only fresh from the calendar:** "Guten Morgen" on its own (also with "Spark", "wie geht's") counts as asking for the day: the switch files it under the calendar and the first round has to call `daily_briefing` (without the briefing: `calendar_events`); not before another request ("Guten Morgen, turn on the light"). The same for the briefing by name, also as one word: "Morgenbriefing", "Gib mir mein Tagesbriefing", "Was ist mein Morgenbriefing?" call `daily_briefing`; only "Was ist das Morgenbriefing?" or "Wie stelle ich es ein?" asks about the function (Spark help). Calendar results mark ended appointments with "(already over)"; briefing and daily overview do not name them as coming up.
