Once an answer has read the calendar, the answer check takes times, dates and quoted appointment titles only from the question, instructions and this answer's results, not from earlier answers, so yesterday's appointments never come back as today's (tool log: "Termin aus früherer Antwort").

**Learning from corrections:** admin switch Features → Conversation → "Learning from corrections", off; released for every profile, never guests or another voice. When a message right after an answer starts like a correction ("Nein, …", "Das stimmt nicht", "Du sollst …", at least three words), the model words a short sentence on the side (`fixes.py`) and the assistant ends with "Soll ich mir merken: …?". It is stored only after a plain yes from the same conversation within 15 minutes. Never proposed: sentences about code words, PINs, passwords, rules, security, confirmations, locks or alarms, world knowledge (scores, amounts) and links; after mail or outside text no proposal, and the earlier answer is not sent along. Tool log entry "Korrektur"; "Send the question to the admin as a test case" puts the question into `quality-cases.json` (at most 30) and it runs with the quality test (all tools offered, each finds nothing, made-up figures are failures). The admin deletes them under Status → Checks → Quality test.

**Memory check:** admin switch Features → Memory check, on by default (protection: what may be out of date is never stated as a fact; reason in `features.DEFAULT_ON`); released for every profile, never guests (`gedaechtnis.py`). Every entry keeps its evidence, also while the switch is off: source (`said` remembered in a conversation, `fix` from a confirmed correction, `auto` learned from a quiet conversation, `hand` changed under Me, `tidy` from an accepted cleanup proposal), conversation (own ones only, never to the model) and last confirmation. Entries without evidence count as `auto` when learned, else `said`. Fixed rules of the panel, no model, mark: **R1** a date it names is more than a day past (never birthdays, "seit", "ab", "jeden"; "Still right" after the date keeps it), **R2** "tomorrow", "next week" … older than 14 days or "currently", "right now" … older than 90 days, **R3** learned automatically, never confirmed and older than "Learned counts as old after" (setting under Features, 1–60 months, default 6), **R4** contradiction: two entries say the same thing with another value (fixed patterns "X is called", "lives in", "works at/as", "drives a", "is married to", "favourite … is", apart by person); the one said or confirmed last wins. Nothing is deleted. The model gets three blocks: sure, learned (with the month), "maybe outdated, never say it as a fact". Me → Memory shows marks (confirmed, learned, outdated?, contradiction), the filter "Outdated", a note card and, on a tap, where from (with "Open the conversation"), remembered, confirmed, the reason and "Still right" (`POST /api/profile/memory/{id}/confirm`, rate limit "memory"). Live: node "Memory" with counts per mark; Logs: area "Memory" (`gedaechtnis: pruefung …`, only when the numbers change); Logs → Requests: step "Memory (panel)" with numbers.

**Versions and check-back:** with Memory check on, a confirmed correction about the same thing ("lives in …", "drives a …", "X is called …") becomes a new version of that entry; ✎ Change keeps the old wording too. Up to 5 earlier versions show in the detail under Me → Memory with "Bring back" (`POST /api/profile/memory/{id}/restore`, login, profile binding, CSRF, 256 bytes, rate limit). Sub-switch Features → Memory check → "Check back in conversations" (`chat.memory_ask`, off; released for every profile): when an entry that may be out of date fits the question (a shared word, fixed rule), the panel adds "Quick check: is this still right: …?". At most once per conversation, three times a day, the same entry once in 30 days; never with someone else's voice, after mail or web pages, during an emergency stop or while another question is open. The answer is open for 15 minutes (`users/<id>/memory-ask.json`): "yes" confirms, "forget it" deletes, "no" changes nothing, a correction goes through "Learn from corrections" and becomes a new version after a yes. Live shows open questions as a number, Logs → Requests the step "Memory check-back (panel)", the journal `gedaechtnis: nachfrage …` and `gedaechtnis: fassung … grund=…`.

**Memory topics:** sub-switch Features → Memory check → "Memory topics" (`chat.memory_topics`, off; released for every profile). In the background (never while someone is talking) the language model in the home network sorts entries without a topic once into a fixed topic: person, home, on the move, work, health, right now, other; any other answer becomes "other", after a failure it retries only after an hour. Me → Memory shows groups; in the detail the person picks another topic (`POST /api/profile/memory/{id}/topic`), which the model never changes again. The topic is only for display, does not go to the model when answering and changes no rights. Live shows "N ohne Thema", the journal `gedaechtnis: themen … gesetzt=N`. Quality test cases "gedaechtnis-veraltet" (outdated never as a fact) and "gedaechtnis-bestaetigt" (confirmed is used).

**Cap without silent throwing away:** a profile has at most 200 entries (`profiles.MAX_FACTS`; earlier versions do not count). A fixed rule of the panel (`profiles._cap`, when remembering and tidying up): above the cap the oldest entries learned by itself and never confirmed make room first (`auto` without "Still right"). Entries said, corrected, changed by hand, tidied or confirmed never go. When only such entries are left, the new entry is refused: `memory_save` answers "Not done: the memory is full …", the assistant says so and names the way Me → Memory ("Suggest tidy-up" or delete entries); learning by itself stores nothing, "Learn from corrections" says "not remembered, memory full". A correction about a fixed thing becomes a new version and needs no room. Journal (area Memory): `gedaechtnis: platz frei … | entfernt=N grund=alt-gelernt` and once a day per profile `gedaechtnis: voll … | abgelehnt=said|auto max=200 | next log in 24 h`, never text. Live: the node "Memory" shows "N Profile voll". Self-test `test_gedaechtnis_grenze`.

**Fixed topics:** with Memory check the panel knows fixed topic places (`gedaechtnis.SLOTS`, one list, R4 reads the same): home, employer, job, partner, children, birthday, diet (vegetarian, vegan …), car, pet (one place per kind of animal), names ("brother is called …") and favourites, apart by person (`WHO`: "brother lives in …" is the place `wohnort:bruder`). Only fixed patterns of the panel recognize them (`gedaechtnis.slot_of`), never the model; a sentence about another person by name ("Anna lives in Ulm") or without a value ("lives near …") fills no place. Allergies are no place (they add up). Per place one entry holds the value: what the person says (`memory_save`), corrects or changes by hand becomes the new wording, the old one stays as an earlier version (at most 5, reason "new value for the same topic", `grund=platz` in the journal). **A value learned by itself never replaces one that was said or confirmed:** it waits as an offer and comes as a card "Learned" under Open (`vorgaenge`, kind `gedplatz`, not urgent, only under Open) with "Take it" and "Discard"; the same buttons under Me → Memory at "Fixed topics" (`POST /api/profile/memory/{id}/offer` with `{"do": "take"|"drop"}`, login, profile binding, CSRF, 256 bytes, rate limit "memory"; also the iPhone app). "Take it" makes the value the wording (the old one becomes a version, reason "learned value taken"), "Discard" keeps the value so it never asks again; if the person says something about the topic first, the offer goes with its card. Learned over learned: the newer wins, the old one stays as a version. The model gets per place only the holding value, never older contradicting entries, versions or offers. Me → Memory shows the box "Fixed topics" on top: every own place (empty as "–") and every other place held, with value, source, "since" and "n earlier"; tapping opens the versions with "Bring back". Also "N of 200", yellow from 180 (`profiles.WARN_FACTS`), red at 200; when the memory is full, a card "Gedächtnis voll – aufräumen" comes under Open ("Show" to Me → Memory, kind `gedvoll`; at most once a day again) and goes by itself once there is room. iPhone: My things → Memory with "Fixed topics", Take it/Discard, "Bring back" (press and hold) and "N of 200"; Android shows the panel. Journal (area Memory): `gedaechtnis: angebot … | platz=wohnort`, `gedaechtnis: angebot uebernommen|verworfen …`, `gedaechtnis: fassung … | grund=platz|gelernt-uebernommen`; never a value. Live: the node "Memory" also shows "N gelernt, wartet auf Übernehmen". Self-test `test_gedaechtnis` (Places, FullCard).

**Remembered note:** when the assistant remembers something (`memory_save`), "✓ Remembered: …" stands under the answer with "Undo" and "Show" (opens Me → Memory). The event `memory` carries the number, state (`rev`, a short fingerprint of wording, source, confirmation and time, `gemerkt.rev_of`) and whether there is something to take back; the conversation keeps only number and state (`mem`, `profiles._clean_msg`), the page takes the text from the memory, so the note stays after reloading and on other devices. "Undo" (`POST /api/profile/memory/{id}/undo` with `{"rev"}`, `gemerkt.py`; login, profile binding, CSRF, 256 bytes, rate limit "memory"; also the iPhone app) takes back exactly what happened: a new entry goes, a fixed place gets its earlier version back as it was (the wording taken back is not kept as a version). Only while the entry is unchanged, otherwise 409 "Changed since"; only own entries, never guests. "Undo that" right after (`aendern.undo`) takes the same way. **What was learned by itself** comes as a card "Remembered by itself" (`vorgaenge`, kind `gemerkt`, key `gemerkt:<conversation>`, up to 3 entries, not urgent, never said into the conversation): on top under "Still open", under Open and as a number on the card Memory of Me → Overview, with "That's right" (confirms), "Undo" and "Show …"; after 7 days the card goes, the entry stays learned, not confirmed. Only its title ("Spark hat sich 2 Dinge gemerkt") reaches a notification after "Later" or Telegram, never an entry's text. Begleiter: Frag Spark shows "Gemerkt: …" with "Rückgängig" (sends "Mach das rückgängig", which asks for a yes like a typed one) and "Gedächtnis ansehen". iPhone: chip under the answer, the card under Open; Android shows the panel. Switch Features → Remembered note (`chat.memory_note`, on by default, reason in `features.DEFAULT_ON`: protection and transparency). Journal (area Memory): `gedaechtnis: rueckgaengig geloescht|zurueckgeholt | weg=chip|karte|sprache`, `gedaechtnis: rueckgaengig abgelehnt grund=geaendert|fehlt|gleich`, `gedaechtnis: gemerkt-karte neu|ergaenzt fakten=N`, `gedaechtnis: gemerkt-karte stimmt fakten=N`; never a value. Live: the node "Memory" with "heute N gemerkt · N Karten · N rückgängig · N bestätigt". Self-test `test_gemerkt`.

**One way to the own data:** memory, conversations, tool log, room mode history, the read marks of learning, remembered matches, interests, the Spark diary and the open question about the memory are read and written by the panel only through `profiles.read_own`, `write_own` and `drop_own`, never by a module itself; so they are sealed in one place. Self-test `test_siegel` (OnlyThroughTheHelpers).

**Memory and conversations sealed:** memory, conversations, tool log and room mode history lie on the disk only sealed; copied or put into another profile, the files show only noise. Files still open are sealed once at start, after a backup "vor-siegel". When a backup comes to another Spark without the move backup, the profile starts empty and nothing is overwritten: the old files lie aside, admins see a hint (Settings → Backup), and after the move backup "Read in again" under Me → Memory brings them back with everything newer. Live shows at the box Memory how many files are sealed and lie aside. Self-test `test_siegel`.

**What was learned sealed too:** sealed as well are the read marks of learning, remembered matches, the open question about the memory, "What I know about you" (interests) and the Spark diary; open files are sealed once at start (backup "vor-siegel"), "Read in again" brings them back too. Every file of memory and conversations in the file register must be sealed (`test_siegel`).

**Download all my data** (no switch: right of access): under Me → Security → My data "Choose …" shows the areas with count and size (each can be left out) and, folded, what stays out and why. "Pack and download" asks a fresh code or passkey every time (without a second step the PIN) and downloads a ZIP with everything of the own profile: memory, conversations and what was learned opened, documents, lists, calendars, settings without secrets, from chats with others only the own messages; with `LIESMICH.txt` (de+en) and `uebersicht.json`. Login and keys, the voiceprint, queues, read mails and the supplies of foreign servers stay out. At most 3 per hour and 2 GB; the pickup link holds 10 minutes and exactly once. iPhone app under Me → Security → My data (with a second step) to share or save, Android app through the download. Live shows only counts at the Memory box. Self-test `test_meinedaten`.

**Language model settings:** under Settings → Language model → Advanced the admin sets the history length (4000–64000 characters, default 24000), web searches per answer (1–3, default 2), the time limit for an answer (60–600 s), top_p and presence_penalty (0 = the server's default) and own keywords that require a tool (one line per tool, e.g. `web_search: elfmeter, aufstellung`; whole words only, at most 20 per tool, built-in words stay). "Restore default" fills in the shipped system prompt, "Show the whole prompt" lists all parts in order and marks the fixed ones. With Features → "Own conversation style" (off) each profile says under Me → Conversation → Answer, in at most 500 characters, how the assistant should talk; the text sits below the system prompt and above the fixed rules, never applies to guests or other voices and cannot be changed with a device key. Fixed: tool rules, handling of outside text, code word, locks and the values of background jobs (learning, briefing, mail sorting, room).

**Roles by voice:** with the admin switch Features → Own conversation style → Roles by voice (off) every profile adds up to 8 roles under Me → Conversation → Answer, one per line like "Butler: polite, dry, with British understatement". "Sei jetzt der Butler", "Wechsle zur Rolle Erklärbär" or "Rolle Butler" switch, "Sei wieder normal" ends, "Welche Rollen hast du?" lists them. The panel recognizes these sentences with fixed rules only in the own latest message and only for saved names ("Sei leise" stays a normal question); that answer has no tools, the model only says the result. The active role applies on all devices of the profile, sits in the prompt after the own conversation style and before the fixed tool rules, and changes only the tone, never the rules. Never guests or other voices; roles change only in the own browser, not with a device key, and admin defaults hand out no roles. Log line: "rolle: set - Butler".

**Wikipedia straight away:** with the admin switch Features → Web search → Wikipedia straight away (off, works without a SearXNG address too) every profile not blocked gets the tool `wikipedia`. For knowledge questions ("What is a quasar?") the panel fetches the intro of the matching article with one request to the MediaWiki API (at most 1,500 characters, answer at most 1 MB) instead of SearXNG and whole pages; the model reads less and starts sooner. With no hit on de.wikipedia.org it asks en.wikipedia.org; an own address set by the admin is used without a second try. Answers are kept in memory for 24 hours (at most 300). The article is outside text: wrapped as data, no switching or saving in the same answer afterwards. "Wikipedia" or "Lexikon" in the question requires the tool; with "only web search and memory" for unclear questions Wikipedia is included. Only the search words leave the Spark. Never guests.

**Own Kiwix archive:** with the admin switch Features → Web search → "Own Kiwix archive" (off) and an address (best in the home network, e.g. `http://192.168.178.20:8080`; changing it needs the second sign-in step) the assistant looks things up in your kiwix-serve for every profile. "Choose books" shows the catalog as a list: search, language (default German and English, "All languages" or one), kind (Wikipedia, TED, PhET, Docs …), grouped, each book with language, variant (maxi/nopic), article count, size and date; chosen books on top as chips with ×. At most 10; without a choice the German and English Wikipedia. The book name is saved without its date: when the Kiwix replaces a file with a newer one (e.g. `…_2025-01` → `…_2025-03`) the Spark takes the newest; on a 404 it reads the catalog again at once, and "Tell me more" finds the article in the new file. "Look in my archive …" always means the Kiwix, never the uploaded documents; the router also recognises "dein/unser Archiv", "im Archiv" and "Archiv durchsuchen", and when the archive is offered the first round must call exactly `archive_search`. When it is not available for the profile the assistant says why (admin switch, address, profile blocked) instead of searching the documents (log line `kiwix: archive asked, not offered`); this note comes only when the admin switched the Kiwix on or "Kiwix" is in the question. `wikipedia` asks the Kiwix's Wikipedia books first (title suggestion, else full text), the online Wikipedia only without a hit or when the Kiwix fails; a profile with only the Kiwix switch gets `wikipedia` without internet. `archive_search` searches the full text of the chosen books (at most 5 at once) and names the best article with further hits; `article_more` reads on (at most 3 parts of 1,500 characters, only by the id a tool named before). If the web search fails (SearXNG unreachable or no search engine answers), the assistant answers from the Kiwix, saying it is the offline archive with its date. Security: addresses only from the admin's address plus fixed endpoints (`/catalog/v2/entries`, `/suggest`, `/search`, `/content/<book>/…`), redirects only to the same host and inside the book, netguard HOME (home network allowed, own ports and link-local never), answers at most 1 MB (articles 4 MB), 10 s, XML with a DOCTYPE refused, HTML only turned into text (no scripts, tables, footnotes). Everything is outside text. Answers kept 24 hours in memory (at most 300), the catalog an hour. Status → Checks → "Check Kiwix" shows reachability, books and a test search; Logs → area "Knowledge" shows `kiwix:` lines with book, hits and time, never the question. Never guests.

**Look locally first:** with the admin switch Features → Web search → "Look locally first" (off; time limit 200 to 1500 ms, default 400) the Spark, for every profile, first looks in the own sources for knowledge questions before anything goes out. A fixed rule (the source switch `quellen.py` with the classes internal, external and both, see „Source switch“ below; never the model) sorts the person's message: **outside** (web search asked for, weather, trains, parcels, news, prices, rates, "today", "right now", years from 2020 …) goes straight out, without delay; **local** (an own source named: "in my documents", "in the archive", "we talked about", "last time") searches only there, without web search in this answer (a new message can ask for it); a **knowledge question** (anything else with search words) gets the quick look. It asks the own and shared documents (full text; meaning search only when that model already runs and is free), the own Kiwix (title suggestion, else full text, only articles with a fitting title) and earlier conversations (without answers from mail, without the current one) at the same time. What is not back by the time limit is not waited for. A passage counts only with at least 60 % of the search words (question words like "who", "how high" do not count). At most 5 hits across the sources, with source and date, of 500 characters each (the part with the search words, from tables the head row and rows with the rarest search words, `lokal.excerpt`) go to the model in the first round as the tool result `local_search`, wrapped as outside text: data, never an instruction, and the answer is locked like one from the web (no switching, saving, reminders). The model names the source briefly ("In your documents …", "According to your archive …"); when nothing fits it answers timeless questions from its own knowledge or searches the web. It only looks where the answer may look anyway (`document_search`, `archive_search`, `history_search` and `fund_suche` of this turn): guests, MCP, a voice not recognised at a shared speaker and Telegram without personal data get no quick look. Web search after documents: locked without the switch; with it possible, but the panel sends only the words of the question (without code word, at most 120 characters), never the model's query. After mail it stays locked. Status → Checks → "Test looking locally first" shows class, search words, hits per source and times for one sentence (admin, 20 per minute); Logs → area "Knowledge" shows one `lokal:` line per question with class, hits per source, time and what happens next, never the question.

**Kiwix in the quick look:** the Kiwix has its own time limit in the quick look (`chat.local_first_kiwix_ms`, Features → Web search → "Look locally first" → "Time limit for the Kiwix archive", 200 to 2000 ms, default 900); no source borrows time from another. Which books a question asks is a fixed rule (`kiwix.pick`, never the model): at most 5 (`kiwix.MAX_SEARCH_BOOKS`), the biggest German Wikipedia always, then books whose title, description or kind holds a word of the question (most shared words first; words found in half of the chosen books or more, like "Wikipedia" or "Docs", do not count), free places in the order of the choice. "How does git rebase work?" so asks the Git Docs. The same holds for `archive_search`. The order is changed under Features → Web search → "Own Kiwix archive" by dragging or ◀. An archive article from the quick look stands under the answer as a source with book and date ("Albert Einstein (Wikipedia, Stand 2025-01)"), linked into the own Kiwix; with "Address for links" (`chat.kiwix_link_url`, empty = the Kiwix address, changing it needs the second sign-in step) the links point to another name, such as behind the own proxy, while the Kiwix is still asked at the home address. When the book is older than a year (`frisch.ARCHIV_TAGE`) and the answer does not name its year, the answer check adds "Not quite current: archive: as of 2024-11." (`frisch.note_archive`, `frisch.nachsatz`), also for `archive_search` and `wikipedia` from the Kiwix. A chosen book the catalog no longer knows is skipped while the catalog is readable; "Check Kiwix" names it ("No longer in the Kiwix and skipped"), journal `kiwix: chosen books missing in catalog n=1`. Mistyped long words still fit: `lokal.same` allows one wrong letter for 6 to 9 letters and two from 10 letters (edit distance, `common.edit_distance`), shorter words must match exactly; this holds for titles and hits of all sources of the quick look. When the whole question finds no title, the panel asks up to 3 further title suggestions per book (`lokal.variants`: a long word at the end cut to its first 4 letters, "Dresdner Frau…", or a long word alone); the title naming most words of the question wins (`lokal.fit`: "Dresdner Frauenkirche" before "Dresdner Bank"). Self-tests `test_lokal.Turn.test_archive_article_despite_a_typo`, `test_lokal.Turn.test_the_archive_has_its_own_limit`, `test_kiwix.Pick`, `test_kiwix.ArchiveAge`.

**reMarkable notes:** a profile connects its my.remarkable account (reMarkable 2, Paper Pro, Paper Pro Move); the picked notebooks then show in the document search, also with meaning search, document card and "Look locally first". Switches: Features → Own documents → Read pictures and scans → "reMarkable notes" (admin, off); released for every profile, connecting under Me → reMarkable; never guests. Pairing: get a one-time code for a desktop app on my.remarkable.com (eight letters) and enter it under Me → reMarkable (own browser only, never with a device key; with the second sign-in step a fresh code). The Spark signs in as device "desktop-linux"; the long-lived device token is sealed in the vault (`users/<p>/remarkable.json`), the short-lived user token lives in memory only, neither is ever logged. It uses the unofficial sync protocol ("sync15") like rmapi, the Obsidian plugin and remarkable-mcp (MIT, used as a model); if reMarkable changes it, the error shows under Me → reMarkable, exporting a PDF and uploading it stays as a way. Without a Connect subscription reMarkable syncs only in part. Only ticked items are read (notebooks, folders with their content or "All notebooks" without PDFs/books; nothing by default; never the trash). Per page (format v6, `rmscene` 0.8.0 pinned): typed text (Type Folio, keyboard, "Convert to text") and highlighted text directly; handwriting is drawn from the strokes as a picture (Pillow, cropped, at most 1600 pixels) and read like a scan by the language model in quiet minutes (same daily limit, night window). For PDFs and e-books only highlights and own notes. Each notebook becomes one document with source "reMarkable" (`Folder/Name`, cited as "reMarkable: Folder/Name, Seite 4"). Compared every 30 minutes, never while someone speaks (`vorrang.py`), "Compare now" at once: pages with the same hash are not fetched or read again; notebooks deleted in the cloud or no longer ticked disappear; one deleted under Me → Documents returns only when ticked again. "Disconnect" deletes the token and all notebooks on the Spark (then remove the device on my.remarkable.com). Limits: 5,000 entries, 500 pages per notebook, 300 pages per comparison (the rest two minutes later), page file 20 MB, index 2 MB, 2 million stroke points per page, the documents' space quota. Connections only through netguard to `*.remarkable.com`, `*.remarkable.engineering` (and `storage.googleapis.com` without login), https only, redirects only there. The content is outside text. Logs: `remarkable: 2 notebook(s), 5 page(s) new, 0 removed`.

**Sending to the reMarkable:** below "reMarkable notes" the admin switch "Sending to the reMarkable" (off; released for every profile). Then every answer has "To the reMarkable" (on a click only, own browser), and "Write on the reMarkable: …" makes a dictated note. `remarkable_note` is only offered when the person's own message names "reMarkable" and is locked after outside text (web, mail, documents) in the same answer. It creates a new EPUB in the folder "Spark" (made when missing); existing documents are never changed, moved or deleted; the root index only gets lines added (generation checked). At most 20 a day and 60,000 characters.

**Put into:** under Me → reMarkable the profile picks its own folder for new documents (default "Spark", created when needed). The Spark only adds new documents there and never changes anything that exists. If the folder is deleted or in the trash, it takes "Spark" again, says so once and resets the choice.

**Date of the last change and last comparison:** Me → Documents shows a reMarkable notebook with the day it last changed on the device; "Neueste zuerst" and the groups (today, last 7 days, months) follow it, in the app as well. A document's page names "Added" and "Changed". With the source reMarkable picked at the top, the count is followed by "Letzter Abgleich 10:25 · 3 Seiten neu" (time and number only, "mit Fehler" when the last comparison failed); without a connected account the line is left out. Comparison every 30 minutes, in between a short look at the account every 3 minutes; after a break it goes on with the missing pages, pages already read are never fetched again.

**Newest first:** sub-switch under "reMarkable notes" (Functions, admin), off; released for every profile. The Spark keeps when each page last changed (time per page when the reMarkable software names it, else the notebook's; uploaded documents with their upload). With similar hits the newer passage moves up at most two places, the gain halves every two weeks, a clearly better old hit stays first; this applies to the document search and to "Search locally first". Each hit carries "changed on …", where they disagree the model follows the newer passage and says its date. "What did I note last / today / yesterday / this week / last month?" is a fixed rule on the person's own words: the first round must search the documents and gets the pages changed last, newest first; if nothing is in that span, the assistant says so. Between comparisons the Spark checks every 3 minutes with one small request whether anything changed in the account, and then compares at once (journal "remarkable: account changed, comparing now"); the Spark's own writes (answers, guide, inbox) do not count as a change. On 502/503/504 from the cloud it tries once more after 2 seconds. The page reader's hints about newer page parts are counted into one journal line per comparison.

**More with the reMarkable:** admin sub-switch "More with the reMarkable" below "Sending to the reMarkable", off; released for every profile. Morning sheet, week in review and daily inbox are on and can be switched off under Me → reMarkable (code: `app/panel/rmplus.py`). *Morning sheet:* at the chosen time (default 06:30, two hours' window) the daily briefing as a PDF with lines for notes. *@Spark:* "@Spark" and a question on a page that is read; after the comparison (for handwriting after reading) "Antwort: …" lies next to it, from the own documents and, if there is one, the web search; each question only once. *Tasks:* lines with ☐, [ ], "TODO" or "Aufgabe:" show up under Me → reMarkable, a reminder only after "Remind". *Meetings:* notebook or first line with "Besprechung", "Meeting", "Protokoll" …: 30 minutes after the last change a summary with decisions and tasks as a new document plus a note. *Week in review:* on Fridays from 5 pm a PDF with the week's main points. *Web pages and attachments:* `remarkable_page` (a public page as EPUB) and `remarkable_attachment` (the newest PDF or EPUB attached to a fitting mail of the last 30 days, whole files up to 30 MB only), only when the person's own words name an address or an attachment. *Daily inbox:* dictated notes gather in "Eingang DD.MM.YYYY". *Spark guide:* an e-book about the reMarkable functions and the service (comparison, privacy, limits, disconnecting), each function marked on/off; the Spark compares the text every 10 minutes and renews the same document when it changes. A self-test demands a section for every function below "reMarkable notes". Only the guide and the day's inbox belong to the Spark and are renewed (your marks stay); everything else is a new document. At most 30 documents of its own a day; never while someone is talking. Notes only go to the language model as data, without tools.

**Paper for the reMarkable:** admin sub-switch "Paper for the reMarkable" below "Sending to the reMarkable" (`chat.remarkable_paper`, off), released for every profile, never for guests; only "Tidy up by itself" is on and can be switched off under Me → reMarkable → Paper (code: `app/panel/rmpapier.py`). **Folders by kind** (`rm_folders`): what the Spark puts on the reMarkable goes below "Spark" into Morgenblatt, Wochenrückblick, Besprechungen, Antworten, Web und Anhänge, Eingang or Archiv; folders are kept by id (renaming or moving is fine, a missing one is made again), the guide is pinned on top; "Sort in now" moves its loose PDFs and e-books (only its own, known from the list `made` or old titles, never notebooks, at most 30 at once). **Tidy up** (`rm_tidy`, needs the folders): once a day morning sheets and week reviews without handwriting go to the archive after 7 days, archived ones without handwriting to the reMarkable's trash after 30 more days (recoverable there). **Designed sheets** (`rm_paper`): morning sheet, week review, summaries, answers, inbox and the guide come as PDF (fpdf2, DejaVu) in the device's screen format: reMarkable 1/2 157.8 × 210.4 mm, Paper Pro 179.7 × 239.6 mm, Paper Pro Move 91.8 × 163.2 mm (detected from the page size or chosen under Paper), with a header band, numbered sections, cards and lines; web pages stay EPUB without boxes. The box "Kann weg" (can go) sits top right; on the morning sheet every reminder of the day has a box. **Reading:** only sheets it made itself with stored boxes (`papers`) are read; a copy on the device never triggers anything. On each look into the cloud (at most 12 changed sheets) the strokes (rmscene, without highlighter and eraser) are converted to mm and each box is checked geometrically: at least 3 mm of ink in the box (2 mm margin) = ticked, 70 % filled = taken back, ink only beside it = unclear (nothing happens, Paper shows "unclear"), else empty. Fixed rules, no model: "Kann weg" puts the sheet into the trash, into the archive with handwriting on it (without folders it stays); a tick completes a reminder, filling the box in brings it back; each box acts once per state. Both can be taken back under Paper (`POST /api/profile/remarkable/papier/zurueck`). A sheet with handwriting is never overwritten: a new version is made and the old one goes to the archive. **Calibration:** the calibration sheet has four circles; the crosses give a mapping per model (least squares, kept only when every circle fits within 2.5 mm). Under Paper a control picture per sheet shows the boxes in the colour of what was read and the strokes on top. Endpoints (profile browser login, CSRF, rate limits `rmsend`, `rmpic`, `rm`, body 1 KB): `GET /api/profile/remarkable/papier`, `GET …/papier/{id}/{page}.png`, `POST …/papier/abgleich`, `POST …/papier/einsortieren` (409 while a sync runs), `POST …/papier/zurueck`. One cloud job per profile at a time; the sync writes back only its own fields so a sheet placed meanwhile is not lost. Journal with numbers only ("remarkable: sheet read | 2 ticked, 0 unclear, 1 done"), live overview "Blatt gelesen: n angekreuzt, n unklar".

**reMarkable documents well formatted:** every document for the reMarkable reads its text through one shared reader (`rmpapier.blocks`): headings ("## …" and lines that are only "**part**"), paragraphs with single lines, bullets, numbered steps, tables, quotes, rules and code blocks; "**bold**", "*italic*", "`code`" and "[link](url)" lose their marks, a left-out emoji leaves no gap. The designed sheet (`Sheet.text`) draws sections, bullets, numbers (1. 2. 3. in the accent colour) and tables of up to 3 columns with a head row; wider ones become "column: value" lines. Small head (title 13 pt, sections 10 pt). Without "Designed sheets" the morning sheet and the weekly review come as the same sheet in black and white without boxes (`rmpapier.plain_pdf`, device format); answers, meetings, inbox, guide and web pages as EPUB with real headings, lists and tables (`remarkable.epub_body`); answers are not flattened first. Documents from "Create documents" come in the device's page format instead of A4 (`docrender.render(…, page=…)`, narrow pages with small margins and a smaller title, `docrender.SMALL_PAGE`); letters and own templates stay A4. Guard test `test_rmformat`: builds a sample sheet for every kind (`rmpapier.ARTEN`, guide, calibration sheet, document) on every model and turns red on leftover Markdown, text past the right margin, lost numbers or a new kind without a sample.

**Crosses in other units:** some sheets deliver strokes in other units than the page says (e.g. 1620 instead of 954 points across). The calibration tries every possible width (`rmpapier.widths`, `fit`), and when reading, `best_map` takes another width instead of the calibration only when the ink then lies clearly better in the boxes (twice as much and at least `INK_MIN`); handwriting alone moves nothing. The journal names page size, units and where the ink lies ("page 954x1696 … units 1620 | ink x … y …"). Self-tests `test_rmpapier.Tasks.test_ticks_in_larger_units_than_the_page_says_still_hit_their_boxes` and `test_handwriting_alone_never_moves_the_mapping`.

**"Gelesene Blätter":** of calibration and task sheets the list shows only the newest (`rmpapier.ONE_SHOWN`), sheets deleted on the device not at all, ones put away by "Kann weg" one more day (`GONE_SHOWN`); unread ones show when they were sent. Self-test `test_rmpapier.Reading.test_the_list_keeps_one_sheet_per_replaced_kind`.

**Crosses from another device:** when a sheet's page names another model than the one it was built for (one account, two devices), it never takes the other device's calibration: `rmpapier.calib_key` then gives "move@pro", without a calibration of its own the page size counts. Journal "remarkable: sheet for move written on pro". With answers side by side the control picture shows the point once above and only the answer at each box, in the sheets' own font (arrows, umlauts). Self-test `test_rmpapier.Tasks.test_a_page_from_another_model_never_takes_this_models_calibration`.

**Reasons in the log when the reMarkable cloud stalls:** the journal names reasons in fixed words and numbers, e.g. "remarkable: 15 of 75 entries not read | file HTTP 429 ×12, ReadTimeout ×3" (`remarkable.why`, `why_count`, `why_long`; the cloud's own text never reaches the journal). On 429 or 500/502/503/504 the Spark waits once (Retry-After honoured, at most 10 s) and tries again. Self-tests in `test_remarkable`.

**Task sheet to tick:** admin sub-switch "Task sheet to tick" below "Paper for the reMarkable" (`chat.remarkable_tasks`, off, never guests; code: `app/panel/rmaufgaben.py`), with no profile switch of its own: the first sheet comes by itself, after that there is always one (see "Always a task sheet" below); "Send task sheet" under Me → reMarkable → Paper or by voice fetches a new version at once. A living PDF in the folder "Spark/Aufgaben" with what is open, each point with boxes for fixed answers: tasks from the handwriting (remind tomorrow 9:00 / remove / later, `rm_todo`), memory entries "maybe outdated" or "contradiction" (true / not true / later, "Check memory"), proposals for the profile's own settings (take / no). Only for an admin profile (Admin role): open proposals of the settings assistant except stages C, U, I and P (take / reject / later, ticked = done directly; role and switches are checked again when acting) and the field test after an update (the new lines from CHANGELOG.md, works / does not work; "does not work" becomes a case under Status → Improve). The panel fixes what each answer does when it builds the sheet (`rmaufgaben.OPTS`, `_do`); the paper only picks. Evaluated at once when "Fertig – bitte auswerten" is ticked, else after 15 minutes without a change; two ticks in one point = unclear, nothing happens, the point comes again; each point acts once. The emergency stop holds everything. Then a new version with "Erledigt seit Version N" (at the earliest after 10 minutes, else every morning from the morning sheet's time); the old one with the ticks goes to the archive. "Take back" under Paper for a reminder, a deleted memory entry, a setting and an assistant proposal (`POST /api/profile/remarkable/papier/aufgaben/zurueck`, body 1 KB, rate limit `rm`); "Send task sheet" `POST /api/profile/remarkable/papier/aufgaben` (rate limit `rmsend`), both with the profile's browser login, CSRF, `guard.log`. By voice: "Leg mir das Aufgabenblatt aufs reMarkable" (`remarkable_sheet`). Journal numbers only ("remarkable: task sheet read | 1 done, 0 unclear"), live "Aufgabenblatt" as background work.

**Note on the task sheet:** below the points the sheet has four lines "Anmerkung" (`Sheet.note_area`, a field of kind `notiz`, never judged as a box). When at least 12 mm of changed handwriting lie there (`rmaufgaben.NOTE_MIN`), the Spark renders only these strokes as a picture and lets the language model read them (`wissen.read_page`, only with "Read pictures and scans"; otherwise the note waits, journal "note not read | reading pictures is off"). One line remains, without the picture description and "[unleserlich]", at most 200 characters. The text is data: it stands in the receipt ("gelesen: …") and as a point "Aus deiner Anmerkung" on the next version with remind (tomorrow 9:00) / remove / later; only the tick makes a reminder, "Take back" under Paper takes it back. When the fixed rule of "Own tasks" (`beobachten.parse`, never the model) finds a watch sentence ("Sag mir Bescheid, wenn es eine neue Firmware gibt"), watches are allowed (`agent.watch_on`) and there is room under `beobachten.MAX_WATCH`, "Als Auftrag" is added: the tick makes the watch under Me → Jobs (`created_by` "remarkable"), where it can be paused, changed and deleted; "Take back" removes it. Everything is checked again when acting; if it cannot be done, the note stays open. One note per sheet (writing on replaces it), at most 6 wait. Only strokes lying almost wholly in a box count for choosing the units; lines of handwriting move nothing. Journal numbers only ("remarkable: task sheet note read | 42 characters"), live "Anmerkung gelesen". Self-tests `test_rmpapier.Tasks.test_the_anmerkung_is_read_and_comes_back_as_a_point_never_a_command`, `test_the_anmerkung_waits_while_reading_pictures_is_off`, `test_note_text_keeps_only_what_was_written`, `test_a_note_asking_to_watch_offers_a_task_only_by_the_panels_rule`, `test_a_task_ticked_after_watches_were_switched_off_does_nothing`, `test_a_tick_and_long_lines_of_writing_never_move_the_mapping`.

**Morning sheet in sections:** the morning sheet takes the sorted daily overview (`chat.morning_briefing(..., markdown=True)`, `BRIEF_MD_SYSTEM`): per area with content a line **Termine**, **Erinnerungen**, **Mails**, **Wetter**, **Themen** with short bullets; on the sheet each becomes a numbered section (`rmpapier.heads`). Self-test `test_rmplus.SheetOnRequest.test_the_morning_sheet_is_sorted_into_sections`.

**Task sheet with five parts of one's choice:** each profile sets under Me → reMarkable → Paper → "What goes on the sheet" which parts come on its task sheet (`rmaufgaben.PARTS`, profile settings `rm_tasks_cal`, `rm_tasks_lists`, `rm_tasks_mail`, `rm_tasks_new`, `rm_tasks_note`, all on until the profile turns them off; no function switch of its own). A part only comes while its function is released for the profile, otherwise the page says what is missing. **Today in the calendar:** today's appointments still to come (at most 6, `calendars.events`) with Passt / 30 min vorher erinnern / Auf morgen; all-day ones without "remind". Only a reminder is made (30 minutes before, or "Von gestern verschoben: …" tomorrow at 9:00), the calendar stays unchanged; when the appointment is over by evaluation, the receipt says "schon vorbei". **Tasks and shopping:** up to 6 open entries per list (`tasks.items`) with a box "Erledigt" that ticks it off there (`tasks.change`, also in the iCloud calendar); "Take back" puts it back. **Waiting mail:** up to 5 unread mails of the last two days (`mail.find`) with Gelesen / Antwort entwerfen / Später. "Gelesen" only takes the mail off the sheet, the mailbox stays unchanged. "Antwort entwerfen" only when the profile may put drafts (`schreib.draft_ok`): the writing help writes the answer (the mail is data, never an order), the draft lies in the drafts folder (`tidy.make_draft`) and is never sent. **Quick note:** three lines and one tick for the kind: reminder today (18:00, after 17:00 in an hour), tomorrow (9:00), task or shopping (both only with released lists). Read like the note (`read_new`, only with "Read pictures and scans"); each line becomes one entry, at most 6. The tick is the yes: without a tick nothing happens, two ticks = unclear; "Take back" removes the entries again. **Note:** as above. When a service does not answer, only its part is missing from this version (journal "remarkable: task sheet mails not read: …"). A new version comes every morning from the morning sheet's time. The receipt shows "gelesen" instead of ✎, which the font does not have. `mail.message` is the one place that fetches a mail by id (also for `mail.read`). Journal fixed words and numbers only ("remarkable: task sheet quick note read | 2 line(s)"). Self-tests `test_rmpapier.TaskParts`.

**Always a task sheet:** there is always a sheet in the folder "Aufgaben", also when nothing is open. The first one comes by itself once the task sheet is released for the profile and a reMarkable is paired (`rmaufgaben.due` "erst"). "Kann weg" or deleting it on the device puts only this version away (trash, with handwriting the archive); at the next check, at the earliest 10 minutes after the last version, a new one lies there ("fehlt"). Every morning from the morning sheet's time a new version comes, also without changes ("morgen"), and after evaluation ("neu"). Whoever wants no sheet switches the function off. With nothing open the sheet shows (`rmaufgaben.calm`): "Alles erledigt" with the cards "0 offen · Stand DD.MM. HH:MM" and "n erledigt · seit Version N", what the Spark keeps an eye on (parts switched on: appointments, lists, mail, and the sources notes, memory, proposals), "Zuletzt erledigt" (the receipt), "Als Nächstes" (tomorrow's first appointment, only with the part appointments; "Neue Version spätestens morgen um …") and still Schnell notieren and Anmerkung. Calendar text is data and is cut and cleaned like every point. Journal only fixed words ("remarkable: task sheet | version 5, 0 point(s), why fehlt", reason from `rmaufgaben.WHY`). Self-tests `test_rmpapier.Tasks.test_kann_weg_puts_only_this_version_away_a_new_one_comes`, `test_a_new_version_every_morning_also_when_nothing_changed`, `test_nothing_open_says_so_with_the_time_and_what_comes_next`.

**Tidying the configuration:** `admin._tidy` drops, when the configuration is read, whatever the running version does not know: keys `config.default.json` does not have, presets in `chat.defaults` that `profiles.clean_settings` refuses, Melder in `berater_off` that are gone, the old entry `chat.new_profile_on` and old entries in `chat.deny` (deleted profiles, removed functions). So no save of an admin switch fails on leftovers. What the admin sends is checked strictly by `validate`. Self-test `test_features`.

**Answer onto the reMarkable:** "Summarize the political situation and put it on my reMarkable" puts the whole answer as a new document into the folder "Spark". A fixed rule on the question's own words decides this (put, send, save … plus "on/to my reMarkable", no colon), not the language model: the model only writes the answer, the panel sends it and says "The answer is now on your reMarkable …" (or why not). This also works after a web search, after which `remarkable_note` is locked; the destination is fixed, outside text only shapes the content, never where it goes. Same switches and daily limit as above, only in the own browser or at a speaker with the recognised voice, never guests. Logs → Requests shows "remarkable (Panel)".

**"Yes" to "Shall I put it on the reMarkable?":** when an answer offers that (fixed rule `remarkable.offered` on its own sentence: "Soll ich/Möchtest du/Willst du … reMarkable …?", "shall I … reMarkable?"), the panel keeps the text before it as the profile's one waiting proposal (`remarkable.propose`, `profiles.pending_set`, 15 minutes, same device and conversation). A plain "yes" (`calendars.confirms`) then puts it there like "… and put it on my reMarkable" (`send_answer`, folder "Antworten"); the model only says the result. Any other answer drops the proposal, so does the Notaus. Never after mail or other outside text, never for guests, only with "Send to the reMarkable" and a paired device. With "Show steps and approvals" the yes/no card ("reMarkable") stands below. "erstellen", "anlegen" and "machen" also count as asking for the answer there ("Create a note on the reMarkable with the recipe for cherry cake"), also spelled "Remarkabel". Tool rule 5 (`chat.TOOL_RULES`): when the user already said what they want and the tool is there, the model does it at once instead of asking, and never offers anything without a tool. Journal only fixed words ("remarkable: answer proposed | waiting for yes", "remarkable: answer sent | after yes", "remarkable: answer not sent | no yes"). Self-test `test_remarkable.AnswerToRemarkable.test_an_offered_answer_waits_for_a_plain_yes`. For "Look locally first" a plain "yes" or "no (thanks)" is never a knowledge question (`lokal.classify` → "Antwort auf Rückfrage"), and when the panel already carried out something confirmed in this turn (appointment, mail, reMarkable …) it does not look locally. Self-test `test_lokal.Rule.test_a_short_answer_to_a_question_before_looks_nothing_up`.

**My status:** under Me → My status every profile sees what the Spark does for it in the background and whether its connected services answer. Switches: Features → My day → "My status" (admin, off); released for every profile; never guests. **In the background**: reading documents (page x of y, n of 100 pages today), preparing meaning search, document cards, comparing the reMarkable, tidying the inbox (per mailbox), fetching contacts, jobs, morning briefing, tidying memory, each with its state (running, waiting, fine, needs you), the reason it waits ("paused while someone speaks", "waits for a quiet minute", "waits for the night", "daily limit reached"), last and next time, a progress bar and the history of the last 7 days (at most 50 entries per job, `users/<p>/hintergrund.json`, only for profiles with the switch). **Connected**: mailboxes, calendars (task lists too), address books, Home Assistant, Telegram, iPhone app, Pebble, speakers. A service's state is the outcome of its last real request (`netguard.client(seen=…)`, IMAP login), the page never asks itself; after a restart it says "not asked yet" until the service was needed (`state/dienste-zustand.json`). "Compare now" (reMarkable) and "Fetch now" (contacts) use the existing buttons. Only fixed words, numbers and own names (document, mailbox, calendar, device); never subjects, senders, events, document text or a server's error text (reduced to a fixed reason). When something needs the profile (sign-in refused, address not found, storage full, tidying stopped, a memory proposal waiting), a red dot shows at "Me" (asked once a minute, `?brief=1`). The page refreshes every 10 seconds. The iPhone app reads the same source (`GET /api/profile/hintergrund`, key "app", read only). Status → Summary "My status of all profiles" shows the admin counts only (`GET /api/admin/hintergrund`).

**Targeted tool choice:** a "switch" in the panel (`intent.py`) sorts every question by fixed rules into a group: smart home, calendar, mail, web search, reminder, list, weather, transit, parcel, contact, memory, document, message, agent, iPhone, small talk or "unclear". It reads only the person's own latest message, never outside text or model output, and writes one `weiche:` line per question to the journal (intent, matching word, number of tools, required tool, lock); the diagnosis filter "Chat" shows it. With the admin switch Features → Speed and accuracy → "Targeted tool choice" (off, then for every profile, never guests) the model sees only the tools of a clear question's group (plus "remember"); a group with one tool (weather, web search, transit) makes the first round call exactly that one. The switch only takes away what the rights check allowed, never more. After an answer from the web, calendar, a document or a picture, a new request in the person's own words lifts the lock and the old answer is no longer sent along; if the request points at it ("Setz das auf die Liste", "Erinner mich daran", "Und …"), everything stays locked. Mail has its own lock rule. When something is locked the model always gets the real reason in one sentence (also with the switch off). Optionally the language model sorts questions no rule recognizes into a group (only the own message, answer only from the fixed list, default off). Below the admin switch: "Show the rules" lists every group with built-in words and tools, "Try it" sends one sentence through the switch (no model, nothing stored; admin only, rate limit, at most 500 characters), and "Own words per group" (`chat.route_words`, one line per group like `smarthome: sauna, kamin`; whole words only, at most 20 per group, checked on save and restore) adds to the built-in words, never replaces them. Test bench with more than 90 example sentences: `app/tests/test_intent.py`.

**The switch shown and set up (Features → Routing):** a page of its own beside "Features" shows the switch as a picture. "Try" sends a sentence through the same rules as a real question and shows its way in six boxes: question (points back or not), checks first (small talk, about the Spark, message to someone), group (with the matching word, built in or own), tools (from the group, extra, always kept), locks (protection rules with a lock) and result (read directly, required tool, instant sentence). No model, nothing stored. Below, the groups as a list with details: built-in words, own words as chips (`chat.route_words`), **"Not with"** (`chat.route_not`: a word takes the group out again), **extra tools** (`chat.route_extra`, at most 6, only the switch's own tools and only when the question is offered them anyway) and **narrowing per group** (`chat.route_off`: group recognized but every allowed tool). All of it only takes away or stays within the rights; the protection rules (nothing switched or remembered after mail or outside text, emergency stop, at most 2 lookups) are no setting. **Test sentences:** built-in (`app/panel/intent_bench.py`, also used by `test_intent.py`) and own ones (`chat.route_tests`, at most 200, a sentence up to 200 characters with the expected group). "Check and save" runs every sentence through the new rules first; when one lands elsewhere the page lists it and saves only after "Save anyway". "Undo" puts the version before back (the last 20 in `STATE/weiche-history.json`). **"Wrongly sorted …"** comes after a try, at every switch step in Status → Logs → Requests (the question is never recorded, it is typed again) and as "Check the switch" in the hint under an answer in the admin's own chat: pick the right group, the switch suggests words of the sentence by a fixed rule (at least 4 letters, not a common word, not in any group yet) with the number of test sentences each would change; "Apply" changes the rules on the page and keeps the sentence as a test. **Statistics:** per day and group only counts, tools and direct reads (8 days, `STATE/weiche-stats.json`), never a word of the question; the live overview shows today's numbers at the switch node. Admin only (not the manager role), rate limit, body at most 40 KB, checked on save and restore. The settings form never puts older switch rules back.

**A smarter switch (Features → Routing):** the switch is measured on **everyday sentences** (`app/panel/intent_alltag.py`) that are never used to set the rules up; the test sentences in `intent_bench.py` always land right because every reported mistake became one. The page shows the **everyday score** (right, kept by a hint, open, wrong) and the sentences that do not land right; `test_intent_alltag.py` holds the numbers (good may not go down, wrong not up). **Hint words** per group (`intent.HINTS`, built in, shown at the group): when no rule recognizes a question for sure ("We need milk"), they keep their group's tools with "lean" (`chat.route_model: lean`) and beside the model's or the classification's pick. A hint never narrows and never gives more than the rights and locks. **Follow-ups** ("And tomorrow?", "Turn it off again") get the group of the person's own question just before in the same conversation: only when short (up to 80 characters), pointing back and recognized by no rule; answers or outside text never count. "Try" has a "question before" field for it. The rules recognize many everyday questions (meetings, letters from an office, the DAX, opening hours, an umbrella, "write a notice of termination" as a file, "send me … every morning" as a job); language and nature questions ("What is Fenster in English?") do not go to devices or contacts. **One place for the switch:** "Questions no rule recognizes" (`chat.route_model`), the classification's threshold (`chat.systemone_min`) with "Measure classification" and the must words (`chat.tool_words`) are only under Features → Routing and are saved there with "Check and save" (with undo); the switches "Targeted tool choice" and "Classification with probabilities" stay under Features. The live overview shows at the switch node how many questions had a hint today and how many were follow-ups.

**Tune the switch** (`chat.route_tune` below "Targeted tool choice", off; admin only; `panel/weiche_abstimmen.py`): on *Features → Routing* "Tune" starts a run. The everyday sentences are split by a fixed rule: even places are the **practice half** (plus the admin's own test sentences), odd ones the **check half**. For each group with at least 2 missed sentences of the practice half (at most 8 groups) the language model proposes up to 6 words (JSON; sentences as data in `<<< >>>`, the question goes through `vorrang` behind every conversation). Each word is measured in memory and stays only when no test sentence flips (`weiche.flips`: `intent_bench.BENCH` and the own ones), the practice half gets at least one more right and none more wrong and the check half none fewer right and none more wrong. The result is a proposal with the numbers of both halves; "Apply" saves through the switch's normal save (`chat.route_words`, the same checks, undo) and only when the words did not change since the run. Only the admin's own words change, never the built-in rules (`test_intent_alltag` measures without them). The check half is the honest number. The model decides nothing, it only proposes words. Log only `weiche: abstimmen …` with numbers. Endpoints `GET|POST|DELETE /api/admin/weiche/abstimmen`, `POST /api/admin/weiche/abstimmen/uebernehmen`; self-test `test_weiche_abstimmen`.

**Unclear questions, anonymous** (`chat.route_unklar` below "Targeted tool choice", off; preference `unklar_anon` per profile, on, under *Me → Conversation → Privacy*; `panel/weiche_unklar.py`): when no rule recognizes a question of a profile, it shows on *Features → Routing* at "unclear" in the list "Unclear questions, anonymous". Redaction is fixed rules only: first the redactor of "Report a problem" (names of profiles, devices and accounts, mail addresses, links, host names, IP and MAC addresses, phone numbers, coordinates, long codes, every secret from the settings), then names from the own address book, a name after "Herr/Frau/Dr." and every number (`[PERSON]`, `[ZAHL]` …). Then the leak check of "Report a problem" runs again; if it finds anything, the question is dropped and only counted. Sentences with the code word, from guests or an unknown voice at a speaker are never kept. Only the redacted sentence is kept with count and day, never profile, device or time of day; next to it the group whose tool the model took afterwards (from `weiche.learn`). The file `state/weiche-unklar.json` is sealed, at most 100 sentences, gone after 7 days. "Belongs to …" opens "Wrongly sorted" with the sentence and the model's group: words checked against all test sentences, "Apply", "As test sentence", Undo; a placeholder is never offered as a word. Afterwards the sentence is gone. One by one or "Delete all" (`DELETE /api/admin/weiche/unklar[/{id}]`, admin log), only admin and co-admin. Live shows only the number at the switch node. Log only `weiche: unklare Frage anonym gemerkt|verworfen (Prüfung)` and `… gelöscht <number>`. Self-test `test_weiche_unklar`.

**Switch check:** „Terminal“ does not count as an appointment. A price or a strike goes to the web search, „Call me“ to the Spark calling back, „a list of …“ in a file stays with „Create documents“, „difference between …“ is knowledge. The everyday sentences also measure the telephone, chats, pictures, the state and tools a module offers only by their own word (volume, undo, pause notes, NAS, reMarkable): such a sentence counts as right when the module's fixed word rule matches. A photo with a wish to change it („Remove the beard“) shows under Logs → Requests as the group pictures („Foto ändern“); the model does not have to pick. **Learning:** when no rule recognizes a question and the model calls a tool of one group, the switch counts that group and the fixed hint word that named it (never a word of the question, 7 days). A hint word that led to the same group at least three times is offered under Features → Routing, „Statistics“, as a word of that group; when the admin takes it over, „Check and save“ with all test sentences and Undo applies. The switch never changes its rules by itself.

**A more precise switch** (no switches of its own; everything under Features → Routing): **normalization** before every rule (`intent.normalize`, also for the must words in `chat.needed`): a word without umlauts or with "ss" ("Strassenbahn", "Kueche", "Haustuer") gets the spelling of the word lists (compared with `homeassistant._norm`, no second helper), a glued device word is set apart ("Wohnzimmerlampe", "Deckenlicht", "Heizlüfter"; never "Taschenlampe", "Tageslicht", "Schulpflicht"), a few English phrases get the German words ("Search the internet", "What do you know about me?", "What are my tasks?"). **Knowledge questions** ("What is a lamp?", "How does a socket work?", "Explain …", "What is a thermostat?"), a joke, poem or story about a thing and "I am in the kitchen" get no acting group (smart home, iPhone, calendar, documents, agent, list, contacts, messages, memory; `intent.KNOW_DROP`), so web search and Wikipedia stay; with an own thing or a room ("How does my heating work?") the group stays. The Spark help answers "What is a calendar?" only when the Spark itself is meant (`hilfe.SPARK_NAMED`). "List" alone only counts with an own or place word ("on the list", "my DIY list"), never "Give me a list of plants". **Calls:** an emergency number ("Call 112", police, fire brigade, emergency doctor) gets no tool (reason "Notruf", the person dials it), a call for later or on a condition ("at 6", "tomorrow", "when I am home") is a reminder, never the instant call; "Call it a day" calls nobody. **Follow-up** only with a real reference back ("And …", "again", "too", "of it"; never a bare "it", "there" or "her": "What time is it?" after the weather is no weather question) and only up to 5 minutes after the own question before in this conversation (`intent.asked_age`, memory only, at most 2,000 conversations; `chat_turn` passes the time in, the rule reads no clock); otherwise the journal says "weiche: Folgefrage zu alt". **One source:** one list of switching verbs (`SWITCH_VERB`, "Alles aus" counts, "Mach die Sauna an" does not), `CALL_ME` once (the reminder words build on it), "Bescheid" only unambiguous (an official notice only as a compound or with the office → documents, "Sag Anna Bescheid" → messages), the function names for "Switch on web search" compiled once into one expression. The computer's tools (pc_action, pc_send, pc_look) stay when narrowing only when the own words name the computer or the question came from the companion (`begleiter.offer` "keep", `werkzeug_abdeckung.BY_WORD`). **Counter check** (`intent_bench.GEGEN`, 101 sentences: knowledge questions with everyday words, negations, small talk, stop and cancel, calls) that must get no group or not a given one: 1 wrong, 0 forced; `test_intent.Genauer` holds both numbers (they may only go down). `python3 app/panel/intent_bench.py` (in `app/`) prints test sentences, everyday sentences, counter check, confusions per group (expected → got) and wrong sentences; Features → Routing shows the counter check beside the everyday sentences. Test sentences 259/259, everyday sentences 204 right, 11 kept, 1 open, 0 wrong.

**Source bench and fixes** (no switches of its own): **bench** `app/panel/quellen_bench.py`: 66 sentences (German and English, with traps like „How do I put my iPhone into flight mode?“ and questions about own data without a word of ownership) with the expected class (internal, external, both) and the sources needed (mail, attachment, document, appointment, contact, conversation, list, knowledge book, web). `measure()` follows today's way (switch, narrowing over every tool, „Local first“, every switch released) and counts how often class and sources are right; `test_quellen_bench` holds the floor, it never goes down (now 55 of 66, 83.3 %: internal 28/34, external 16/17, both 11/15, 1 mail question sent outside). **Rules:** own mails are a normal source for questions about own things, not only when asked for mails; the documents hint names Find for own things beside `document_search` for the wording. `fund_suche` always stays when narrowing if it is offered (`intent.NARROW_KEEP`; only the own profile, the own browser or the owner's voice, never guests, strangers' voices or MCP). A parcel word with the own mail („What did Thomann write to me about the delivery?“) is the mail; with an own order („my order“) it does not count as a question outside for „Local first“. „What does … cost according to the offer, invoice or contract“ is an own paper, never the web search. Reading questions without a search word and another group: „Did the bank write to me?“ (a named sender) becomes „mail“, „Did the plumber get back to me?“ and „What did I order from Thomann?“ become „finden“, never with a newspaper, the web or the archive; „Did anyone write to me?“ stays with the hint words. A question with a group of own data (mail, calendar, contact, Find …) does not count as a question outside for „Local first“ because of „today“. Mails stay outside text (wrapped, mail lock unchanged). The decision internal/external/both is made by the source switch (below).

**Source switch and all sources:** no switches of its own; it hangs on „Look locally first“ (Features → Web search → Look locally first) and for the sources on Find (Features → Find in all data). After the switch, for knowledge and reading questions the **source switch** (`app/panel/quellen.py`, `quellen.klasse`) decides by fixed rules, never the model: **internal** (own source, group of own data, word of ownership like „my insurance“, document word), **external** (weather, prices, news, offices, years) or **both** (timeless knowledge, how-to questions about the own device). Otherwise a **hit probe** in the own Find index decides (`fund.suche`, at most 3 hits in 150 ms, own thread, never on the event loop, at most 2 at a time): strong (at least 60 % and 2 search words, or sender, company, person named) internal, weak or too slow both, none external. The probe runs only for the own profile in the own browser or with the owner's voice, never for guests, strangers' voices, Telegram without personal data or MCP; a collection of the conversation holds. Without Find no probe (then documents, Kiwix, earlier conversations; the model decides about the web). **Internal** and **both** ask every released source through Find at once (mails and attachments, appointments, contacts, documents, NAS, reMarkable, conversations, lists, knowledge book), plus documents and earlier conversations, for both the Kiwix, within the time limit of „Look locally first“. At most 5 hits go to the model, taking turns, with source and date as outside text; a mail or attachment among them locks the answer like a read mail (nothing to the internet with the model's words, no switching, saving, reminders). **For both** the panel searches the web itself with the question's own words (no code word, at most 120 characters) before any own data is read, so no mail text reaches the web; the result (at most 2000 characters) comes with sources under the answer, the model's same search does not run twice. Not with the kill switch, after an answer from mail or while the service watch sees the web search as gone. A clear reading request the panel fetches itself needs no look before it. **Bench:** `test_quellen_bench` with a fixed stand-in index, 66 of 66, floor 66 (an upper bound; new misses come in as new sentences). **See:** Logs → area „Knowledge“ (`lokal: Klasse intern (Probe stark) | Probe 3 Treffer 42 ms | Dokumente 1, Verlauf 0, Finden 2 | 120 ms | weiter: Treffer an das Modell, Mail gelesen`), Logs → Requests „Erst lokal (Panel)“, Status → Live at the node Find, Status → Checks → „Test looking locally first“ (never the web). Self-tests `test_quellen`, `test_quellen_bench`, `test_lokal`.

**Origin and „Also search the web“:** hangs on „Look locally first“. With own hits the panel counts (`lokal.herkunft`, never the model) a **line of origin** under the answer, only kinds and numbers („From: 2 mails, 1 document, 1 appointment“, maybe „, the web“), never titles, senders or words; with Find hits also **„Show in Find“**. Was the question **internal** and without the web, **„Also search the web“** appears: the panel keeps only the own words (`lokal.own_words`, next message on the same device, at most 15 minutes) and searches exactly those on the web (`chat.web_search_own`) without looking at own data again. **Never** after a mail (`chat.LOCKED_AFTER_MAIL`), at the emergency stop, without available web search, for guests, other voices at a shared speaker or MCP. Panel, Android, iPhone, Begleiter and Telegram show line and buttons; the line never goes into the history. Live (Find): „Herkunft N-mal, Internet-Chip N angeboten, N genutzt“. Log (Knowledge): `lokal: Herkunft N Treffer | Internet-Chip angeboten` or `| kein Internet-Chip`, `lokal: Klasse extern (Internet-Chip) | Web 1 | …`, `lokal: Internet-Chip abgelehnt (Websuche gesperrt)`. Self-tests `test_quellen.Herkunft`, `test_quellen.Chip`, practice test P769.

**Source note** (Features → Conversation → "Source note", switch `chat.source_note`, off; released for every profile; `panel/quellenangabe.py`): under every answer that wants to know something it says where it came from, written by the panel with fixed rules from what really ran in this answer, never by the model: every reading tool that did not fail, with its readable name (`werkzeuge.label`, "Web search", "Read appointments", "Own documents"), plus the hits of "Look locally first" as kinds and numbers ("2 mails", "own data, no hits"). Without any source: "Source: nothing looked up, model knowledge only". Tools that change something (`taten.KIND`) do not count; a plain action ("light on") gets no line. Whether a tool reads the own data or the world comes from the switch's groups (`intent.GROUPS` with `quellen.OWN_GROUPS` and `WORLD_GROUPS`). **Note when the source does not fit:** the source switch's class (from "Look locally first" or, without it, the same fixed rules without the probe) against the sources that ran: internal without own data "Note: not looked up in your own data", external without the web, archive, weather or trains "Note: not looked up on the web". The line comes in the same event as the line of origin of "Look locally first" and replaces it, so panel, Android, iPhone, Begleiter and Telegram show it without a change of their own. **"Where did you get that?"** ("Source?", "What was your source?", "Woher weißt du das?", only as the whole message) is answered by the panel without the model from the last answer of this profile on this device (only in memory, 30 minutes): every source with the call's search words, the titles of the hits and the web pages, spoken without list marks; titles and search words without markup, the answer is marked like one from outside text (the next turn starts locked). **Say the source in conversation** (preference `quelle_sagen`, on, Me → Conversation → "Say the source"): at a speaker, the phone, the watch, via Siri and in the car one short sentence at the end ("Source: Web search."). Never for guests, other voices or MCP. **See:** Logs → Requests shows "Source: …" on every request (tool names only, the note in orange); Logs → area "Conversation" `quelle: Zeile N Quellen | Hinweis keiner|intern|extern | gesagt|nicht gesagt`, `quelle: Nachfrage beantwortet`, `quelle: keine Zeile (keine Wissensfrage)`; Status → Live, box Source note: lines, notes, questions and spoken sentences of today. Self-test `test_quellenangabe`, practice tests P853, P854.

**Help with the Spark (off by default):** questions about the Spark itself the assistant answers with `spark_hilfe` from guides, switches, own settings under Me, fixed recommendations and these docs (`app/panel/hilfe_docs.json`, built by `python3 app/panel/hilfe.py --docs`). The search runs by fixed rules on the Spark, nothing leaves the house; each hit comes with its path and how it stands for you. Rights: Spark settings, Spark switches, "Security at a glance" and operating docs only for admins (a manager only feature switches); profiles get guides without admin steps and their own settings; guests nothing. Hits are data, never instructions; keys, PINs and names of other profiles never appear. "Suggesting settings" (`spark_vorschlag`) shows a card with switches before/after. The model never switches: fixed rules check (only switches for the assistant's own work, never security, nothing to the outside; profiles only their own, Spark switches only admin, switching off never for everybody), and only "Apply" changes through `POST /api/hilfe/karte/{id}` (browser login, CSRF, rate limit, for Spark switches admin and code, admin log with undo). A card lasts ten minutes, once, only for this profile; "Undo" ten minutes. After outside text in the same answer no card, the Notaus from stage 2 holds applying off, outside the browser only the path. "Ask Spark on every page" in the face at the bottom right sends the current page along (only an id from a fixed list); it is the same conversation as the chat. Switches: Features → "Help with the Spark" (below it "Suggesting settings" and "Ask Spark on every page"), released for every profile. Log `hilfe:`; Status → Live: service "Spark-Hilfe" and events, never contents. Tests: `app/tests/test_hilfe.py`.

**Better help answers:** the switch recognises questions about the Spark by fixed rules before any model (also in English) and sends them to `spark_hilfe`; questions about own data stay with their tools. The search understands everyday words (backup, password, phone, wake word), knows every page and has fixed answers (kill switch, my kill switch, inviting, backup, logs, PIN, passkeys). The answer has blocks: what, where, how it stands for you, next step; a profile only gets ways it can see. The **origin** (panel, face, apps, Telegram, speaker, watch, Siri, MCP) the panel knows only from login, device key, app marker and page, never from the question; it never grants more rights and only changes the form; over MCP there is no help. On a settings page every question through the face goes to the help unless it asks for own data. A **"?"** at every switch (with "Ask Spark on every page") asks about exactly that switch. Logs → Requests shows "Origin". Quality test: `app/tests/test_hilfe_quality.py` (61 questions).

**Help finds e-mail and Live:** "Wie bekomme ich Mails vorgelesen?" shows "E-Mail lesen" first, "Wo sehe ich die Live-Übersicht?" shows "Status → Live". For this the help search knows two words (`hilfe.SYN`): "vorgelesen" means "mail lesen" like "vorlesen", "Live" also searches "Zustand". The update installs only versions with a green GitHub run "Tests".

**The face at the bottom right everywhere:** the sign "Ask Spark" at the top is there for every login (not for guests) on every page, over Me and in the phone view, also without the Spark help. One tap starts listening and opens the window with "Open conversation", "Stop" and a field to type in; a second tap stops listening, the second tap of a double tap does not count. Keyboard: tab to the sign, then Enter or Space. The current page only goes along with "Ask Spark on every page". The wall monitor (`/live`) stays a pure display without a face.

**Folding in the face's window:** the × at the top right of the window or Escape folds it in, also while "Set up" runs (its steps come back the next time it opens, "Done" ends them). Listening and speaking go on. On narrow screens (below 760 pixels wide or 600 pixels high, also in the iPhone app) the window takes at most half the height and scrolls inside; the × stays visible at the top.

**Suggesting functions (off by default):** the assistant points out a switched-off function when it would help, and helps setting it up. Fixed rules in `tipps.py` decide, never the model: a wish without its tool (group recognized, function off), half set up (e.g. iPhone app without notifications, second sign-in step without passkey), repetition (e.g. five web searches in seven days without "Look locally first"; only the kind is counted, never the words), newly released and to set up yourself under Me → Let's go. The hint goes once it is set up or "Check" reports "Set up", at the latest 14 days after the release. A plain profile setting comes as an "Apply" card (as in "Suggesting settings", ten minutes "Undo"), otherwise the guide's steps and "Open the page"; passwords only into the form. If it is off on the Spark, "Ask the admin" sends the admins a notification (at most once a day). Me → Overview shows at most three suggestions; under Proactive at most one a week as a notification (10 am to 7 pm, never in quiet hours). At most one hint per conversation and three a week, "Do not show again" is for good; none after text from mail or the web, under the emergency stop, at a speaker, watch or Siri, on Telegram, over MCP, never for guests or a device key. Stored per profile only shown and declined hints and question counts per kind (`tipps.json`, 14 days). Switches: Features → below "Help with the Spark" "Suggesting functions" (below it "… also by itself"); can be switched off under Me → By itself → "Suggest a function". Log: lines `tipps:`; Logs → Requests `hinweis`; Status → Live events "Suggesting functions", never contents. Tests: `app/tests/test_tipps.py`.

**Show steps and approvals (off by default):** below every answer a folded "3 tools used · 2.1 s", unfolded per tool ✓ or ⚠ and time; only name (`werkzeuge.NAMES`), success (`taten.failed`) and milliseconds, never inputs or findings. When a proposal waits for a "Ja", a card "Waiting for your yes: Appointment" with "Yes" and "No" stands below; a tap sends the word like a typed answer, the proposing module decides by fixed rules (`calendars.confirms` …). The card carries nothing out and names only the kind (`ablauf.KINDS`); at most one per answer, only this device and conversation, never under the emergency stop, never for guests or other voices. Panel, Android and iPhone app (events `step`, `confirm`). Switch: Features → "Show steps and approvals". Log `chat: freigabe-karte gezeigt | n`. Tests: `app/tests/test_ablauf.py`.

**Question cards:** part of "Show steps and approvals".
